## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [ProLaViT: Learning Progressive Latent Visual Thoughts in Structured Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02907)
*Peiming Li, Yifan Wang, Xiaotian Zhang, Zhiyuan Hu, Shiyu Li, Zheng Wei, Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: ProLaViT 提出了一种在连续潜空间中进行渐进式视觉思维推理的框架，通过自蒸馏机制和程序化合成数据，让 MLLM 在潜空间中执行结构化视觉推导，无需外部模型或显式生成，在视觉推理任务上取得高效且可解释的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在复杂视觉推理任务中表现不足，显式视觉生成计算成本过高，而现有的潜空间方法依赖外部专家模型或缺乏严谨的认知逻辑。作者希望让 MLLM 在连续潜空间中自主进行结构化、多步的视觉推理。

Method: 提出 ProLaViT 框架，核心包括：(1) 内生自蒸馏机制，利用模型自身的视觉编码器监督潜空间中的视觉思维；(2) 可扩展的程序化合成数据流水线，让模型内化算法精度；(3) 两种推理范式：Coarse-to-Fine Causal Chain（空间任务）和 Dialectical Reasoning Chain（逻辑任务，含反事实验证）；(4) Distance-Weighted Diversity Loss，施加拓扑感知约束，防止特征退化。

Result: 在多个视觉推理基准上，ProLaViT 优于基线方法，在准确率和可解释性上均有提升，同时保持了较高的推理效率。

Conclusion: ProLaViT 证明了在连续潜空间中进行结构化视觉推理的有效性，为 MLLM 的视觉推理提供了一种高效、可解释且无需外部工具的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ProLaViT，在连续潜空间中进行渐进式视觉推理（visual thought），属于多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning 的范畴，与 canonical subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent reasoning for MLLMs, a top-priority subtopic, with a novel self-distillation framework. Signals: continuous latent visual thought, self-distillation mechanism, structured latent space reasoning, coarse-to-fine and dialectical reasoning chains, programmatic synthesis pipeline

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress but still struggle with complex visual reasoning tasks requiring multi-step perception and logical deduction. While explicit visual generation incurs prohibitive computational costs, existing latent approaches often rely on external experts or lack rigorous cognitive logic. In this paper, we introduce ProLaViT (Progressive Latent Visual Thought), a framework empowering MLLMs to perform structured visual derivation in the continuous latent space. Unlike works dependent on heterogeneous external models, ProLaViT leverages an endogenous self-distillation mechanism, utilizing the model's own visual encoder to supervise latent thoughts. To facilitate this, we construct a scalable programmatic synthesis pipeline enabling the model to internalize algorithmic precision without inference time tools. We design two reasoning paradigms: (1) Coarse-to-Fine Causal Chain for spatial tasks, guiding attention from global context to local targets. (2) Dialectical Reasoning Chain for logical tasks, incorporating counter-factual thinking for verification. Furthermore, we propose a Distance-Weighted Diversity Loss to impose topology-aware constraints, preventing feature degeneration by enforcing semantic distinctiveness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ProLaViT outperforms baselines on vision-centric benchmarks, achieving superior accuracy and interpretability with high efficiency.

</details>

## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [2] [RADIO1D: Elastic Representations for Condensed Vision Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03624)
*Greg Heinrich, Mike Ranzinger, Collin McCarthy, Natan Bagrov, Eugene Khvedchenya, Bryan Catanzaro, Jan Kautz, Andrew Tao, Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 值得关注 (74.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RADIO1D 通过多教师知识蒸馏和自编码器设计，将图像压缩为紧凑的可变长度 1D token 序列，在 VLM 中提供灵活的精度-效率权衡，以更低的计算开销实现有竞争力的多模态基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战 VLM 必须使用固定 patch 式 2D 视觉特征的假设，发现 VLM 训练中视觉编码器表征变得抽象且空间一致性降低，因此希望设计更紧凑、灵活的视觉表征。

Method: 通过多教师知识蒸馏（结合 SigLIP2、DINOv2 等）和自编码器架构，将图像编码为可变长度的 1D token 序列，支持从单 token 到多 token 的灵活配置。

Result: 单 token 即可实现准确的场景理解；支持可组合的图像检索；在 VLM 中通过可调节 token 数量提供灵活的精度-效率权衡，在多个多模态基准上以更低计算开销取得有竞争力性能。

Conclusion: RADIO1D 验证了紧凑 1D 视觉表征可以替代传统 2D patch 特征，为高效 VLM 提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RADIO1D，将图像压缩为紧凑、可变长度的 1D token 序列，使用多教师知识蒸馏和自编码器设计，属于连续视觉 tokenizer 与表征自编码器方向，与 canonical subtopic "continuous_visual_tokenizer" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous visual tokenization with variable-length 1D tokens, enabling flexible accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs for VLMs, a core priority subtopic. Signals: continuous visual token, representation autoencoder, variable-length 1D token, multi-teacher distillation, VLM efficiency

Abstract: This paper challenges the assumption that vision-language models (VLMs) require fixed patch-based 2D vision features. Analyzing fine-tuned vision encoders, we find that representations become increasingly abstract and less spatially coherent during VLM training. Notably, models trained with image-text alignment (such as SigLIP2) develop a small number of specialized tokens that effectively summarize global image content. Building on this, we introduce RADIO1D, which compresses images into a compact, variable-length 1D token sequence using multi-teacher knowledge distillation and an autoencoder design. The resulting representations exhibit strong hierarchical summarization, enabling accurate scene understanding - even with a single token - and support improved composition-aware image retrieval. In VLMs, RADIO1D provides flexible accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs through adjustable token counts, delivering competitive performance on diverse multimodal benchmarks with lower computational overhead and better accuracy.

</details>

## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [3] [Training Hybrid Block Diffusion Language Models with Partial Bidirectionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02805)
*Pranshu Chaturvedi, Parth Shroff, Tarun Suresh, Hangoo Kang, Kaiyue Wen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出BDLM Mamba-Hybrid模型，通过将反向Mamba扫描限制在活跃去噪块内，实现了块间精确缓存，解决了混合扩散语言模型中双向Mamba与因果生成不兼容的问题。在87M和350M参数规模上达到有竞争力的困惑度，并在长上下文推理中相比基线实现19.7倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文生成中，自回归解码受限于内存带宽而非计算能力，因为每一步都需要流式读取累积的KV缓存。块扩散生成和高效注意力/Mamba是两种并行方案，但将它们结合存在技术挑战：早期混合扩散模型（如DiffuMamba）使用双向Mamba混合，其反向扫描需要扫描整个序列，无法在块级扩散中精确复用缓存。

Method: 提出BDLM Mamba-Hybrid架构，将反向Mamba扫描限制在当前活跃的去噪块内，而非整个序列，从而支持跨块的精确缓存复用。模型结合了Mamba的高效线性时间混合与注意力机制，在块扩散框架下进行语言建模。

Result: 在87M参数DCLM实验中，BDLM Mamba-H在C4-en验证集上达到最佳困惑度。在350M参数下与BDLM注意力基线竞争力相当。长上下文推理中，在65K token时相比全序列DiffuMamba-H实现19.7倍吞吐量提升，在262K token时相比BDLM注意力实现3.7倍吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: Mamba混合架构是长上下文扩散语言模型的有潜力的架构方向，通过限制反向扫描范围实现了高效的块级缓存复用，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升长序列推理吞吐量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出混合块扩散语言模型（BDLM），结合Mamba与注意力机制进行连续语言建模中的块级扩散生成，属于连续语言模型与语言流（continuous_language_flow）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model efficiency via hybrid diffusion with Mamba, aligning with top-priority subtopic and boost keywords. Signals: continuous language model, hybrid diffusion, Mamba, long-context inference, block caching

Abstract: High-throughput long-context generation is one of the central challenges for large language models. Generation is typically memory-bandwidth-bound rather than compute-bound: each decoding step must stream the accumulated key/value (KV) cache from memory, so bandwidth demand grows with context length while only one token is emitted. Two parallel approaches have therefore emerged: reducing memory access with efficient attention variants and linear-time mixers such as Mamba, or increasing parallel computation by generating blocks of tokens at once. However, technical challenges arise when combining these two ideas. Earlier hybrid diffusion models such as DiffuMamba use bidirectional Mamba mixing, including a reverse-direction scan relative to causal generation. This reverse scan needs to scan the entire sequence, so its states are not prefix-only and cannot be precisely reused as a cache even when diffusion is performed block by block. We propose a BDLM Mamba--attention hybrid that addresses this challenge by restricting the reverse Mamba scan to the active denoising block, which enables exact caching across blocks. In an 87M-parameter DCLM sweep, BDLM Mamba-H achieves the best C4-en validation perplexity compared to BDLM attention and full-sequence baselines. At 350M parameters, it remains competitive with BDLM attention. For long-context inference, BDLM Mamba-H reaches 19.7x the throughput of full-sequence DiffuMamba-H at 65K tokens and 3.7x the throughput of BDLM attention at 262K, showing that Mamba hybrids are a potential long-context diffusion architecture.

</details>


### [4] [dOPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04428)
*Phuong Tuan Dat, Qi Li, Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 dOPSD，一种用于扩散语言模型（dLLM）的在策略自蒸馏方法。通过让教师模型从学生自身的去噪轨迹中获取特权信息（利用更晚解码步骤的信息来评估掩码位置），而非依赖外部标签，dOPSD 在 Dream 和 LLaDA 模型上显著提升了数学推理和代码生成能力，优于监督微调和在策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过迭代去噪掩码序列生成文本，是一种有前景的并行生成范式。然而，如何通过后训练（post-training）激发其强推理能力仍然困难：监督微调是离策略的，存在曝光偏差；强化学习仅提供稀疏的序列级奖励，且难以在没有可处理序列似然的情况下应用。现有在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法虽提供密集的 token 级监督，但教师需要特权信息（如真实标签），导致学生蒸馏出的策略提升有限。

Method: 本文提出 dOPSD，核心创新在于教师模型的特权信息来源于学生自身的去噪轨迹：在去噪过程的某个时间步，教师利用同一轨迹中更晚、更解码充分的步骤的信息来评估当前掩码位置，而非依赖外部标签。这样，教师的优势来自模型自身的解码过程，实现了真正的在策略自蒸馏。该方法在 Dream 和 LLaDA 两种扩散语言模型上进行了验证。

Result: 在 Dream 和 LLaDA 模型上，dOPSD 在领域内数学推理任务和领域外代码生成任务上均取得了显著提升，优于监督微调（SFT）和在策略基线方法。

Conclusion: dOPSD 通过从模型自身去噪轨迹中提取特权信息，有效解决了扩散语言模型后训练中的在策略监督问题，为提升 dLLM 的推理能力提供了一种无需外部标签的高效方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（dLLM）的自蒸馏方法，属于连续语言模型范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow），该 subtopic 涵盖扩散语言模型、flow matching language model 等连续语言建模方法，与本论文主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model training with a novel on-policy self-distillation method that improves reasoning in diffusion LLMs, a core priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, on-policy self-distillation, reasoning improvement, privileged information from own trajectory

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising a masked sequence, offering a parallel alternative to autoregressive models, but eliciting strong reasoning through post-training remains difficult: supervised fine-tuning is off-policy and suffers from exposure bias, while reinforcement learning gives only sparse, sequence-level rewards and is hard to apply without tractable sequence likelihoods. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising alternative, using one model as both student and teacher to provide dense, token-level, on-policy supervision, but its effectiveness hinges on giving the teacher privileged information (PI) - typically an instance-specific ground-truth reference unavailable at inference - so the student ends up distilling a weak PI-free consensus policy that yields little improvement on dLLM reasoning. We introduce dOPSD, which instead derives the teacher's privilege directly from the student's own denoising trajectory, evaluating masked positions using later, more-decoded steps of that same trajectory rather than an external label, so the teacher's advantage emerges from the model's own decoding process; on Dream and LLaDA, dOPSD improves both in-domain math reasoning and out-of-domain code generation, outperforming supervised and on-policy baselines.

</details>


### [5] [Full Glyph Images Beat Token Embeddings: A Controlled Study for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03994)
*Shuyang Xiang, Hao Guan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (76.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文挑战了语言模型中离散 token embedding 的默认假设，提出用字符序列的光栅化图像替代索引式 token embedding，通过视觉编码器（ResNet+ViT）处理。在受控双分支框架下，基于视觉的模型在所有测试解码器上均优于基于索引的基线，准确率提升21%，收敛速度更快，且对字符损坏具有鲁棒性。该优势在中文上显著，但在英文上不直接迁移，表明汉字均匀的视觉密度和部首结构是使能条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型普遍将文本表示为离散 token embedding 序列，这一假设很少被质疑。作者希望挑战这一表示方式，探究是否可以用连续的视觉表征（字符图像）替代离散 token 进行语言建模，从而揭示 Transformer 对输入模态的无关性。

Method: 构建双分支受控框架：Vision-based 模型用共享 ResNet 和浅层 ViT 编码字符序列的光栅化图像，index-based 基线使用标准 token embedding。两个分支共享完全相同的解码器骨干、训练目标、优化器和数据课程，确保性能差异仅来自输入模态。在中文和英文上分别进行实验，并进行了消融研究。

Result: Vision-based 模型在所有解码器骨干上一致优于基线，峰值准确率 0.429 vs 0.355（相对提升21%），收敛所需 epoch 数约为基线的一半。优势在前5个 epoch（总数据21%以内）即显现，且在中等字符损坏下仍能保持（损坏的 Vision 模型匹配干净的 index 基线）。消融表明优势需要空间连贯输入和带2D位置编码的 ViT。英文实验显示该优势不直接迁移到字母文字系统。

Conclusion: Transformer 比通常假设的更具模态无关性，离散 tokenization 并非中文语言建模的基本要求。汉字均匀的视觉密度和部首结构是视觉语言建模优势的关键使能条件。

Classification reason: 该论文用连续视觉表征（光栅化字符图像）替代离散 token embedding 进行语言建模，属于连续语言模型范畴，与 canonical 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly challenges discrete tokenization for Chinese language modeling using continuous visual input, aligning with your top-weighted continuous language model subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, visual token embedding, Chinese character modeling, modality-agnostic transformer, controlled dual-branch framework

Abstract: Modern language models generally represent text as sequences of discrete token embeddings, an assumption deeply rooted in current practice but rarely questioned. We challenge this representation, especially for Chinese, by replacing index-based token embeddings entirely with a single rasterized image of the character sequence, processed by a vision encoder composed of a shared ResNet and a shallow Vision Transformer. To isolate the role of input representation, we construct a dual-branch controlled framework in which both a Vision-based model and an index-based baseline share an identical decoder backbone, training objective, optimizer, and data curriculum. Any performance difference is therefore attributable to the input modality only. Across all tested decoder backbones, the Vision-based model consistently outperforms the baseline, reaching a peak accuracy of 0.429 versus 0.355 for the index-based baseline,that is, a 21% relative improvement, while converging in about half the number of training epochs. The advantage emerges especially within the first five epochs (under 21% of total data) and persists under moderate character corruption: the corrupted Vision model matches the clean index-based baseline. Ablation studies reveal that the advantage requires both spatially coherent input and a ViT encoder with 2D positional encodings. A cross-script comparison on English shows the advantage does not transfer directly to alphabetic writing systems, suggesting that the uniform visual density and radical structure of Chinese characters are enabling conditions. These findings suggest that transformers are more modality-agnostic than commonly assumed, and that discrete tokenization is not a fundamental requirement for Chinese language modeling.

</details>

### [6] [iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02542)
*Yuan Zhang, Jingfei Ni, Guanchen Lu, Shiqi Zhang, Qingshan Xu, Chi Liu, Xin Nie, Wenjie Xu, Lin Gao, Zhiyuan Cheng, Mingxin Zhou, Jiajia Wu, Diyuan Liu, Jia Pan, Chao Ji*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni 是一个统一的多模态基础模型，通过共享多模态自注意力机制联合建模视觉（视频和图像）、语言和动作。其设计采用"脑-小脑协作"架构：VLM和视频生成模型作为高层"大脑"负责指令理解、任务规划与未来状态预测，动作生成模型作为低层"小脑"将子目标转换为可执行动作。采用四阶段训练策略，结合动作标注和无标注的具身视频数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体方法通常将视觉-语言推理、视频世界建模和动作生成分离为独立模块，级联流水线在接口处存在瓶颈且会累积预测误差。需要一种统一框架来联合建模多模态输入、环境动态和动作输出，实现端到端的具身任务执行。

Method: 提出 iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni 统一多模态基础模型，包含三个核心组件：视觉语言模型（VLM）、视频生成模型（VGM）和动作生成模型（AGM），三者通过共享多模态自注意力机制通信。采用"脑-小脑协作"架构：VLM+VGM作为高层脑负责指令理解、任务规划、进度跟踪和未来视觉状态预测；AGM作为低层小脑将子目标和多模态上下文转换为动作块。构建包含动作标注和无标注的具身视频数据集（人类演示和机器人交互），结合具身推理、具身感知和通用图文数据。采用四阶段训练策略：依次训练VLM、VGM、AGM，最后联合微调整个模型。

Result: 论文为技术报告，未提供定量实验结果，但描述了模型架构设计、数据构建和训练策略的完整方案。

Conclusion: iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni 通过统一的多模态框架和脑-小脑协作设计，为通用具身智能体提供了一种端到端的解决方案，有望克服级联流水线的瓶颈问题，实现更鲁棒的指令理解、环境预测和动作生成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一多模态基础模型，联合建模视觉、语言和动作，用于具身智能体的任务理解、未来状态预测和动作生成，核心属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。同时其VLM+视频生成+动作生成的统一架构也涉及多模态理解与生成一体化，因此匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model, unified multimodal, and embodied reasoning priorities, with a novel brain-cerebellum architecture. Signals: world model, unified multimodal, embodied agent, video generation, action generation

Abstract: General-purpose embodied agents must understand multimodal instructions, anticipate how their environment will evolve, and produce precise control actions over extended horizons. Existing approaches typically specialize in visual-language reasoning, video-based world modeling, or action generation, while cascaded pipelines that first synthesize future observations and then infer actions can introduce interface bottlenecks and compound prediction errors. We present iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni, a unified multimodal foundation model that jointly models vision(videos and images), language, and action within a single Omni framework. Its modality-specific visual-language, video-generation, and action-generation components communicate through shared multimodal self-attention. This design establishes brain-cerebellum collaboration: the vision-language modeland video generation model form a high-level brain for instruction understanding, task planning, progress tracking, and future visual-state prediction, whereas the action generation modelserves as a low-level cerebellum that directly converts planned subgoals and shared multimodal context into executable action chunks. To develop these capabilities, we combine action-annotated and action-free embodied videos from human demonstrations and robot interactions with embodied reasoning, embodied perception, and general-purpose image-text data to construct a comprehensive dataset. We further adopt a four-stage strategy that progressively trains the VLM, VGM, and AGM before jointly fine-tuning the complete model.

</details>


### [7] [Learning 3D Affordances for Blade Insertion in Cluttered Stowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02549)
*Tianyu Li, Harpreet Sawhney, Minju Jung, Aditya Mehrotra, Kunal Mehrotra, Mudit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VulcanVoxel 提出了一种基于3D占用场的掩码自编码器方法，用于在杂乱仓储环境中学习刀片插入的自由空间可操作性（affordance），将推理保持在空间域而非姿态参数域，在真实仓储数据上显著优于基于姿态的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操作任务需要推理自由空间可操作性（free-space affordance），即发现扩展刚性工具可以安全导航的体积。仓储场景中，刀片需要在杂乱织物箱内扫开物品以创造插入空间。现有方法将可操作性推断为SE(3)姿态分布，但姿态参数缺乏结构来推理未观测放置是否可行，且生成式目标（如flow matching）只能忠实学习执行策略产生的单峰分布，无法恢复几何替代方案。

Method: VulcanVoxel 将可操作性推理保持在空间域：使用3D占用场上的掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder），以场景几何为条件重建刀片占用（blade occupancy），在每个体素局部计算可行性，从单峰数据中恢复多模态预测。训练使用10,000个真实仓储操作片段，无需人工标注。此外，通过蒸馏得到一个学生网络，支持RGB到体素的快速推理（30ms vs 1.4s）。

Result: VulcanVoxel 在真实仓储数据上达到 top-5 覆盖率 0.89，而最佳基于姿态的基线为 0.71。蒸馏后的学生模型将推理时间从 1.4 秒降至 30 毫秒。

Conclusion: 刀片可操作性本质上是空间对象，是3D空间的子集，由几何可行性定义。将推理保持在空间域而非姿态参数域，能够从单峰数据中恢复多模态几何替代方案，显著优于传统姿态分布方法。该方法在真实仓储场景中验证了有效性，并发布了相关数据集。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人操作中的自由空间可操作性（affordance）推理，使用3D占用场上的掩码自编码器预测刀片插入的可行空间，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic affordance learning for blade insertion, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: 3D affordance learning, masked autoencoder, robotic stowing, spatial reasoning, real-world warehouse data

Abstract: Many manipulation tasks require reasoning about free-space affordances: discovering volumes where an extended rigid tool can safely navigate, complementary to surface contact affordances for grasping. Robotic stowing is a canonical instance, where a blade must sweep items aside inside cluttered fabric bins to create insertion space. Production stow systems generate millions of such episodes, but standard approaches with unimodal data infer affordances as SE(3) pose distributions, a geometric question asked in the wrong domain. VulcanVoxel keeps inference spatial: a masked autoencoder over 3D occupancy fields reconstructs blade occupancy conditioned on scene geometry, computing feasibility locally at each voxel and recovering multi-modal predictions from unimodal data. Blade affordances are spatial objects, subsets of 3D space defined by geometric feasibility. Pose parameters carry no structure for reasoning whether unobserved placements are feasible, and standard generative objectives including flow matching faithfully learn the unimodal distribution produced by execution policies and cannot recover geometric alternatives. Trained on 10,000 real warehouse stow episodes without human annotation, VulcanVoxel achieves top-5 coverage of 0.89 versus 0.71 for the best pose-based baseline, with a distilled student providing RGB-to-voxel inference in 30 ms. vs. 1.4 s. for voxel-to-voxel. We have released a dataset of real blade insertion cycles with RGB-D observations and pose trajectories at . html.

</details>


### [8] [TACO: TActile World Model as a Self-COrrector forScalable VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02840)
*Shengbang Liu, Yueru Jia, Yuyang Yan, Jiaming Liu, Xinran Zhang, Qiuxuan Feng, Yandong Guo, Shiji Zhou, Boxin Shi, Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TACO 提出了一种触觉感知的世界模型框架，通过 Recognize-Imagine-Label 循环，利用触觉感知世界模型生成局部纠错轨迹，并结合知识隔离的触觉适应与优势条件训练，对 VLA 策略进行后训练，显著提升接触密集型机器人操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在接触密集型操作任务中容易因微小接触扰动导致不可恢复的失败，而仅依赖视觉的世界模型无法感知接触一致性。人工标注触觉纠错数据成本高昂，因此需要一种可扩展的触觉感知世界模型来自动生成纠错监督信号。

Method: TACO 包含三个模块：(1) 进度-动作模型（Progress-Action Model），通过进度估计识别失败邻近状态；(2) 视觉-触觉生成模型（Visuo-Tactile Generation Model），生成局部纠错片段；(3) 进度-动作模型为纠错片段标注可执行动作。结合知识隔离的触觉适应（Knowledge-Insulated Tactile Adaptation）与优势条件训练（Advantage-Conditioned Training），使策略从想象纠错中学习而不破坏预训练的视觉-语言先验。

Result: 在真实世界的接触密集型操作任务中，TACO 相比基础策略实现了 44% 的绝对成功率提升，相比不使用知识隔离触觉适应的策略提升了 32%。

Conclusion: TACO 证明了触觉感知世界模型可以有效为 VLA 策略提供可扩展的后训练纠错监督，知识隔离的触觉适应策略能够在不破坏预训练先验的前提下有效整合触觉信息，为接触密集型机器人操作提供了实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TACO，一个触觉感知的世界模型框架，用于机器人 VLA 策略的后训练纠错，核心是具身智能场景下的世界模型驱动策略改进，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Tactile world model for VLA post-training is novel but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: tactile world model, VLA post-training, contact-rich manipulation, knowledge-insulated adaptation, robotics

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising generalization in robotic manipulation, but they still struggle with contact-rich tasks, where minor contact perturbations can cause unrecoverable failures that are hard to detect from vision alone. Since these failures are localized rather than task-level semantic errors, tactile-aware corrective post-training offers an efficient way to improve recovery. However, scaling such supervision through human intervention is costly. Recent works have explored world models to synthesize imagined rollouts for policy improvement, but vision-only world models may produce visually plausible yet contact-inconsistent trajectories. We therefore introduce TACO, a tactile-aware world-model-driven framework for scalable VLA post-training in contact-rich manipulation. Given real robot rollouts, TACO follows a Recognize-Imagine-Label loop with a tactile-aware world model: a unified progress-action model recognizes failure-adjacent states using progress estimates, a visuo-tactile generation model imagines local correction segments, and the progress-action model labels them with executable corrective actions. To incorporate tactile corrective supervision into VLA post-training, TACO combines knowledge-insulated tactile adaptation with advantage-conditioned training, enabling the policy to learn from imagined corrections without degrading pretrained visual-language priors. These components enable TACO to convert real-world failures into imagined visuo-tactile corrections for iterative VLA post-training. Experiments on real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks show that TACO achieves 44% absolute success rate improvement over the base policy and 32% over the policy without knowledge-insulated tactile adaptation.

</details>


### [9] [CLEAR: Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning at Scale for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02841)
*Yunxiao Shi, Hong Cai, Mohammad Ghavamzadeh, Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (85.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CLEAR 提出了一种面向端到端自动驾驶的闭环强化学习系统，通过在预训练 VLA 策略上学习残差路径点策略，并设计异构计算流水线以大规模并行仿真环境，在 CARLA 和 Bench2Drive 基准上取得了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLA 的端到端自动驾驶策略主要采用模仿学习，在开环训练与闭环推理之间存在分布偏移，导致闭环规划性能次优。为此，作者希望引入闭环强化学习来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出 CLEAR 系统：(1) 在预训练 VLA 策略的路径点先验上学习残差路径点策略，保留预训练知识的同时进行 RL 优化；(2) 设计异构流水线，将仿真器与 VLA 学习器放在不同计算组上，大幅增加并行仿真环境数量，避免资源竞争并保持训练稳定性。

Result: 在 CARLA longest6 v2 和 Bench2Drive 基准上，CLEAR 显著优于此前方法，取得了新的 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 闭环强化学习可以有效提升端到端自动驾驶策略的闭环规划性能，异构流水线设计使得大规模并行 RL 训练成为可能，为自动驾驶中的 VLA 策略优化提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究端到端自动驾驶中的闭环强化学习，属于具身智能中的规划控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model and RL scaling for autonomous driving, but not directly aligned with priority subtopics. Signals: world model, reinforcement learning, autonomous driving, closed-loop training, VLA policy

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) aims to directly map raw sensor information to driving actions. Recently, with the rapid advancement of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs), researchers have proposed the paradigm of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for E2E-AD, where it seeks to integrate visual perception, language understanding and action prediction within a single policy. However, existing VLA-based policies largely adopts imitation learning, where it only learns to drive by optimizing distance-based metrics w.r.t. logged expert trajectories. Such distribution shift between open-loop training and closed-loop inference leads to suboptimal performance in closed-loop planning. To close this gap, we present CLEAR, a system that enables closed-loop training using Reinforcement Learning (RL) at scale for E2E-AD. We propose to learn a novel residual waypoint policy around the waypoint prior from pretrained VLA policies, effectively harnessing the knowledge within. On another front, one of the key challenges to scale up RL for vision-based policies is the number of parallel simulation environments since RL is data hungry. To that end, we design a heterogeneous pipeline that places the simulator and the VLA learner on distinct compute groups, which allows us to dramatically increase the number of simulation environments running in parallel while avoiding resource contention and maintaining training stability. We show that with a simple reward, CLEAR significantly outperforms previous methods and sets new state-of-the-art performance on the challenging benchmarks of CARLA longest6 v2 and Bench2Drive.

</details>


### [10] [DREAMSTEER: Latent World Models Can Steer VLA Policies During Deployment Without Any Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02865)
*Hanchen Cui, Sergio Arnaud, Arjun Majumdar, Daniel Dugas, Elie Aljalbout, Karthik Desingh, Krishna Murthy Jatavallabhula, Franziska Meier*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (92.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DREAMSTEER提出一种部署时引导框架，利用潜空间世界模型和价值模型对预训练VLA策略的动作候选进行想象和排序，无需微调即可显著提升机器人操作在分布偏移下的成功率和指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的VLA策略在零样本泛化上表现良好，但在部署时遇到分布偏移（如未见物体、新环境）时，鲁棒性和指令跟随能力显著下降。现有方法通常需要在新环境中收集演示数据进行微调，成本高昂且不灵活。因此，作者希望在不进行任何微调或参数修改的情况下，在部署时引导VLA策略做出更好的决策。

Method: DREAMSTEER框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 预训练的VLA策略，用于生成候选动作块；(2) 动作条件潜空间世界模型（latent world model），用于想象候选动作的未来结果；(3) 语言条件价值模型（language-conditioned value model），用于对想象的轨迹进行评分。在部署时，从VLA策略和预定义的运动基元中采样候选动作块，通过潜空间世界模型预测其后续潜状态轨迹，再由价值模型根据语言指令对轨迹排序，选择最优动作执行。

Result: 在四个真实世界机器人操作基准上，面对未见物体，DREAMSTEER将任务成功率从23.75%提升至66.25%，指令跟随准确率从38.75%提升至56.25%。

Conclusion: DREAMSTEER证明了利用潜空间世界模型可以在部署时有效引导预训练VLA策略，无需任何微调即可显著提升其在分布偏移下的表现。该方法为提升机器人策略的部署鲁棒性提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出DREAMSTEER框架，利用潜空间世界模型（latent world model）和价值模型在部署时引导预训练的VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略，无需微调即可提升机器人操作的成功率和指令跟随能力。这属于具身智能中的世界模型用于规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous latent representation steering for VLA policies, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: latent world model, deployment-time steering, no finetuning, VLA policy improvement, continuous latent imagination

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies show promising zero-shot generalization, but often fail under deployment-time distribution shift, leading to decreased robustness and inconsistent instruction following. While prior work commonly tackles this by finetuning on in-distribution data, it assumes demonstrations collected on tasks in the target environment. In this work, we propose DREAMSTEER, a deployment-time steering framework for pretrained VLAs without any finetuning or parameter modifications. The key insight in DREAMSTEER is to leverage a latent world model and a value model to steer pretrained VLA policies. During deployment, DREAMSTEER samples candidate action chunks from a VLA policy and predefined motion primitives, imagines their outcomes using an action-conditioned latent world model, and ranks the imagined trajectories with a language-conditioned value model. Across four real-world manipulation benchmarks with unseen objects, DREAMSTEER improves task success rate from 23.75% to 66.25% and instruction-following accuracy from 38.75% to 56.25% over the base VLA policy.

</details>


### [11] [Object-Centric Environment Modeling for Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02846)
*Yiyang Li, Tianyi Ma, Zehong Wang, Yijun Ma, Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出OCM（Object-Centric Environment Modeling），一种以对象为中心的环境建模方法，将智能体交互经验组织为可执行的代码化环境模型（对象知识+过程知识），通过在线更新与验证提升LLM智能体在各类任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在积累交互经验时，自由形式的文本记忆难以维护、验证和复用；现有符号化方法要么存储局部过程，要么假设简化动力学，缺乏可执行、可验证、可复用的环境模型。

Method: OCM维护两个相互关联的代码库：对象知识（用Python类定义环境实体与机制）和过程知识（记录可复用的交互模式，需导入并使用对象模型）。在线设置下，每轮交互后OCM反思轨迹、更新两个知识库，并验证所有过程能在更新后的对象模型上正确执行。未来交互时，采用渐进式知识披露策略，先检查紧凑的代码签名，需要时再读取完整源码。

Result: 在多个基准上OCM取得了最佳平均排名，并有效减少了无效动作，证明了构建以对象为中心的环境模型能显著提升智能体性能。

Conclusion: 智能体可以通过构建可执行的对象中心环境模型来系统性地积累和复用交互经验，这比自由文本记忆或局部过程存储更有效，为LLM智能体的持续学习提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出OCM框架，通过构建以对象为中心的代码化环境模型来增强LLM智能体在交互任务中的规划与控制能力，属于具身智能/智能体世界模型范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on agentic world models but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model, object-centric, LLM agent, code-based environment model, online learning

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can improve through accumulated experience, but free-form textual memories become difficult to maintain, validate, and reuse as interactions grow. Recent symbolic approaches learn executable skills or programmatic world models, yet often store local procedures or assume simplified dynamics. We propose Object-Centric Environment Modeling (OCM), which organizes experience into an executable object-centric environment model. OCM maintains two connected code bases: object knowledge, which defines environment entities and mechanisms as Python classes, and procedure knowledge, which records reusable interaction patterns that must import and use the object model. OCM works in an online setting: after each episode, OCM reflects on the trajectory, updates both knowledge bases, and verifies that all procedures execute against the updated object model. During future interaction, the agent uses progressive knowledge disclosure to inspect compact code signatures first and read source code only when needed. Experiments show that OCM achieves the best average rank across benchmarks and reduces invalid actions, demonstrating that agents can benefit from building object-centric environment models.

</details>

Conclusion: Exp2VLA展示了专家策略蒸馏如何帮助机电系统从专用控制模块向更灵活、可复用的机器人智能过渡，为无人机语言引导导航提供了实用方案。

Importance reason: Focuses on drone navigation VLA, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Expert distillation pipeline, VLA for UAV, language-conditioned navigation, sim-to-sim validation, compact model fine-tuning

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models open a new path toward intuitive robot control by directly linking perception, language, and action in a single end-to-end framework. Yet for UAVs, practical adoption remains difficult because existing solutions are either computationally heavy or insufficiently capable in complex environments. In this work, we propose a practical expert-distillation pipeline (Exp2VLA) for language-conditioned drone navigation. The core idea is to distill expert behavior, obtained from reinforcement learning, teleoperation, or other controllers, into training data that can be used to fine-tune compact VLA models. This allows existing control strategies to be transferred into a unified language-guided navigation model, reducing manual system integration and lowering the barrier for deploying new robot behaviors. Experiments in both sim-to-sim and simulation-in-the-loop settings across multi-object scenes show that the fine-tuned models can handle varied semantic commands and generalize to unseen target compositions. The proposed framework demonstrates how expert-policy distillation can help mechatronic systems move from specialized control modules toward more flexible and reusable robot intelligence.

</details>

Result: 在挑战性的仿真遮挡场景中，该方法能够主动且高效地保证碰撞避免。

Conclusion: 所提出的形式化遮挡感知规划框架能够在遮挡场景下兼顾安全性与效率，为自动驾驶安全规划提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向自动驾驶的遮挡感知轨迹规划框架，利用可达性分析和树形运动规划器在遮挡场景下保证碰撞避免，属于具身智能中的规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong formal safety framework for occlusion-aware planning, but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative video. Signals: occlusion-aware planning, reachability analysis, tree-based motion planner, autonomous vehicle safety, contingency planning

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous vehicles in urban environments introduces significant safety challenges, particularly in scenarios with occlusions, where critical traffic participants may be hidden from view. Recent accidents involving driverless vehicles highlight the importance of motion planners that explicitly addresses the risks posed by occlusions. In this work, we propose a formal, occlusion-aware trajectory planning framework that guarantees collision avoidance even when there are possible hidden traffic participants. Building on our previous methods that apply reachability analysis to sequentially determine the possible states of hidden traffic participants, we integrate a tree-based motion planner capable of reasoning over future observations and the absence thereof. This approach reduces conservativeness while maintaining safety guarantees. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework in a challenging simulated occluded scenario, showing that it pro-actively and efficiently guarantees collision-avoidance.

</details>


### [14] [AnchorVLA: Bridging Discrete Decisions and Continuous Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03182)
*Qi Liu, Yabei Li, Hongsong Wang, Heng Zhang, Lei He*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (88.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AnchorVLA提出了一种层次化决策锚定的VLA规划框架，通过"决策即锚点"表示将行为级驾驶决策编码为紧凑的锚点token，再通过"决策锚定残差流"在锚点定义的残差空间中生成精细连续轨迹，在Bench2Drive闭环评测上取得了SOTA性能（成功率77.28%，驾驶得分89.92）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA规划器存在两类问题：基于规划头的轨迹预测对连续轨迹生成的约束较弱；全轨迹自回归生成依赖长序列低信息密度坐标token，导致语义-动作对齐困难、离散化误差和推理效率低下。需要一种能桥接高层VLA推理与连续轨迹执行的有效接口。

Method: AnchorVLA包含两个核心模块：(1) Decision-as-Anchor Representation：将行为级驾驶决策表示为锚点token，每个锚点编码完整的局部运动模式而非单个坐标点；(2) Decision-Anchored Residual Flow：在选定锚点定义的残差空间中生成精细连续轨迹，捕获高层决策后的多模态执行细化。通过紧凑语义锚点替代自回归坐标序列，保留LLM决策能力的同时提升推理效率和语义-动作对齐。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环评测基准上，AnchorVLA取得了77.28%的成功率（SOTA）和89.92的驾驶得分，优于现有VLA规划方法。

Conclusion: AnchorVLA通过决策锚点作为高层推理与连续轨迹执行之间的显式接口，有效解决了VLA规划中语义-动作对齐困难的问题，验证了层次化决策锚定框架在自动驾驶规划中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出AnchorVLA，一种面向自动驾驶规划的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，将高层决策锚点与连续轨迹生成相结合。这属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel hierarchical decision-anchored VLA framework that bridges discrete decisions and continuous trajectories, directly relevant to world model and continuous multimodal representation priorities. Signals: world model, continuous trajectory generation, decision-as-anchor representation, decision-anchored residual flow, VLA planning

Abstract: Autonomous driving planning requires translating navigation intent, traffic rules, dynamic interactions, and language instructions into executable continuous trajectories. Vision-Language-Action models have been introduced into driving planning to improve long-tail generalization, commonsense reasoning, high-level semantic understanding, and explainability. However, existing VLA planners mainly follow planning-head-based trajectory prediction or full-trajectory autoregressive generation. The former only weakly constrains continuous trajectory generation with VLA reasoning, while the latter relies on long sequences of low-information-density coordinate tokens, making semantic-action alignment difficult and leading to discretization errors and inefficient inference. To address these limitations, we propose AnchorVLA, a hierarchical decision-anchored VLA planning framework that uses trajectory-pattern anchors as an explicit interface between high-level VLA reasoning and continuous trajectory execution. Specifically, Decision-as-Anchor Representation represents behavior-level driving decisions with anchor tokens, each encoding an entire local motion pattern rather than a single coordinate point. Decision-Anchored Residual Flow then generates fine-grained continuous trajectories in the selected anchor-defined residual space, capturing multi-modal execution refinements after high-level decision making. By reasoning over compact and semantically meaningful anchors instead of autoregressively generating waypoint sequences, AnchorVLA preserves LLM-based decision making while improving inference efficiency, semantic-action alignment, and continuous generation flexibility. Experiments on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark show that AnchorVLA achieves a state-of-the-art Success Rate of 77.28 and a competitive Driving Score of 89.92.

</details>

*Junwu Xiong, Jiaxuan Gao, Wei Chai, Renxing Chen, Yuzhen Li, Yu Guo, Yucheng Guo, Mingxi Luo, Wenyang Ma, Yiyun Mou, Yifei Zhang, Chen Zhou, Yongjian Guo*

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身算子"（embodied operators）概念，将具身智能系统中的可复用功能模块定义为独立可组合的算子单元，构建了涵盖五类算子的分类体系，并提出了多维度的基准评测框架，旨在推动可复用、可部署的具身智能系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身智能研究多聚焦端到端策略模型，但实际系统中需要大量可复用的功能模块（如检测、定位、规划等），这些模块缺乏统一的定义、标准化接口和系统化评测，阻碍了具身智能系统的可复用性和可部署性。

Method: 定义了"具身算子"的概念边界，强调任务语义、标准化输入输出契约、可部署性、可复用性和多层可优化性。构建了五类算子分类体系：(1) 检测与分割；(2) 空间定位与3D理解；(3) 手部运动恢复；(4) 具身基础模型与任务决策算子；(5) 规划、控制与系统支持算子。同时提出多维基准框架，从正确性、端到端效率、资源使用、时间稳定性、可移植性、接口兼容性、部署可靠性和下游任务效用等维度评估算子。

Result: 提出了完整的具身算子分类体系和多维评测基准框架，讨论了算子组合、数据标准化、世界模型、VLA安全、边缘部署等开放挑战，为可复用、可扩展、可验证的具身智能系统提供了基础性框架。

Conclusion: 具身算子应作为整体可部署组件进行优化和评估，这为构建可复用、可扩展、可验证的具身智能系统奠定了基础。未来工作需关注算子组合、数据标准化、世界模型集成、VLA安全及边缘部署等方向。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦具身智能系统中的可复用算子模块（embodied operators），涵盖检测分割、空间定位、手部运动恢复、具身基础模型、规划控制等，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied intelligence system modularity and benchmarking, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Embodied operators, Benchmarking framework, Reusable modules, World model mention, Not core priority subtopics

Abstract: Embodied intelligence systems require not only end-to-end policy models, but also reusable functional modules that transform multimodal observations, robot states, human demonstrations, and task contexts into structured representations, decisions, trajectories, control references, and system services. This work defines these modules as embodied operators and studies them as independent yet composable units in embodied intelligence pipelines. We clarify their definition boundary, emphasizing task semantics, standardized input-output contracts, deployability, reusability, and multi-layer optimizability. We further construct a taxonomy covering five categories: detection and segmentation, spatial localization and 3D understanding, hand motion recovery, embodied foundation models and task-decision operators, and planning, control, and system support operators. For each category, we summarize representative functions, technical paradigms, application roles, and practical limitations. Beyond taxonomy, we propose a multi-dimensional benchmark framework that evaluates embodied operators in terms of correctness, end-to-end efficiency, resource usage, temporal stability, portability, interface compatibility, deployment reliability, and downstream task utility. We also discuss workflow-level operator acceleration and open challenges in operator composition, data standardization, world models, VLA safety, edge deployment, and real-world application value. Overall, this work argues that embodied operators should be optimized and evaluated as holistic deployable components, providing a foundation for reusable, scalable, and verifiable embodied intelligence systems.

</details>

TL;DR: HiMe 提出一种层次化具身记忆框架，将 VLA 模型解耦为高频执行器（Executor）、工作记忆（Sentry）和长期规划器（Planner），并引入基于跨模态语义模式的动态知识管理系统，通过"添加、更新、删除"操作保持记忆可塑性，有效平衡实时执行与慢思考规划之间的矛盾，在长时程任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型依赖即时观测，难以处理需要长期记忆和推理的非马尔可夫任务。现有方案存在"频率-能力悖论"：强推理模型太慢无法实时控制，快速模型又缺乏足够推理能力。需要一种架构来解耦实时执行与慢思考规划。

Method: 提出 HiMe 层次化具身记忆框架，包含三个层次：高频 Executor 负责实时执行、Sentry 负责工作记忆管理、Planner 负责长期策略规划。引入基于跨模态语义模式的动态知识系统，通过"Add、Update、Delete"操作实现记忆可塑性管理。

Result: 在长时程任务中显著优于平面记忆基线方法，并展现出基于人类偏好自我修正内部知识的新能力。

Conclusion: 层次化记忆设计有效解决了实时执行与慢思考规划之间的冲突，为长时程具身任务提供了一种可行的架构方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出层次化具身记忆框架 HiMe，面向长时程机器人操作任务，涉及具身智能中的记忆、规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a hierarchical memory architecture for VLA that addresses real-time vs. planning trade-off, but is in robotics rather than core multimodal generation or world model directions. Signals: Hierarchical embodied memory, VLA model decoupling, cross-modal semantic schemas, long-horizon task improvement, self-correction from human preferences

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at robotic manipulation but often struggle with non-Markovian tasks requiring long-term memory and reasoning due to their reliance on immediate observations. Existing solutions face a ''frequency-competence paradox,'' where stronger reasoning models are too slow for real-time control, while faster models lack sufficient reasoning capabilities. To resolve this architectural misalignment, we propose HiMe, a Hierarchical Embodied Memory framework that decouples embodied intelligence into a high-frequency Executor for execution, a Sentry for working memory, and a Planner for long-term strategy. We also introduce a dynamic knowledge system based on cross-modal semantic schemas and active management mechanisms, allowing robots to maintain memory plasticity through ''Add, Update, and Delete'' operations. This hierarchical design effectively balances the conflict between real-time execution and slow thinking planning, significantly improving success rates in long-horizon tasks. Experiments demonstrate that this approach not only outperforms flat memory baselines but also exhibits the novel ability to self-correct its internal knowledge based on human preferences.

</details>


### [17] [ADP: Adversarial Dynamics Priors for Physically Grounded Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03454)
*Seokju Lee, Jeongtae Lee, Jeonghyeok Lim, Jeonguk Kang, Byungwook Lee, Seungho Han, Keun Ha Choi, Dongil Park, Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动先验的方法（如AMP）通过模仿运动学特征来诱导自然运动风格，但未直接正则化动力学特征（如质心运动、接触力等），导致在受到外部扰动时恢复能力不足。

Method: 从轨迹优化构建参考数据集，提取动力学特征（CoM运动、质心动量、接触力、接触状态）作为对抗性判别器的目标；判别器评估策略生成的时序窗口是否与参考动力学特征一致，通过对抗训练使策略输出保持在参考支撑集附近。

Result: 相比AMP基线，ADP在80%成功脉冲阈值（J80）上提升16.7%，方向平均恢复时间降低47.9%，速度跟踪误差降低35.4%。

Conclusion: 直接正则化动力学特征比运动学特征更有效地提升人形机器人的扰动鲁棒性，ADP为具身运动控制提供了新的对抗性先验范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人运动控制，使用对抗性动力学先验（ADP）进行扰动鲁棒控制，属于具身智能/机器人领域的运动规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanoid locomotion control, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: adversarial dynamics priors, humanoid locomotion, perturbation-resilient control, trajectory optimization, world model application

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Adversarial Dynamics Priors (ADP) for perturbation-resilient humanoid locomotion control. Existing motion prior-based methods induce natural motion styles by imitating kinematic motion features, but they do not directly regularize dynamics features, such as CoM motion, centroidal momentum, contact forces, and contact states. To address this limitation, we replace kinematic motion-style feature with selected dynamics features extracted from locomotion trajectories as the target of adversarial this end, we use trajectory optimization to construct a reference dataset and train a discriminator to evaluate whether policy-induced temporal windows are consistent with the resulting reference explicit motion tracking, ADP encourages policy rollouts to remain close to the reference support, even after perturbations. Classification reason: 该论文提出 CoRE-VLA，一个面向机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过条件路由专家机制处理异构传感器配置和传感器缺失问题，属于具身智能中的机器人策略学习与动作规划，因此映射到 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced generalist robotic manipulation, yet real-world deployment reveals a fundamental challenge: robots are equipped with diverse and heterogeneous sensor configurations, auxiliary sensors can fail unexpectedly during operation, and different robot embodiments often lack certain sensors by design. A unified policy that can exploit auxiliary perceptual inputs when available while remaining reliable under sensor absence, whether incidental or by design, is therefore essential for practical deployment. However, existing VLA policies couple action generation to a fixed sensor set through shared dense computation, making them brittle when sensors are missing and limiting their ability to specialize across diverse tasks and long-horizon behaviors. We propose CoRE-VLA, a scalable and robust VLA framework that formulates action generation as context-conditioned sparse computation. Sensor availability gates modality-specialized experts, enabling graceful degradation under missing sensors without retraining. Task intent further routes action-side representations to task-relevant experts, improving specialization across diverse tasks and long-horizon subgoals. While the framework is designed to accommodate different auxiliary sensors, we focus on depth as a representative and practically important auxiliary modality in our experiments. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop, and real-world dual-arm manipulation show that CoRE-VLA achieves strong results on long-horizon and multi-task benchmarks, and outperforms both a dense-action-generator ablation and a strong pretrained VLA baseline, including in zero-shot generalization to unseen scenarios. Modality analysis shows that CoRE-VLA can exploit auxiliary depth when available while remaining robust when depth is unavailable during deployment.

</details>

*Chongkei Chang, Zhidong Deng*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在操控动态移动物体时面临挑战，直接将世界模型集成到高性能基础 VLA 架构中可能导致微调不当而降低预训练 VLA 模型的性能。作者希望提出一种不破坏基础 VLA 性能、同时能适应多种 VLA 检查点的世界模型。

Method: DynaWM 包含三个编码器：Mamba-3 动作编码器编码基础 VLA 输出的动作块为动作条件表征，V-JEPA 2.1 视觉编码器提取多视角观测历史特征，本体状态编码器编码机械臂本体状态。这些特征共同条件化一个 flow-matching DiT 模型，用于重新生成运动感知的动作轨迹。同时构建了 DynaGrasp-32 基准和 DynaGrasp-1600 数据集用于评估。

Result: 在微调 VLA 检查点上，DynaWM 相比 SmolVLA、X-VLA、π0 和 π0.5 分别提升 7.19、45.31、1.88 和 10.94 个百分点；在粗调 VLA 检查点上分别提升 35.13、44.06、35.69 和 26.13 个百分点。消融实验表明视觉编码提升 27.50% 成功率，去除动作条件则降低 45.44%。

Conclusion: DynaWM 通过基础 VLA 引导的方式有效提升了动态物体操控的性能，且能适配多种 VLA 检查点，验证了动作条件化和视觉编码在动态操控中的关键作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DynaWM，一个基于 VLA 引导的世界基础模型，用于动态物体操控任务，涉及机器人动作规划、世界模型与具身智能，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and flow matching priorities, with direct application to dynamic object manipulation. Signals: world model, flow-matching DiT, VLA-guided, moving-object manipulation, DynaGrasp benchmark

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) models have received widespread attention, many challenges remain in manipulating dynamic moving objects. In most existing approaches, end-to-end forward or inverse dynamics models, i.e., world models, are incorporated into high-performance base VLA architectures, which may degrade the performance of well-pretrained base VLA models due to inappropriate fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose DynaWM, a base-VLA-guided world foundation model that adapts to a wide variety of fine-tuned and coarse-tuned base-VLA checkpoints for moving-object manipulation. DynaWM uses a Mamba-3-based action encoder to encode the base action chunk produced by the base VLA into an action-conditioning representation, a V-JEPA 2.1 vision encoder to extract features from multi-view observation history, and a proprioceptive state encoder to encode robotic-arm proprioceptive states. These feature representations jointly condition a flow-matching DiT to regenerate motion-aware action trajectories for moving-object manipulation. For systematic evaluation, we construct the DynaGrasp-32 benchmark, covering six categories of moving-object manipulation tasks, including velocity variation, trajectory variation, and multi-object manipulation, as well as the DynaGrasp-1600 dataset, which consists of 32 scenarios, 1,600 demonstration trajectories, and approximately 1.53M images. For fine-tuned base-VLA checkpoints, DynaWM achieves percentage improvements of 7.19, 45.31, 1.88, and 10.94 over SmolVLA, X-VLA, {\pi}0, and {\pi}0.5, respectively. For coarse-tuned base-VLA checkpoints, performance increases by 35.13, 44.06, 35.69, and 26.13 percentage, respectively. Ablation experiments show that visual encoding enhances success by 27.50%, while reducing success by 45.44% if action conditioning is removed.

</details>


### [20] [Look Before You Leap: Distilling Tree Search into Action Evaluation for Frozen VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03751)
*Xinyi Xie, Zican Hu, Zhanyu Liu, Yicheng Dong, Wenhao Wu, Zhenhong Sun, Haoran Li, Chunlin Chen, Zhi Wang, Pichao Wang*

TL;DR: 提出SVA框架，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索探索冻结VLA模型的输出分布，将搜索知识蒸馏为轻量级Q值评估模型，在部署时选择最优动作，无需模拟器。该方法在保持VLA泛化能力的同时显著提升任务成功率，且9B模型通过测试时评估扩展可超越27B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽经大规模预训练获得广泛具身能力，但其泛化性远不如LLM和VLM。现有后训练方法（SFT/RL）提升任务性能但损害了预训练的通用能力。作者发现VLA失败不仅源于动作生成，更源于动作评估——冻结VLA的输出分布中已包含足够好的动作（pass@32达92%），但缺乏选择好动作的评估机制。

Method: 提出SVA（Search, Value, Act）框架：1）Search阶段：在模拟器中使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）充分探索冻结VLA的输出分布，收集带经验回报的多样化轨迹；2）Value阶段：将搜索知识蒸馏为轻量级Q值模型，预测候选动作的预期后果；3）Act阶段：部署时冻结VLA提出多个候选动作，评估器选择不确定性正则化Q值最高的动作，无需模拟器访问。

Result: 在多个具身基准上，SVA一致提升了未见任务的泛化性能，展现出强测试时扩展行为。9B VLA模型搭配SVA以27%更低推理延迟超越27B VLA模型7个点，表明测试时评估扩展比模型规模扩展更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 通过将动作提议与后果评估解耦，SVA在保持VLA骨干网络泛化能力的同时大幅提升任务成功率。测试时评估扩展是一种比扩大模型规模更高效的提升路径，为VLA模型的后训练提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在具身任务中的动作评估与规划，使用蒙特卡洛树搜索探索动作分布并蒸馏为Q值模型，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel distillation of tree search into action evaluation for frozen VLA models, with strong test-time scaling and generalization gains. Signals: world model, test-time scaling, action evaluation, distillation, frozen VLA

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models acquire broad embodied capabilities through large-scale pretraining, yet their generalization remains far more fragile than that of LLMs and VLMs. The prevailing remedy, post-training via supervised fine-tuning or reinforcement learning, improves task-specific performance but narrows the generalist capability that makes pretraining valuable. We identify a key bottleneck: VLA failures stem not only from action generation but also from action evaluation. A diagnostic pass@k study confirms that frozen VLAs already contain competent behaviors in their output distribution, with overall success rates rising from 33% at pass@1 to 92% at pass@32. Inspired by this, we propose SVA (Search, Value, and Act), a simple framework that equips frozen VLA policies with long-term consequence awareness. SVA first uses Monte-Carlo tree search in simulation to fully explore the VLA's output distribution and collect diverse trajectories annotated with empirical returns; this knowledge is then distilled into a lightweight Q-value model that predicts the expected consequence of candidate actions; at deployment, the frozen VLA proposes multiple candidates and the evaluator selects the one with the highest uncertainty-regularized Q-value, requiring no simulator access. By decoupling action proposal from consequence evaluation, SVA preserves the generalization capacity of the VLA backbone while substantially improving task success rates. Experiments across embodied benchmarks show that SVA consistently improves generalization on unseen tasks and exhibits strong test-time scaling behavior. Strikingly, SVA enables a 9B VLA to outperform a 27B VLA by 7 points at 27% lower inference latency, suggesting that scaling test-time evaluation is more cost-effective than scaling model size.

</details>


### [21] [High-Fidelity One-Step Generative Visuomotor Policy via Recursive Correction, Frequency Consistency, and Contrastive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03865)
*Yuran Chen, Xinye Cai, Zhonglin Gong, Yang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高保真一步生成式视觉运动策略框架，通过递归一致动作流（RCAF）、双时间步频率一致性（DTFC）和对比流匹配（CFM）三种机制，解决一步生成策略中的空间偏差、频率失真和模式平均问题，在多个机器人平台上以单次前向传播达到与10步扩散策略相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配等生成模型在机器人视觉运动策略中表现出色，但其多步采样/ODE求解导致推理延迟高，难以满足实时控制需求。现有的一步加速方法将整个生成过程压缩为单次大更新，导致空间偏差、频率失真和模式平均问题，亟需一种高保真的一步生成策略。

Method: 提出三种互补机制：(1) 递归一致动作流（RCAF），通过递归校正补偿空间截断误差，使一步预测与精细流轨迹对齐；(2) 双时间步频率一致性（DTFC），通过跨流时间步的自适应频谱一致性保留高频操作细节；(3) 对比流匹配（CFM），通过基于边界的排斥目标分离纠缠的动作流，减少多模态操作中的模糊动作。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboTwin 2.0、Adroit、DexArt及真实机器人平台上，该方法以1 NFE（单次前向传播）达到或超越强10步生成策略基线的性能，实现了低延迟的视觉运动控制。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过精心设计的一步生成机制（递归校正、频率一致性和对比流匹配），可以在不牺牲生成质量的前提下大幅降低推理延迟，为机器人实时控制中的生成式策略提供了可行的加速方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向机器人视觉运动策略（visuomotor policy）的高保真一步生成框架，使用流匹配（flow matching）生成机器人动作，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and efficient inference for robotic visuomotor control with novel one-step generative flow matching. Signals: world model, efficient inference, flow matching, one-step generation, robotic visuomotor policy

Abstract: Generative models such as diffusion and flow matching have advanced robotic visuomotor policies by modeling multimodal action distributions, but their multi-step sampling or ODE solving introduces inference latency. Existing one-step acceleration methods often compress the whole generation process into a single large update, leading to spatial deviation, frequency distortion, and mode averaging. This paper proposes a high-fidelity one-step generative visuomotor policy framework that addresses these issues with three complementary mechanisms. Recursive Consistent Action Flow (RCAF) uses recursive correction to compensate for spatial truncation errors and align one-step predictions with refined flow trajectories. Dual-Timestep Frequency Consistency (DTFC) preserves high-frequency manipulation details through adaptive spectral consistency across flow timesteps. Contrastive Flow Matching (CFM) separates entangled action flows with a margin-based repulsive objective, reducing ambiguous actions in multimodal manipulation. Experiments on RoboTwin, RoboTwin 2.0, Adroit, DexArt, and real-world robot platforms show that the proposed method achieves competitive or superior performance compared with strong 10-step generative policy baselines while requiring only one forward pass (1 NFE), enabling low-latency visuomotor control.

</details>

Conclusion: LH-AVLN 揭示了长时序多模态导航任务的挑战性，音频线索在非视线搜索中有帮助但也可能成为干扰，未来需要更强大的多模态导航智能体。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身导航中的长时序音频-视觉-语言导航任务，涉及多目标任务执行、空间语义地图构建与渐进式目标规划，属于具身智能中的环境建模与规划控制，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio-visual navigation benchmark, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Long-horizon navigation, audio-visual-language benchmark, multi-goal missions, training-free agent, indoor 3D environments

Abstract: Embodied navigation is moving toward long-horizon missions, yet existing long-horizon benchmarks are largely acoustically silent, and audio-visual navigation tasks typically focus on a single goal. We introduce LH-AVLN, a benchmark for Long-Horizon Audio-Visual-Language Navigation that combines multi-goal mission execution, heterogeneous goal specifications, and persistent spatialized acoustic cues. In LH-AVLN, an agent receives a global mission of two to four goals specified by category, language description, or reference image, and navigates with RGB-D observations, pose, and binaural audio in indoor 3D environments. The benchmark supports both ordered and unordered missions, where alternating goal-associated sounds can guide non-line-of-sight search but may also become distractors as mission progress changes. We further develop PAG-Nav, a training-free reference agent that maintains a temporal uniform semantic map and performs progressive goal-state planning, using sound for search while reserving completion for visual-semantic verification. Experiments show that existing vision-language, memory-based, and audio-visual agents struggle to complete full LH-AVLN missions, and that PAG-Nav provides a stronger diagnostic baseline while leaving substantial room for future progress.

</details>

Conclusion: 结合3D中心的世界-动作联合建模，可以在不依赖大规模真实机器人数据的情况下实现可泛化的机器人基础模型，为通用机器人系统提供了一条实用且经济的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in embodied AI have established robot foundation models (RFMs) as the dominant approach for generalist robotic systems to date. By leveraging imitation learning on extensive robot demonstrations, RFMs have achieved impressive capabilities in mapping visual observations and language instructions to continuous robotic actions. However, current RFMs lack an inherent ability to reason about physical dynamics and the causal effects of robot behaviors on the 3D physical world. This creates a fundamental mismatch between 2D-centric visual perception and 3D-centric embodied interaction, severely limiting the generalization ability of RFMs in real-world address this gap, we present WSA$_1$, a novel RFM built upon proposed 3D-Centric World-Spatial-Action modeling paradigm. It not only learns 3D world-aware visual thought for future robot behaviors, but also models mutual constraints between 3D world state transitions and robotic actions to enhance behavior generalization. Notably, WSA$_1$ achieves highly data-efficient pre-training with 6k hours of expert demonstration data (only 1k hours from real robot), while delivering competitive manipulation performance (93% success rate) on RoboTwin2.0 simulation benchmark and achieving +20% average boosted performance over state-of-the-art RFMs on real-world robot control tasks. These results reveal that generalizable RFM can be attained without large-scale real robot data when paired with 3D-centric world-action joint modeling, which offers a practical and affordable pathway to generalist robotic systems.

Result: 在零样本多步方程构建（数字立方体）和约束检索任务上，ACE分别达到50%和70%的成功率，而标准端到端基线几乎无法完成这些逻辑复杂的任务。

Conclusion: 显式的工作流推理与掩码介导的闭环控制为可适应的机器人操作提供了一条鲁棒且实用的路径，无需任务特定的重新训练即可实现零样本泛化。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ACE框架，面向具身桌面操作（tabletop manipulation），结合工作流推理、视觉接地、闭环验证与多时间尺度记忆，实现零样本任务规划与执行。这属于具身智能中的规划控制与闭环执行，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied manipulation workflow reasoning, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: zero-shot workflow reasoning, mask-mediated vision-action interface, multi-timescale memory, tabletop pick-and-place, closed-loop verification

Abstract: Open-ended tabletop manipulation requires agents to not only understand natural language but also adapt to dynamic environments and execution failures. We present ACE (Agentic Control for Embodied Manipulation), a zero-shot workflow reasoning framework for tabletop pick-and-place from natural language. Rather than relying on direct low-level action mapping, ACE combines agentic workflow reasoning with two robot-facing executable skills: a visual grounding interface and a reusable pick-and-place primitive. To bridge semantic reasoning and physical control, the active sub-goal is grounded into a mask-mediated vision-action interface. This unified mask specifies the target object and destination, is tracked over time, exposed for human verification, and ultimately passed to a task-agnostic downstream policy for execution. Crucially, ACE operates in a closed loop supported by a multi-timescale memory. After an action is executed, the system automatically verifies whether the intended sub-goal succeeded, using the outcome to advance, retry, repair, or replan. This enables online adaptation to user corrections, scene changes, and physical failures. We evaluate ACE on logically complex, long-horizon tasks, including zero-shot multi-step equation formation with number cubes and constraint-based object retrieval. ACE demonstrates task-level zero-shot generalization on novel semantic constraints and randomized tabletop scenes without task-specific retraining. Specifically, while standard end-to-end baselines struggle to complete these logically demanding tasks, ACE achieves a 50% success rate in equation formation and a 70% success rate in constraint retrieval. This contrast demonstrates that explicit workflow reasoning and mask-mediated control offer a robust, practical route toward adaptable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [25] [XS-VLA: Coupling Coarse-grained Spatial Distillation with Latent Flow Matching for Lightweight Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04171)
*Lei Iok Tong, Qingchen Xie, Wei Huang, Ying Jie Yap, Yujie Zhang, Qianzhi Li, Xiaolong Liu, Zhidong Deng*

TL;DR: XS-VLA 提出两阶段轻量级机器人操控框架：先通过粗粒度空间语义蒸馏将小模型（0.25B）转化为空间感知引擎，再结合 CVAE 与潜流匹配（Latent Flow Matching）策略建模多模态动作分布，在 LIBERO 基准上以 <0.5B 参数达到 SOTA，相比 SmolVLA 0.25B 提升 7.2% 平均成功率，长任务提升 23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型（LVLM）虽具备强多模态理解与空间定位能力，但计算开销大，难以用于实时机器人控制；轻量模型适合边缘部署，但存在"空间盲区"（空间预测能力弱）；在混合人类演示数据上训练 VLA 模型会因行为多样性导致策略性能下降。现有方法未能同时解决轻量模型的空间感知与多模态动作分布建模问题。

Method: 提出 XS-VLA 两阶段框架：第一阶段，将 Qwen3-VL-4B 的空间语义知识蒸馏到 SmolVLM2-0.25B 骨干网络中，通过在粗粒度空间描述数据上微调，使轻量模型具备空间感知能力；第二阶段，利用增强后的骨干网络作为条件，构建基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）与流匹配（Flow Matching）动力学的潜流匹配策略，建模多模态动作分布。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上，XS-VLA 在 <0.5B 参数模型中达到 SOTA，平均成功率比 SmolVLA 0.25B 基线提升 7.2%，其中 LIBERO-Long 任务提升 23%；性能超越更大的 2.2B 参数 SmolVLA；消融实验表明空间调优与生成式潜流控制显著提升轻量 VLA 性能，任务执行速度相比此前轻量流匹配策略提升 3.2 倍。

Conclusion: XS-VLA 通过粗粒度空间蒸馏与潜流匹配策略的结合，有效解决了轻量 VLA 模型的空间盲区与多模态动作分布建模难题，在保持高效推理的同时实现了优越的机器人操控性能，为边缘部署的具身智能系统提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向轻量级机器人操控的 VLA 框架，核心是结合空间蒸馏与潜流匹配策略进行动作生成与控制，属于具身智能中的机器人规划控制，因此映射到 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。同时，论文使用了视觉语言模型进行空间蒸馏，也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent flow matching for lightweight world models, a core user priority. Signals: Latent Flow Matching, lightweight VLA, spatial distillation, world model for robotics, continuous multimodal representation

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown strong multimodal understanding and spatial grounding, but their computational cost limits real-time robotic control. In contrast, lightweight models are suitable for edge deployment but often suffer from "spatial blindness", namely weak native spatial prediction ability. Training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on mixed human demonstrations can also degrade policy performance due to highly diverse behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose XS-VLA, a two-stage framework for efficient and spatially grounded robotic manipulation. First, we distill spatial semantic knowledge from Qwen3-VL-4B into the SmolVLM2-0.25B backbone by fine-tuning on curated coarse-grained spatial descriptions, turning the lightweight model into a spatially grounded engine. Second, we use this enhanced backbone to condition a Latent Flow Matching policy. Unlike deterministic controllers, our policy combines a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) with Flow Matching dynamics to model complex multimodal action distributions. On the LIBERO benchmark, XS-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance among models with fewer than 0.5B parameters. It improves average success rates by up to 7.2 percent, including a 23 percent gain on LIBERO-Long, over the SmolVLA 0.25B baseline, and outperforms the larger 2.2B vanilla SmolVLA. Ablations show that spatial tuning and generative latent flow control substantially improve lightweight VLA performance, delivering a 3.2 times speedup in mission execution over the previous lightweight flow matching policy.

</details>

*Bowen Jing, Mingxin Wang, Ruiyang Hao, Chenchen Ge, Hanwen Shen, Junjie He, Yang Cui, Yiming Hou, Weitao Zhou, Jiawei Wang, Minglei Li, Dandan Zhang, Ding Zhao, Houde Liu, Xiaofan Li, Si Liu, Ping Luo, Haibao Yu*

TL;DR: SoftVTBench是一个面向可变形物体机器人操作的安全感知视觉-触觉基准，在Isaac Sim中构建，提供多视角RGB、触觉传感、本体感知和语言指令，分别报告任务成功率和物理安全成功率，实验表明仅关注任务成功率会高估策略性能，而触觉传感可提升安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准主要关注任务完成（如抓取成功），很少评估操作过程中是否保持物理安全（如无滑落、形变在安全阈值内），导致策略在实际部署中存在安全隐患。

Method: 在Isaac Sim中基于有限元仿真构建可变形物体，提供多视角RGB、RGB触觉传感（含标记点运动）、本体感知和语言指令，定义4组任务套件（按物体类型和变化轴划分），分别报告Goal Success和Safety Success（要求无掉落且峰值形变低于物体特定阈值），并基于pi0.5实现基线方法。

Result: 仅关注任务成功率会显著高估策略性能，大量完成任务的操作仍违反物理安全约束；引入触觉传感可将安全成功率从21.4%提升至35.6%（物体中心可变形任务），同时保持相当的任务成功率。

Conclusion: SoftVTBench为研究物理交互约束下的视觉-触觉可变形物体操作提供了可复现的基准，证明了安全感知评估和触觉传感对提升操作安全性的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注机器人操作中的物理安全约束（防滑落、防过度形变），在Isaac Sim仿真环境中构建了具身操作基准，属于具身智能与机器人操作范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation safety, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: robotics benchmark, deformable objects, tactile sensing, safety-aware evaluation, Isaac Sim

Abstract: Deformable object manipulation poses challenges beyond task completion: successful execution must also maintain safe physical interaction, holding the object stably without slip or drop while avoiding excessive deformation. However, existing manipulation benchmarks are predominantly success-oriented and rarely evaluate whether a policy remains physically safe throughout execution. We present SoftVTBench, a safety-aware visuo-tactile benchmark for physically constrained deformable object manipulation. Built in Isaac Sim with finite-element-simulated deformable objects, SoftVTBench provides multi-view RGB observations, RGB tactile sensing with marker motion, proprioception, and language instructions, and defines four matched task suites over object type (deformable vs. rigid) and variation axis (object vs. spatial). It separately reports Goal Success and Safety Success; the latter additionally requires no drop and peak deformation below a calibrated object-specific threshold, measured from policy-hidden privileged Finite Element Method (FEM) states. We implement pi0.5-based baselines under this protocol. Experiments show that success-only evaluation substantially overstates policy performance, as a large fraction of goal-completing rollouts still violate physical safety. Furthermore, incorporating tactile sensing improves Safety Success (e.g., from 21.4% to 35.6% on object-centric deformable tasks) and reduces object deformation during execution, while maintaining comparable Goal Success. SoftVTBench provides a reproducible benchmark for studying visuo-tactile deformable manipulation under physical interaction constraints.

</details>

TL;DR: HALO-WA 提出一种混合注意力潜空间引导的在线强化学习框架，通过轻量级 actor-critic 适配器利用世界-动作模型生成过程中的潜特征和动作先验，实现快速在线适应真实部署误差。在四个精密操作任务上将平均成功率从 26.4% 提升至 87.1%，仅需 45-75 分钟在线训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WA model）在通用机器人操作中能生成长程动作块，但在真实世界的精密任务中，由于标定、感知和接触动力学误差，常在最后几毫米的对准或插入阶段失败。现有方法缺乏对部署阶段误差的快速在线适应能力。

Method: HALO-WA 引入混合注意力结构（hybrid-attention），在保持动作块时序一致性的同时，从 WA 潜空间中读取任务相关信息（视觉上下文和末端修正需求），通过轻量级 actor-critic 适配器对 WA 生成的动作块进行精炼。该框架利用 WA 生成过程中的潜特征和动作先验，实现快速在线强化学习适应。

Result: 在四个真实世界精密操作任务上，HALO-WA 将平均成功率从 WA-base 的 26.4% 提升至 87.1%，比最强基线高出 19.2 个百分点，每个任务仅需 45-75 分钟在线训练。在 RoboTwin 仿真实验中进一步验证了可复现性。

Conclusion: HALO-WA 通过混合注意力潜空间引导的在线 RL 有效解决了世界-动作模型在精密操作中的部署误差问题，实现了高效、快速的在线适应，为具身智能中的精密操作提供了实用解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HALO-WA，一种面向世界-动作模型（WA model）的混合注意力潜空间引导在线强化学习框架，用于通用机器人精密操作。论文核心是具身智能场景下的世界模型与动作规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model + RL adaptation for robotics, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, online RL adaptation, robotic manipulation, hybrid-attention latent, action chunk refinement

Abstract: World-action (WA) models can generate long-horizon action chunks for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but they remain vulnerable to calibration, perception, and contact-dynamics errors in real-world precision tasks, often failing in the final few millimeters of alignment or insertion. We propose HALO-WA, a hybrid-attention latent-guided online reinforcement learning (RL) framework for WA models, which leverages latent features and action priors from the WA generation process through a lightweight actor-critic adapter to enable fast online adaptation to real deployment errors. HALO-WA introduces a hybrid-attention structure that preserves the temporal consistency of action chunks while reading task-relevant information from WA latents conditioned on visual context and end-stage correction requirements, thereby producing refined action chunks. We validate HALO-WA on four real-world precision manipulation tasks, where it improves the average success rate from 26.4\% for WA-base to 87.1\%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 19.2 percentage points while requiring only 45--75 minutes of online training per task. To facilitate reproducibility, we further conduct supplementary simulation experiments in RoboTwin and release the code at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略评测基准存在局限：任务简单、时域短、技能范围窄；且大多仅在仿真或仅在真实世界中进行，仿真缺乏物理部署挑战，真实世界评测成本高、耗时长、难以复现。需要一个统一的仿真-真实评测框架来系统评估通用机器人操作策略的能力。

Method: RoboDojo 包含 42 个仿真任务和 18 个真实世界任务，覆盖互补的操作能力。仿真评测从泛化、记忆、精度、长时程执行和开放词汇指令跟随五个维度评估策略。系统基于 Isaac Sim 实现异构并行仿真，并提供 RoboDojo-RealEval 可复现真实世界评测系统（含远程云访问、标准化硬件、场景重置、评测协议和部署接口）。同时构建 XPolicyLab 策略库，集成 30 种策略，实现一次集成即可在仿真和真实环境中评测。

Result: 集成了 30 种机器人操作策略并在 RoboDojo 上进行了系统评测，建立了公开排行榜，对当前策略的性能进行了系统分析。

Conclusion: RoboDojo 提供了一个统一的仿真-真实评测框架，弥补了现有机器人操作策略评测在任务覆盖度和部署环境多样性上的不足，有助于推动通用机器人操作策略的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RoboDojo，一个统一的仿真与真实世界机器人操作策略评测基准，涵盖泛化、记忆、精度、长时程执行和开放词汇指令跟随等维度，属于具身智能/机器人操作策略的系统评测。这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for robot manipulation but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: robot manipulation benchmark, sim-and-real evaluation, generalist policies, Isaac Sim, real-world reproducibility

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies have advanced rapidly, yet existing benchmarks remain limited in systematically evaluating their capabilities. Many rely on simple, short-horizon, or skill-narrow tasks with limited capability coverage, and are often conducted only in simulation or only in the real world. Simulation enables scalable feedback but misses physical deployment challenges, while real-world evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce. We introduce RoboDojo, a unified sim-and-real benchmark for comprehensive evaluation of generalist robot manipulation policies. RoboDojo includes 42 simulation tasks and 18 real-world tasks covering diverse and complementary manipulation capabilities. The simulation benchmark evaluates five dimensions: generalization, memory, precision, long-horizon execution, and open-vocabulary instruction following, while the real-world benchmark exposes policies to challenging physical-world deployment conditions. RoboDojo supports scalable evaluation through heterogeneous parallel simulation in Isaac Sim and provides RoboDojo-RealEval, a reproducible real-world evaluation system with remote cloud access, standardized hardware, scene reset, evaluation protocol, and deployment interface. Together with XPolicyLab, policies can be integrated once and evaluated across simulation and real-world settings with minimal adaptation. We integrate 30 policies into XPolicyLab and evaluate them on RoboDojo, establishing a public leaderboard and systematic analysis of current policy performance. The website is available at .

</details>

TL;DR: Mask2Real-WM 提出两阶段动作条件世界模型，将像素预测解耦为分割掩码动力学模型和渲染模型，利用分割空间的 sim-to-real 差距小，在 50+ 小时仿真数据上预训练后微调于 <2.5 小时真实数据，实现 23 自由度灵巧操作的精细动作可控预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型在灵巧操作任务中难以实现细粒度、每关节级别的动作可控预测，且仿真到真实的迁移成本高。作者希望利用分割掩码空间 sim-to-real 差距小的特性，解耦动力学预测与视觉渲染，实现高效可控的灵巧操作世界模型。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 动力学模型从历史分割掩码和 23-DoF 动作序列预测未来分割掩码；(2) 渲染模型使用 ControlNet 增强的 Stable Video Diffusion 将预测掩码映射为逼真 RGB 图像。动力学模型先在 50+ 小时仿真数据上大规模预训练，再在 <2.5 小时真实演示数据上微调。

Result: 在灵巧抓取放置基准上，掩码条件化和仿真预训练对 23 个自由度的每关节动作可控性均不可或缺。整体式基线只能捕捉粗略的手部和末端执行器轨迹，无法可靠反映细粒度的每关节动作效果。

Conclusion: 分割掩码作为 sim-to-real 桥梁，结合解耦的动力学-渲染架构，能够高效地从仿真迁移到真实场景，实现灵巧操作中细粒度、每关节可控的动作条件世界模型。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and efficient sim-to-real transfer priorities, with direct relevance to dexterous manipulation and controllable prediction. Signals: world model, dexterous manipulation, sim-to-real transfer, segmentation masks, action-conditioned prediction

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models allow robots to predict the future consequences of candidate actions without additional physical interaction, supporting policy evaluation, planning, and data augmentation. We present Mask2Real-WM, a two-stage action-conditioned world model for dexterous manipulation that decouples pixel prediction into a dynamics model and a rendering model. The dynamics model predicts future segmentation masks from past masks and 23-DoF action sequences. The rendering model maps the predicted masks to photorealistic RGB using a ControlNet-augmented Stable Video Diffusion backbone. The smaller sim-to-real gap in segmentation space enables the dynamics model to benefit from large-scale pretraining on over 50 h of synthetic simulation data, followed by fine-tuning on fewer than 2.5 h of real demonstrations. Experiments on a dexterous pick-and-place benchmark show that mask conditioning and simulation pretraining are both required for per-DoF action controllability across all 23 degrees of freedom. In contrast, monolithic baselines capture broad hand and end-effector trajectories but do not reliably reflect fine-grained, per-joint action effects.

</details>

Conclusion: 演示数据的组织方式对VLA学习至关重要，由简到繁的结构化策略能有效提升长时域操作任务的学习效果，为高效技能获取、可扩展数据集构建和长时域机器人操作提供了实用见解。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在机器人操作中的演示收集策略，属于具身智能与机器人动作规划控制范畴，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on demonstration organization for VLA, which is a niche robotics topic not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Robotics manipulation, VLA learning, demonstration collection, task decomposition, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and robot action generation. Existing research has primarily focused on improving model architectures, training strategies, and dataset scale, while little attention has been paid to how demonstrations are collected and organized. We identify demonstration organization as a fundamental yet overlooked aspect of imitation learning, as it directly affects policy learning efficiency, training stability, and policy generalization. To address this gap, we propose a simple-to-complex structured demonstration collection strategy for VLA learning using a dual-arm robotic platform. Our approach systematically organizes data through three general principles: (i) decomposing complex manipulation tasks into progressively learnable sub-skills, (ii) standardizing the interaction environment to reduce unnecessary variability, and (iii) organizing demonstrations according to progressively increasing task complexity. This structured design enables VLA models to first acquire fundamental manipulation skills before learning increasingly complex task compositions, facilitating more effective learning of long-horizon manipulation tasks. We evaluate the proposed strategy on two representative robotic manipulation tasks: block grasping and sorting, and towel folding. Experimental results show consistent improvements in task success rate and training stability compared with the baseline method of directly collecting end-to-end complete task trajectories. These findings highlight demonstration organization as a previously underexplored but important factor in VLA learning and provide practical insights into efficient skill acquisition, scalable dataset construction, and long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>

*Dijia Zhan, Xuemiao Xu, Jinyi Li, Jie Tang*
