TL;DR: SEAM是一种无需训练的推理时方法，通过速度引导损失修正（VLS）在flow matching VLA策略中平滑相邻动作块之间的不连续过渡，在LIBERO-10上降低28%边界抖动和27%块间不连续性，同时保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略执行固定长度动作块时，相邻块因独立高斯潜变量可能收敛到不兼容的轨迹模式，导致块边界处出现突变不连续。现有方法要么需要反向传播、拒绝采样或重新训练，计算成本高或影响任务可靠性。

Method: 提出SEAM，核心是Velocity-guided Loss Steering (VLS)机制。利用前一动作块未执行尾部作为一致性参考，在flow matching的每个Euler步后施加闭式修正，无需通过策略网络反向传播，实现训练自由的推理时平滑。

Result: 在LIBERO-10基准上使用pi_0.5策略，SEAM将边界抖动降低28%，块过渡不连续性降低27%，保持基线水平的任务成功率，且去噪循环开销接近未引导基线。

Conclusion: SEAM提供了一种轻量级、无需训练的推理时方法，有效解决VLA策略中动作块执行的不连续问题，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升动作平滑性，具有实际部署价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SEAM方法，用于VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略中动作块执行的平滑过渡，属于机器人动作规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses a practical inference-time smoothing method for VLA policies, but is narrowly focused on robotics execution rather than the user's priority subtopics. Signals: robotics, flow matching, action chunking, inference-time correction, no training required

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies that execute fixed-length action chunks can exhibit multimodal bifurcation: a cross-chunk inconsistency in which adjacent chunks generated from independent Gaussian latents can converge to incompatible trajectory modes, producing abrupt discontinuities at chunk boundaries. Existing remedies either require backpropagation through the policy at each denoising step, rely on rejection sampling, or require retraining, each trading computational cost or task reliability for smoother transitions. We propose SEAM (Smooth Execution of Action-Chunked Motion), a training-free inference-time method for flow matching VLAs. SEAM exploits a simple synchronous-execution insight: after the robot consumes the executed prefix, the previous chunk's unexecuted tail is already available as an analytic consistency reference. Its core mechanism, Velocity-guided Loss Steering (VLS), derives a time-dependent target from this tail and applies a closed-form correction after each Euler step without backpropagating through the policy network. On LIBERO-10 with pi_0.5, SEAM reduces boundary jerk by 28%, reduces chunk transition discontinuity by 27%, preserves baseline-level task success, and keeps denoising-loop cost near the unguided baseline.

</details>

Abstract: Linear-deformable manipulation remains challenging due to the complex deformations of objects such as cables and ropes. Prior data-driven approaches, particularly imitation learning, have shown some promise in narrowly defined settings but typically require thousands of demonstrations for specific tasks and cable types, limiting scalability and generalization. We introduce a sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) framework for multi-stage cable routing that leverages GPU-parallelized simulation to approximate linear deformable behaviors. Training across thousands of parallel simulations enables the learned policies to generalize across diverse cable geometries and deformation patterns. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we propose a novel deployment strategy that combines a Simulation In the LOop (SILO) execution framework, localized RL policies, and robust cable state estimation. On real-world cable routing tasks, our approach achieves higher success rates and 2x reduction in cycle times compared to prior state-of-the-art learning methods. To our knowledge, this is the first successful sim-to-real transfer of RL policies for multi-stage cable routing. Videos and additional visualizations are available at

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly treated as end-to-end action policies conditioned on natural-language task descriptions. In practice, however, their behavior often depends sharply on how the instruction is phrased, suggesting that language is not merely a task label but an optimizable conditioning input. We study whether frozen VLA policies can be improved by optimizing language space rather than updating action weights. Our method introduces a language-conditioning space policy that translates a human instruction into a short VLA-grounded command using object appearance, spatial relations, and target-grounding cues. The language-conditioning space policy is initialized with a failure-derived command-space prior and optimized with reinforcement learning from sparse task-completion rewards, while the downstream VLA remains fully frozen. This yields language-conditioning space optimization: RL discovers which VLA-grounded commands best elicit successful behavior from the frozen action policy. Experiments on RL4VLA and VL-Think show that language-conditioning space optimization improves success on instruction-sensitive, symbolic, and multi-object manipulation tasks, demonstrating that language can serve as an optimizable variable for a robot foundation models. Signals: world model for robotics, latent velocity as affordance, frozen video backbone, diffusion policy conditioning, no rollout cost

Abstract: Learning manipulation from few demonstrations requires visual priors that capture not only where to interact, but also how the interaction should begin; static priors such as segmentation masks encode only the former. We present KAM-WM, a framework that extracts a coarse directional interaction cue from a frozen latent video world model without rollout or world-model fine-tuning. KAM-WM queries a Flow Matching image-to-video backbone once and interprets its single-step latent velocity as a Kinematic Affordance Map (KAM), which provides task-conditioned interaction regions and coarse motion structure. A lightweight Perceiver compresses KAM into tokens that condition a diffusion policy together with RGB observations and proprioception. Across LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, KAM-WM reaches 90.6% average success on LIBERO and achieves 65.7% and 22.4% success rates in the Easy and Hard settings on RoboTwin2.0, respectively. Controlled comparisons against a zero-order mask prior suggest that part of the gains comes from directional information beyond spatial localization alone. These results indicate that, in the evaluated settings, a frozen video model can provide a useful first-order visual prior for control without the test-time cost of future rollout.

</details>


### [35] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Mean What They Say? On the Role of Faithfulness in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04681)
*Matthew Foutter, Matteo Cercola, Lena Wild, Yunshan Wang, Michelle Li, Daniele Gammelli, Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中具身思维链（Embodied CoT）的忠实性问题，区分了功能性推理（提升任务性能）与忠实性推理（真实反映内部决策过程）。通过人类评估发现当前对齐策略下的推理质量与轨迹质量之间存在不一致耦合。作者提出Pinocchio——一个学习型批评器，用于评估观察锚定与步骤一致性，并将其作为密集奖励信号通过强化学习对具身策略进行后训练。实验表明，后训练在保持下游任务性能的同时，将忠实性提升了4%-18%，并在分布外反事实场景中将策略响应能力提升了1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然能生成可解释的思维链推理，但这些推理是否真实反映策略的内部决策过程尚不清楚。不忠实的推理可能掩盖因果联系、限制泛化能力，因此需要系统研究并提升具身推理的忠实性。

Method: 1) 通过人类评估分析SoTA自动驾驶推理模型，揭示推理质量与轨迹质量的不一致耦合；2) 提出Pinocchio——一个学习型批评器，通过评估观察锚定（observation grounding）和步骤一致性（stepwise coherence）作为忠实性的行为代理；3) 将Pinocchio的评分作为密集奖励信号，通过强化学习对具身策略进行后训练。

Result: 在保留的驾驶基准上，后训练策略在忠实性上分别比SoTA对齐基线和轨迹误差后训练基线提升4%和18%，同时保持竞争性的下游任务性能。在合成分布外反事实测试集上，忠实性后训练使策略对罕见场景的响应能力提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: 忠实性推理是提升具身智能鲁棒性、泛化性和可解释性的关键。通过Pinocchio批评器进行强化学习后训练，可以有效提升VLA模型的推理忠实性，而不会牺牲任务性能。忠实性推理有助于构建更可靠的具身智能系统。

Classification reason: 论文研究VLA模型在具身推理中的忠实性（faithfulness），涉及机器人决策、规划与强化学习后训练，属于具身世界模型与规划控制这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时论文也涉及多模态推理（Embodied CoT），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model faithfulness and embodied reasoning, aligning with high-priority world_model direction and boosting key keywords. Signals: world model, embodied reasoning, faithfulness, reinforcement learning, autonomous driving

Abstract: Embodied Chain-of-Thought has emerged as a promising mechanism to enhance robot decision-making and interpretability in black-box Vision-Language Action (VLA) models. However, whether this verbalized Chain-of-Thought truthfully reflects the policy's underlying decision process remains poorly understood. We distinguish between functional reasoning, in which reasoning improves task performance, and faithful reasoning, in which reasoning truly reflects the policy's internal decision process. We argue that SoTA alignment strategies offer a necessary but insufficient notion of faithfulness, admitting reasoning whose intermediate steps can mask the causal links in action prediction through confounding factors (e.g., reasoning that is ungrounded in the environment and internally disconnected or inconsistent), restricting policy generalization. We study this gap through a human evaluation of a SoTA reasoning model for autonomous driving, revealing an inconsistent coupling between reasoning quality and downstream trajectory improvement. We then operationalize a behavioral surrogate for embodied faithfulness through a learned critic, Pinocchio, scoring observation grounding and stepwise coherence, and use this critic as a dense reward signal in post-training an embodied policy with reinforcement learning. Across withheld driving benchmarks, our post-trained planner improves faithfulness by 4% and 18% over SoTA alignment and trajectory error post-training baselines, respectively, while maintaining competitive downstream task performance. Finally, on a synthetic out-of-distribution test set, post-training for faithfulness improves policy responsiveness to rare counterfactual scenarios by 1.6x that of a SoTA policy, suggesting that faithful reasoning traces contribute to more robust, generalizable, and interpretable embodied intelligence. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实驾驶场景中，相机观测常受遮挡、运动模糊、光照变化和传感器噪声影响，现有BEV方法不加区分地融合时序信息，导致轨迹规划不稳定、碰撞风险上升。亟需一种能显式建模特征质量和时序一致性的规划框架。

Method: 提出RCT-AD框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 可靠上下文感知模块，对每帧特征进行可靠性评分，通过质量门控FILO记忆机制选择性保留可靠特征，并从历史可靠上下文中重建退化观测；(2) 时序轨迹规划器，捕获长程依赖和多智能体交互，生成平滑且安全的轨迹；(3) 联合检测-分割头，将语义和运动线索注入共享BEV空间以增强场景理解。

Result: 在nuScenes自动驾驶基准上，RCT-AD达到61.5 NDS、52.9 mAP和52.3 mIoU，在感知精度、运动预测和规划鲁棒性上超越近期端到端基线，同时保持适合实时部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: RCT-AD通过显式建模观测可靠性和时序一致性，有效提升了自动驾驶在退化感知条件下的规划安全性和鲁棒性，为端到端自动驾驶中的感知-规划一体化提供了新的可靠性保障思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向自动驾驶的可靠上下文感知与时间规划框架（RCT-AD），核心是BEV空间中的感知-规划一体化，包含可靠性感知模块、时序轨迹规划器，属于具身智能/机器人领域的规划控制世界模型，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]（CANONICAL）。

Importance reason: Strong engineering for autonomous driving but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: autonomous driving, BEV perception, temporal planning, reliability-aware, nuScenes benchmark

Abstract: Safe operation of autonomous vehicles in dense urban traffic depends on perception and planning that remain reliable when onboard sensing is degraded. In real driving conditions, camera observations are frequently corrupted by occlusion, motion blur, illumination change, and sensor noise, and when such degraded observations are aggregated indiscriminately over time, trajectory planning becomes unstable and collision risk rises for both the ego vehicle and surrounding road users. Recent Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) approaches unify perception and planning through a shared spatial representation, but most fuse temporal information across frames without assessing the reliability of the underlying observations. We present a Reliable Context-Aware and Temporal Planning framework for Autonomous Driving (RCT-AD) that explicitly models feature quality and temporal consistency to support safer, more consistent planning. A Reliable Context Awareness module scores per-frame reliability and selectively retains trustworthy features through a quality-gated First-In-Last-Out (FILO) memory mechanism, reconstructing degraded observations from reliable historical context so that corrupted inputs do not destabilize the scene representation. A Temporal Trajectory Planner captures long-term dependencies and multi-agent interactions to produce smoother, safety-aware trajectories, while a joint detection-and-segmentation head injects semantic and motion cues into the shared BEV space to strengthen scene understanding. Experiments on the nuScenes autonomous driving benchmark show that RCT-AD improves perception accuracy, motion prediction, and planning robustness over recent end-to-end baselines, achieving 61.5 nuScenes Detection Score, 52.9 mean Average Precision, and 52.3 mean Intersection over Union, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency suitable for real-time deployment.

</details>

Method: GeoMoLa 学习离散运动潜码，通过预测点云在操作过程中的时空演化（4D目标：空间几何随时间变化）来提取运动模式，而非重建视觉观测。模型仅需单视角RGB-D输入，学习到的潜码编码物理运动而非外观模式。

Result: 在多个操作基准上达到SOTA性能，而现有方法需要多视角重建。消融实验验证了几何预测是性能提升的关键。学习到的运动潜码在新场景中能产生物理一致的运动变换，在真实世界杂乱环境中的鲁棒操作实验也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的机器人控制运动潜码应通过理解运动的三维几何效应而非像素级模式来学习。几何感知的运动潜码能够以更少的演示数据在杂乱环境中实现鲁棒操作。

Classification reason: 该论文学习机器人操作的运动潜码，通过预测点云在操作过程中的几何演化来提取运动模式，直接服务于机器人操作策略学习。这属于具身智能中面向机器人动作规划和控制的世界模型范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics manipulation paper but only tangentially related to your core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Geometry-aware motion latents, 4D point cloud prediction, Single-view RGB-D manipulation, World model for robotics, Scene generalization

Abstract: Learning motion latents for robotic manipulation heavily relies on extracting motion patterns from visual sequences, yet effective action abstractions require understanding three-dimensional geometric transformations. Here, we introduce GeoMoLa (Geometry-Aware Motion Latents), which learns discrete motion latent codes by predicting how point clouds evolve during manipulation rather than reconstructing visual observations. This four-dimensional objective -- spatial geometry changing through time -- forces latent representations to encode actual physical motion rather than appearance patterns. GeoMoLa achieves state-of-the-art performance using only single-view RGB-D input, while existing methods require multi-view reconstruction, succeeding across diverse manipulation benchmarks. Our ablations reveal that geometric prediction is the key to driving performance, quantitatively validating that manipulation depends on spatial understanding. Furthermore, the learned codes exhibit effective motion abstraction: applying them to novel scenes produces physically consistent transformations regardless of visual context. Our real-world experiments also confirm this robustness capability, achieving robust manipulation with minimal demonstrations in cluttered environments where geometric reasoning determines success. Thus, we demonstrate that effective motion latents for robot control can better emerge from understanding motion through its three-dimensional effects rather than pixel-level patterns.

</details>

Method: CAC-VLA 在 VLM 内部学习一个轻量级潜动作接口，训练 VLM 从未来动作片段中编码出从粗到细的潜动作表征（structured latent actions），并通过上下文门控（context gate）自适应地调节这些潜动作指导对动作专家生成的影响，实现 VLM 原生的动作条件化。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上达到 98.3% 平均成功率，在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 89.5% 平均成功率，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 上下文门控的潜动作条件化是一种有效的连续专家控制接口，能够在不依赖独立动作生成框架的情况下提升 VLA 模型的操作性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CAC-VLA，一种面向机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过上下文门控潜动作条件化来改进连续动作生成。这属于具身智能中的机器人操作与动作规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot manipulation (cs.RO) and action conditioning, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics of continuous language/multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: VLA models, latent-action interface, context gating, robot manipulation, LIBERO benchmark

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a promising paradigm for generalist robot manipulation, where visual-language representations are used to condition continuous action generation. However, these representations are not explicitly optimized for action conditioning, leaving the action expert to bridge the gap between multimodal understanding and precise motor control. Recent action-reasoning methods introduce additional modules to generate explicit action plans or action-space reasoning signals, demonstrating the benefit of action-level guidance but often requiring separate action-generation frameworks. We propose CAC-VLA, a Context-Gated Action Conditioning framework that learns a lightweight latent-action interface directly within the VLM. Instead of generating executable trajectories, CAC-VLA trains the VLM to predict coarse-to-fine latent actions, which are structured representations encoded from future action segments, and adaptively leverages them to condition the action expert via a context gate. This enables VLM-native action conditioning while calibrating the influence of latent-action guidance on expert action generation. Experiments on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of CAC-VLA, achieving 98.3% average success rate on LIBERO and 89.5% LIBERO-Plus, suggesting that context-gated latent-action conditioning is an effective interface for continuous expert control.

</details>

TL;DR: Athena-WBC 提出了一种能力对齐的策略专家（capability-aligned policy experts）方法，用于解决人形机器人全身控制中的长尾运动问题。通过动态专家（tracking-focused, constraint-aware）和平衡专家（gravity curriculum）分别处理高动态过渡和平衡关键运动，再通过 DAgger 蒸馏和 RL 微调压缩为单一控制器，在真实全尺寸人形机器人上取得了优于 SONIC 基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模人形运动跟踪控制器通过重分配训练努力（如对困难运动更多采样、分拆为子集或分配专家）来改进，但即使在针对性训练下，仍有一批可行的训练片段无法被解决，尤其是高动态过渡和平衡关键运动。作者认为失败原因不仅是训练不足，更是运动需求与默认训练配方所诱导的有效能力之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出 Athena-WBC 紧凑型教师-学生流水线：(1) 动态专家使用跟踪导向、约束感知的目标函数，移除保守努力和时间控制惩罚，同时保留物理可行性约束；(2) 平衡专家使用重力课程（gravity curriculum）提高早期训练存活率；(3) 特权教师通过运动路由进行 DAgger 蒸馏，压缩为单一控制器，使用可部署观测，再经 RL 微调。

Result: 在全尺寸人形机器人上的实验表明，Athena-WBC 相比强基线 SONIC 配方，在训练集长尾运动的恢复和未见过的运动跟踪上均有更好表现，且仅使用了少量专家。

Conclusion: 人形机器人全身控制中的长尾运动失败不仅源于训练不足，更源于运动需求与训练诱导能力之间的不匹配。通过能力对齐的策略专家设计（动态专家+平衡专家），可以更有效地覆盖长尾运动分布，实现更好的全身控制性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人全身控制（whole-body control），属于具身智能与机器人控制范畴，与 canonical 子主题"具身世界模型与规划控制"（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanoid whole-body control, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and lacks alignment with the specified direction weights and boost keywords. Signals: humanoid robotics, whole-body control, long-tail motion, policy distillation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large-scale humanoid motion-tracking controllers are commonly improved by reallocating training effort: difficult motions are sampled more often, isolated into smaller subsets, or assigned to specialized experts. We show that this view is incomplete. In strong whole-body-control baselines, a residual set of feasible training clips remains unsolved even under targeted training, especially for high-dynamic transitions and balance-critical motions. These failures arise not only from insufficient exposure, but from a mismatch between the motion demands and the effective capability induced by the default training recipe. We propose Athena-WBC, a compact teacher-student pipeline with capability-aligned policy experts for long-tail humanoid whole-body control. Dynamic experts use a tracking-focused, constraint-aware objective that removes conservative effort and temporal-control penalties while preserving physical feasibility constraints; balance experts use a gravity curriculum to improve early-training survivability. The resulting privileged teachers are motion-routed for DAgger distillation and then compressed into a single controller with deployable observations followed by RL fine-tuning. Experiments on a full-size humanoid show improved recovery of training-set long-tail motions and better held-out tracking than a strong SONIC-recipe baseline, using only a small number of experts.

</details>

Conclusion: PRISM通过图像驱动的场景和运动合成，有效弥合了仿真与真实环境之间的差距，为个性化机器人数据集生成提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，有助于提升机器人策略在用户特定环境中的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PRISM，从单张图像和自然语言指令生成个性化机器人数据集，用于训练机器人策略。这属于具身智能中面向机器人策略学习的数据生成方法，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong robotics dataset generation pipeline but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: single-image to dataset, digital cousin scenes, no teleoperation, LIBERO benchmark, real-world manipulation

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale pretrained vision-language-action models have improved robot policy learning, but directly deploying such policies in user-specific environments remains challenging due to limited generalization, which inevitably requires collecting a dataset tailored to the target environment. Teleoperation yields well-aligned data but is costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation scales easily but struggles to resemble the target environment and generate task-specific trajectories. To meet both simultaneously, we propose PRISM, an end-to-end pipeline that generates personalized robotic datasets from a single image and a natural-language instruction. PRISM constructs digital cousin scenes that are semantically and geometrically aligned with the user environment yet diverse at the instance level, and synthesizes executable demonstrations without human teleoperation. Extensive experiments show that policies trained on PRISM-generated datasets outperform those trained on baseline-generated datasets on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, achieve up to 100\% success rate on three real-world manipulation tasks, and maintain stronger performance when evaluated in environments that differ from those seen during training.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）擅长物理接地执行，但缺乏 VLM-based VLA 中的显式语言级规划接口来分解粗粒度指令；同时，WAM 与 VLA 之间缺乏在相同平台、数据与评估标准下的公平真实机器人对比。

Method: 提出 DSWAM 双系统架构：System 1 为 WAM 执行器，通过动作预测与视频联合训练（co-training）学习世界感知的动作生成，推理时直接预测动作块而不显式生成未来视频；System 2 为可选的视觉语言子任务规划器，在需要时从短期视觉历史与全局任务提示中预测可执行的子任务。集成 TensorRT 加速、异步执行与实时分块（RTC）实现实际机器人部署。

Result: 在 DeMaVLA 真实变形物体操作设定下，DSWAM 在精细操作任务上取得了有竞争力的表现，验证了双系统设计的有效性，并提供了 WAM 与 VLA 在相同平台上的公平对比结果。

Conclusion: DSWAM 通过双系统设计弥合了 WAM 在语言级规划上的不足，同时提供了 WAM 与 VLA 在真实机器人上的公平对比基准，展示了世界动作模型在精细操作任务中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DSWAM，一个面向精细机器人操作的双系统世界动作基础模型，包含 System 1 WAM 执行器和 System 2 视觉语言子任务规划器，属于具身智能中的世界模型用于机器人规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model and robot manipulation paper, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: World action model, Robot manipulation, Dual-system planning, TensorRT acceleration, DeMaVLA benchmark

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) provide a promising alternative to Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies by using video-based world modeling as dense supervision for robot action learning. Existing WAMs excel at physically grounded execution, but typically lack the explicit language-level planning interface in VLM-based VLAs for decomposing coarse instructions. Such decomposition becomes important when household tasks involve complex multi-step goals, where coarse user commands need to be converted into sequences of fine-grained executable subtasks. Meanwhile, the field still lacks a fair real-robot comparison between VLA and WAM execution capabilities, since existing systems often differ in data, robot embodiments, and task protocols. To address both the decomposition gap and the need for a controlled WAM-VLA comparison, we introduce DSWAM, a Dual-System World Action Foundation Model for fine-grained robot manipulation. DSWAM keeps a System 1 WAM executor as the default control path and optionally activates a System 2 vision-language subtask planner only when task decomposition is useful. The planner predicts executable subtasks from short-term visual history and a global task prompt, while the WAM executor performs world-aware action generation for each instruction or subtask. The executor is trained with action prediction and video co-training, but inference directly predicts action chunks without explicit future video generation. To make this execution path practical on real robots, we further integrate TensorRT acceleration, asynchronous execution, and real-time chunking (RTC) so that policy queries do not block robot control. To provide a fair real-robot comparison with VLA policies, we build and evaluate DSWAM under the DeMaVLA real-world deformable manipulation setting with matched robot platform, pretraining data, post-training data, and evaluation criteria.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在8个新的开放 VA 任务基准上评估（4个仿真任务 + 4个真实世界任务），GaP 的成功率显著优于基线方法。具体数值在论文中有详细报告。

Conclusion: GaP 通过将机器人技能模块化为有向计算图并结合内部仿真自学习，有效弥合了变分自动化任务的可靠性差距，为商业和工业机器人应用提供了一种可解释、可迭代优化的解决方案。

Abstract: For robots to work reliably in commercial and industrial applications, can recent advances in agentic coding systems combine interpretable robot programming with the open-world adaptability of model-free policies? We focus on "Variational Automation" (VA), a class of tasks that have larger variations in object geometry and pose than fixed automation. Model-free policies often struggle to close the reliability gap for VA tasks, which must be executed persistently and reliably in commercial and industrial applications. Motivated by prior work on Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) and the Robot Operating System (ROS), we introduce Graph-as-Policy (GaP), a multi-agent coding harness that generates directed computation graphs with perception, planning, and control nodes from a Modular Open Robot Skill Library (MORSL). GaP then generates an internal simulation environment to rehearse task instances with different graphs in parallel to iteratively refine the graph structure and parameters to improve success rates and throughput. Evaluation with 8 new open VA task benchmarks, 4 in-simulation and 4 in real-world, suggests that GaP can achieve success rates that significantly outperform baselines.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Cortex 框架包含：(1) 将操作子任务标准化为 32 个规范技能原语，并注入可执行性约束（代表性物体属性、轨迹可达性）；(2) 自动标注 4000+ 小时开源视频数据并生成 30 小时仿真数据；(3) 事件平衡采样策略用于微调训练数据，处理子任务转换时的规划歧义；(4) 推理时通过任务上下文到技能约束的工程化设计增强规划质量。

Conclusion: Cortex 通过双向对齐的规划接口有效弥合了高层规划与低层执行之间的鸿沟，为长程操作任务提供了一种可行的层次化解决方案，并展示了通用 VLM 与微调 VLA 结合在零样本泛化方面的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Cortex，一个面向长程操作任务的具身智能体框架，通过双向对齐将高层 VLM 规划与低层 VLA 执行连接起来，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Strong embodied world model work but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: embodied agent, world model, long-horizon manipulation, VLM-VLA alignment, zero-shot real-world tasks

Abstract: While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise toward generalist manipulation policies, they struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their Markovian nature-relying solely on current observations. Hierarchical dual-system methods address this but suffer from a gap between high-level planning semantics and low-level execution kinematics. We introduce Cortex, a bidirectionally aligned embodied agent framework with a customized planning interface that conveys executable and tractable subtask plans from high-level VLM to low-level VLA. Specifically, we standardize manipulation subtasks into 32 canonical skill primitives and inject tractability principles, such as representative object attributes and improved trajectory reachability, into the data generation pipeline. This enables automatic annotation of over 4k hours of open-source video data and generation of 30 hours of simulation data. We further devise an event-balanced sampling strategy to construct training data for fine-tuning the framework to better handle planning ambiguity during subtask transitions, enhanced by carefully designed harness engineering from task contexts to skill constraints during inference. Both open-loop VLM and closed-loop system evaluations demonstrate Cortex's efficacy, e.g., it outperforms monolithic baselines by 3.1% on Libero-long and 4.1% on RoboTwin. Notably, Cortex's generalist VLM enables zero-shot completion of unseen real-world long-horizon tasks, such as multi-stage chemistry experiments, by simply combining with a fine-tuned VLA-a capability infeasible through VLA fine-tuning alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期感知系统通常被动地响应物体变化（仅在重新访问场景后更新地图），缺乏主动推理物体持久性的能力。机器人需要根据场景上下文（如停车位的车 vs 红绿灯前的车）提前预测物体可能停留的时长，以支持长期自主决策。

Method: PreSIST 包含两个变体：(1) PreSIST-Lang 使用 VLM 从场景上下文推理物体持久性先验；(2) PreSIST-Vis 使用 PreSIST-Lang 生成的伪标签训练纯视觉模型，实现高效部署。两者均将先验与概率持久性滤波器结合，随观测更新预测。

Result: 在野外物体持久性标注的新数据集上，PreSIST-Lang 和 PreSIST-Vis 在开放世界持久性预测任务上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PreSIST 证明了利用 VLM 的场景理解能力进行物体持久性预测的有效性，为机器人长期自主感知与规划提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人在开放世界场景中预测物体持久性（物体是否会在未来时间停留在原位），属于机器人长期感知与场景动态建模，与具身世界模型中的环境建模、规划控制高度相关，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot long-term perception and VLM-based persistence prediction, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and generative video/image generation. Signals: VLM for scene reasoning, object persistence prediction, open-world robot perception, probabilistic persistence filter, pseudo-label training for vision-only model

Abstract: Robots deployed over long periods must reason about environments that change over time. Existing long-term perception systems often address object change reactively, updating their maps only after revisiting a scene and observing that an object has moved. Instead, robots should reason proactively about how long objects are likely to persist using the context in which they appear. For example, a car at a traffic light and a car in a parking spot share the same semantic class, but their contexts imply different persistence durations. We propose PreSIST (Predictive Scene-conditioned Instance Survival over Time), a method for predicting whether an observed object will remain in its last seen pose at arbitrary future times. PreSIST estimates instance-level persistence priors from object properties and scene context, then integrates these priors with a probabilistic persistence filter as observations become available. Its key insight is that the reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) can relate scene context to likely object use and human activity, enabling persistence prediction before long-term observations are available. We develop two interchangeable variants: PreSIST-Lang, which estimates persistence priors using a VLM, and PreSIST-Vis, a novel vision-only model trained using PreSIST-Lang pseudo-labels for efficient deployment. Experiments on a new dataset of in-the-wild object persistence annotations show that PreSIST-Lang and PreSIST-Vis outperform baselines on open-world persistence prediction.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相机-雷达融合模型通常依赖任务特定的监督训练，缺乏可复用的表征学习。作者希望利用预测式世界模型预训练，在仅有相机和雷达的传感器配置下学习可迁移的驾驶场景表征，从而提升下游任务性能。

Method: CRISP 包含三个关键设计：(1) 增强型雷达编码器，更好地提取雷达距离和多普勒信息；(2) 雷达增强的时间自注意力机制，将雷达线索注入 BEV 时序传播；(3) 多模态特征渲染与模态创新门控，允许 BEV token 选择性融合相机和雷达证据。预训练阶段以历史多视角图像和雷达扫描为输入，预测未来 LiDAR 点云作为监督信号（LiDAR 仅在预训练中使用）。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上，CRISP 在长时点云预测任务上表现优异，并有效迁移到 3D 检测、跟踪、在线地图、运动预测、未来占用预测和规划等多个下游任务，验证了预测式相机-雷达预训练的有效性。

Conclusion: CRISP 表明，在仅有相机和雷达的实际传感器配置下，通过预测未来 LiDAR 点云进行世界模型预训练是一种有前景的路径，能够学习可扩展的驾驶场景表征，为自动驾驶感知与规划提供统一的基础模型。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CRISP，通过预测未来 LiDAR 点云进行表征学习预训练，学习统一的 BEV 世界表征，用于自动驾驶下游任务（检测、跟踪、规划等），属于面向具身智能/自动驾驶的世界模型预训练方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong world-model pretraining for driving but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model pretraining, camera-radar fusion, BEV representation, LiDAR as privileged supervision, nuScenes benchmark

Abstract: Camera-radar (CR) fusion is a practical sensing configuration for autonomous driving, but existing models are typically trained with task-specific supervision, limiting reusable representation learning. We present CRISP, a spatiotemporal CR backbone pretrained through forecasting-based representation learning. Given historical multi-view images and radar sweeps, CRISP learns a unified bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation by predicting future LiDAR point clouds. LiDAR is used only as privileged supervision during pretraining; the deployed model requires only camera and radar. To make forecasting-based pretraining effective for CR fusion, CRISP introduces an enhanced radar encoder, radar-enhanced temporal self-attention, and multimodal feature rendering with modality innovation gating. These components inject radar range and Doppler cues into BEV temporal propagation and allow BEV tokens to selectively incorporate camera and radar evidence. Experiments on nuScenes show that CRISP improves long-horizon point cloud forecasting and transfers effectively to downstream tasks, including 3D detection, tracking, online mapping, motion forecasting, future occupancy prediction, and planning, suggesting that predictive CR pretraining is a promising path toward scalable driving representations under practical sensor configurations. The project website is .

*Ruslan Rakhimov, George Bredis, Yuriy Maksyuta, Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: Qantara 提出了一种桥流联合训练方法，使 JEPA 世界模型在单个检查点下同时支持潜空间规划、行为克隆和逆动力学三种推理范式，在 LeWM 控制套件上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 世界模型在训练时只能选择单一推理范式（轨迹优化或行为克隆），无法在部署时根据计算成本或观测条件灵活切换。作者希望训练一个统一的模型，将范式选择推迟到推理阶段。

Method: Qantara 采用端到端 JEPA 架构，联合训练目标包含两部分：状态轴上的 Brownian-bridge 插值（连接相邻干净潜变量）和动作轴上的噪声到数据流匹配。训练时在 (动作-时间, 状态-时间) 噪声正方形的边缘集中质量，使模型能同时支持潜空间规划、行为克隆动作采样和视频-逆组合推理三种范式。

Result: 在 LeWM 控制套件上，Qantara 达到 91.2 SR 的三种子平均成功率，在 OGBench-Cube 上比 DINO-WM 高 7.7 SR、比 LeWM 高 19.7 SR。同一权重下，行为克隆和视频-逆路径在 Push-T 上达到 82-83 SR，在 Cube 上达到 71-73 SR。

Conclusion: Qantara 将 JEPA 世界模型从单范式规划器推进到多范式控制器，证明了桥流联合训练能够统一多种推理范式，且训练时边缘质量集中对性能至关重要。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Qantara，一种基于JEPA的潜空间世界模型，支持潜空间规划、行为克隆和逆动力学三种推理范式，用于机器人控制。这与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度吻合，属于该规范子主题的典型工作。

Importance reason: Novel bridge-flow training for JEPA world models enables multi-paradigm control, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, JEPA, multi-paradigm control, flow matching, latent planning

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) underpin a growing family of latent world models for control from raw pixels, but every existing JEPA world model commits at training time to a single inference paradigm: either trajectory optimisation in a learned dynamics model, or direct behaviour cloning. A single checkpoint that serves both would defer this choice to inference, when deployment constraints (rollout cost, observation accessibility) determine which path wins. We present Qantara, an end-to-end JEPA whose joint training objective pairs a Brownian-bridge interpolant between consecutive clean latents on the state axis with noise-to-data flow matching on the action axis. The same checkpoint serves three inference paradigms without retraining: latent planning, behaviour-cloning action sampling, and inverse dynamics, which we query through a video-inverse composition that first predicts the next latent without action conditioning, then extracts the action. Training concentrates mass on the edges of the (action-time, state-time) noise square, where inference queries the predictor: replacing it with uniform interior sampling drops Push-T planning from 90.1 to 53.3 SR at matched compute. On the LeWM control suite, Qantara reaches a 91.2 SR three-train-seed average and sets new SOTA on OGBench-Cube (+7.7 SR over DINO-WM, +19.7 over LeWM). From the same weights, the behaviour-cloning and video-inverse paths reach 82-83 SR on Push-T and 71-73 SR on Cube. These results move JEPA world models from single-paradigm planners to multi-paradigm controllers.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用层解析线性探针（layer-resolved linear probing）检测历史帧信息在各层的可解码性；使用因果干预（causal interchange interventions）分析历史信息对动作输出的因果贡献。在三种VLA模型（来自两个架构家族）上进行实验，并引入了一种无需训练的时间部署审计方法。

Result: 过去帧内容在整个网络中保持线性可解码；但历史帧中独有的信息几乎不存在，存储的历史主要是当前帧的冗余副本；历史信息仅在当前帧严重遮挡时才被因果使用；不同架构在相同遮挡条件下表现出不同的历史依赖策略（fallback vs. non-fallback）；在fallback模式下重新注入历史无法修复遮挡或消除动作歧义，而在另一种模式下相同干预可以可靠地引导动作。

Conclusion: VLA并没有忘记过去，但它们很大程度上未能将历史表征为与当前帧不同的信息。未来的记忆增强应注入过去独有的信息，而非简单地增加更多历史数据。模型的可干预性取决于历史如何被部署而非是否被编码。

Classification reason: 该论文研究冻结的VLA（视觉-语言-动作模型）如何编码和利用历史视觉信息进行动作决策，属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型研究。论文关注历史帧信息在VLA中的表征与因果使用，与"具身世界模型与规划控制"这一[CANONICAL]子主题高度匹配。

Abstract: A frozen vision-language-action model (VLA) receives recent observations at every decision step, yet prior work has focused on adding memory rather than asking how existing history is represented and used. We study this temporal axis using layer-resolved linear probing and causal interchange interventions across three VLAs from two architecture families. We find a three-part dissociation. First, past-frame content remains linearly decodable throughout the network. Second, information unique to history beyond the current frame is nearly absent, indicating that stored history is largely a redundant copy of the present. Third, history is causally deployed only when the current frame is heavily degraded, while the action readout progressively loses dependence on history through the network. Although all models encode history similarly, their deployment strategies differ: under the same occlusion, one architecture increasingly relies on history as a fallback, whereas the other relies on it less. We further introduce a training-free temporal deployment audit that distinguishes these regimes. In the fallback regime, re-injecting history neither repairs occlusion nor disambiguates actions, confirming the redundancy of the stored representation. In the other regime, the same intervention reliably steers the predicted action toward the donor history. These results show that steerability depends on how history is deployed rather than whether it is encoded. VLAs do not forget the past; they largely fail to represent it as information distinct from the present. Our findings suggest that future memory augmentation should inject information unique to the past rather than simply more history.

</details>

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人数据上的实验表明，CamVLA 在多种未见过的相机视角下均能持续提升任务成功率，显著优于现有基线方法，展现出良好的视角泛化能力。

Conclusion: CamVLA 通过解耦动作生成与视角几何，实现了无需标定的视角鲁棒机器人操作策略，为实际部署中相机配置变化场景提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CamVLA，一种面向机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过解耦相机中心动作预测与手眼矩阵估计，实现无需标定的视角鲁棒操作。这属于具身智能中机器人策略学习与动作规划的核心问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel calibration-free view-robust VLA for robotics, but only tangentially touches user's core continuous multimodal and video generation priorities. Signals: world model, robotics VLA, calibration-free, view-robust, camera-centric action

Abstract: Real-world robot deployment rarely maintains the training-stage camera setup, where cameras often experience repositioning or remounting depending on actual scenarios. Existing view-robust Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies tolerate such camera variations only when the camera extrinsics are explicitly provided, making them fragile and hard to use especially when view robustness is critical. We argue that the policy should not be told where the camera is, but rather figure it out by itself. To this end, we introduce Camera-Centric VLA (CamVLA), a new VLA model that decouples manipulation controls from camera geometry by predicting (i) a camera-centric end-effector action expressed in the local camera frame, and (ii) a 6-DoF hand-eye matrix relating cameras to the robot base. A deterministic geometric transformation composes the two predictions into a robot base-frame action. This disentangles how I should move in pose-independent camera-centric action generation from where I am looking from in camera-perspective geometric grounding. The resulting policy is calibration-free, depth-free, and single-view, requiring only a single monocular RGB image as the visual observation and task instruction at deployment. Evaluations in both simulation and real-world robot data show that CamVLA consistently improves success rates across diverse unseen viewpoints. Project page: .

Conclusion: 统一多模态模型作为世界模型具有巨大潜力，统一化能够带来一致的性能提升和更深层次的多模态世界建模洞察。代码和模型将在论文接收后开源。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal world models for robotics, aligning with top priority continuous multimodal and world model directions. Signals: unified multimodal model, world model, vision-language-action, robotic manipulation, BAGEL

Abstract: World models aim to capture environment dynamics in ways that support perception, reasoning, and action, and have recently become a central direction in Vision-Language-Action-World (VLAW) modeling. Meanwhile, unified vision-language models have demonstrated strong multimodal generation capabilities, yet their potential as world models remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce \texttt{WorldBagel}, a unified VLAW framework built on BAGEL, a modern multimodal unified model, and use it to systematically investigate the role of unification in world modeling. Across multi-task robotic manipulation and cross-domain experiments, \texttt{WorldBagel} consistently outperforms task-specific alternatives and learns action representations that are more structured and semantically aligned with visual and linguistic context. Experiments on LIBERO, Language Table, and Franka show that unification is not only an architectural convenience, but also a key factor in learning effective VLAW models, leading to consistent empirical gains and deeper insights into multimodal world modeling. Code and model checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在标准连续控制和机器人操作基准上，该方法恢复的任务充分潜在表征能够捕获所有控制相关因素，基于这些表征的策略在样本效率和泛化能力上均有提升，包括跨技能、跨物体-技能组合以及未见任务的泛化。

Conclusion: 智能体主动探索与结构化世界模型学习的协同能够有效学习任务充分的潜在表征，提升世界模型驱动的决策性能与泛化能力，为构建更高效、更通用的智能体提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何通过智能体主动探索与结构化世界模型学习的协同，学习任务充分（task-sufficient）的潜在表征以提升决策效率与泛化能力。核心是面向决策控制的世界模型学习，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong synergy between agent exploration and structured world modeling for task-sufficient representations, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, structured representation, task-sufficient latent, agentic exploration, sample efficiency

Abstract: Learning and planning in imagination using world models provides an effective paradigm for training agents for decision-making. However, existing approaches often rely on high-dimensional latent spaces or generic visual embeddings that retain many factors irrelevant to control, limiting efficiency and generalization across tasks. To this end, we study how agents can learn world models with representations that are task-specific, minimal, and sufficient for decision-making. We achieve this via a closed-loop synergy between the agent and the world model, in which structured world-model learning distills task-sufficient representations from informative interaction data. On the agent side, agents actively probe the environment to collect informative trajectories that expose task-relevant latent factors, guided by an adaptive curriculum. On the world-model side, we learn structured representations over observations to distill compact, task-sufficient latent states from the collected interaction data. This synergy enables the empirical recovery of task-sufficient latent representations that capture all control-relevant factors. Leveraging these representations, the resulting policies achieve improved sample efficiency and generalization, including generalization across skills, object-skill compositions, and previously unseen tasks on standard continuous-control and robotic-manipulation benchmarks.

</details>


### [51] [Operator-on-F complements value-equivalence: a planning-time diagnostic for latent world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04464)
*Donna Vakalis*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (80.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 operator-on-F 的规划时诊断方法，用于评估 latent world model 在潜空间 rollout 中的误差。该方法通过比较模型在可观测子集 F 上的 k 步潜空间推进行为与环境真实行为，揭示奖励预测误差无法捕捉的规划相关错误。实验表明，operator error 与规划回报损失高度相关（Spearman -0.90），而传统奖励预测误差和 Bellman 残差与回报关系微弱，为世界模型评估提供了互补性诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习中，世界模型通常通过奖励预测和值预测来评估，但这些指标可能遗漏模型在潜空间 rollout 中与规划相关的关键错误。现有评估方法分辨率有限，无法有效区分不同规模模型的规划性能差异，亟需一种能直接诊断潜空间推进行为误差的互补性方法。

Method: 提出 operator-on-F 诊断方法，在可观测子集 F 上比较模型的 k 步潜空间推进行为（latent pushforward）与环境真实行为，使用模型自身的预测器进行评估。在 TD-MPC2 的 cheetah-run 任务上对不同模型规模进行系统实验，并与奖励预测误差、Bellman 残差等传统指标进行对比分析。

Result: 在 cheetah-run 任务上，奖励预测误差在 [0.028, 0.091] 范围内仅约 3 倍变化，分辨率有限；而 operator error 在相同规模范围内从 0.28 到 2.62 跨越近一个数量级。317M 模型 operator error 高达 2.62，规划回报降至 0.9。operator error 与回报损失的秩相关为 -0.90（95% CI [-0.90, -0.70]），而 Bellman 残差和奖励误差与回报的相关性仅为 -0.10 和 -0.30。跨架构比较中，operator 也能有效区分 TD-MPC2 与纯 SSL 潜世界模型。

Conclusion: Operator-on-F 诊断方法能够有效揭示 latent world model 中奖励预测误差无法捕捉的规划相关错误，与世界模型的值等价性评估形成互补。该方法具有更高的分辨率和与规划性能更强的相关性，为基于模型的强化学习中世界模型的评估与选择提供了有价值的诊断工具。

Classification reason: 论文研究基于模型强化学习中世界模型的评估诊断方法，属于世界模型方向。其核心关注 latent world model 在规划中的误差诊断，与具身世界模型/规划控制高度相关，因此复用 canonical subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel diagnostic for latent world models, but focuses on RL planning rather than the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model evaluation, latent rollout diagnostic, planning-time error, operator-on-F, TD-MPC2

Abstract: World-model evaluation for model-based reinforcement learning typically asks whether the learned model predicts reward and value well, which can leave planning-relevant errors in the model's latent rollouts unmeasured. We introduce a complementary diagnostic, operator-on-F, that compares a model's k-step latent pushforward to the environment's on an observable subset F, using the model's own predictor. On a TD-MPC2 size sweep over cheetah-run, reward-prediction error stays within [0.028, 0.091] for every model size - only about 3x variation - so an unnormalized reward-fit check has narrow resolution to distinguish them; the (unnormalized) Bellman residual and reward error themselves have weak relationships with return (Spearman -0.10 and -0.30). Operator error spans 0.28 to 2.62 over the same sizes. At 317M the operator error is 2.62 - an order of magnitude above the 0.28-0.36 cluster - and the planning return collapses to 0.9, while reward-prediction error (0.091) is the highest of the five but stays within the same small [0.028, 0.091] range as the rest of the sweep. The rank correlation between operator error and return loss is -0.90 (anchor-bootstrap 95% CI [-0.90, -0.70] at n=5 sizes; leave-one-out removal of any single size leaves it at -0.80 or stronger). The operator also returns informative, architecture-discriminating estimates in a cross-architecture comparison between TD-MPC2 and a pure-SSL latent world model. The operator diagnostic complements value-equivalence rather than replacing it.

</details>

### [52] [Function-Space Diffusion for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02977)
*Zinuo Chang, Yipu Chen, Byoungwoo Park, Hongzhe Yu, Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

TL;DR: 本文提出 FSD-MP，一种基于函数空间扩散的运动规划方法，将轨迹建模为连续函数并在谱域中进行扩散，使用 DST-FNO 参数化逆过程，实现离散化不变的零样本轨迹生成，在 2D 点机器人和 7-DoF Franka 机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的运动规划方法通常操作在固定长度的路径点序列上，导致模型依赖于分辨率，无法在训练分辨率之外进行零样本泛化。作者希望设计一种离散化不变的扩散运动规划器，使其能够在不同分辨率下保持一致的规划行为。

Method: 将轨迹视为连续函数，在函数空间中进行扩散。前向过程在谱域中定义，使用 Matérn 型协方差的高斯噪声驱动；逆过程由边界兼容的离散正弦变换傅里叶神经算子（DST-FNO）参数化，该算子天然保持起点-终点约束且与分辨率无关。训练时在固定分辨率下学习，推理时可零样本泛化到任意分辨率。

Result: 在 2D 点机器人和 7-DoF Franka 机械臂规划基准上，FSD-MP 在训练分辨率下达到与基线相当的规划性能，并能零样本泛化到高达 16 倍的分辨率，保持一致的规划行为，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 函数空间扩散为离散化不变的运动规划提供了有效框架，能够在不同分辨率下保持一致的规划行为，为机器人运动规划中的扩散模型应用开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于函数空间扩散的运动规划方法（FSD-MP），将轨迹建模为连续函数并在函数空间中进行扩散，实现离散化不变的轨迹生成。这属于机器人运动规划中的环境动力学建模与交互式规划，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel function-space diffusion for motion planning, but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: function-space diffusion, motion planning, discretization-invariant, zero-shot generalization, DST-FNO

Abstract: Diffusion-based motion planners have demonstrated strong performance in generating diverse and high-quality robot trajectories in cluttered environments with multiple feasible solutions. However, existing approaches typically operate on fixed-length waypoint sequences, making the learned model resolution-dependent, thereby preventing zero-shot generalization across resolutions. In this work, we propose Function-Space Diffusion for Motion Planning (FSD-MP), a diffusion-based motion planner that models trajectories as continuous functions and performs diffusion directly in function space, achieving discretization-invariant trajectory generation. We define a mode-wise forward process in the spectral domain, driven by Gaussian noise with a Matérn-type covariance, and parameterize the reverse process with a boundary-compatible Discrete Sine Transform-based Fourier Neural Operator (DST-FNO) that preserves start-goal constraints across resolutions. We evaluate FSD-MP on 2D point robot and 7-DoF Franka manipulator planning benchmarks. Our method achieves competitive planning performance at the training resolution and generalizes zero-shot across resolutions up to 16$\times$ higher, preserving consistent planning behavior without retraining. These results demonstrate that function-space diffusion provides an effective framework for discretization-invariant motion planning.

</details>

*Junru Gu, Lijin Yang, Jianing Huang, Shu Liu, Zhongzhan Huang, Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶闭环仿真器大多依赖日志回放或基于规则的智能体，行为多样性不足，难以覆盖长尾场景。需要一种能生成多样化、可交互、语义丰富的仿真场景的方法来更全面地评估自动驾驶规划系统。

Method: 提出Agent-driven仿真框架，使用指令跟随大语言模型通过结构化动作接口控制周围交通参与者，实现有意图且反应式的行为，同时保持物理合理性。并构建SemanticPlan基准，在真实nuPlan场景基础上增加多个遵循不同语言指令的交互式智能体，形成长尾且语义丰富的闭环规划测试集。

Result: 实验表明，当前最先进的规划器在SemanticPlan基准上仍难以一致地实现安全有效的任务完成，说明这些长尾场景对现有方法具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM驱动的智能体仿真能有效生成多样化的长尾交互场景，为自动驾驶闭环评测提供了新的范式，同时揭示了现有规划器在复杂语义场景下的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于LLM驱动的智能体仿真框架用于自动驾驶闭环评测，属于可交互世界建模与物理仿真方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and interactive simulation for autonomous driving, a key priority subtopic. Signals: world model, interactive simulation, long-tail scenario generation, LLM-driven agents, closed-loop evaluation

Abstract: Evaluating autonomous driving systems in closed-loop settings requires realistic and interactive simulation, yet existing simulators largely rely on log replay or rule-based agents, limiting behavioral diversity and long-tail coverage. We propose an agent-driven simulation framework in which surrounding road participants are controlled by instruction-following large language models through a structured action interface, enabling intentional and reactive behaviors while preserving physical plausibility. Furthermore, we introduce SemanticPlan, a benchmark of closed-loop planning in long-tail and semantically rich scenarios that augment real nuPlan scenes with multiple interactive agents following diverse language instructions. Evaluation results show that state-of-the-art planners still struggle to consistently achieve safe and effective task completion, suggesting that these long-tail scenarios remain challenging.

</details>


### [54] [Reduced-Order Models: The Mother of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03198)
*Rajat Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG, math-ph

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文论证世界模型的核心功能结构早在几十年前的模型降阶（MOR）与控制文献中就已独立发展出来，包括POD潜空间、编码器-解码器架构、动作条件预测以及可验证的误差界。文章对比了MOR与学习型世界模型的各自优势，指出可验证性是部署于关键系统的核心障碍，并提出了融合两条研究路线的物理可验证世界模型研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习型世界模型虽然预测精度高，但在电力、热控、过程控制等不可失败系统中缺乏可验证性，无法保证闭环控制的安全性。作者希望通过回溯MOR与控制理论的历史，为世界模型引入形式化的验证框架。

Method: 通过跨社区文献追踪，系统对比了三个领域（湍流降阶建模、特征脸方法、基于测量的POD热控框架）中的模型结构，将其映射到世界模型的编码器-潜状态-动作条件-解码器-验证层这一功能解剖结构上。

Result: 揭示了MOR社区贡献了验证性、物理基础与极端数据效率，而学习型世界模型贡献了非线性表征、可迁移性与长时域预测能力。指出当前世界模型在关键系统中的主要障碍不是预测保真度，而是可验证性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个融合物理基础与可验证性的世界模型研究议程，呼吁将MOR的误差界分析与学习型世界模型的表达能力相结合，以推动世界模型在安全关键系统中的实际部署。

Classification reason: 该论文从模型降阶（MOR）和控制理论视角重新审视世界模型，强调物理一致性、可验证性与环境动力学建模，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）这一规范子主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model verifiability, a critical gap for deploying in high-stakes systems, and bridges classical MOR with modern learned world models. Signals: world model, verifiability, model-order-reduction, physical grounding, error bounds

Abstract: World models -- compressed latent representations of an environment that support action-conditioned prediction and planning -- are typically presented as a product of modern self-supervised learning. This paper argues that the functional anatomy of a world model was independently developed, deployed, and formally analyzed decades earlier in the model-order-reduction (MOR) and control literature, under different names and for a different purpose: the real-time operation of physical systems. We trace the anatomy across three communities. Low-dimensional models of turbulence built on proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) supplied latent dynamics learned from data of a chaotic environment; eigenface methods in early computer vision supplied the encoder-decoder half, including a primitive runtime validity check; and measurement-based POD frameworks for facility thermal control assembled the complete loop -- POD coefficients as latent state, parametric dependence on actuator setpoints as action conditioning, modal reconstruction as decoding, and, critically, a priori analytical error bounds as a verification layer that certified when the model's predictions could be trusted in closed loop. We then examine what each tradition possesses that the other lacks: MOR contributes verification, physical grounding, and extreme data efficiency; learned world models contribute nonlinear representation, transferability, and horizon. We argue that the outstanding obstacle to deploying world models in systems that cannot fail -- power, thermal, process control -- is not predictive fidelity but verifiability, and we outline a research agenda for physics-grounded, verifiable world models that unifies the two lineages.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋机器人仿真器主要面向自主系统，缺乏人机交互支持和真实的物理环境建模，难以满足操作员培训、任务预演和人机协同等需求。

Method: 提出 LOTUSim 仿真架构，支持实时多用户交互与大规模异构海洋机器人编队仿真；设计了一个基于 Ekman 理论的分层水下洋流模型，以计算高效的方式捕捉风驱、深度依赖的洋流动力学。

Result: 验证了系统在保持严格实时执行和高视觉保真度的同时，可扩展到大规模异构海洋无人机群；洋流模型相比常用的随机 Gauss-Markov 模型，在海洋再分析数据验证中表现出显著更高的精度。

Conclusion: LOTUSim 可作为面向操作员在环的海洋机器人研究的有效仿真平台，兼具实时交互性、大规模可扩展性和物理保真度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LOTUSim，一个面向海洋机器人的多域仿真器，支持多用户交互、实时执行、物理保真的水下洋流模型，属于可交互物理世界建模方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "interactive_physical_world"（可交互与物理世界建模）。

Importance reason: Strong world model and simulation paper, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Multi-domain marine robotics simulator, Real-time human-in-the-loop interaction, Ekman-inspired layered current model, Scalable to large heterogeneous swarms, Open-source and validated against reanalysis data

Abstract: Simulation is essential for maritime robotics, supporting operator training, mission rehearsal, and human-vehicle interaction in environments where real-world testing is costly or hazardous. Existing simulators focus primarily on autonomy systems and often lack human-in-the-loop interaction and realistic environmental physics. This paper introduces LOTUSim, an open-source, real-time maritime simulator supporting multi-user interaction across aerial, surface, and underwater robotic systems for coordinated naval-style operations. The first contribution of this work is enabling real-time interactive performance for users while ensuring scalability to large fleets operating within a shared interactive simulation environment. Validation demonstrates robust human-in-the-loop performance, maintaining strict real-time execution and high visual fidelity while scaling to large heterogeneous maritime drone swarms. The second contribution is a computationally efficient, Ekman-inspired layered, underwater current model that captures wind-driven, depth-dependent flow dynamics with sufficient physical fidelity for large-scale simulations. Validation against ocean reanalysis data demonstrates substantially improved accuracy compared to commonly used stochastic Gauss-Markov current models. These results confirm LOTUSim's suitability as a simulation platform for operatorin-the-loop maritime robotics research.

</details>

### [56] [Deform360: A Massive Multi-view Visuotactile Dataset for Deformable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05390)
*Hongyu Li, Wanjia Fu, Xiaoyan Cong, Zekun Li, Binghao Huang, Hanxiao Jiang, Xintong He, Yiqing Liang, Rao Fu, Tao Lu, Srinath Sridhar, Kevin A. Smith, George Konidaris, Yunzhu Li*

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: Deform360 是一个大规模多视角触觉数据集，包含 198 个日常物体、1980 个交互序列和超过 215 小时的环绕相机与触觉观测数据，用于系统评测可变形物体的世界模型，并揭示了 2D 视频模型与 3D 粒子模型之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体的动力学预测（世界建模）是机器人操作中的基础挑战，但由于缺乏多样化、大规模的真实世界数据，当前 2D 像素空间和 3D 几何空间两种世界模型范式的相对优劣尚不明确。

Method: 构建 Deform360 数据集：使用 41 个环绕相机和双手触觉夹爪采集 198 个日常物体的交互数据；开发无标记的视觉-触觉 3D 跟踪管线提取稠密几何与运动信息；系统评估当前最先进的 2D 视频世界模型与 3D 粒子世界模型。

Result: 揭示了 2D 视频模型与 3D 粒子模型在可变形物体动力学预测中的结构性先验与可扩展性之间的权衡，并通过机器人规划任务初步验证了数据集的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: Deform360 为可泛化可变形物体中心的世界建模提供了坚实的基准，有助于推动该领域的系统性研究。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个大规模多视角触觉数据集 Deform360，用于可变形物体世界模型的系统评测与基准建立，属于世界模型方向下的数据集与评测工作，因此复用 world_model 方向下的现有 subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集）。

Importance reason: Provides a valuable benchmark for deformable world models, but its focus on robotics and tactile data is less aligned with your core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: World model benchmark, Deformable object dynamics, Visuotactile dataset, 2D vs 3D model comparison, Robotics manipulation focus

Abstract: Predicting object dynamics (i.e., world modeling) is a fundamental challenge for robotic manipulation, and modeling deformable objects presents a particularly difficult case due to their high-dimensional state spaces and complex material properties. While current world models approach this through two distinct paradigms: learning the dynamics over the 2D pixel space or more explicit 3D geometric space. A systematic understanding of their relative strengths and limitations remains elusive due to the lack of diverse, large-scale real-world data. To address this, we present Deform360, a large-scale visuotactile dataset featuring 198 daily-life objects, 1,980 interaction sequences, and over 215 hours of observations from 41 surround-view cameras and bimanual tactile grippers to capture both global motion and contact-induced local deformations. Leveraging a novel markerless visuotactile 3D tracking pipeline to extract dense geometry and motion, we systematically evaluate current state-of-the-art world models, comparing 2D video models against 3D particle models. Finally, we provide a preliminary demonstration indicating the real-world applicability of our dataset by performing robot planning tasks on deformable objects. Our analysis reveals key insights into the trade-offs between structural priors and scalability, providing a solid benchmark for future research in generalizable deformable object-centric world modeling. Project website:

### [57] [GigaWorld-1: A Roadmap to Build World Models for Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02642)
*GigaWorld Team, Angyuan Ma, Boyuan Wang, Bohan Li, Chaojun Ni, Guo Li, Guan Huang, Guosheng Zhao, Hao Li, Hengtao Li, Jingyu Liu, Jiwen Lu, Qiuping Deng, Tingdong Yu, Xuancheng Xu, Xinyu Zhou, Xiuwei Xu, Xinze Chen, Xiaofeng Wang, Xiaoyu Tian, Yang Wang, Yifan Chang, Yukun Zhou, Yun Ye, Zhenyu Wu, Zhanqian Wu, Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了用于机器人策略评估的世界模型，构建了WMBench基准（包含真实机器人遥操作数据和匹配的策略轨迹），分析了7个视频世界模型、4种动作表示方案和超过324,000次模拟策略评估。核心发现：评估器质量主要取决于长时序、动作一致的 rollout 一致性而非短期视觉真实感；预训练收益不仅来自数据规模，还来自通用世界知识与机器人特定可控性的平衡；架构选择（动作编码、记忆设计、评估器导向的后训练）对与真实机器人行为对齐有决定性影响。基于这些发现，提出了GigaWorld-1世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人基础模型的评估是一个关键瓶颈：与可通过数字基准高效评估的大语言模型不同，机器人策略需要缓慢、昂贵的真实世界部署，受限于硬件和人工监督。世界模型作为替代性策略评估器引起了广泛兴趣，但使世界模型可靠用于策略评估的关键属性仍不明确。

Method: 构建了WMBench基准，包含真实机器人遥操作数据和匹配的策略轨迹，覆盖多种操作任务。使用该基准系统分析了7个视频世界模型、4种动作表示方案、超过324,000次模拟策略评估（与真实机器人执行配对），并结合CVPR 2026 GigaBrain Challenge的大规模社区提交、精选合成轨迹和超过12,000小时的训练视频进行分析。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 评估器质量主要取决于长时序、动作一致的 rollout 一致性，而非短期视觉真实感；(2) 预训练收益不仅来自数据规模，还来自通用世界知识与机器人特定可控性的平衡；(3) 架构选择（动作编码、记忆设计、评估器导向的后训练）对与真实机器人行为对齐有决定性影响。基于这些发现实现了GigaWorld-1世界模型。

Conclusion: 本文系统揭示了使世界模型可靠用于机器人策略评估的关键属性，提出了设计路线图并实现了GigaWorld-1。完全开源了代码、模型、数据集和工具包，以推动具身基础模型的可扩展评估研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究用于机器人策略评估的世界模型，构建了WMBench基准并分析了7个视频世界模型，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model for robot policy evaluation, a core subtopic with high weight, and aligns with user's priority on video generation world models. Signals: world model, video world model, robot policy evaluation, long-horizon consistency, action-faithful rollout

Abstract: Evaluating embodied robot foundation models remains a critical bottleneck; unlike large language models efficiently assessed via digital benchmarks, robotic policies require slow, costly real-world rollouts limited by hardware and human supervision, which has driven interest in world models as surrogate policy evaluators, yet the key properties that make a world model reliable for policy assessment remain poorly understood. This work presents a systematic study of world models for robotic policy evaluation and introduces WMBench, a benchmark constructed from real-robot teleoperation data and matched policy rollouts covering diverse manipulation tasks to enable controlled comparisons across model families, action encodings, rollout horizons, and evaluation metrics. Using WMBench, we analyze 7 video world models, 4 action representation schemes, and over 324,000 simulated policy rollouts paired with real robot executions, further enriching our analysis with large-scale community submissions from the CVPR 2026 GigaBrain Challenge, curated synthetic trajectories, and a training videos spanning more than 12,000 hours. Our experiments deliver three core insights: evaluator quality is dominated by long-horizon, action-faithful rollout consistency rather than short-term visual realism; pretraining gains stem not only from data scale but from balancing general world knowledge with robot-specific controllability; and architectural choices including action encoding, memory design, and evaluator-focused post-training strongly determine alignment with real-world robot behavior. Drawing on these results, we derive a practical design roadmap and realize it in \textit{GigaWorld-1}, a world model specially optimized for policy evaluation, and we fully release our code, models, datasets, and toolkits to advance scalable evaluation research for embodied foundation models.

</details>


### [58] [Worldscape-MoE: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts World Model for Scalable Heterogeneous Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03964)
*Jianjie Fang, Yongyan Xu, Ziyou Wang, Chen Gao, Yuchao Huang, Zhaolu Wang, Rongze Tang, Mingyuan Jia, Baining Zhao, Weichen Zhang, Xin Zhang, Haisheng Su, Yu Shang, Wei Wu, Xinlei Chen, Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Worldscape-MoE 是一个基于 Diffusion Transformer 和混合专家架构的统一世界模型，通过模态感知的控制注入、共享与专用专家以及渐进式 MoE 微调策略，实现对异构动作信号（机器人操作、运动控制、手部控制等）的可扩展统一建模，实验表明异构监督能提升而非干扰各控制能力，并展现出良好的泛化与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成世界模型被组织在孤立的控制接口上（如相机轨迹、机器人动作、手部关节信号），缺乏统一且可扩展的学习框架来吸收异构动作监督，这成为扩展瓶颈。作者希望构建一个能统一多种异构动作控制信号、共享世界动力学建模的可扩展框架。

Method: 基于 Diffusion Transformer 构建 MoE 世界模型，核心设计包括：(1) 模态感知控制注入（modality-aware control injection），将不同动作模态编码后注入扩散过程；(2) 共享专家与控制专用专家（shared & control-specific experts）的混合架构，共享专家学习通用世界动力学，专用专家捕获各控制模态特有模式；(3) 渐进式 MoE 微调策略（progressive MoE tuning），支持持续扩展新动作模态而无需从头训练。

Result: 在 WorldArena 基准上取得强结果，在运动控制和手部控制指标上均有提升，展现出鲁棒的分布外泛化能力，并且随着更多控制数据和专家的加入表现出良好的扩展行为。

Conclusion: 异构动作监督不仅不会干扰各控制能力，反而能相互促进；Worldscape-MoE 为构建统一、可扩展的世界模型提供了有效范式，有望成为具身智能和交互式智能体的核心基础设施。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 Diffusion Transformer 和 MoE 的统一世界模型 Worldscape-MoE，支持异构动作控制（机器人操作、运动控制、手部控制等），属于视频生成式世界模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with a scalable MoE framework for heterogeneous action control, aligning with top-weighted world model and diffusion video generation priorities. Signals: world model, diffusion transformer, mixture-of-experts, heterogeneous action control, scalable video generation

Abstract: World models are rapidly becoming a core infrastructure for embodied intelligence and interactive agents: they provide controllable simulators in which agents can perceive, act, forecast, and acquire scalable experience. Yet current video generation world models are still organized around isolated control interfaces, such as camera trajectories, robot actions, or hand-joint signals. This fragmentation is increasingly a scaling bottleneck. The central challenge is not the absence of controllable generators, but the lack of a unified and extensible learning framework that can absorb heterogeneous action supervision while preserving a shared model of world dynamics. In this work, we introduce Worldscape-MoE, a Mixture-of-Experts world model built on Diffusion Transformers for scalable heterogeneous action control. Our key observation is that different controls specify different interfaces to the same underlying world: although their representations differ, they constrain shared physical regularities, scene dynamics, and interaction semantics. Worldscape-MoE operationalizes this observation through modality-aware control injection, shared and control-specific experts, and a progressive MoE tuning strategy that supports continual extension to new action modalities. Experiments across locomotion, robotic manipulation, and egocentric hand control show that heterogeneous supervision improves rather than interferes with individual control capabilities. Worldscape-MoE achieves strong results on WorldArena, improves locomotion and hand-control metrics, exhibits robust out-of-distribution generalization, and demonstrates scaling behavior as additional control data and experts are integrated.

</details>


### [59] [MoP-JEPA: Hard-Assigned Predictor Mixtures for Stochastic JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05238)
*Zhi Song, Ximing Xing, Zhenchao Tang, hanbo Huang, Tianxu Lv, minghao Yang, Zhongzheng Niu, He Bing, Lusheng Wang, Jianhua Yao*

TL;DR: MoP-JEPA 提出硬分配预测器混合（MoP）替代传统 JEPA 世界模型中的单确定性预测器，解决了随机环境中回归最优预测器输出条件均值（对应不存在状态）的结构失效问题。理论证明硬分配预测器收敛为转移分布的量化器（每头对应一个后继模式），在 OGBench 离线数据上规划成功率从 0.02-0.09 提升至 0.85，并设计了验证协议防止覆盖作弊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 世界模型使用单一确定性预测器通过潜回归训练来预测下一潜状态，但在随机环境中，当存在分支转移时，回归最优预测器会输出后继嵌入的条件均值——即位于真实下一状态之间的点，不对应任何实际状态，导致规划失败。作者旨在解决这一结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出 MoP-JEPA，用硬分配预测器混合（Mixture of Predictors with hard assignment）替代单确定性预测器。每个预测器头负责一个后继模式，通过硬路由分配，一次前向传播即可枚举所有可能后继。同时设计了验证协议：输入无关码本控制、打乱上下文测试、路由器门控读出、转移精度守卫、验证路由准则（模型盲提转移图，仅用真实数据验证结果），防止多预测评测中的覆盖作弊。

Result: 在 OGBench 离线数据上，单预测器 rollout 规划成功率仅 0.02-0.09，而 MoP-JEPA 预测模式规划成功率最高达 0.85，在所有任务上优于确定性、门控 MoE 和变分预测器。在验证路由准则下，MoP-JEPA 在所有三个迷宫中比最强软替代方法好 2-5 倍。在真实环境执行中，在最难迷宫中排名第二（共七个基线）。

Conclusion: 多模态动力学决定了 JEPA 世界模型能否进行规划；硬分配预测器混合是一种最小且可验证的修复方案。MoP-JEPA 有效解决了随机环境中 JEPA 世界模型的结构失效问题，为世界模型在随机环境中的规划提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MoP-JEPA，一种用于随机环境的 JEPA 世界模型，通过硬分配预测器混合来解决单确定性预测器在随机分支转换中的结构失效问题。论文聚焦于世界模型中的潜状态预测与规划，属于世界模型方向，具体匹配 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model planning in stochastic environments with a verifiable hard-assigned predictor mixture, highly aligned with your priority on video world models and continuous representations. Signals: world model, stochastic JEPA fix, hard-assigned predictor mixture, OGBench planning success 0.85, verification protocol

Abstract: JEPA world models predict the next latent state with a single deterministic predictor trained by latent regression. We show that this fails structurally when the environment is stochastic: at a branching transition, the regression-optimal predictor outputs the conditional mean of the successor embeddings, a point between the true next states that corresponds to no state at all. We prove this collapse for deterministic and gated mixture-of-experts predictors, and prove that MoP-JEPA's hard-assigned predictors converge instead to a quantizer of the transition distribution: one head per successor mode, enumerable in a single forward pass, which is the interface a planner consumes. On official OGBench offline data with leak-free evaluation, planning over single-predictor rollouts performs poorly ($0.02$--$0.09$ success) while planning over our predicted modes reaches up to $0.85$, ahead of deterministic, gated-MoE, and variational predictors on every task. Because multi-prediction evaluation invites coverage freeloading, a verification protocol is part of the method: an input-agnostic codebook control, a shuffled-context test, router-gated readouts, transition-precision guards, and a verified-route criterion in which the model proposes its transition graph blind and ground truth is used only to check the result. Under this criterion our method outperforms the strongest soft alternative on all three mazes ($2$--$5\times$), and the protocol identifies the remaining gap in that baseline's raw scores as routes through predicted transitions that do not exist. The same model executes in the real environment, placing second of seven against the published OGBench baselines on the hardest maze. Multimodal dynamics decide whether a JEPA world model can plan at all; a mixture of predictors with hard assignment is a minimal and verifiable fix.

</details>

TL;DR: DynaVieW 提出了一种动态 schema 引导的世界模型，通过层次化的状态-转换序列建模视频中的视觉动态，并采用混合专家架构联合学习转换预测与状态模拟，提升了视觉叙事生成与世界模拟的一致性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在系统性地建模视频或多图像序列中视觉场景的时间演化方面存在困难，难以预测或模拟多个层次的动态成分（如动作及其导致的环境变化）。现有方法缺乏对视觉动态的结构化理解。

Method: DynaVieW 学习交错的状态-转换序列（state-transition sequences），其中状态覆盖视频关键帧的广泛视觉场景，转换捕获层次化 schema 中的综合动态成分。采用混合专家（MoE）架构联合建模转换预测与状态模拟，并引入跨专家选择性注意力（cross-expert selective attention）和 schema token 重加权损失（schema token re-weighted loss）来确保有效和鲁棒的学习。

Result: DynaVieW 在视觉叙事生成和世界模拟任务上取得了改进的一致性、可控性和指令跟随能力，验证了其对视觉动态的深入理解能够提升下游性能。

Conclusion: DynaVieW 通过层次化 schema 引导的世界模型有效建模了视觉动态，为多模态 LLM 在视频理解和世界模拟方面提供了新的结构化范式，展示了在叙事生成和交互式模拟中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DynaVieW，一种基于动态 schema 引导的世界模型，用于理解视频/多图像序列中的层次化视觉动态，包括状态-转换预测与模拟。这属于世界模型方向下的视频世界模型（video_world_model）[CANONICAL] 子主题。同时，论文使用多模态 LLM 进行视觉动态理解与叙事生成，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向下的 vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal understanding with a novel schema-guided approach for hierarchical visual dynamics. Signals: world model, hierarchical visual dynamics, schema-guided, mixture-of-experts, state-transition sequences

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs struggle to systematically model the temporal evolution of visual scenes in videos or multi-image sequences. Such inputs require models to predict or simulate multiple levels of dynamic constituents, such as actions taken in the visual sequence, and the associated changes to the visual environment that result. To address this challenge, we propose a dynamic schema-guided world model, DynaVieW, optimized for visual dynamic prediction and simulation. DynaVieW achieves an in-depth understanding of visual dynamics by learning interleaved state-transition sequences, where states cover broad visual scenes from video keyframes, and transitions capture comprehensive dynamic constituents within a hierarchical schema. DynaVieW jointly models transition prediction and state simulation under a mixture-of-experts architecture, with a cross-expert selective attention and a schema token re-weighted loss, to ensure effective and robust learning. DynaVieW's understanding of visual dynamics boosts its downstream performance in visual narrative creation and world simulation, showing improved consistency, controllability, and instruction-following.

</details>

*Abu Anas Ibn Samad*

TL;DR: 本文提出PULS（Predictive Unified Latent Space），一个连续语义世界模型管道，包含KSD Bridge（4.9亿参数）将V-JEPA运动学表征映射到Qwen3-VL文本对齐的语义流形，以及ASP（1680万参数）进行未来状态预测。在UCF-Crime上达到0.8994的chunk-level AUROC，零样本迁移到XD-Violence达0.8162。验证了"潜空间清晰度假说"：JEPA预测的未来表征比当前观测表征在语义上更可分离。ASP在14-way零样本VQA上达44.5%准确率，比观测基线高9.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测方法主要依赖反应式的多实例学习（MIL），将时空特征坍缩为标量分数，缺乏对未来异常的预测能力。本文旨在构建一个能够预测未来异常的世界模型，利用物理运动学表征到语义空间的映射，实现视频异常的前瞻性检测。

Method: 提出PULS管道，包含两个模块：(1) KSD Bridge（Kinematic-to-Semantic Distillation），一个4.9亿参数的模型，将V-JEPA 2的物理运动学张量映射到Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B的2048维文本对齐超球面；(2) ASP（Anticipatory State Predictor），一个1680万参数的预测器，用于增强未来潜表征的语义可分离性。采用Triple-Track Lead-Time协议和L1-surprise门控来量化预测优势。

Result: KSD Bridge在UCF-Crime上达到0.8994的chunk-level AUROC，零样本迁移到XD-Violence达0.8162。ASP在14-way零样本VQA上达44.5%准确率（比观测基线+9.6pp）。在T-0.5s时刻达到+8.9pp的预测优势（p<0.001）。验证了"潜空间清晰度假说"：未来预测表征比当前观测表征在语义上更可分离。

Conclusion: 本文验证了利用物理运动学表征进行视频异常预测的有效性，证明了JEPA的时序预测器通过丢弃aleatoric像素噪声保留运动学信息，使得预测的未来表征比当前观测表征更语义可分离。零样本迁移实验表明牛顿不变性运动学表征具有跨分布泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PULS管道，利用V-JEPA物理表征进行视频异常预测（未来帧异常检测），属于视频世界模型中的未来预测与动作条件视频理解范畴，与[视频世界模型]（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous world-model and latent-space reasoning for video anomaly anticipation, aligning with top-priority continuous multimodal and world model subtopics. Signals: continuous latent space, world model, video anomaly anticipation, zero-shot transfer, JEPA kinematics to semantic mapping

Abstract: Continuous video anomaly detection is dominated by reactive Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) that collapses spatiotemporal features into scalar scores. We introduce PULS (Predictive Unified Latent Space), a continuous semantic world-model pipeline comprising two modules: a 490M-parameter KSD Bridge (Kinematic-to-Semantic Distillation) and a 16.8M-parameter Anticipatory State Predictor (ASP). The KSD Bridge maps V-JEPA 2 physical tensors into the 2048-d Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B text-aligned hypersphere, trained on a subset of UCF-Crime. This translation alone yields a chunk-level AUROC of 0.8994 for UCF-Crime and 0.8162 for out-of-distribution XD-Violence without MIL or hierarchical fusion. We introduce and validate the Latent Clarity Hypothesis: because JEPA's temporal predictor discards aleatoric pixel noise while preserving kinematics, anticipated future representations are more semantically separable than observed presents. The ASP sharpens these anticipated future latents, achieving 44.5% mean 14-way zero-shot VQA accuracy (exceeding observation baseline by +9.6 pp). Applying the ASP to Observation Tensors collapses accuracy to 7.3% (random chance), proving Anticipation and Observation occupy distinct sub-manifolds. A Triple-Track Lead-Time protocol with an L1-surprise gate yields a peak +8.9 pp anticipatory advantage at T-0.5s (p < 0.001, N = 1,000 permutation), separating physical anticipation from static scene priors. Zero-shot transfer to XD-Violence confirms that Newtonian-invariant kinematic representations generalize out-of-distribution.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出粗到细两阶段框架 DreamNav：1）粗估计阶段采用三角参数化回归策略，联合建模正弦和余弦分量来预测旋转，缓解角度周期性问题；2）扩散精化阶段利用预训练世界模型模拟候选动作的未来视觉观测，选择与目标视觉差异最小的轨迹。

Result: 在 PairUAV 基准（480万图像对，72场景）上，DreamNav 在精度和泛化性上优于强视觉伺服和基础模型基线，并实现了对未见场景的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 扩散精化结合世界模型的视觉想象能有效提升无人机最后米级导航的精度和泛化能力，为视觉伺服提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DreamNav，利用预训练的世界模型模拟未来视觉观测以选择最优轨迹，属于动作条件视频预测与视觉想象驱动的导航，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: While the paper uses a world model for visual servoing, its focus on UAV navigation is a niche application with limited direct relevance to your core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: world model for trajectory selection, diffusion refinement for visual servoing, coarse-to-fine navigation, large-scale UAV dataset, zero-shot transfer to unseen scenes

Abstract: In this work, we study the last-meter precision navigation for UAVs, e.g., autonomously reaching a target within the final 10 meters using monocular vision. This task is challenging due to scale ambiguity, rotation discontinuities, and the need for fine-grained spatial reasoning. Existing methods often fail under large viewpoint changes or lack generalization to unseen environments. To this end, we propose DreamNav, a coarse-to-fine diffusion-refined aerial visual servoing framework. In the first coarse-estimation stage, a robust regression policy employs a trigonometric parameterization to predict rotation by jointly modeling sine and cosine components, effectively mitigating optimization instabilities caused by angular periodicity. Given this coarse estimate, the second diffusion-refined stage utilizes a pre-trained world model to simulate future visual observations for candidate actions, selecting the trajectory that minimizes visual discrepancy with the target through a process of visual imagination. To support rigorous evaluation, we contribute PairUAV, a large-scale benchmark comprising 4.8 million image pairs across 72 scenes, curated from the University-1652 dataset. Extensive experiments show DreamNav outperforms strong visual servoing and foundation model baselines in accuracy and generalization, with zero-shot transfer to unseen scenes.

</details>

*Santosh Jaiswal*
