TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验研究JEPA自监督潜世界模型在驾驶场景中的跨域泛化能力，发现地理多样性比数据量更能提升模型对未见城市和传感器配置的零样本泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督潜世界模型可以无需标注为驾驶场景分配"惊喜度"分数，但这类模型能否将复杂度概念泛化到未见过的地理区域和传感器配置尚不清楚。本文旨在探究地理多样性与数据量对跨域泛化的相对重要性。

Method: 在nuPlan数据（匹兹堡、波士顿、新加坡）上训练基于JEPA的世界模型，并在Argoverse 2验证集（迈阿密、奥斯汀）上进行零样本迁移评估。通过匹配规模消融实验（每组63,000场景，3个随机种子）比较地理多样性训练与单地域训练的效果，并进一步对比200,000场景的单地域训练。

Result: 地理多样性训练的平均惊喜度分数比单地域训练低16.5%（0.228 vs 0.273）。即使使用3倍数据量的单地域训练（200,000场景），其惊喜度（0.264）仍高于仅63,000场景的地理多样性训练，表明地理多样性是比数据量更强的跨域泛化预测因子。

Conclusion: 地理多样性比原始数据量更能提升JEPA驾驶世界模型的跨域泛化能力，为自动驾驶场景中自监督世界模型的数据收集策略提供了重要指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于JEPA的自监督潜世界模型在驾驶场景中的跨域泛化能力，属于视频世界模型范畴，与[CANONICAL] video_world_model（视频世界模型）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model generalization for driving, a priority subtopic, with a strong diversity-over-volume finding. Signals: world model, driving, cross-domain generalization, geographic diversity, JEPA

Abstract: Self-supervised latent world models can assign a surprise score to driving scenarios without any human labels. A natural follow-up question is whether such a model, trained on driving data from one geographic region, can generalize its notion of complexity to unseen cities and sensor configurations. We study this question through a controlled transfer experiment: we train JEPA-based world models on nuPlan data (Pittsburgh, Boston, Singapore) and evaluate zero-shot on held-out Argoverse 2 validation scenarios from Miami and Austin. We find that models trained on geographically diverse data generalize significantly better than models trained on equal amounts of single-geography data. In a matched-scale ablation at 63,000 scenarios per condition (n=3 seeds each), combined training reduces mean surprise score by 16.5% relative to nuPlan-only training (0.228 +/- 0.015 vs 0.273 +/- 0.008). Notably, training on 200,000 AV2-only scenarios (3x more data from one geography) still produces higher surprise (0.264) than the combined 63K model, suggesting that geographic diversity is a stronger predictor of cross-domain generalization than raw data volume.

</details>

*Mengmeng Liu, Diankun Zhang, Jiuming Liu, Jianfeng Cui, Hongwei Xie, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Francesco Nex, Hao Cheng, Michael Ying Yang*

TL;DR: UNIVERSE 提出一种统一的视频-动作模型，基于单个掩码调制的 Diffusion Transformer，通过联合训练未来视频潜变量和自车轨迹 token，使密集视频监督直接指导轨迹去噪，实现更强的跨域动作泛化。引入模态解耦可见性掩码，在共享历史上下文的同时阻止未来视频与轨迹 token 之间的相互注意力，防止未来目标泄露，并支持仅轨迹推理（速度提升 4.3 倍）。在 NAVSIM 上达到 91.0 PDMS，零样本迁移到 nuScenes 和 Bench2Drive。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）采用级联或双 DiT 设计，将视频想象与动作预测分离，导致视频学习到的世界动态知识无法有效传递到轨迹分支，动作模型仍可能过拟合数据集特定的驾驶先验。作者希望设计一个统一架构，让密集视频监督直接塑造轨迹去噪过程，从而提升跨域动作泛化能力。

Method: UNIVERSE 基于单个掩码调制的 Diffusion Transformer（DiT），将未来视频潜变量和自车轨迹 token 在共享生成参数内联合训练。提出 Modality-Decoupling Visibility Mask（模态解耦可见性掩码），在共享历史上下文的同时，阻止未来视频 token 与轨迹 token 之间的相互注意力，防止未来目标泄露。该设计支持三种推理模式：仅视频、仅轨迹（移除未来视频去噪，速度提升 4.3×）、以及联合视频-轨迹 rollout。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上达到 91.0 PDMS（优于双 DiT 变体的 89.6），无需微调即可零样本迁移到 nuScenes 和 Bench2Drive。消融实验验证了单 DiT 统一架构、视频联合训练和基于掩码的模态解耦的重要性。

Conclusion: UNIVERSE 通过统一的单 DiT 架构和模态解耦掩码设计，有效弥合了视频世界模型与轨迹生成之间的鸿沟，在自动驾驶动作泛化上取得了显著提升，同时支持高效的仅轨迹推理部署。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video generation for autonomous driving, aligning with top-priority world_model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model, video diffusion transformer, mask-modulated generation, autonomous driving, zero-shot transfer

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have shown strong potential for improving action generalization in autonomous driving by using future video prediction as dense supervision for scene dynamics and temporal causality. However, it remains unclear which architecture better transfers video-modeling benefits to trajectory generation. Existing cascaded or dual-DiT designs separate video imagination from action prediction, weakening the transfer of video-learned world dynamics to the trajectory branch: the action model may still overfit dataset-specific driving priors, while the video model only indirectly regularizes planning. We propose UNIVERSE, a unified video-action model built upon a single mask-modulated Diffusion Transformer. By co-training future video latents and ego-trajectory tokens within shared generative parameters, UNIVERSE allows dense video supervision to directly shape trajectory denoising, leading to stronger cross-domain action generalization. To ensure causal validity and efficient deployment, we introduce a Modality-Decoupling Visibility Mask, which shares historical context across modalities while blocking mutual attention between future video and trajectory tokens. This prevents future-target leakage and enables trajectory-only inference by removing future-video denoising at test time, achieving a $4.3\times$ speedup over joint video-action rollout while maintaining comparable planning accuracy. The same model also supports video-only and joint video-action rollouts. Experiments show that UNIVERSE achieves 91.0 PDMS on NAVSIM (vs. 89.6 for the Two-DiT variant), and demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer to nuScenes and Bench2Drive without fine-tuning, while ablations confirm the importance of single-DiT unification, video co-training, and mask-based modality decoupling.

</details>

Method: 使用潜扩散模型（5B参数），以多智能体动作流为条件，在潜空间中建模四玩家Rocket League比赛视频。采用视频编解码器压缩观测，扩散模型学习潜空间中的未来帧预测，并设计多人条件方案使模型能正确归因每个玩家的动作对场景变化的影响。训练数据为10,000小时公开机器人对局数据。

Conclusion: 本文成功展示了首个可实时运行的多人交互世界模型，证明了潜扩散模型在多智能体动态场景中的有效性。模型在远超训练视界的 rollout 中保持稳定，为未来多人世界模型研究提供了数据集、代码库和实时演示支持。

Importance reason: Pioneers multiplayer interactive world models with representation autoencoders, directly advancing the user's top-priority continuous multimodal and world model research. Signals: world model, representation autoencoder, continuous latent diffusion, video generation, interactive world model

Abstract: We introduce the first multiplayer world model for highly dynamic environments governed by complex physical interactions. Whereas single-player world models treat the other agents as part of the environment, ours conditions on the action streams of multiple agents, learning to attribute changes in the scene to the correct player and to stay coherent under arbitrary combinations of their actions. We study this problem in the game of Rocket League, where players compete and cooperate under fast, tightly coupled dynamics. Trained on 10,000 hours of gameplay collected with publicly available bots, our 5-billion-parameter latent diffusion model generates four-player matches in real time, producing 20 frames per second on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Although trained only on short clips, its rollouts stay stable far beyond the training horizon: distributional quality holds steady out to five minutes, the longest horizon we measure, and in practice we observe rollouts continuing for hours with no sign of collapse. We systematically investigate the central design choices: the video codec, the generative objective, and the multiplayer conditioning scheme. In addition, we characterize how behavior changes with model and data scale, including the capabilities that emerge and the failure modes that persist. We further develop targeted evaluations that probe the model's physical understanding rather than visual appearance alone. To support continued research on multiplayer world models, we release our dataset, our full training and inference codebase, and a live demo.

</details>

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D point cloud completion, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong overlap with user's key interests in continuous language/video/image generation. Signals: 3D completion, single-view guidance, modality alignment, geometry enhancement, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: View-based point cloud completion aims to recover a complete 3D shape from a partial point cloud, guided by a single-view image. However, existing approaches often suffer from limited performance due to weak modality alignment and limited self-geometry enhancement. To overcome these challenges, we propose a unified geometry-aware framework that integrates efficient modality alignment and adaptive geometry enhancement, mainly to address cross-modal geometric inconsistency of view-guided point cloud completion. Specifically, we propose a geometry-aware modality alignment by integrating a shared self-attention Transformer and cross-modality reconstruction supervision, which aims to bring features of the image and point cloud close to each other in a shared latent space describing the 3D object. To enhance the perception of global shape and local geometric details, we propose an adaptive geometry-aware self-attention module, which simultaneously considers local geometry-aware attention computation and the spatially-variant feature fusion. In addition, we apply a geometry-perceptive anchor refinement module to reorganize the anchor points (representing a local region of the shape) under appropriate supervision, further boosting the completion performance of our method. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over existing approaches. Our code will be available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D多孔介质重建在地质与石油工程中至关重要，但获取真实3D训练数据（如微CT扫描）成本高昂且耗时。现有方法往往需要3D数据或缺乏对岩石物理性质（如孔隙度）的控制。本文旨在仅利用易获取的2D薄片图像生成具有可控孔隙度的3D体积，降低数据获取门槛。

Method: 提出条件GAN框架，包含3D生成器（3D卷积）和2D判别器（2D卷积）。生成器输出3D体积后，沿三个正交轴提取2D切片送入判别器，使网络从2D训练数据学习3D一致结构。孔隙度作为条件输入生成器，通过增强U-Net从2D图像分割提取孔隙度标签。在两种碳酸盐岩样本（白云石-硬石膏和纯白云石）上验证。

Result: 框架成功生成了逼真的3D体积，捕捉了硬石膏包裹体和细晶纹理等岩相特征。孔隙度控制R²达0.93，异质样本平均绝对误差0.019，均质样本0.010。

Conclusion: 本文证明仅用2D薄片图像即可训练生成具有可控孔隙度的3D多孔介质体积，为缺乏3D数据的岩石物理建模提供了可行方案。混合2D/3D架构有效从2D数据学习3D结构，孔隙度条件控制精度高。

Abstract: This study presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) framework for generating 3D porous media volumes with controlled porosity, trained exclusively on 2D thin section images. The key innovation lies in combining property-conditioned generation with 2D-to-3D reconstruction, eliminating the need for expensive 3D training data while maintaining control over petrophysical properties. The framework employs a hybrid architecture with a 3D generator and 2D discriminator, where multi-axis slice extraction enables learning 3D-consistent structures from 2D training data. Porosity labels are extracted using an Enhanced U-Net segmentation model. The methodology was demonstrated on two carbonate samples with different lithologies: dolomite-anhydrite and pure dolomite. Results show that the framework successfully generates realistic 3D volumes capturing lithological features such as anhydrite inclusions and fine crystalline textures. Porosity control achieved an $R^2$ of 0.93, with mean absolute errors of 0.019 and 0.010 for the heterogeneous and homogeneous samples, respectively.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在室内（ScanNet）和室外（KITTI）基准上，VLRC 一致提升了 3D 重建精度和相机估计性能，同时实现了更连贯的多视图语义融合，在零样本开放词汇 3D 语义分割任务上取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: VLRC 提供了一种可扩展的、无需额外 3D 标注的语义监督信号，能够有效改进前馈 3D 重建模型的训练，并赋予模型更好的开放词汇 3D 场景理解能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models are commonly trained using either expensive geometric supervision or self-supervised photometric objectives, both of which provide incomplete learning signals. We introduce Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency (VLRC), a scalable auxiliary objective that exploits frozen vision-language representations as semantic multi-view supervision. Given a predicted 3D reconstruction, VLRC reprojects dense vision-language features across views and enforces feature consistency between corresponding image locations, requiring no additional 3D annotations. The objective integrates seamlessly with both self-supervised monocular reconstruction and supervised-pretrained feed-forward 3D models during unlabeled adaptation. By aligning geometry with language-grounded features, VLRC not only improves depth and camera estimation but also enables more coherent multi-view semantic fusion for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding. Experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in 3D reconstruction accuracy and zero-shot open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [69] [Provable Pruning for Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting via Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02721)
*Waseem Mousa, Alaa Maalouf*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 coreset（核心集）理论的 3DGS 可证明剪枝方法。作者首先证明在无限制设置下不存在非平凡的 3DGS 乘法近似核心集，然后证明在给定渲染分辨率下可以构造加权核心集。该方法通过敏感性（sensitivity）计算每个高斯的重要性分数进行采样，在无微调或极短微调场景下达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在实际场景中通常包含数百万个高斯体，需要压缩才能在有限硬件上部署。现有剪枝方法大多基于启发式策略，无法提供渲染目标的乘法近似保证，且严重依赖昂贵的后剪枝微调来恢复质量。本文旨在探索能否用更小的加权子集（核心集）可证明地替代原始 3DGS 场景。

Method: 首先理论分析表明无限制设置下不存在非平凡乘法 3DGS 核心集，但给定渲染分辨率时存在。提出基于敏感性（sensitivity）的加权核心集构造方法，计算每个高斯体在全场景目标中的重要性分数，据此进行重要性采样。在显式有效性和对数透射率稳定性假设下，将目标保证转化为渲染保证。

Result: 在无微调（prune-only）和极短微调场景下达到 SOTA 性能，证明基于理论的重要性估计既具有理论意义又具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文首次为 3DGS 提供了可证明的加权核心集构造定理，表明在给定分辨率下可以实现乘法近似保证。该方法在激进压缩且无/极少恢复计算的情况下表现最佳，为 3DGS 的高效部署提供了理论支撑的实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的剪枝压缩方法，属于 3D 场景表示与渲染的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical contribution for 3DGS compression but low alignment with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3DGS coreset theory, provable pruning guarantee, resolution-dependent approximation, sensitivity-based sampling, no finetuning needed

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality real-time novel-view synthesis, but practical scenes often contain millions of Gaussians, making compression essential for deployment on limited hardware. Existing reduction methods are effective but mostly heuristic: they provide no multiplicative approximation guarantee for the rendered objective, and thus rely heavily on costly post-pruning finetuning to recover quality. We ask a basic question: can a 3DGS scene be provably replaced by a much smaller weighted subset (coreset) while preserving the objective of interest? We first show that, in the unrestricted setting, no non-trivial multiplicative 3DGS coreset exists. We then show that multiplicative guarantees are not impossible, but resolution-dependent. For a prescribed rendering resolution, such as representative views or grids of views/rays, we provide the first weighted coreset construction theorem for 3DGS. The construction samples Gaussians by sensitivity: provable importance scores measuring each Gaussian's role in the full-scene objective. Finally, under explicit validity and log-transmittance stability assumptions, we turn this objective guarantee into a rendering guarantee. Empirically, our method is strongest where deployment needs it most: aggressive compression with no or minimal recovery compute. In prune-only and very short finetuning regimes, it achieves state-of-the-art performance, showing that principled importance estimation can be both theoretically meaningful and practically useful. Open-source code is available at .

</details>

*Kun-Yu Lin, Chengke Bu, Zhenguo Li, Kai Han*

TL;DR: 本文提出全息字幕（holo-captioning）任务，旨在为3D场景生成结构化文本描述，涵盖所有实体的语义标签、空间位置、属性和关系。作者构建了包含15K+场景的大规模基准，并提出HoloScribe模型，采用实例感知解耦流水线和锚点感知实例链接机制，显著优于现有3D密集字幕生成器和3D LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解方法（如3D密集字幕生成、3D VQA）缺乏对场景中所有实体及其空间关系和属性的全面结构化描述，无法实现3D场景的"文本等价物"。本文旨在填补这一空白，为3D场景提供完整、结构化的文本表征。

Method: 提出HoloScribe模型：(1) 实例感知解耦流水线，分别生成每个实体实例的详细描述；(2) 锚点感知实例链接机制，识别具有空间关系的实例对并生成关系描述；(3) 构建大规模基准（15K+场景）用于训练和评估；(4) 提出HoloScore综合评估指标和人工标注测试集。

Result: 实验结果表明，HoloScribe在多个指标上显著优于现有最先进的3D密集字幕生成器和3D LLM通用模型，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次定义了全息字幕任务，并证明了通过实例感知解耦和锚点感知实例链接可以生成高质量的3D场景结构化文本描述，为3D场景理解与文本生成建立了新的基准和方向。

Importance reason: Novel 3D captioning task but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D scene understanding, structured text generation, new task formulation, benchmark contribution, no autoregressive or continuous token focus

Abstract: This work introduces holo-captioning, a novel task that strives to seek the text equivalent of 3D scenes. As the initial step, we formulate holo-captioning as generating a structured textual description that comprehensively depicts all entities within a 3D scene -- including their semantic tags, spatial locations, attributes, and inter-entity relations. To tackle this challenging task, we first develop an effective captioning engine to produce detailed descriptions of individual entity instances and instance pairs, and contribute a large-scale benchmark comprising over 15K scenes for training and evaluation. Building upon this foundation, we propose HoloScribe, a novel model that features an instance-aware decoupled pipeline for generating structured holo-captions, and further incorporates anchor-aware instance linking to identify relational instance pairs. Additionally, we propose a comprehensive evaluation metric named HoloScore, and provide a human-curated test set to ensure reliable model assessment. Experimental results demonstrate that HoloScribe significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 3D dense captioners and 3D LLM generalists, underscoring the effectiveness of our approach. Project page:

</details>


### [71] [TemporalGS: Training-Free Plug-and-Play Acceleration for 3D Gaussian Splatting Rendering via Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03390)
*Yuhongze Zhou, Zihao Yang, Xinxin Zuo, Juwei Lu*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (59.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TemporalGS 是首个无需训练、即插即用的 3DGS 渲染加速算法，利用时序先验（几何与外观缓冲）通过时序动态剔除和选择性渲染两种策略，减少连续帧间高斯预处理、排序和光栅化的冗余操作，实现高达 1.48× 加速且保持渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在新视角合成中取得了快速且高质量的渲染效果，但在高 FPS 和低延迟场景下，尤其是大量高斯椭球体出现时，渲染效率仍面临挑战。现有加速方法多依赖后训练或后处理，缺乏无需额外训练的即插即用方案。

Method: 提出 TemporalGS，基于 tile-based 软件光栅化，利用时序先验（几何与外观缓冲）设计两种加速策略：(1) 时序动态剔除——过滤掉对当前帧渲染贡献较小的高斯体；(2) 选择性渲染——仅渲染无法由时序先验近似的少量 tile。两种策略自适应交替执行，实现无需训练的即插即用加速。

Result: 在多个场景上，TemporalGS 达到最高 1.48× 渲染加速，与现有基于后训练/后处理的 SOTA 方法相比，取得了相当甚至更优的性能，同时保持了有竞争力的渲染质量。该方法还可扩展到硬件光栅化 3DGS 上。

Conclusion: TemporalGS 证明了利用时序先验可以有效加速 3DGS 渲染，且无需任何训练或后处理，是一种简单有效的即插即用方案，具有良好的可移植性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 TemporalGS，一种无需训练的即插即用 3DGS 渲染加速方法，属于 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）的渲染优化，因此归入 [CANONICAL] 的 "3D 生成与重建" 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS rendering acceleration, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3DGS acceleration, training-free, temporal priors, rendering speed-up, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized novel-view synthesis with its fast and high-fidelity rendering. However, rendering at high FPS and low latency across various scenes remains a challenge, especially when large amounts of 3D Gaussian ellipsoids appear in the scene. To address this issue, we introduce TemporalGS, to the best of our knowledge, the first training-free plug-and-play algorithmic approach to accelerate 3DGS rendering without any post-training or post-processing, implemented on top of tile-based software rasterization. The key idea is that, instead of rendering frames independently as 3DGS, we leverage the temporal priors, represented by novel geometry and appearance buffers, etc., to reduce redundancy of Gaussian preprocessing, sorting, and rasterization operations of consecutive frames. Specifically, we propose two acceleration strategies: (1) temporal dynamic culling, which filters out Gaussians that contribute less to current frame rendering; (2) selective rendering, which renders only a small portion of tiles that cannot be approximated by the temporal priors. By adapting and interleaving these two strategies, TemporalGS yields a simple but effective plug-and-play solution for 3DGS rendering speed-up without any training. Extensive experiments show that TemporalGS achieves comparable or even better performance compared to existing state-of-the-art post-training or post-processing-based 3DGS rendering acceleration approaches. TemporalGS can significantly enhance the rendering speed of various 3DGS methods, achieving up to $1.48\times$ acceleration, while maintaining competitive rendering quality. We further extend our TemporalGS to hardware rasterization-based 3DGS to show the portability of our algorithm.

</details>


### [72] [SAF3R: Dynamic Sparse Attention for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03612)
*Jianing Deng, Yuanzhe Li, Jialu Wang, Song Wang, Tianlong Chen, Huanrui Yang, Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

TL;DR: SAF3R 提出了一种无需训练的动态稀疏注意力框架，专门针对前馈式3D重建Transformer（F3R）设计。通过分析发现F3R中全局注意力模式具有高度异质性、动态性和稀疏性，SAF3R 结合离线头部特征分析与在线自适应策略，在保持重建质量的同时大幅加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D重建Transformer在处理长图像序列时，跨视图全局注意力的二次复杂度成为计算瓶颈。现有压缩或稀疏注意力方法未能充分利用全局注意力中固有的稀疏性和动态行为，因此需要更高效的注意力机制。

Method: 首先对多个F3R Transformer的全局注意力模式进行全面分析，揭示其高度异质、动态且极度稀疏的特性。基于此发现，提出SAF3R框架：集成定制化的稀疏注意力机制，结合离线头部特征分析（head profiling）和高效的在线自适应策略，以匹配输入依赖的注意力行为。

Result: SAF3R在保持相机位姿估计和3D重建质量的前提下实现了高稀疏率，相比现有方法在F3R Transformer上取得了显著的端到端加速效果。

Conclusion: 通过利用F3R Transformer中全局注意力的固有稀疏性和动态特性，SAF3R无需训练即可高效加速3D重建，为可扩展的3D重建模型提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注前馈式3D重建（Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction）中的稀疏注意力机制优化，属于3D场景/物体重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D reconstruction efficiency, which is not a top priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D reconstruction, dynamic sparse attention, feed-forward transformer, inference acceleration, training-free

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction (F3R) transformers have recently achieved remarkable success. However, scaling them to long image sequences remains challenging, as the quadratic complexity of cross-view global attention quickly becomes the dominant computational bottleneck. While recent efforts attempt to improve efficiency through compressed or sparse attention, they fail to fully exploit the inherent sparsity and dynamic behavior of global attention. In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of global attention across multiple F3R transformers and reveal that attention patterns are highly heterogeneous, dynamic, and extremely sparse across layers and attention heads. Motivated by these findings, we propose SAF3R, a training-free dynamic sparse attention framework tailored to F3R transformers. SAF3R integrates tailored sparse attention mechanisms with offline head profiling and an efficient online adaptation strategy to match input-dependent attention behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAF3R achieves high sparsity ratios while preserving camera pose estimation and 3D reconstruction quality, translating into substantial end-to-end speedup on F3R transformers compared to existing methods. Code is available at

</details>

*Liang Han, Bangcai Wei, Junsheng Zhou, Yu-Shen Liu, Zhizhong Han*

Result: 在DTU和Tanks-and-Temples数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在稀疏视角表面重建任务上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 法线引导的深度传播与异常深度边缘感知正则化能有效提升3DGS在稀疏视角下的几何表面重建质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的稀疏视角表面重建，属于3D重建与生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: While solid for 3D reconstruction, it lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3DGS surface reconstruction, sparse-view, depth propagation, normal priors, no priority subtopic match

Abstract: 3D reconstruction from sparse views is a challenging task in 3D computer vision. Recent studies on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable results with sparse views in novel view synthesis, yet reconstructing high-quality geometric surfaces from sparse views remains a challenge, due to the limited geometry clues and the discreteness of Gaussians. In this paper, we propose a novel 3DGS-based method for high-fidelity surface reconstruction from sparse views. Our key insight is to introduce a normal-guided depth propagation approach, which can extend depth information from high-confidence regions to constrain the depth in low-confidence areas. Additionally, we propose an abnormal depth edge-aware regularization to address depth discontinuities caused by the discreteness of Gaussians. Extensive experiments on DTU and Tanks-and-Temples datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in sparse view surface reconstruction. Project page: .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用 SAM 3 生成精确的建筑掩码，从中提取建筑学上显著的边缘；在训练过程中，将渲染图像梯度与提取的边缘进行对齐，迫使高斯体收敛到锐利的结构几何形状。

Conclusion: SharpSplat 通过语义边缘正则化有效提升了 3DGS 在建筑重建中的边缘锐利度，为大规模数字孪生中的高保真建筑建模提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS edge regularization for urban building reconstruction, which is not aligned with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, UAV building reconstruction, edge regularization, SAM, not autoregressive

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity 3D building models from UAV imagery is essential for large-scale digital twin development. However, existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) techniques often struggle with building facades, failing to capture sharp geometric transitions. To address this, we propose a semantic edge regularization framework that supervises 3DGS to produce crisp architectural boundaries. Our method leverages SAM 3 to generate precise building masks, from which we extract architecturally significant edges. During training, we align rendered image gradients with these extracted edges, forcing the Gaussians to converge into sharp structural geometries. Evaluations across campus environments, dense urban centers, and custom residential datasets demonstrate significant improvements in edge fidelity without requiring architectural modifications to the 3DGS pipeline. Our approach proves robust across diverse building types, roof geometries, and urban densities.

</details>

TL;DR: InSpace 是一个结构感知的3D室内场景生成框架，从单张360°全景图像出发，通过三阶段流程（部分几何估计、粗场景结构生成、细粒度布局与资产纹理生成）重建完整的3D室内场景，并提出了配套的 ERP-FRONT 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图到3D生成方法主要关注物体级资产生成，忽略了室内场景的结构布局。而单张有限视场的图像无法覆盖全局布局信息。作者利用360°全景图像提供更完整的空间覆盖，旨在生成具有结构布局的完整3D室内场景。

Method: InSpace 包含三个阶段：(1) 部分场景几何估计——从ERP图像估计深度和法线作为空间先验；(2) 粗场景结构生成——使用视图选择性交叉注意力生成粗略场景结构；(3) 细粒度布局与资产纹理生成——通过全局-局部混合注意力机制，使用 flow matching 生成详细的布局、资产几何和纹理。同时构建了 ERP-FRONT 数据集（基于3D-FRONT的配对ERP图像到3D室内场景数据集）。

Result: 实验表明 InSpace 能从单张ERP图像生成完整的3D室内场景，包含结构布局和分离的带纹理资产，在3D和2D指标上均取得了强性能。

Conclusion: InSpace 证明了利用360°全景图像进行结构感知的3D室内场景生成是有效的，能够同时恢复场景布局和资产细节，为室内场景生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从单张360°图像生成完整3D室内场景，包含结构布局和带纹理的资产，属于3D场景生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D scene generation from 360° images, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D scene generation, 360° image input, structure-aware framework, flow matching, ERP-FRONT dataset

Abstract: Recent advances in single image-to-3D generation have enabled high-quality asset synthesis, yet extending these capabilities to indoor scene generation remains challenging. Existing methods focus on asset-level generation while neglecting the structural layout, which is essential for downstream applications and serves as the spatial anchor for grounding assets. However, a single image with a limited field of view lacks the spatial coverage to recover a coherent global layout. To this end, we use a 360° image represented in equirectangular projection (ERP) and propose InSpace, a structure-aware framework for 3D indoor scene generation. InSpace comprises three stages: (1) estimating partial scene geometry as spatial priors, (2) generating coarse scene structure with view-selective cross-attention, and (3) producing detailed layout and asset geometry with textures through a global-local hybrid attention, using flow matching. We also propose ERP-FRONT, a paired ERP-Image-to-3D indoor scene dataset based on 3D-FRONT. Experiments show that InSpace generates complete 3D indoor scenes with structural layout, along with separate textured assets from a single ERP image, achieving strong performance across 3D and 2D metrics. Project Page:

</details>

Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建方法预测像素对齐的高斯原语，导致大量冗余表示。简单剪枝会引入严重伪影且需要推理时微调，破坏了前馈范式的优势。作者希望在不破坏前馈范式的前提下，显式控制高斯原语数量，实现高效重建。

Result: 在RE10K、ACID、DL3DV、Tanks and Temples和DTU等多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性，消融实验证明了各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需推理时微调、保持前馈范式的自适应高斯分配方法，通过纹理信息指导高斯原语的分配，在保持重建质量的同时显著减少冗余。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D Gaussian pruning for feed-forward reconstruction, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D reconstruction, Gaussian splatting, texture-aware pruning, feed-forward, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Current feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods predict pixel aligned Gaussian primitives, resulting in highly redundant representations. A natural solution is to prune the redundant Gaussians, but naive pruning introduces severe artifacts and often requires inference time fine-tuning, breaking the feed-forward paradigm. Based on previous works, high frequency regions require more Gaussian primitives, while low frequency regions can be represented with significantly fewer primitives. Motivated by this, we propose a novel approach to explicitly control the number of Gaussians by leveraging local texture information. Our approach achieves this through three key components: (1) texture estimation to capture spatial variation in scene detail, (2) texture-aware pruning that removes redundant Gaussians from low frequency regions, and (3) an adaptive Gaussian head that predicts the modified attributes of the retained primitives without breaking the feed-forward paradigm. Experiments on RE10K, ACID, DL3DV, Tanks and Temples, and DTU demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, while ablation studies validate the contributions of its key components.

</details>


### [77] [Targeted Structure Completion for Sparse-View 3D Reconstruction in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04661)
*Guoqing Wang, Pin Tang, Xiangxuan Ren, Liping Hou, Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FocusGS 提出了一种面向自动驾驶稀疏视角3D重建的目标结构补全框架，通过3D几何歧义流形定位高不确定性区域，并仅在这些区域进行轻量级高斯查询补全，在保持重建质量的同时减少约74%的高斯数量并降低34%的渲染时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于体素的高斯方法在稀疏视角3D重建中虽然结构完整度高，但存在大量计算冗余（均匀处理整个体积）；而基于像素的高斯方法效率高但结构完整性不足。本文旨在弥合两者差距，实现效率与质量的更好权衡。

Method: 提出 FocusGS 框架，核心思路是将结构补全与确定性区域解耦，仅对几何歧义区域进行计算。具体包括：(1) 3D几何歧义流形（3D Geometric Ambiguity Manifold）准确定位因遮挡和高几何不确定性导致的局部区域；(2) 轻量级目标结构补全模块，在该稀疏拓扑子空间内选择性实例化并优化连续高斯查询。

Result: 在驾驶场景基准上取得了最先进的性能，同时自然减少了约74%的高斯总数，渲染时间降低约34%，实现了优越的效率-质量权衡。

Conclusion: FocusGS 通过将计算集中在几何歧义区域而非全局稠密化，有效解决了稀疏视角3D重建中效率与完整性的矛盾，为自动驾驶场景下的高效3D重建提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注自动驾驶场景下的稀疏视角3D重建，使用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行场景结构补全，属于3D场景重建与生成范畴，因此映射到 canonical 的 "3D 生成与重建" 子主题。

Importance reason: FocusGS improves sparse-view 3D reconstruction efficiency for autonomous driving, but its core topic (3D reconstruction for driving) is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D reconstruction, autonomous driving, sparse-view, Gaussian splatting, efficiency improvement

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scene structures from sparse, low-overlap observations remains a fundamental challenge in autonomous driving. Recent state-of-the-art frameworks achieve promising results by incorporating voxel-based Gaussians, but incur substantial computational redundancy due to a uniform volumetric processing strategy. To bridge the gap between the efficiency of pixel-based Gaussian methods and the structural completeness of voxel-based Gaussian approaches, we propose FocusGS, a simple yet effective framework that shifts the paradigm from global densification to targeted structural completion. Our central insight is that structural completion should be decoupled from deterministic regions, with computation concentrated exclusively on areas exhibiting geometric ambiguity. Specifically, FocusGS addresses the localization challenge by deriving a 3D Geometric Ambiguity Manifold to accurately isolate localized areas prone to occlusion and high geometric uncertainty. To overcome the subsequent manifold completion challenge, we design a lightweight targeted structure completion module that selectively instantiates and optimizes continuous Gaussian queries strictly within this unstructured, sparse topological subspace. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FocusGS achieves a superior efficiency-quality trade-off, advancing state-of-the-art performance on driving-centric benchmarks while naturally reducing the total number of Gaussians by ~74% and decreasing rendering time by ~34%.

</details>


### [78] [CGGS: Consistency-Augmented Geometric Gaussian Splatting for Ego-centric 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03819)
*Zhenyu Sun, Xiaohan Zhang, Qi Liu, Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: CGGS 是一个文本驱动的自我中心 3D 场景生成框架，通过一致性增强的多视图潜在扩散模型、光流引导的深度估计和基于熵的互信息深度损失，结合层次化优化方案，在 3D 高斯泼溅重建中提升视觉质量和几何结构准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心 3D 场景生成面临视角重叠有限和个体视角主导场景理解的问题，导致视角不一致、语义不对齐以及几何结构失真。现有方法难以生成连贯且几何准确的自我中心 3D 场景。

Method: CGGS 包含三个模块：(1) Ego-centric Generator：微调多视图潜在扩散模型，使用一致性增强损失生成与文本描述对齐的一致高保真 2D 内容；(2) Layout Decorator：利用光流和点轨迹对应估计深度，从自我中心 2D 先验生成稠密点云作为粗略布局；(3) Geometric Refiner：提出基于熵的互信息深度损失（MID）结合层次化优化方案，增强 3D 高斯重建的几何结构。

Result: 综合实验表明，CGGS 在生成连贯且准确的文本驱动 3D 场景方面优于先前方法，在视觉质量和几何结构上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: CGGS 通过一致性增强生成、光流引导深度估计和几何精炼器，有效解决了自我中心 3D 场景生成中的几何失真和视角不一致问题，为文本驱动的 3D 场景生成提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CGGS，一个文本到 3D 场景生成框架，使用 3D Gaussian Splatting 进行自我中心场景的几何一致生成，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on ego-centric 3D scene generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous language/multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian splatting, text-to-3D, ego-centric generation, multi-view diffusion, depth estimation

Abstract: Challenges remain in ego-centric 3D scene generation due to limited view overlap and the dominant influence of individual perspectives on scene interpretation. These factors hinder the creation of viewpoint-consistent and semantically aligned visual content, as well as the construction of accurate geometric structures. In this paper, we propose CGGS, a text-to-3D framework aiming to enhance 3D-content-awareness and address geometric distortions in ego-centric scene generation. Firstly, the Ego-centric Generator is proposed by fine-tuning a Multi-View Latent Diffusion Model with consistency-augmented loss to generate consistent, high-fidelity 2D content aligned with textual descriptions. Then, Layout Decorator leverages optical flow and point-track correspondence to estimate depth, therefore producing dense point clouds as coarse layouts from the ego-centric 2D priors. Building on this initialization, Geometric Refiner is proposed to enhance 3D Gaussian reconstruction via an entropy-based Mutual Information Depth Loss (MID) combined with a hierarchical optimization scheme for improving visual quality and geometric structure. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that \textcolor{softred}{CGGS} outperforms previous methods in generating coherent and accurate text-driven 3D scenes. Project page: .

</details>


### [79] [SparseOcc++: Geometry-Aware Sparse Latent Representation for Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04732)
*Pin Tang, Zhongdao Wang, Guoqing Wang, Xiangxuan Ren, Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SparseOcc++ 提出了一种几何感知的稀疏潜空间表示方法，用于3D语义占用预测。通过将场景补全显式解耦为符号距离回归（SCF），并仅在几何验证区域进行语义分割，实现了更高效、更精确的3D占用预测，在 nuScenes 和 SemanticKITTI 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D语义占用预测方法存在两个主要问题：(1) 密集体素表示在大量空区域上浪费计算；(2) 现有稀疏方法（如 SparseOcc）将场景补全与语义预测纠缠在一起，向空区域无差别传播高维特征，导致计算开销大且几何模糊。作者希望显式解耦这两个任务，实现几何感知的高效稀疏表示。

Method: SparseOcc++ 提出几何感知稀疏框架：(1) 场景补全场（SCF）将补全任务重新定义为稀疏锚点体素上的符号距离回归，结合正交分解与离散化距离学习来建模复杂室外几何；(2) 几何引导传播模块将 SCF 转换为完整体素场景，并限制语义分割仅在几何验证区域进行，从而避免在空区域上浪费计算。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上，SparseOcc++ 相比 SparseOcc 提升 IoU 2.3 个点，速度快 3.9 倍；在 SemanticKITTI 上，相比 OccFormer 实现 5.9 倍加速，达到新的 SOTA。

Conclusion: SparseOcc++ 通过显式解耦场景补全与语义分割，并引入几何感知的稀疏潜空间表示，在显著提升计算效率的同时提高了3D语义占用预测的精度，为自动驾驶场景下的高效3D感知提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于视觉的3D语义占用预测（semantic occupancy prediction），属于3D场景理解与重建范畴。虽然更偏向感知而非生成，但3D语义占用预测与3D场景重建高度相关，且现有 taxonomy 中 three_d_generation 是最接近的 [CANONICAL] 主题，涵盖3D场景生成与重建。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D occupancy prediction for autonomous driving, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative-keyword areas. Signals: 3D semantic occupancy, sparse latent representation, autonomous driving, SOTA on nuScenes, geometry-aware decoupling

Abstract: Vision-based 3D semantic occupancy prediction is essential for autonomous driving, yet dense voxel representations waste computation on largely empty space, while BEV and TPV projections compromise fine-grained 3D structure. Fully sparse representations offer an attractive alternative, but existing methods, including SparseOcc, entangle scene completion with semantic prediction by indiscriminately propagating high-dimensional features into empty regions and applying voxel-wise classification. This creates excessive activations, computational overhead, and geometric ambiguity. We present SparseOcc++, a geometry-aware sparse framework that explicitly decouples scene completion from semantic segmentation. SparseOcc++ reformulates completion as signed-distance regression on sparse anchor voxels through a scene completion field (SCF). To model complex outdoor geometry robustly, it combines orthogonal decomposition with discretized distance learning. A geometry-guided propagation module then converts the SCF into a complete volumetric scene and restricts semantic segmentation to geometrically verified regions. Experiments establish new state of the art: SparseOcc++ improves IoU by 2.3 points and is 3.9x faster than SparseOcc on nuScenes, while achieving a 5.9x speedup over OccFormer on SemanticKITTI.

</details>


### [80] [GUSH3R: Everyone Everywhere All at Once as Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05243)
*Keito Abe, Kaede Shiohara, Takashi Otonari, Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GUSH3R 是一个前馈式框架，从单目视频中同时重建动态人体和静态场景为3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）表示，支持新视角合成，相比优化方法显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法能高效预测几何，但通常限于非真实感表示（点云、网格），或无法处理非刚体对象（特别是动态人体）。需要一种能从单目视频中联合建模场景几何、相机运动和非刚体人体动态并实现真实感渲染的方法。

Method: 提出 GUSH3R 框架，采用前馈方式从单目人体-场景视频中一次性重建动态人体和静态场景为3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）图元，保持几何一致性并支持新视角合成。

Result: 在单目人体-场景数据集上，该方法在新视角合成质量上达到有竞争力的水平，同时相比基于优化的方法显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: GUSH3R 实现了从单目视频到动态人体-场景的快速、高质量3DGS重建，填补了前馈方法在非刚体动态人体处理上的空白。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从单目视频中重建动态人体与静态场景的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）表示，属于3D场景重建与神经渲染范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong 3D reconstruction work but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, feed-forward reconstruction, dynamic human-scene, monocular video, novel view synthesis

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic human-scene environments from monocular videos is a challenging problem that requires jointly modeling scene geometry, camera motion, and non-rigid human dynamics while enabling photorealistic rendering. Recent feed-forward methods can efficiently predict geometry, but they are often limited to non-photorealistic representations such as point clouds and meshes, or they fail to handle non-rigid objects, particularly dynamic humans. To fill this gap, we present GUSH3R (Gaussian-Unified Scene Human 3D Reconstruction), a feed-forward framework for online dynamic human-scene reconstruction. From a monocular human-scene video, our method reconstructs dynamic humans (everyone) and static scenes (everywhere) in a single forward pass (all at once) as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) primitives (as gaussians), which are geometrically consistent and capable of novel view synthesis. Experiments on monocular human-scene datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive novel view synthesis quality while significantly improving inference efficiency compared to optimization-based methods.

</details>


### [81] [WildSplat: Feedforward Gaussian Splatting from Unposed In-the-Wild Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05347)
*Xiyu Zhang, Jingyu Zhuang, Hongjia Zhai, Zizheng Yan, Jinwei Chen, Guofeng Zhang, Qingnan Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WildSplat 是首个面向野外非配准图像的前馈式 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，通过双分支架构解耦几何与外观，实现外观条件新视角合成与外观编辑，在单次前向传播中达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式 3D 重建方法在光照条件变化的野外场景中表现不佳，而基于优化的方法速度慢且无法泛化。作者希望设计一个既能处理光照变化、又能快速泛化的前馈式 3DGS 框架。

Method: 提出双分支架构：几何分支提取外观不变的 3D 结构并联合预测相机位姿；外观分支通过全局预调制交叉注意力机制将目标外观线索注入内容特征。同时引入联合多参考训练策略防止特征纠缠，实现单次前向传播的外观条件新视角合成。

Result: 在多个野外场景数据集上，WildSplat 在稀疏输入的新视角合成和外观编辑任务中超越了现有基于优化和前馈的方法，达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: WildSplat 成功将前馈式 3DGS 扩展到野外非配准图像场景，通过显式解耦几何与外观实现了高效且泛化的新视角合成与外观编辑，为实际应用中的 3D 场景重建提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WildSplat，一种前馈式 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，用于野外非配准图像的外观条件新视角合成与外观编辑。核心方法基于 3DGS 进行场景重建与渲染，属于 3D 生成与重建范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Strong 3D reconstruction contribution but lacks direct alignment with priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting, Unposed in-the-wild images, Appearance-conditioned novel view synthesis, Dual-branch geometry-appearance decoupling, Sparse input SOTA

Abstract: While feedforward 3D reconstruction excels at efficient novel view synthesis, it typically falters when faced with scenes under varying illumination. To this end, we introduce WildSplat, the first feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework capable of appearance-conditioned novel-view synthesis for unposed in-the-wild images. To handle inconsistent photometric conditions, we propose a dual-branch architecture that explicitly decouples geometry from appearance. The geometry branch extracts an appearance-invariant 3D structure and jointly predicts camera poses. To govern the rendering appearance, the appearance branch injects target appearance cues into the content features via a globally pre-modulated cross-attention mechanism. To further prevent feature entanglement, we introduce a joint multi-reference training strategy that stabilizes the training process. Extensive experiments show that WildSplat surpasses existing optimization-based and feedforward methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in in-the-wild novel view synthesis and appearance editing from sparse inputs in a single forward pass.

</details>


### [82] [ReCal3R: Reliability-Calibrated Learning Rates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05356)
*Xinze Li, Yiyuan Wang, Pengxu Chen, Wentao Fan, Weifeng Su, Weisi Lin, Wentao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (46.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReCal3R 提出了一种可靠性校准的学习率方法用于流式3D重建，通过估计状态token的可靠性来校准候选学习率，抑制不可靠token的激进更新，同时保留对信息丰富帧的适应能力。作为CUT3R的无训练校准规则，在长序列上取得了显著性能提升（ATE降低3.7倍），且运行时间和内存开销相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建依赖紧凑的循环场景状态来处理长图像序列，但重复更新会逐渐破坏该状态，导致可靠的历史信息被噪声或模糊观测覆盖。现有方法缺乏对状态token可靠性的显式建模，无法区分哪些token应该保守更新、哪些应该积极适应新信息。

Method: ReCal3R 从维护的场景状态中估计每个状态token的可靠性，利用token对齐、状态重建残差和近期更新压力来校准候选学习率。最终的token级学习率在保守基率和候选率之间插值，对不可靠token抑制激进更新，对信息丰富帧保持适应能力。该方法作为CUT3R的训练无关校准规则使用。

Result: 在长序列上，ATE降低3.7倍，深度和重建质量均有显著提升，运行时间和内存开销与基线相当。

Conclusion: 可靠性校准的学习率方法能有效缓解流式3D重建中状态退化问题，在保持计算效率的同时大幅提升长序列重建质量，是一种简单有效的训练无关改进方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究流式3D重建，属于3D场景重建范畴，与[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D reconstruction efficiency, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3D reconstruction, streaming, learning rate calibration, CUT3R, no training

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction relies on a compact recurrent scene state to process long image streams in linear time and bounded memory. However, repeated updates can gradually corrupt this state, causing reliable historical information to be overwritten by noisy or ambiguous observations. We introduce ReCal3R, a reliability-calibrated learning rate method for recurrent 3D reconstruction. Instead of directly applying a candidate learning rate, our method estimates state token reliability from the maintained scene state and uses it to calibrate a candidate learning rate derived from token alignment, state reconstruction residual, and recent update pressure. The resulting token-wise learning rate interpolates between a conservative base rate and the candidate rate, suppressing aggressive updates on unreliable tokens while preserving adaptation to informative frames. Applied to CUT3R as a training-free calibration rule, ReCal3R reaches strong performance on long sequences in pose, depth, and reconstruction quality, including a 3.7$\times$ reduction in ATE, with comparable runtime and memory. Code is available at: .

</details>


### [83] [PixWorld: Unifying 3D Scene Generation and Reconstruction in Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05373)
*Sensen Gao, Zhaoqing Wang, Qihang Cao, Dongdong Yu, Changhu Wang, Jia-Wang Bian*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PixWorld 提出了一种统一的像素空间扩散范式，将 3D 场景生成与重建统一在单个模型中，通过在渲染图像上直接进行扩散监督，避免了潜空间方法的信息损失和对预训练 VAE/RAE 的依赖，并引入几何感知损失以提供 3D 结构监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 重建和生成通常采用分离的范式（重建用像素回归，生成用潜扩散），近期统一工作虽在潜空间进行但存在扩散目标定义在潜特征而非 3D 表征上、潜编码导致信息损失、依赖预训练 VAE/RAE 等问题。

Method: 提出 PixWorld，在像素空间（渲染图像）上定义扩散过程，统一处理 3D 重建和生成；引入几何感知损失，利用预训练 3D 基础模型的几何感知特征空间对齐渲染视图与真实视图，提供 3D 结构监督。

Result: PixWorld 在 3D 生成任务上持续优于先前的潜空间方法，在重建任务上达到与最先进方法相当的性能，验证了统一像素空间方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 像素空间扩散范式能够有效统一 3D 场景生成与重建，消除潜空间方法的信息损失和额外依赖，几何感知损失进一步提升了 3D 结构保真度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PixWorld，在像素空间（渲染图像）上统一 3D 场景生成与重建，属于 3D 生成与重建的核心范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Unifies 3D generation and reconstruction in pixel space, directly relevant to your high-weighted continuous multimodal and world model priorities. Signals: pixel-space diffusion, 3D scene generation and reconstruction, geometry perception loss, avoids latent space information loss, aligns with continuous multimodal representation

Abstract: 3D reconstruction and generation are commonly tackled by separate paradigms: pixel-based regression for reconstruction, and latent diffusion for generation. Recent works attempt to unify them in latent space, but with notable drawbacks: the diffusion objective is defined on latent features rather than the underlying 3D representation, and both branches suffer from information loss introduced by latent encoding, while requiring a pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) or Representation Autoencoder (RAE). In this paper, we reformulate these two tasks under a unified pixel-space diffusion paradigm and introduce PixWorld, a single model that jointly addresses 3D reconstruction and generation. By supervising diffusion directly on rendered images, PixWorld removes the above limitations and aligns optimization with 3D scene fidelity. Beyond photometric and perceptual supervision that operates at the 2D image level and lacks 3D geometric awareness, we further introduce a geometry perception loss that aligns rendered views with their ground truth in the geometry-aware feature space of a pretrained 3D foundation model, providing 3D structural supervision. PixWorld consistently outperforms prior latent-space generation methods and matches state-of-the-art reconstruction methods, demonstrating the superiority of a unified pixel-space approach.

</details>


### [84] [SynCity 3000: Bootstrapping Scene-Scale 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05392)
*Paul Engstler, Iro Laina, Christian Rupprecht, Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SynCity 3000 提出了一种将图像到 3D 生成器扩展为卷积算子以生成全局一致的大规模 3D 场景的方法，通过合成数据引擎解决训练数据稀缺问题，实现了任意尺寸和复杂度的 3D 场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到 3D 生成器能生成高质量单物体 3D 资产，但无法扩展到整个场景级别；同时 3D 场景训练数据稀缺，限制了场景级生成能力。

Method: 1) 提出合成数据引擎生成场景级 3D 数据以解决数据稀缺问题；2) 将图像到 3D 生成器微调为卷积算子，使其可滑动地应用于大尺寸输入；3) 根据用户提示生成整个场景的 dimetric 图像，再用卷积生成器逐块处理，得到任意大小和复杂度的连贯 3D 场景。

Result: 在多种提示和布局下，SynCity 3000 能生成大规模、连贯且细节丰富的 3D 场景，优于先前的 3D 场景生成方法。

Conclusion: 通过将图像到 3D 生成器改造为卷积算子并结合合成数据引擎，SynCity 3000 有效解决了场景级 3D 生成中的连贯性和数据稀缺问题，为大规模 3D 场景生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SynCity 3000，一种从单张图像生成大规模 3D 场景的框架，属于 3D 场景生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Scene-scale 3D diffusion is tangentially related to your focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation, but lacks direct alignment with your priority subtopics. Signals: 3D scene generation, diffusion model, synthetic data engine, convolutional operator, global coherence

Abstract: We present SynCity 3000, a framework for generating 3D scenes that are globally coherent while enabling fine-grained layout control. Building on the ability of current image-to-3D generators to produce complex 3D assets from a single image, we extend this capability to the scale of entire scenes by adapting the generator to be applicable as a convolutional operator. We achieve this by fine-tuning the model on scene-like data generated by a new synthetic data engine, which we propose to address the scarcity of 3D scene data for training. The convolutional generator is then applied to a dimetric image of the entire scene, generated from the user prompt, resulting in 3D scenes of arbitrary size and complexity. Across diverse prompts and layouts, SynCity 3000 produces large, coherent, and detailed scenes, addressing the shortcomings of prior approaches to 3D scene generation.

</details>


### [85] [SceneFrom3D: Geometry-Conditioned Outdoor 3D Scene Generation via View Scheduling with Object-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04540)
*Geonung Kim, Jeongeun Park, Nuri Ryu, Di Liu, Sunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SceneFrom3D 是一个几何条件驱动的室外3D场景生成框架，通过自动视图调度（构建有向生成图）和对象级条件控制（身份图像+几何遵循参数），实现了高质量、可控的室外3D场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何条件3D场景生成方法在室外场景中面临视图调度瓶颈——室外几何大、无结构、无边界，难以自动获得覆盖充分且生成稳定的视图序列。同时，现有方法缺乏对场景中单个物体的细粒度外观和几何控制能力。

Method: SceneFrom3D 提出自动视图调度机制：构建有向生成图，节点表示锚定视图，边表示插值轨迹，定义哪些视图需要合成、哪些视图对需要插值以及生成顺序。此外，通过对象级条件控制增强可控性：为每个物体分配身份图像用于外观引导，以及几何遵循参数用于区域级控制输入几何的遵循程度。

Result: 实验表明 SceneFrom3D 在几何条件室外3D场景生成上达到最先进水平，生成高质量场景，并实现了可控的物体外观和几何遵循。

Conclusion: SceneFrom3D 通过自动视图调度和对象级条件控制有效解决了室外3D场景生成中的视图调度瓶颈和细粒度控制问题，为几何条件3D场景生成提供了新的框架思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出几何条件驱动的室外3D场景生成框架，属于3D场景生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Outdoor 3D scene generation with view scheduling is tangentially related to your multimodal generation focus but lacks direct alignment with your priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: 3D scene generation, geometry-conditioned, view scheduling, object-level control, outdoor scenes

Abstract: Geometry-conditioned 3D scene generation enables the creation of 3D environments from user-provided geometry, offering direct control over scene structure and object layout. To generate such 3D scenes, current methods commonly adopt a three-stage design that first defines a view schedule, then synthesizes multi-view observations along the scheduled views, and finally reconstructs a 3D representation from the generated images. However, defining the view schedule becomes a major bottleneck for outdoor scenes, where large, unstructured, and unbounded geometry makes it difficult to obtain views that provide sufficient coverage while supporting stable generation. To address this bottleneck, we present SceneFrom3D, a framework that automatically schedules views from outdoor input geometries. SceneFrom3D constructs a directed generation graph whose nodes represent anchor views and whose edges represent interpolation trajectories, defining which views to synthesize, which view pairs to interpolate, and in which order generation should proceed. Beyond automatic view scheduling, SceneFrom3D further improves controllability through object-level conditioning, assigning each object an identity image for appearance guidance and a geometry-adherence parameter for region-wise control over the input geometry. Experiments demonstrate that SceneFrom3D achieves state-of-the-art geometry-conditioned outdoor 3D scene generation, producing high-quality scenes with controllable object appearance and geometry adherence.

</details>

### [86] [GrowFields: Compositional 4D Neural Fields for Topology-Changing Plant Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03330)
*Joaquin Gajardo, Michele Volpi, Marko Mihajlovic, Siyu Tang, Lukas Roth, Sergey Prokudin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (51.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GrowFields 是一种组合式动态神经场表示方法，将植物分解为器官并在各自规范坐标系中对齐，学习共享的连续神经变形场，实现拓扑变化的 4D 植物生长建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长建模面临非刚性变形、拓扑变化（新器官出现）以及缺乏时序对应关系等独特挑战，现有通用场景方法难以处理这些拓扑变化和异步器官生长。

Method: 将植物分解为组成器官，每个器官对齐到各自的规范坐标系，学习一个共享的连续神经变形场，以可学习的每器官潜码为条件，建模所有器官的时间动态。

Result: 在四个植物物种的生长序列上评估，在几何拟合和器官追踪精度上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GrowFields 通过模块化但统一的表示自然地适应植物器官的异步发育，为植物生长建模提供了有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GrowFields，一种组合式动态神经场表示，用于从点云时间序列进行器官感知的 4D 植物生长建模。这属于动态 4D 场景生成与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on plant growth modeling, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 4D neural fields, plant growth, topology changes, compositional representation, point cloud time series

Abstract: Quantifying plant growth dynamics from sparse longitudinal 3D observations is fundamental for agriculture and plant sciences. Yet, plants pose unique challenges: they undergo intricate non-rigid deformations, exhibit changing topology as new organs emerge, and often lack explicit temporal correspondences between consecutive data acquisitions due to newly formed tissue. Methods designed for general scenes struggle to model topology changes and asynchronous organ growth characteristic of plants. To address these challenges, we introduce GrowFields, a compositional dynamic neural field representation for organ-aware 4D plant growth modelling from point cloud time series. Our approach decomposes a plant into its constituent organs and aligns each organ into its own canonical coordinate frame, isolating intrinsic growth patterns from global plant motion. We then learn a shared continuous neural deformation field that models temporal dynamics across all organs, conditioned on learnable per-organ latent codes capturing organ identity and growth characteristics. The resulting modular yet unified representation naturally accommodates the asynchronous development of plant organs while remaining grounded in the practical setting of organ-level plant tracking. We evaluate GrowFields on growth sequences from four plant species, assessing geometric fitting and organ tracking accuracy using manually annotated leaf-tip trajectories. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in spatial precision, temporal coherence, and morphological fidelity over a range of existing representations.

</details>


### [87] [DeGenseGS: Geometrically and Semantically Decoupled Surgical Scene Understanding in 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04761)
*Yimo Wang, Bin Kang, Shuojue Yang, Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DeGenseGS 提出了一种几何与语义解耦的 4D 高斯泼溅框架，通过 HexPlane 时空纠缠模块、光栅化原生语义提取和角度对齐优化策略，实现了手术场景中语义与几何变形的独立建模，在 CholecSeg8k 和 EndoVis18 数据集上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉语言模型集成到可变形场中时采用刚性耦合方案，导致语义特征与几何变形紧密绑定，造成语义与物理解剖之间的严重错位，无法实现实时、文本提示的 4D 手术场景重建。

Method: 1) HexPlane 时空纠缠模块：使用共享运动学潜变量同步语义变化与场景动态，同时显式解耦语义更新与几何变形；2) 光栅化原生语义提取机制（Rasterization-Native Semantic Extraction）：从拓扑连续的特征图中推断语义，增强对重建伪影的鲁棒性；3) 角度对齐优化策略：符合原生超球面潜空间，防止语义失真。

Result: 在 CholecSeg8k 和 EndoVis18 数据集上的大量实验表明，DeGenseGS 达到了最先进的性能，在剧烈组织变形和拓扑转变下仍能实现空间连续的语义分割，几何完整性和语义-解剖对齐均得到增强。

Conclusion: DeGenseGS 通过几何与语义解耦的 4D 高斯泼溅框架，有效解决了手术场景中语义与几何错位的问题，为自主手术交互中的实时、文本提示 4D 重建提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DeGenseGS，在 4D Gaussian Splatting 框架下进行手术场景的几何与语义解耦重建，属于动态 4D 场景生成与重建方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。同时涉及语义理解，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Surgical scene 4D reconstruction is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 4D Gaussian Splatting, surgical scene understanding, HexPlane spatiotemporal entanglement, semantic-geometric decoupling, CholecSeg8k/EndoVis18 datasets

Abstract: Real-time, text-promptable 4D reconstruction is indispensable for autonomous surgical interaction. Severe misalignment between semantic meaning and physical anatomy still persists, largely because existing solutions integrate Vision-Language Models into deformable fields via a rigid coupling scheme that tightly binds semantic features to geometric warping. In this paper, we propose DeGenseGS, Geometrically and Semantically Decoupled Surgical Scene Understanding in 4D Gaussian Splatting, a novel framework that independently models semantic evolution and geometric deformation. Specifically, we propose a HexPlane-based spatiotemporal entanglement module that uses shared kinematic latents to synchronize semantic mutations with scene dynamics, while explicitly disentangling semantic updates from geometric deformation. To further ensure robustness against reconstruction artifacts, we devise a Rasterization-Native Semantic Extraction mechanism that infers semantics from topologically continuous feature maps. Additionally, we incorporate an angular-aligned optimization strategy that conforms to the native hyperspherical latent space, thereby preventing semantic distortion. Extensive evaluations on the CholecSeg8k and EndoVis18 datasets demonstrate that DeGenseGS achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our framework yields enhanced geometric completeness and robust semantic-anatomic alignment, enabling spatially continuous segmentation despite drastic tissue deformation and topological transitions.

</details>


### [88] [MV-Forcing: Long Multi-View Video Generation via 4D-Grounded Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05376)
*Gal Fiebelman, Hadar Averbuch-Elor, Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MV-Forcing 提出一种结合时序与视角自回归的扩散模型框架，通过 4D 几何桥接（3D 重建+渲染几何先验）连接顺序生成的视角，并引入联合去噪机制实现时间无界生成，配合分布匹配蒸馏与时空自强迫策略，仅用单步学生模型即可生成长序列、多视角一致的动态场景视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型要么能生成长时序单视角视频（时序自回归），要么能生成短多视角视频（双向注意力），但无法同时实现长时序和多视角一致的动态场景视频生成。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 在扩散模型中组合时序自回归与视角自回归，利用 3D 重建模型作为自动回归生成视角之间的几何桥接：从已完成源视图重建 3D 结构并渲染下一目标视角的几何先验，再由扩散模型精化为高质量视频。2) 引入联合去噪训练机制，两个视角槽位均从噪声初始化，突破教师模型的固定时间窗口限制，实现时间无界生成。3) 通过分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）与时空自强迫（Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing）缩小训练-推理暴露偏差。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验表明，MV-Forcing 能够使用单个少步学生模型，生成长度任意、视角数量任意的几何一致多视角动态场景视频。

Conclusion: MV-Forcing 成功实现了长序列多视角动态场景视频生成，通过 4D 几何桥接和自回归组合策略解决了现有方法的局限性，为多视角视频生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MV-Forcing 框架，通过 4D 几何桥接实现长序列多视角动态场景视频生成，属于动态场景生成与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel 4D-geometric bridging for long multi-view video generation aligns with user's high-priority video generation and world model interests. Signals: autoregressive video generation, video diffusion, world model, 4D dynamic scene, multi-view consistency

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have enabled either long single-view generation through temporal autoregression, or short multi-view synthesis through bidirectional attention. However, generating long, multi-view consistent videos of dynamic scenes remains unsolved. In this work, we present MV-Forcing, a framework that composes temporal and view-wise autoregression within a single diffusion model by introducing a 4D geometric bridge between sequentially generated views. Our key insight is that an autoregressive 3D reconstruction model naturally interfaces between autoregressively generated views. Given a completed source view, we reconstruct its 3D structure and render a geometric prior of the next target viewpoint, which the diffusion model refines into a high-quality video. To extend generation beyond the teacher's fixed temporal window, we introduce a joint denoising regime where both view slots are initialized from noise during training, enabling temporally unbounded generation. We distill the model via Distribution Matching Distillation with Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing, closing the train-inference exposure bias gap for both temporal and view-sequential autoregression. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that MV-Forcing produces geometrically consistent multi-view videos of dynamic scenes at arbitrary lengths and viewpoint counts using a single few-step student model.

</details>

### [89] [Aura: Consistent Multi-Subject Video Generation via VLM-Grounded Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04311)
*Zixiang Zhou, Zhentao Yu, Yifeng Ma, Hongmei Wang, Wenqing Yu, Cong Wang, Zilin Yang, Rui Chen, Jiarong Ou, Yezhou Liu, Yuan Zhou, Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 高优先级 (91.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Aura 提出一个统一框架，通过 VLM 提取多模态语义特征、两阶段对齐策略、主体感知 RoPE-Shift 机制和 Memory Tokens 等技术，实现高保真、身份一致的多主体视频生成，在单主体和多主体场景下均达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动视频生成方法在保持身份一致性和建模多主体间复杂关系方面仍存在困难，且容易产生复制-粘贴伪影（copy-paste artifacts）。

Method: 1) 利用 VLM 结合可学习查询提取文本和视觉参考的多模态语义特征；2) 设计两阶段对齐策略将 VLM 特征渐进映射到 DiT 特征空间；3) 采用 token 拼接注入参考信息；4) 提出主体感知 RoPE-Shift 机制区分不同主体类型；5) 引入主体感知可学习 token 区分不同类别的参考图像；6) 引入 Memory Tokens 平衡不同参考主体数量的训练信号；7) 推理时使用 Progressive-APG 缓解过饱和并改善语义对齐。

Result: 在单主体和多主体视频生成任务上均达到 SOTA 性能，有效保持了身份一致性并减少了复制-粘贴伪影。

Conclusion: Aura 通过 VLM 引导的语义对齐和多种创新机制，成功实现了高保真、身份一致的多主体可控视频生成，为多元素视频合成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多主体（multi-subject）视频生成，核心是保持主体身份一致性（identity consistency）并建模多主体间关系，属于参考图引导的个性化/主体驱动生成。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "主体与概念驱动生成"（customization_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's high-priority continuous multimodal and video generation directions, with novel VLM-grounded alignment for multi-subject consistency. Signals: video generation, subject-driven generation, controllable generation, diffusion transformer, VLM grounding

Abstract: Subject-driven and multi-element video generation are central to controllable video synthesis, but existing methods still struggle to preserve identity consistency and model complex relationships among multiple subjects. In this paper, we propose Aura, a unified framework for high-fidelity and identity-consistent video generation. To better capture scene dynamics and subject interactions, we introduce AI director-level captions that provide dense and structured descriptions of video content. We further leverage a vision-language model (VLM) with learnable queries to extract multimodal semantic features from textual and visual references, covering both global semantics and fine-grained visual cues. To bridge the representational gap between the VLM and the Diffusion Transformer (DiT), we design a two-stage alignment strategy that progressively maps VLM features into the DiT feature space. For visual conditioning, we adopt token concatenation to inject reference information directly into the generation process. To distinguish heterogeneous subject types and reduce common copy-paste artifacts, we develop a subject-aware RoPE-Shift mechanism. To further differentiate reference images of different categories, we introduce subject-aware learnable tokens. In addition, we introduce Memory Tokens to balance the training signal across examples with different numbers of reference subjects. During inference, Progressive-APG (Adaptive Prompt Guidance) further alleviates oversaturation and improves semantic alignment with user prompts. Finally, we build a high-quality video-subject image dataset through a dedicated data construction pipeline. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both single-subject generation and more challenging multi-element scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [LILAC: Layer-Wise Independent LoRAs and Cascaded Conditioning for Multi-Concept Customization of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04801)
*Marian Lupascu, Sebastian Ripa, Mihai Trascau, Mariana-Iuliana Georgescu, Ionut Mironica*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 值得关注 (67.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LILAC 提出一种无需联合训练的多概念个性化图像生成框架，通过将每个主体作为独立图像层、在推理时依次激活单个 LoRA 适配器并级联条件化，避免了参数级干扰和身份混淆，支持线性扩展且与骨干网络无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在融合多个独立训练的概念适配器时，由于在共享权重空间中进行合并（如联邦平均、梯度融合、正交约束），会导致身份混淆、风格渗透，且需要联合重训练。本文旨在解决多概念个性化生成中的身份保持与场景连贯性问题。

Method: LILAC 将每个主体视为独立的图像层，在推理时依次激活单个 LoRA 适配器，每个新主体以前置已放置主体的冻结合成结果为条件进行生成，从而避免参数级干扰。该方法无需联合训练，概念数量线性扩展，且与骨干网络无关。

Result: 在 Orthogonal Adaptation 协议下，LILAC 应用于 Qwen-Image-Edit 达到 ArcFace 检测率 0.861，而 Orthogonal Adaptation 原设置为 0.745。

Conclusion: LILAC 通过层式独立 LoRA 与级联条件化，有效实现了多概念个性化图像生成，避免了身份混淆和风格渗透，无需联合训练，具有良好的可扩展性和骨干网络无关性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多概念个性化图像生成方法，属于主体与概念驱动生成（customization_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Novel multi-concept customization method but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: multi-concept customization, diffusion models, LoRA composition, no joint training, backbone-agnostic

Abstract: Personalizing text-to-image diffusion models to render several specific subjects in a coherent image remains challenging: the model must preserve each subject's identity while keeping the scene spatially and visually coherent. Methods that fuse independently trained concept adapters in a shared weight space (via federated averaging, gradient fusion, or orthogonality constraints) suffer from identity confusion and style bleeding and require joint retraining. In this work, we show that composing concepts as separate image layers, instead of merging their adapters in a shared weight space, avoids parameter-level interference. We introduce LILAC, a framework that composes independently trained low-rank adapters at inference time: each subject is conditioned on the frozen composite of previously placed subjects, with exactly one adapter active at a time, therefore identities never interfere at the parameter level. LILAC composes the adapters without any joint training, scales linearly with the number of concepts, and is backbone-agnostic. Under the Orthogonal Adaptation protocol, LILAC applied on Qwen-Image-Edit reaches an ArcFace detection rate of 0.861, while Orthogonal Adaptation reports 0.745 in its original setting. Adaptation reports 0.745 in its original setting. Code is available at .

</details>
