### [91] [SE-UNet: Singular Equivariant Imaging for Real-World Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02628)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SE-UNet 提出了一种基于奇异等变（Singular Equivariant）的 UNet 框架，通过 D4 群几何等变性和奇异值门控作为强归纳偏置，在无需大规模预训练的情况下实现零样本图像修复，在 CIFAR-10 上 80% 像素缺失的修复任务中 PSNR 超过 DIP 基线 4 dB 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像合成中表现出色，但应用于真实世界逆问题时需要大量数据集且难以施加严格的物理约束。作者希望在不依赖大规模预训练的情况下，通过强归纳偏置解决不适定成像任务（如图像修复）。

Method: 提出 SE-UNet 框架，将生成视为受几何等变性（D4 群对称性）和奇异值门控约束的优化问题。通过奇异等变设计标准化解空间，利用强归纳偏置实现零样本修复。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上，80% 像素缺失的零样本修复任务中，SE-UNet 的 PSNR 超过 Deep Image Prior (DIP) 基线 4 dB 以上，并展现出"奇异快照"收敛特性——快速锁定到信号流形。

Conclusion: SE-UNet 提供了一种数据高效的约束生成路径，证明了强归纳偏置（几何等变性和奇异值门控）可以有效替代大规模数据预训练，为实际部署中的约束生成任务提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出SE-UNet用于零样本图像修复（inpainting），属于图像编辑中的inpainting任务，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）。

Importance reason: Focuses on constrained generation with geometric equivariance, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: zero-shot inpainting, singular equivariant UNet, strong inductive biases, data-efficient pathway, no large-scale pretraining

Abstract: While diffusion models have revolutionized image synthesis, their application to real-world inverse problems is often hampered by the need for massive datasets and the difficulty of imposing strict physical constraints. In this work, we introduce \textbf{SE-UNet} (Singular Equivariant UNet), a framework designed to solve ill-posed imaging tasks without extensive pre-training. By treating generation as an optimization problem constrained by geometric equivariance ($D_4$ group) and singular value gating, SE-UNet effectively standardizes the solution space. We demonstrate that these strong inductive biases allow for state-of-the-art zero-shot inpainting results (80\% missing pixels) on CIFAR-10. Our method surpasses Deep Image Prior (DIP) baselines by over 4 dB in PSNR and exhibits a characteristic "singular snap" convergence -- rapidly locking into the signal manifold. SE-UNet thus offers a data-efficient pathway for constrained generation, aligning with the ReALM-GEN goal of bridging theoretical priors with practical deployment.

</details>


### [92] [Global Pose Control for Generative View Synthesis in Normalized Object Coordinate Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02712)
*Zhibing Li, Amogh Gupta, Behnoosh Parsa, Dan Casas*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于归一化物体坐标空间（NOCS）的全局姿态控制方法，用于生成式新视角合成。该方法将NVS重新表述为图像编辑问题，利用SOTA编辑模型，通过上下文多模态条件注入相机token，并引入文本描述消除NOCS歧义，实现了从任意无位姿图像生成准确、一致的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式新视角合成方法要么使用输入图像相对相机位姿（缺乏全局控制），要么只能生成稀疏的全局视角，缺乏直观的全局位姿控制能力，限制了NVS在下游任务中的应用。

Method: 1) 将NVS重新表述为图像编辑问题，基于SOTA编辑模型构建；2) 在NOCS空间中定义绝对相机位姿，无需输入图像的相机位姿或相对世界坐标系；3) 通过上下文多模态条件策略注入专用相机token；4) 引入文本描述显式定义物体的规范坐标框架以消除NOCS歧义；5) 构建了具有一致对齐方向和对应NOCS文本定义的高质量数据集。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能从任意无位姿图像中鲁棒地生成具有准确一致朝向的新视角，在多种物体类别上实现了SOTA的图像质量和保真度。

Conclusion: 本文通过将NVS转化为图像编辑问题并引入NOCS全局位姿控制，有效解决了现有方法缺乏直观全局视角控制的问题，为NVS在更多下游任务中的应用打开了可能性。

Classification reason: 该论文将新视角生成（NVS）重新表述为图像编辑问题，并基于SOTA编辑模型构建，通过相机token实现全局姿态控制。这与[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）高度匹配，因为论文的核心方法是将NVS视为编辑任务，利用编辑模型的泛化能力。

Importance reason: Novel view synthesis with global pose control is interesting but not a top priority for your configured focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: NOCS-based camera control, generative view synthesis, image editing formulation, in-context multimodal conditioning, text-disambiguated NOCS

Abstract: Novel View Synthesis (NVS) enables the generation of unseen views of a scene from a single or multiple images, allowing users to freely explore an object from any viewpoint. Despite the recent impressive qualitative improvements of generative models for this task, existing methods struggle to provide global and intuitive control of target viewpoints because they either use input-relative camera poses or are limited to generating sparse global views. This lack of global pose control severely limits the number of downstream tasks potentially enabled by NVS. To address this limitation, we propose a novel approach for precise camera control in a customizable Normalized Object Coordinate Space (NOCS), requiring single or few unposed images. Our method operates solely on the absolute camera pose of the target view in NOCS, eliminating the need for a relative world frame or camera poses of the input images. Unlike previous methods that treat NVS as a standalone generation task, we formulate it as an image editing problem and build upon state-of-the-art editing models to leverage their superior generalization capability. Camera information is injected as dedicated camera tokens via an in-context multi-modal conditioning strategy. To alleviate the inherent ambiguity of NOCS, we incorporate text descriptions that explicitly define the object's canonical coordinate frame, which also enhances generalization to unseen object categories. Furthermore, we curate a high-quality dataset with consistently aligned orientations and corresponding NOCS text definitions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method robustly generates novel views with accurate and consistent orientations from arbitrary unposed images across diverse categories, achieving state-of-the-art image quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [93] [ReLo-IRR: Reflection-Guided LoRA Framework for Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02957)
*Chaoqun Wang, Yuehuan Wei, Haoxiang Cao, Shaobo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 ReLo-IRR，一种基于反射引导 LoRA 的框架，利用 rectified flow 模型实现图像反射去除。通过轻量级反射强度估计器提供图像先验，并结合时间条件机制使 LoRA 调制随去噪过程动态演化，实现对不同反射强度的鲁棒抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的反射去除方法采用图像无关的适配策略（如全参数微调或 ControlNet），对所有图像施加统一的反射抑制，导致强反射残留、弱反射细节丢失。需要一种能根据反射严重程度自适应调节的反射去除方法。

Method: 提出 ReLo-IRR 框架：1）设计轻量级反射强度估计器，预测每张图像的反射强度描述子，提供显式的反射主导先验；2）引入时间条件机制，将反射描述子与时间步嵌入融合，使 LoRA 调制随粗到细的去噪过程动态演化；3）基于 rectified flow 模型构建整体框架，实现反射强度与去噪动力学的联合建模。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上验证了有效性，展示了优越的去反射性能和鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ReLo-IRR 通过反射引导的 LoRA 调制和时间条件机制，实现了对不同反射条件的自适应鲁棒去除，为图像反射去除任务提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究单图像反射去除（SIRR），属于图像恢复/编辑任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow image restoration task with limited connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Image reflection removal, LoRA adaptation, rectified flow model, time-conditioned modulation, not autoregressive

Abstract: Single-image reflection removal (SIRR) aims to recover the clean transmission layer from a reflection-contaminated image. Although recent methods achieve promising results with large diffusion models, they rely on image-agnostic adaptation strategies, e.g., fine-tuning or ControlNet, that enforce uniform suppression regardless of reflection severity. As a result, heavy reflections often leave residuals, while weak ones suffer from detail loss. To this end, we propose ReLo-IRR, a reflection-guided LoRA framework built upon the rectified flow model. First, a lightweight estimator is designed to predict the reflection strength descriptor, providing an explicit prior of reflection dominance for each image and enabling image-dependent LoRA modulation. Second, we introduce a time-conditioned mechanism that fuses this reflection descriptor with timestep embeddings, enabling LoRA modulation to evolve consistently with the coarse-to-fine denoising process. By jointly modeling reflection strength and denoising dynamics, our ReLo-IRR achieves robust suppression of diverse reflection conditions. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks validate the effectiveness of ReLo-IRR, demonstrating superior dereflection performance and robust generalization. The code is released at .

</details>


### [94] [Handwriting Trajectory Recovery with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03422)
*Hiroki Nagamatsu, Shoji Toyota, Seiichi Uchida*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于扩散模型的手写笔迹轨迹恢复框架，将离线手写图像中的在线笔迹轨迹恢复建模为图像条件生成任务，通过去噪扩散模型生成与墨迹一致的笔迹轨迹。在CASIA-OLHWDB数据集上的实验表明，该方法在时序相似性（DTW/LDTW）和形状保真度（AIoU）上显著优于PEN-Net和Cross-VAE等现有方法，并能跨脚本泛化（中文训练→拉丁字母恢复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离线手写图像中恢复在线笔迹轨迹（即笔画顺序和动态信息）在笔画级编辑、笔迹鉴定和法医分析中有重要应用。现有方法（如PEN-Net、Cross-VAE）在处理复杂多笔画字符时恢复精度有限，亟需更强大的生成式方法。

Method: 将笔迹轨迹恢复建模为图像条件生成问题，采用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）框架。模型以离线手写图像为条件，通过逐步去噪过程从随机噪声中采样出与墨迹一致的在线笔迹轨迹（包含笔迹坐标序列）。

Result: 在CASIA-OLHWDB 1.0-1.1数据集上，该方法在DTW、LDTW和AIoU等指标上全面超越PEN-Net和Cross-VAE等基线方法。模型能够准确恢复复杂多笔画字符的笔迹轨迹，捕捉通用的笔画顺序倾向，并展现出跨脚本迁移能力（中文训练后可合理恢复拉丁字母的笔画顺序）。

Conclusion: 扩散模型为手写笔迹轨迹恢复这一离线到在线转换任务提供了有效的解决方案，在恢复精度和泛化能力上均优于现有方法，为笔画级编辑和笔迹分析等应用奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从离线手写图像恢复在线笔迹轨迹（stroke recovery），本质上是图像到轨迹的条件生成任务，属于图像编辑/可控生成范畴。虽然任务特殊，但"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）是现有[CANONICAL]中最接近的类别，涵盖图像到图像/轨迹的转换与编辑任务。

Importance reason: Novel application of diffusion models to handwriting trajectory recovery, but niche subtopic with low alignment to user's priority subtopics. Signals: First diffusion-based framework for stroke recovery, strong quantitative gains over prior methods, cross-script generalization shown, no direct link to continuous language/multimodal models, video generation, or world models, negative keywords absent but no boost keywords matched.

Abstract: Recovering online pen trajectories from offline handwriting images, often referred to as handwriting trajectory recovery (stroke recovery), is an offline-to-online conversion task with applications in stroke-level editing and forensic analysis. We propose, to the best of our knowledge, the first diffusion-model-based framework for this task. Our method formulates trajectory recovery as image-conditioned generation and uses a denoising diffusion model to sample pen trajectories consistent with the observed ink trace. Through extensive quantitative evaluations on CASIA-OLHWDB (1.0-1.1), we verify that the proposed approach enables accurate recovery even for complex multi-stroke characters, substantially improving both temporal similarity (DTW/LDTW) and shape fidelity (AIoU) over representative prior methods such as PEN-Net and Cross-VAE. We further show that the model captures general stroke-order tendencies and generalizes to classes unseen during training, exemplified by cross-script transfer: a model trained on Chinese characters can recover reasonable stroke orders for Latin letters to some extent.

</details>


### [95] [ProxyUp: Training-Free Proxy-Conditioned Video Generation for Controllable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03732)
*Zanwei Zhou, Jiazhong Cen, Jiemin Fang, Yumeng He, Chen Yang, Sikuang Li, Fanpeng Meng, Zhikuan Bao, Wei Shen, Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (86.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ProxyUp 是一种无需训练的视频生成框架，利用物理仿真或真实录制的代理视频作为动态载体，通过区域潜空间加噪和随机流松弛（SFR）技术，在保持代理视频运动轨迹的同时生成与文本提示一致的新内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以精确控制复杂动态，仅靠文本提示无法指定细粒度的物理合理运动。作者希望利用代理视频（如物理仿真或真实录制）作为动态载体，实现无需训练的可控视频生成。

Method: ProxyUp 框架包含两个核心组件：1) 区域潜空间加噪（Region-wise Latent Noising）：将代理视频反演为潜表示，保留运动关键区域的潜变量，对需要文本驱动重生成的区域注入噪声；2) 随机流松弛（Stochastic Flow Relaxation, SFR）：在 ODE 采样前逐步将组合后的潜变量向模型学习到的分布松弛，缓解分布不匹配和前景-背景耦合弱的问题。

Result: 在仿真和真实代理视频上的实验表明，ProxyUp 在动态保真度和文本对齐方面优于强视频编辑和运动迁移基线方法。

Conclusion: ProxyUp 证明了利用代理视频作为动态载体进行无需训练的可控视频生成的可行性，区域潜空间加噪和随机流松弛有效解决了潜空间组合带来的分布不匹配问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于代理视频（proxy video）控制前景物体运动的视频生成方法，属于视频编辑/可控生成范畴，与 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free controllable video generation with proxy dynamics directly supports your high-priority video generation and world model interests. Signals: training-free video generation, controllable dynamics, proxy-conditioned generation, region-wise latent noising, stochastic flow relaxation

Abstract: Precise control over complex dynamics remains challenging for modern video generative models, as text prompts alone often cannot specify physically plausible, fine-grained motion and interactions. We introduce $\textit{proxy-conditioned video generation}$, where a coarse proxy video from physics-based simulation or real-world recording serves as a dynamics carrier to control foreground object motion. Given a proxy video and a text prompt, the goal is to synthesize a new video that preserves the proxy dynamics while generating novel content and plausible interactions aligned with the prompt. Since paired proxy-target videos are difficult to obtain, we propose $\textbf{ProxyUp}$, a training-free framework built on pretrained video generative models. ProxyUp first inverts the proxy video into an intermediate latent representation and applies $\textbf{region-wise latent noising}$, preserving motion-critical proxy latents while injecting noise into regions intended for text-driven regeneration. To mitigate the distribution mismatch and weak foreground-background coupling introduced by this heuristic latent composition, we further propose $\textbf{Stochastic Flow Relaxation (SFR)}$, which progressively relaxes the composed latent toward the model's learned distribution before ODE sampling. Experiments on both simulation and real-world proxies show that ProxyUp outperforms strong video editing and motion transfer baselines in dynamic fidelity and text alignment.

</details>


### [96] [Video Generation Models Are Inherent Lighting Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04674)
*Ziqi Cai, Shuchen Weng, Kaiqi Liu, Zifeng Wang, Zhiquan Zhang, Minggui Teng, Han Jiang, Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: V-LITE 提出利用视频生成模型的内在光照理解能力，通过将光照估计转化为引导式视频修补任务，在场景中插入合成铬球来生成物理合理的反射，并设计 HDR-aware VAE 和 LoRA 微调策略，实现从单段野外视频中恢复时域一致的 HDR 环境贴图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单段野外视频中恢复动态环境贴图对于逼真渲染至关重要，但现有方法难以从视频中提取时域一致的光照信息。视频生成模型本身具备对复杂光照的内在理解能力，如何解锁这一能力用于光照估计是本文的核心动机。

Method: 将光照估计重新定义为引导式视频修补任务：在输入视频中插入合成铬球，利用视频扩散模型根据周围时空上下文生成物理合理的反射。设计 HDR-aware VAE 将 LDR 原生模型扩展到 HDR 域，采用 LoRA 高效微调策略。构建混合数据集，包含高保真 HDR 图像和野外 HDR 视频，提供 HDR 先验和动态时空上下文。

Result: 实验表明 V-LITE 能够生成时域一致的 HDR 环境贴图，在光照估计质量上优于现有方法，证明了视频扩散模型不仅是合成器，更是强大的物理场景光照估计器。

Conclusion: 现代视频扩散模型内在地具备对物理光照的理解能力，通过合适的任务重构（如引导式视频修补）可以有效地解锁这一能力用于光照估计，为视频生成模型在物理场景理解方面的应用开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文将光照估计重新定义为引导式视频修补（guided video inpainting）任务，利用视频生成模型进行环境贴图恢复，属于视频编辑/修补范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）。

Importance reason: Novel use of video generation for lighting estimation, but outside core priority subtopics. Signals: video generation model, lighting estimation, HDR environment map, guided video inpainting, LoRA fine-tuning

Abstract: Recovering dynamic environment maps from a single in-the-wild video is crucial for photorealistic rendering, yet remains a challenge. Recent video generation models can produce photorealistic scenes with complex lighting, possessing an inherent understanding of lighting. In this paper, we introduce V-LITE (Video generation models are inherent lighting estimators), a framework that unlocks this internal knowledge by reframing lighting estimation as a guided video inpainting task. Inspired by VFX industry practices, we insert a synthetic chrome ball into the scene to compel the model to generate physically plausible reflections from the surrounding spatio-temporal context. To bridge the gap from LDR-native models to the HDR domain, we design an HDR-aware VAE and employ an efficient LoRA-based fine-tuning strategy. We then construct a mixed dataset comprising high-fidelity HDR images to provide realistic HDR priors, and in-the-wild HDR videos to provide dynamic spatio-temporal context. Extensive experiments demonstrate that V-LITE produces temporally coherent HDR environment maps, revealing that modern video diffusion models are not merely synthesizers but also powerful, inherently capable estimators of physical scene lighting.

</details>

Conclusion: 单一 LoRA 适配器结合免训练结构引导即可实现高质量、可控的图像风格迁移，为风格迁移提供了一种更简洁高效的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图像引导的风格迁移（image-guided style transfer），属于图像编辑与风格化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Style transfer is a niche application, not aligned with priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Style transfer, single LoRA, diffusion-based, image-guided, structural guidance

Abstract: Image-guided style transfer aims to apply the artistic characteristics of a style image to a content image while preserving its semantic structure and layout. Despite advances in diffusion-based methods, existing approaches often face challenges in disentangling content and style, particularly when independently optimized adapters are naively combined, causing conflicts between adapters and limiting controllability over the content-style balance in inference. We further demonstrate that training-free structural guidance directly derived from the content image through the internal attention of pre-trained model outperforms a dedicated content LoRA adapter in terms of structural fidelity and computational efficiency. Building on these observations, we propose AnyStyle, a streamlined framework for image-guided style transfer. The framework adopts a unified single-adapter paradigm for coherent style capture from the style image and incorporates training-free structural guidance from the content image, thus avoiding complex entanglement between multiple adapters and improving controllability and stability. Extensive experiments show that our method delivers competitive quantitative performance and significantly improved perceptual quality. Code is available at .

</details>


### [98] [From Open Loop to Closed Loop: A Test-Time Iterative Optimization Framework for Reference-Consistent Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04691)
*Baixuan Zhao, Xinyu Zhang, Huayu Zheng, Shuaicheng Liu, Xiongkuo Min, Guangtao Zhai, Xiaohong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测试时迭代优化框架，将参考一致的可控图像生成从开环前馈过程转变为闭环动态跟踪问题。利用修改的PID算法，在推理阶段基于生成输出与参考目标之间的差异迭代优化潜空间控制信号，无需训练、模型无关，可无缝集成到现有扩散管线中。在ID保持、姿态控制和深度控制任务上，相比计算量匹配的开环基线，人脸相似度提升最高25.36%，姿态误差降低27.71%，深度一致性误差降低28.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控图像生成方法（如ControlNet、IP-Adapter）本质上是开环系统，控制信号以严格前馈方式注入，缺乏主动反馈和纠错机制，无法保证对参考条件的严格忠实。作者希望将经典控制理论中的闭环反馈思想引入生成模型，实现参考一致性的动态优化。

Method: 将预训练生成模型视为被控对象（control plant），设计传感器-控制器架构。传感器提取生成输出与参考目标之间的差异（误差信号），控制器采用改进的PID算法，基于误差信号迭代更新潜空间控制信号（如ControlNet的条件嵌入或IP-Adapter的image prompt embedding）。整个过程在测试时进行，无需额外训练，可即插即用于现有扩散模型。

Result: 在ID保持、姿态控制和深度控制三个任务上，相比计算量匹配的开环基线，该方法在面部相似度上提升最高25.36%，姿态对齐误差降低27.71%，深度一致性误差降低28.50%。

Conclusion: 本文证明了可控图像生成可以有效地作为动态反馈系统来管理，将经典控制理论的严谨原理引入生成模型优化。该框架训练免费、模型无关，为提升参考条件生成的一致性提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种测试时迭代优化框架，通过PID控制算法在推理阶段闭环优化潜空间控制信号，以提升参考条件（ID保持、姿态控制、深度控制）下图像生成的一致性。这属于可控图像生成/编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel closed-loop control for reference-consistent generation, but outside priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous models or world models. Signals: test-time optimization, PID control, controllable generation, diffusion model, training-free

Abstract: While controllable image generation has made significant strides by incorporating visual reference conditions, existing methods predominantly operate as open-loop systems. They inject control signals in a strictly feed-forward manner, failing to guarantee strict fidelity to the reference due to the absence of active feedback and error correction mechanisms. To address this fundamental limitation, we propose a novel test-time iterative optimization framework that reformulates reference-consistent generation as a closed-loop dynamic tracking problem. By treating the pre-trained generative model as a control plant, our framework employs a sensor-controller architecture driven by a modified Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) algorithm. This mechanism iteratively optimizes the latent control signals at test time based on the sensed discrepancy between the generated output and the reference target. Notably, this approach is entirely training-free, model-agnostic, and integrates seamlessly around existing diffusion pipelines. Extensive evaluations across ID-preserving, pose-controlled, and depth-controlled generation tasks validate the universality of our method. Empirical results demonstrate improvements over computation-matched open-loop baselines, achieving relative performance gains of up to 25.36\% for facial similarity, alongside spatial error reductions of up to 27.71\% for pose alignment and 28.50\% for depth consistency. More broadly, this work offers a new conceptual perspective: it demonstrates that controllable generation can be effectively managed as a dynamic feedback system, bringing the rigorous principles of classical control theory into the optimization of generative models. Code is available at .

</details>


### [99] [When Does High-CFG Diffusion Inversion Fail? A Controlled Study of Prompt--Latent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04731)
*Yan Zeng, Yusuke Hosoya, Huyen T. T. Tran, Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了高CFG（classifier-free guidance）扩散反演失败的原因。通过可控的生成-反演-重建实验，发现提示词可分为三类：易提示（多数潜变量可重建）、难提示（多数失败）和中间提示（成败取决于提示-潜变量配对）。提出了"提示压力"（prompt pressure）指标衡量CFG对去噪轨迹的影响，并设计了一种轨迹一致性干预方法，通过局部放松不稳定反演步的引导来改善重建和Prompt-to-Prompt编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散反演是图像编辑的核心步骤，实践中常使用比生成/编辑时更低的CFG尺度进行反演，这种不匹配虽然有效但缺乏理论解释。作者希望回答一个基本问题：当目标图像由高CFG轨迹生成时，该轨迹何时可以被成功反演？

Method: 在可控的生成-反演-重建设置下，使用现有扩散编辑基准中的提示词，在高CFG下生成图像并用定点反演（fixed-point inversion）重建。定义了"提示压力"（prompt pressure）作为逐步度量，衡量CFG使去噪更新偏离无条件轨迹的强度。通过分析提示压力与重建质量的相关性，以及文本侧分析（视觉主体、措辞对反演难度的影响），最后设计了一种轨迹一致性干预方法，仅在局部不稳定的反演步放松引导。

Result: 揭示了三种提示词重建行为：易提示（多数潜变量可重建）、难提示（多数失败）和中间提示（成败取决于提示-潜变量配对）。总提示压力与重建质量相关，能区分易/难提示，但无法解释中间提示的成功/失败。文本分析表明主要视觉主体和措辞会影响反演难度。提出的轨迹一致性干预方法在受控设置下改善了重建和Prompt-to-Prompt编辑效果。

Conclusion: 高CFG反演失败需要局部的、轨迹感知的分析，而非全局的CFG尺度降低。提示压力是一个有用的分析工具，但不足以完全预测反演成败。局部放松不稳定反演步的干预策略是有效的，支持了需要细粒度轨迹分析的观点。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本引导的扩散模型反演（diffusion inversion）在图像编辑中的应用，核心关注高CFG尺度下的反演失败原因及Prompt-to-Prompt编辑的改进，属于图像编辑方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion inversion failure analysis, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: diffusion inversion, high CFG, prompt-latent interaction, image editing, trajectory analysis

Abstract: Text-guided diffusion inversion is central to image editing, where an image is mapped to an initial latent and then edited by replaying the denoising process under a modified prompt. In practice, however, inversion is often performed with a lower classifier-free guidance(CFG) scale than the one used for generation or editing. This mismatch is empirically useful but leaves a basic question unresolved: when a target image is generated by a high-CFG trajectory, when can that trajectory actually be inverted? We study this question in a controlled generation--inversion--reconstruction setting, where the true initial latent and denoising trajectory are known. Using prompts taken from an existing diffusion-editing benchmark, we generate images under high CFG and reconstruct them with fixed-point inversion using the same prompt and guidance setting. The results reveal three types of prompt-level reconstruction behavior: easy prompts that reconstruct for most initial latents, hard prompts that fail for most initial latents, and intermediate prompts whose success depends on the prompt--latent pairing. To analyze the generation side, we define prompt pressure, a step-wise measure of how strongly CFG moves the denoising update away from the unconditional trajectory. Total pressure correlates with reconstruction quality and separates easy from hard prompts, but it does not explain the success or failure of intermediate prompt--latent pairs. Text-side analyses further show that the main visual subject and wording can change inversion difficulty. Finally, we evaluate a compact trajectory-consistency intervention that relaxes guidance only at locally unstable inverse steps. This diagnostic check improves reconstruction and Prompt-to-Prompt editing in our controlled setting, supporting the view that high-CFG inversion failure requires local, trajectory-aware analysis.

</details>

<details>

Result: 大量定性和定量实验表明，EquiEdit 在时序一致性和可编辑性方面均表现优异，同时对输入视频具有很好的保真度。

Conclusion: EquiEdit 有效平衡了视频编辑中时序一致性与可编辑性的矛盾，为高质量文本引导视频编辑提供了新方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出文本引导视频编辑框架 EquiEdit，属于视频编辑方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing (图像与视频编辑)" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Video editing is a niche subtopic not in your priority list, and the paper lacks strong signals for your top-weighted continuous language/multimodal or world model directions. Signals: video editing, diffusion models, temporal consistency, Mamba, Fourier noise injection

Abstract: Recently, diffusion models have achieved considerable success in the text-guided video editing domain. However, existing works often struggle to balance the trade-off between temporal consistency and editability in video editing, with consistency and editability typically being inversely related. To address this, we propose a high-quality video editing framework enhanced for consistency and editability, named EquiEdit, which improves coordinatively the temporal consistency and editability of the edited videos while achieving a balance between the two. In terms of temporal consistency, the proposed temporal Mamba module with a tailored temporal-aware scanning scans fused video sequences following four designed directions, effectively enhancing the inter-frame consistency of edited videos. For editability, we design a noise injection strategy based on the spectral transformation to increase editing flexibility, where the Fourier transform is used to preserve the hidden structure in the initial latent noise used for editing, ensuring inter-frame consistency of the edited video and fidelity to the input video. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in terms of temporal consistency and editability, as well as its great fidelity to the input video itself.

</details>


### [101] [FlowMark: Mask-Guided Video Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05261)
*Vishal Asnani, Shruti Agarwal, John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (56.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowMark 是一个基于自动预测掩码引导的视频水印框架，通过端到端训练的 Mask Predictor 网络自动识别最优水印嵌入区域，结合区域感知编码和噪声增强训练，在保持高感知质量的同时抵抗压缩、几何变换、时序编辑及社交媒体再编码，实现 128-bit 消息嵌入，PSNR 达 50.08 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频水印方法要么需要用户手动提供掩码引导区域，要么缺乏对视频时序编辑（如帧交换、插入、删除）的鲁棒性，且难以在社交媒体分发管道中保持水印可恢复性。FlowMark 旨在实现全自动、内容自适应的视频水印嵌入，同时兼顾鲁棒性和感知质量。

Method: FlowMark 包含三个核心组件：1) Mask Predictor 网络，自动学习预测水印嵌入的最优区域掩码；2) 区域感知编码器，将掩码信息融入水印嵌入过程；3) 噪声增强训练，模拟压缩、几何变换、时序编辑等攻击，提升鲁棒性。整体架构端到端可训练，水印信号与视频自然动态保持一致，消除感知闪烁。

Result: 在图像和视频数据集上，FlowMark 可嵌入 128-bit 消息，PSNR 高达 50.08 dB。在压缩、几何变换、内容变化下保持鲁棒性，对帧交换、插入、删除、重采样、插值等时序编辑以及 YouTube/Facebook 再编码均能可靠恢复水印。

Conclusion: FlowMark 是一种全自动、内容自适应的视频水印框架，通过掩码预测实现区域感知嵌入，在感知质量、鲁棒性和时序编辑抵抗方面均优于现有方法，适用于内容溯源、时序真实性验证和视频完整性保护等场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视频水印框架 FlowMark，属于视频内容保护与编辑范畴。视频水印可视为一种视频编辑/保护技术，与 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）的主题高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Watermarking is a niche application with low alignment to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: video watermarking, mask-guided, robustness, compression, temporal edits

Abstract: We present FlowMark, a video watermarking framework guided by automatically predicted object masks. In contrast to prior region-based approaches that require user-supplied mask guidance, FlowMark learns to identify optimal regions for watermark embedding through a dedicated Mask Predictor network. Our end-to-end trainable architecture combines region-aware encoding with noise-augmented training to ensure robustness against compression, geometric transformations, and content variation, while preserving high perceptual quality. Our content-adaptive masking keeps watermark signals coherent with natural video dynamics, effectively eliminating perceptual flicker. Beyond compression robustness, FlowMark maintains reliable watermark recovery under video-native temporal edits (e.g., frame swap, insertion, deletion, resampling, and interpolation) and real-world social media distribution pipelines (e.g., YouTube and Facebook re-encoding). Experimental results on both image and video datasets show that FlowMark reliably embeds $128$-bit messages with up to $50.08$ dB PSNR, offering strong performance for content provenance, temporal authenticity verification, and video integrity protection.

</details>


### [102] [Geometric Reciprocity: Unlocking Self-Supervision for Stereoscopic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05354)
*Jingyi Lu, Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个自监督单目到立体视频转换框架，通过几何互易定理（GRT）实现从单目视频中学习立体视频生成，无需立体配对数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目到立体转换方法中，基于DIBR的立体修复是关键瓶颈。有监督方法依赖稀缺的立体配对数据或存在域差距的合成数据，限制了实际应用。本文旨在利用无限的单目视频进行自监督学习，解决立体修复中的数据依赖问题。

Method: 提出几何互易定理（GRT）：在最近邻DIBR公式下，合成目标视图时的遮挡掩码等于从目标视图反向扭曲回源视图时丢失的像素掩码，从而可直接从单目图像解析计算测试时的遮挡掩码。基于此实现训练-测试一致的循环一致性自监督学习框架，利用无限单目视频进行训练。

Result: 在多个基准上，该方法显著优于无训练和有监督的SOTA方法，展示了自监督学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 几何互易定理为单目到立体转换提供了自监督学习的新范式，消除了对立体配对数据的依赖，为大规模立体视频生成开辟了新途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究单目到立体视频的转换，核心是立体视频生成中的遮挡区域修复（stereo inpainting of disocclusions），属于视频编辑/修复范畴。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑" (visual_editing)。

Importance reason: Novel self-supervised framework for stereoscopic video generation, but outside user's core priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: self-supervised learning, stereoscopic video generation, geometric reciprocity theorem, monocular-to-stereo conversion, no autoregressive or diffusion focus

Abstract: Monocular-to-stereo conversion synthesizes stereoscopic content from 2D videos for immersive 3D experiences. In modern Depth-Image-Based Rendering (DIBR) approaches, stereo inpainting of disocclusions is the critical bottleneck. Training-based methods achieve superior quality but rely on scarce stereo pairs or synthetic data with domain gaps. We address this through the first self-supervised framework learning from monocular videos via cycle consistency. Our key contribution is the Geometric Reciprocity Theorem (GRT): under the nearest-neighbor DIBR formulation, the disocclusion mask when synthesizing a target view equals the mask of pixels lost when warping back from target to source, enabling analytical computation of test-time disocclusion masks directly from monocular images. This yields train-test consistency for the stated warping formulation, supporting self-supervised learning from unlimited monocular videos and substantial improvements over training-free and supervised state-of-the-art methods. Project page:

</details>

### [103] [EmoteGPT: 3D Human Facial Expressions from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02674)
*Haoran Wang, Mohit Mendiratta, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (69.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EmoteGPT 提出从自然语言描述生成3D面部表情的方法，通过引入 Txt2Emote 基准数据集和基于 MLLM 的 <Expr> token 机制，将文本映射到3DMM参数空间，实现细粒度的表情控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D人脸生成方法将身份、表情和纹理联合生成，难以对表情进行细粒度控制。缺乏从详细语言描述到精确表情参数的配对数据是主要瓶颈。

Method: 将文本驱动的表情合成建模为3DMM解耦参数空间中的回归问题。构建 Txt2Emote 基准数据集（含显式/隐式文本描述），提出 EmoteGPT 框架，基于 MLLM 引入专用 <Expr> token 语义化表情表征，再解码为3DMM参数，并利用大规模图像到3DMM数据增强训练。

Result: 在情感识别指标和感知表现力上超越现有文本到3D人脸合成方法，可集成到虚拟人管线中实现逼真/风格化3D化身及3D一致的2D人脸合成。

Conclusion: EmoteGPT 通过解耦表情参数空间和专用 MLLM token 机制，有效实现了从文本到3D面部表情的精细控制，为虚拟人交互提供了新方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从自然语言描述生成3D面部表情，属于虚拟数字人/面部动画范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D facial expression generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: 3D facial expression, text-to-3D, MLLM token, benchmark dataset, avatar pipeline

Abstract: Precise control of 3D facial expressions from text is crucial for virtual avatars, animation, and human-computer interaction, yet existing text-to-3D methods jointly generate identity, expression, and texture, making fine-grained expression control difficult. We instead formulate text-driven expression synthesis as a regression problem in the disentangled parameter space of a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM). This setting, however, requires paired data linking detailed language to precise expression parameters, which are missing from existing resources. To fill this gap, we introduce Txt2Emote, a benchmark of diverse 3D facial expressions with fine-grained textual annotations obtained from GPT-4o and a high-fidelity face tracker, providing both explicit descriptions detailing facial features and implicit descriptions referencing the situational context behind the expression. Leveraging this dataset, we present EmoteGPT, a text-to-3D expression framework based on a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) with a dedicated <Expr> token to semantically ground expression representations, which are then decoded into 3DMM parameters. We further improve EmoteGPT by augmenting training with large-scale image-to-3DMM data, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art text-to-3D face synthesis methods on emotion recognition metrics and in perceived expressiveness. Integrated into avatar pipelines, our method enables photorealistic and stylized 3D avatars, as well as expressive 3D-consistent 2D face synthesis from textual input.

</details>


### [104] [Conversational Human Audio-visual Talking Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02799)
*Junhao Song, Lluis Guasch, Xilin He, Zhongyu Yang, Yingfang Yuan, Weicheng Xie, Linlin Shen, Haijun Lin, Shizhe Liu, Wei Pang, Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出CHAT框架，从单一文本提示生成双人交互式音视频对话片段，结合大语言模型与说话人脸模型及交互式音频/面部行为精炼模块，生成内容多样、身份各异且相互响应的对话片段。合成的CHAT-AVD-50k数据集可作为下游交互式头部生成的预训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模双人交互式音视频对话数据集是开发类人交互虚拟代理和数字人的基础资源，但采集此类数据耗时、昂贵且涉及伦理敏感问题。现有方法无法生成双人相互响应的对话片段。

Method: CHAT框架包含：(1) 大语言模型生成对话脚本；(2) 说话人脸模型生成面部动画；(3) 交互式音频精炼模块和面部行为精炼模块，确保双人对话的相互响应性和对齐性。整体流程从单一文本提示出发，生成成对的、相互响应的语音-面部对话片段。

Result: 在客观和主观评估上均优于现有相关方法。合成的CHAT-AVD-50k数据集作为预训练数据，能持续提升PerFRDiff和ReactDiff在REACT 2024上的性能。

Conclusion: CHAT提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，避免了真实双人交互数据采集的高成本和伦理敏感问题，为数字人和虚拟代理研究提供了有效的数据生成工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出对话式音视频对话生成框架CHAT，生成双人交互的说话面部对话片段，属于虚拟人/说话头/面部重演/肖像动画范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "avatar_motion_generation (数字人与动作生成)"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dyadic audio-visual dialogue generation, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous language/multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal generation, digital human, talking face, dialogue generation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large-scale dyadic interactive audio-visual dialogue (DIAD) datasets provide fundamental data resources for developing humanoid interactive virtual agents and digital humans. However, collecting such data is time-consuming, expensive, and ethically sensitive. To address this, we propose CHAT, a new dyadic interactive audio-visual dialogue generation (DIADG) framework that generates diverse, paired, and mutually responsive speech-face dialogue clips from a single textual prompt. CHAT unifies large language models and talking face models with interactive audio and facial behaviour refinement modules, enabling the generation of aligned dyadic dialogue clips with diverse contents and facial identities. Experiments show that CHAT outperforms existing related methods designed for similar tasks under both objective and subjective evaluations. Moreover, our synthesised CHAT-AVD-50k dataset serves as effective pre-training data for downstream interactive head generation, consistently improving PerFRDiff and ReactDiff on REACT 2024. CHAT offers a scalable alternative to the costly and ethically sensitive collection of real dyadic interaction data.

</details>


### [105] [How to Build Digital Humans? From Priors to Photorealistic Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04341)
*Wojciech Zielonka, Tobias Kirschstein, Timo Bolkart, Simon Giebenhain, Vanessa Sklyarova, Xiang Deng, Donglai Xiang, Shunsuke Saito, Yebin Liu, Matthias Niessner, Justus Thies*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR, eess.IV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于可控3D数字人创建的综述报告，系统梳理了从先验学习到个性化虚拟人创建再到动画驱动的完整流程，对全身和头部虚拟人的表示方法、分层分解（手部、头发、衣物）以及底层原理进行了分类总结，并讨论了开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D数字人创建是计算机视觉与图形学的重要研究方向，但该领域发展迅速、方法众多，缺乏系统性的分类与梳理。本文旨在为研究者提供一个清晰的分类体系，帮助新人快速入门，并指出当前方法的共性与未解决的挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，首先定义了当前3D虚拟人的表示方式（如参数化模型、神经场、3D高斯等），然后提出一个多轴分类法，按身体区域（全身、头部）和所用先验（数据驱动先验、物理先验等）对现有工作进行分类，并进一步分解为手部、头发、衣物等分层表示。文章还梳理了先验学习、个性化创建和动画驱动三个阶段的典型方法。

Result: 本文提供了一个全面的3D数字人创建分类体系，总结了全身和头部虚拟人的主流方法，归纳了分层表示（手、头发、衣物）的独立建模策略，并指出了当前方法的局限性（如泛化性、物理一致性、实时性等）。

Conclusion: 3D数字人创建已取得显著进展，但仍面临数据获取成本高、细粒度控制不足、物理仿真与外观建模分离等挑战。未来方向包括更高效的先验学习、更精细的分层建模、以及端到端的统一创建流程。

Classification reason: 该论文是一篇关于可控3D数字人生成的综述，涵盖全身和头部虚拟人创建、先验学习、个性化建模等，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: A comprehensive survey on 3D avatar creation, but it is a review paper and not directly aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Survey paper, 3D avatar creation, Prior learning, Photorealistic avatars, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: This state-of-the-art report provides an overview of controllable 3D human avatar creation. We describe current 3D avatar systems, which typically consist of three stages: (i) learning priors of human appearance and motion, (ii) creating a personalized avatar, and (iii) animating the avatar. To limit the scope, we focus on the prior learning and avatar creation stages. We define current avatar representations and introduce a taxonomy that categorizes existing work along multiple axes, including body regions and employed priors. We review methods for full-body and head avatars, as well as layered representations that decompose the body into components such as hands, hair, and garments. Finally, we outline common underlying principles, reference key literature for newcomers, and discuss open challenges and future research directions.

</details>

### [106] [PhysMirror: Physics-Aware Mirror Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03470)
*Xuan-Bach Mai, Duy-Phuc Nguyen, Quoc-Van Le, Tam V. Nguyen, Thanh-Toan Do, Huu Le, Duong-Van Nguyen, Minh-Triet Tran, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (69.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhysMirror 是一个物理感知的镜面反射图像生成框架，通过将提示物体提升为 3D 网格并构建精确的镜面场景，提取深度图和分割图作为条件信号引导扩散模型，生成符合投影几何约束的镜面反射图像，并提出了无参考的物理正确性度量 MCS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型在生成镜面反射时难以满足严格的几何约束，常产生幻觉（如反射方向错误、几何不一致），限制了其在具身AI和机器人感知合成训练数据中的应用。因此需要一种物理感知的生成方法来保证反射的几何正确性。

Method: PhysMirror 框架包含三个步骤：(1) 将提示中的物体自动提升为 3D 网格，在模拟环境中构建精确的镜面场景；(2) 渲染该 3D 场景，提取深度图和分割图等 2D 条件信号；(3) 将这些条件信号输入下游扩散模型，引导其生成物理正确的镜面反射图像。同时提出 Mirror Consistency Score (MCS)，利用密集特征匹配和消失点收敛性量化物理正确性。

Result: 在构建的 MirrOB 数据集上，PhysMirror 在反射准确性和物理真实性方面优于现有 SOTA 基线，同时保持了良好的文图语义对齐。

Conclusion: PhysMirror 通过显式 3D 几何先验有效解决了扩散模型生成镜面反射时的几何不一致问题，为具身AI数据生成提供了可靠的管线，MCS 指标也为物理正确性评估提供了自动化方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PhysMirror，利用 3D 空间先验引导扩散模型生成物理正确的镜面反射图像，属于文本到图像生成中的可控生成方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散。

Importance reason: Focuses on mirror reflection generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: physics-aware generation, mirror reflection, diffusion model conditioning, 3D spatial priors, no autoregressive or continuous token focus

Abstract: Synthesizing physically accurate mirror reflections remains a fundamental challenge for modern text-to-image diffusion models, which are increasingly critical for generating synthetic training data for embodied AI and robotic perception. These models typically struggle with strict geometric constraints, leading to hallucinations that degrade the utility of the synthetic data. To address this, we introduce a novel, end-to-end physics-aware generation framework namely PhysMirror that natively enforces projective geometry through explicit 3D spatial priors. Our method automatically lifts prompted objects into 3D meshes and constructs a lightweight, mathematically exact mirror scene within a simulated environment. By rendering this explicit 3D scene, we extract precise 2D conditioning elements, such as depth maps and segmentation maps, that serve as robust guiding signals for downstream diffusion models, guiding them to generate images with physically correct mirror reflections. Moreover, we introduce Mirror Consistency Score (MCS), reference-free, fully automated metric that quantifies physical correctness using dense feature matching and vanishing point convergence. Experimental results on our newly constructed MirrOB dataset demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in reflection accuracy and physical realism, while maintaining strong text-to-image semantic alignment, providing a reliable pipeline for embodied AI data generation. The source code is released at .

</details>


### [107] [Learning to Generate Multiple Objects from Dense and Occluded Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03488)
*Bach-Hoang Ngo, Si-Tri Ngo, Hieu Le, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (70.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像扩散模型在密集遮挡场景中无法正确生成物体数量的问题，提出布局感知注意力偏置和模态平衡损失，并构建了 OverlapDepth-45K 基准数据集，显著提升了计数准确率并防止实例合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型在密集场景中生成多个重叠物体时，实例会坍塌为不可区分的结构，尽管视觉上看似合理但物体计数不准确。作者将此问题归因为"实例所有权坍塌"——重叠物体的 token 通过注意力机制自由交互，而严重遮挡的实例因可见区域小导致监督信号弱。

Method: 1) 布局感知注意力偏置（Layout-Aware Attention Biases）：在注意力计算中引入软偏置，促使 token 交互趋向区域一致的分组，抑制跨实例的注意力泄漏。2) 模态平衡损失（Amodal-Balanced Loss）：根据遮挡程度放大被遮挡物体的梯度信号，增强其学习信号。3) 构建 OverlapDepth-45K 基准数据集，包含密集重叠场景及其模态监督标注。

Result: 在 OverlapDepth-45K 及现有基准上，该方法显著提升了物体计数准确率，有效防止了实例合并，同时保持了生成图像质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的布局感知注意力偏置和模态平衡损失有效解决了扩散模型在密集遮挡场景中的实例所有权坍塌问题，为可控多物体生成提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本到图像扩散模型在密集遮挡场景下的多物体生成问题，属于文生图与图像扩散的范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: Addresses a specific counting failure in dense scenes but is narrow in scope and lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics. Signals: layout-aware attention bias, amodal-balanced loss, OverlapDepth-45K benchmark, instance ownership collapse, text-to-image diffusion

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models fail to generate correct object counts in dense scenes, where overlapping instances collapse into indistinguishable structures despite appearing visually plausible. We identify this as instance ownership collapse: tokens from overlapping objects interact freely through attention, while heavily occluded instances receive weak supervision due to their small visible areas. We address this through layout-aware attention biases that softly bias token interactions toward region-consistent grouping and suppress cross-instance leakage, paired with an amodal-balanced loss that amplifies gradients for occluded objects based on their occlusion level. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce OverlapDepth-45K, a benchmark of densely overlapping scenes with amodal supervision. Our approach substantially improves count accuracy and prevents instance merging while preserving image quality. Project page:

</details>
