### [108] [Track the Noise, Move the World:3D-Grounded Motion-Consistent Noise for Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02798)
*Long Vu, Tan Ngo, Animesh Karnewar, Amir Habibian, Binh-Son Hua, Hung Bui, Minh Hoai Nguyen, Phong Nguyen-Ha*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniCaMo 提出一种统一的噪声构造框架，通过在视频扩散模型的输入噪声中嵌入3D点轨迹引导的噪声扭曲和虚拟球面噪声采样，实现对物体运动轨迹和相机视角的联合控制，无需额外适配器或架构修改，仅需轻量 LoRA 微调即可在 Wan 2.1 (14B) 等大模型上取得 SOTA 效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到视频扩散模型在物体运动和相机运动的联合控制上缺乏统一且精确的方法，通常需要额外的控制分支或适配器，限制了生成的可控性和灵活性。

Method: UniCaMo 构建了一个跨视频帧的3D锚定运动一致噪声空间：利用稀疏3D点轨迹将参考帧的高斯噪声沿目标物体轨迹扭曲，同时使用虚拟球面噪声表示为相机运动下新出现的场景区域提供全局一致的噪声值，通过局部轨迹引导噪声扭曲与全局球面噪声采样的结合，在物体运动和视角变化下保持几何与时序一致性。

Result: 在标准可控视频生成基准上，UniCaMo 在视频质量和运动可控性方面均达到 SOTA 结果，且仅需对 Wan 2.1 (14B) 等大模型进行轻量 LoRA 微调。

Conclusion: UniCaMo 证明了仅通过构造输入噪声即可实现视频扩散模型中物体运动与相机运动的统一控制，无需修改模型架构，为可控视频生成提供了一种简洁高效的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniCaMo，通过构造噪声实现视频扩散模型中的物体运动与相机运动联合控制，属于视频扩散模型的可控生成方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散（text_to_video_diffusion）。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video generation via a novel noise-space method, aligning with high-priority video generation and world model subtopics. Signals: controllable video generation, video diffusion, 3D-grounded motion, camera control, LoRA fine-tuning

Abstract: Modern image-and-text-to-video diffusion models can synthesize highly realistic videos by iteratively denoising an initial Gaussian noise tensor conditioned on reference image and text inputs. However, existing approaches still lack precise and unified controllability over both object motion and camera motion within a single generation process. We present UniCaMo, a unified framework that enables simultaneous control of object trajectories and camera viewpoints by directly constructing the input noise of the diffusion model. Specifically, UniCaMo builds a shared 3D-grounded motion-consistent noise space across latent video frames. Sparse 3D point tracks are used to warp the Gaussian noise of the reference frame along desired object trajectories, while a virtual spherical noise representation provides globally consistent noise values for newly revealed scene regions under camera motion. By combining local track-guided noise warping with global sphere-based noise sampling, UniCaMo maintains geometric and temporal consistency under both object movement and viewpoint changes. Because UniCaMo modifies only the input noise, it requires no auxiliary adapters, control branches, or architectural changes to the underlying video diffusion model. With lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on large pretrained video diffusion models, including Wan 2.1 (14B), UniCaMo achieves state-of-the-art results in both video quality and motion controllability on standard controllable video generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [109] [Vidu S1: A Real-Time Interactive Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03118)
*Jintao Zhang, Kai Jiang, Jintao Chen, Xu Wang, Yang Luo, Yuji Wang, Dechuang Chen, Jungang Li, Chengyang Ye, Marco Chen, Hongzhou Zhu, Min Zhao, Yuxuan Jiang, Zhengkun Huang, Chendong Xiang, Kaiwen Zheng, Haoxu Wang, Xiaohang Wang, Qi Jia, Xin Chen, Yimin Chen, Youhe Jiang, Fangcheng Fu, Zhijie Deng, Fan Bao, Jianfei Chen, Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (85.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Vidu S1 是一个支持语音控制数字角色的实时交互式视频生成模型，基于 TurboDiffusion 和 TurboServe 架构，可在消费级 GPU 上以最高 42 FPS 生成 540p 实时视频，支持无限时长、无模糊漂移，并允许用户上传自定义图像和选择不同语音风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型大多为离线生成，缺乏实时交互能力，用户无法在生成过程中通过语音等自然方式控制视频内容。本文旨在构建一个支持实时交互、语音控制、无限时长且视觉质量高的视频生成系统。

Method: Vidu S1 采用 TurboDiffusion 加速扩散采样，结合 TurboServe 推理服务框架实现实时推理。模型支持语音指令作为控制信号，驱动数字角色的动作和表情。系统支持用户上传真实人物、动漫角色和宠物的自定义图像，并选择不同语音音色，实现个性化交互式视频生成。

Result: 在消费级 GPU 上，Vidu S1 能以最高 42 FPS 输出 540p 实时视频，在所有测试指标上达到最佳性能，同时满足实时推理要求。视频生成无模糊、无漂移、无视觉失真，支持无限时长连续生成。

Conclusion: Vidu S1 证明了实时交互式视频生成的可行性，通过语音控制实现了更自然的用户交互方式，为数字角色驱动和个性化视频生成提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出实时交互式视频生成模型 Vidu S1，支持语音控制数字角色、无限时长实时视频生成，属于文生视频与视频扩散方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散"。

Importance reason: Real-time interactive video generation with voice control and infinite length directly advances the user's high-priority video generation and world model directions. Signals: real-time video generation, voice-controlled digital characters, infinite-length video without drift, TurboDiffusion/TurboServe architecture, consumer GPU inference (42 FPS)

Abstract: We introduce Vidu S1, a real-time interactive video generation model supporting voice control of digital characters. Users can control video generation content at any moment through voice instructions. Vidu S1 supports infinite-length real-time video generation without blurring, drift, or visual distortion. Built with TurboDiffusion and TurboServe, Vidu S1 outputs 540p real-time videos at up to 42 FPS on regular consumer GPUs. Users can upload custom images of real people, anime, and pets, and choose different voice tones for personalized experiences. Experiments show that Vidu S1 achieves the best performance across all test metrics while fully meeting real-time inference requirements. A playable online demo is available at .

</details>


### [110] [Flex-Forcing: Towards a Unified Autoregressive and Bidirectional Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03509)
*Xinyin Ma, Julius Berner, Chao Liu, Arash Vahdat, Weili Nie, Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Flex-Forcing 提出一种统一的训练与推理框架，使视频扩散模型能够同时在双向（全局一致性）和自回归（高效流式生成）两种模式下运行，通过灵活的时间轴与去噪步分块机制实现任意顺序、任意时间步的自回归生成，在视频质量和推理速度上均优于固定推理调度的强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法受限于僵化的推理范式：双向扩散模型全局一致性好但推理慢，自回归模型高效但长程一致性差且存在曝光偏差。作者希望统一两种范式的优势，实现灵活、高效且高质量的视频生成。

Method: 提出 Flex-Forcing 框架，核心是联合定义在时间轴和去噪步上的灵活分块机制（flexible chunking）。模型可以：(1) 根据设备预算灵活分块；(2) 跨块进行双向推理以规划全局结构，块内自回归生成帧以实现高效细粒度合成；(3) 支持任意顺序、任意时间步的自回归生成，无需严格因果约束。

Result: 在多个视频生成基准上，Flex-Forcing 在视频质量和长视频稳定性上一致优于固定推理调度的强基线，同时推理速度更快。

Conclusion: Flex-Forcing 成功统一了双向扩散与自回归视频生成范式，为视频扩散模型提供了更灵活、高效的训练与推理框架，在质量和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Flex-Forcing 框架，统一了双向扩散与自回归两种视频生成范式，核心是视频扩散模型。属于多模态生成方向下的文生视频与视频扩散（text_to_video_diffusion）这一规范子主题，该子主题涵盖视频扩散模型方法。

Importance reason: Proposes a unified training and inference framework for video diffusion that bridges bidirectional and autoregressive modes, directly relevant to your priority on efficient and flexible video generation. Signals: Unified autoregressive and bidirectional video diffusion, Flexible chunking over temporal axis and denoising steps, Any-order, any-timestep autoregressive generation, Improves video quality and long-video stability, Faster inference than rigid baselines

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale generative models has substantially advanced video generation, yet existing methods remain constrained by a rigid inference paradigm. Bidirectional diffusion models excel at global coherence and visual fidelity but suffer from slow inference, while autoregressive models offer efficient and streaming generation at the cost of long-range consistency and exposure bias. We introduce Flex-Forcing, a unified training and inference framework that enables a video diffusion model to seamlessly operate under both bidirectional and autoregressive generation regimes. The core idea is a flexible chunking mechanism jointly defined over the temporal axis and denoising steps. This design allows the model to (1) perform flexible chunking according to different device budgets, (2) perform bidirectional inference across chunks for global structure planning, while generating frames autoregressively within each chunk for efficient and fine-grained synthesis, and (3) perform any-order, any-timestep autoregressive generation without the strict causal constraint. Extensive experiments on multiple video generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flex-Forcing achieves consistently better video quality, long-video stability than strong baselines with a rigid inference schedule, while offering faster inference.

</details>


### [111] [Enhancing Video Physical Consistency via Role-aware Joint Training and Modality-decoupled Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04653)
*Guangting Zheng, Haojing Chen, Hao Li, Jingtao Zhang, Zhen Yang, Xiaosong Jia, Xue Yang, Shaofeng Zhang, Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (86.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VPT 框架，通过角色感知信号（将实体分为代理、受控对象、被动对象和背景）和模态解耦去噪策略（视觉与辅助通道独立噪声水平），在保持视觉质量的同时提升视频扩散模型的物理一致性，在 VideoPhy 基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在视觉保真度上表现优异，但难以保持长程物理一致性。传统像素重建目标主要关注外观细节，无法捕捉场景的底层动力学。近期工作引入光流等辅助模态提供物理先验，但存在三个问题：未区分不同实体类型的运动模式、联合建模导致容量冲突削弱预训练视觉先验、辅助模态在推理时累积误差。

Method: 提出 VPT 微调框架：(1) 角色感知信号，将实体分为代理(agent)、受控对象(controlled object)、被动对象(passive object)和背景(background)，对不同物理角色分别建模；(2) 模态解耦去噪策略，视觉通道和辅助通道分配独立噪声水平，结合损失权重衰减，使辅助模态作为软约束而非强依赖，缓解推理时递归预测误差；(3) 跨步自引导(cross-step auto-guidance)进一步增强物理动力学。

Result: 在 VideoPhy 基准上，基于 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 的 VPT 在 SA 指标上相对提升 39.4%，在 PC 指标上相对提升 17.9%，同时在 VideoPhy-2 基准上也取得一致改进，且保持了视觉质量。

Conclusion: VPT 通过角色感知建模和模态解耦去噪有效提升了视频扩散模型的物理一致性，验证了将辅助模态作为软约束而非强依赖的设计思路的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VPT 框架，通过角色感知联合训练和模态解耦去噪提升视频扩散模型的物理一致性，属于视频扩散模型的改进方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散（text_to_video_diffusion）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses video generation physical consistency with role-aware and modality-decoupled denoising, highly relevant to user's priority on video generation and world models. Signals: video diffusion, physical consistency, role-aware, modality-decoupled denoising, world model

Abstract: While modern video diffusion models excel in visual fidelity, maintaining long-range physical consistency remains a formidable challenge. Conventional pixel-reconstruction objectives mainly focus on appearance details and often fail to capture the underlying dynamics of a scene. To mitigate this, recent efforts have integrated auxiliary modalities (e.g., optical flow) to introduce physics priors via joint training with video appearance. However, these methods have three main limitations: (1) they do not distinguish the different motion patterns of different entity types; (2) joint modeling of visual and auxiliary modalities can cause capacity conflicts and weaken the pretrained visual prior; and (3) auxiliary modalities may accumulate errors during inference. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{VPT}, a fine-tuning framework for improving physical consistency in video diffusion models. VPT introduces a role-aware signal that groups entities into agents, controlled objects, passive objects, and background, so that different physical roles can be modeled more clearly. We further propose a modality-decoupled denoising strategy, where the visual and auxiliary channels are assigned independent noise levels. Together with a loss-weight decay strategy, this design makes auxiliary modalities serve as soft constraints rather than strong dependencies, mitigating recursive prediction errors during inference. We also introduce cross-step auto-guidance to further strengthen physical dynamics. Experiments show that VPT improves physical consistency while preserving visual quality, achieving relative gains of 39.4\% in SA and 17.9\% in PC on VideoPhy benchmark over Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, and consistent improvements on VideoPhy-2 benchmark. The project page is available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_generation-efficient_generation'></div>

## 生成模型加速与高效化 [[Back]](#toc)

扩散加速、少步采样、蒸馏、量化、稀疏化等生成模型效率优化。

### [112] [TACG: Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating for Diffusion Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03236)
*Chengcheng Wang, Tingzhang Luo, Wenhao Li, Jianyuan Guo, Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (85.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TACG提出了一种无需训练的轨迹感知提交门控解码器，通过时序隐式logits引导和历史门控机制，在扩散语言模型解码中减少去噪步数并提高生成效率，在代码和数学基准上保持或提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型解码器仅基于当前快照的预测分布提交token，忽略了去噪轨迹中的跨步一致性信息，导致将瞬时不完整上下文下的峰值错误锁定，而真正一致的候选却被延迟。

Method: TACG包含三个组件：1) 时序隐式logits引导（TILG），维护过去logits的指数移动平均作为自参考，在自然参数空间中对比当前logits；2) 历史门控（HG），强制提交前短期提案的持续性；3) 带上限的额外提升预算，形成稳定性约束的提交规则，无需辅助网络或额外前向传播。

Result: 在LLaDA、Dream和LLaDA2-Mini模型上，在HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K、MATH500基准上，TACG通常提升或保持准确率，同时减少去噪步数并提高每前向传播的token数（TPF）。

Conclusion: TACG通过轨迹感知的提交门控有效提升了扩散语言模型的解码效率和质量，验证了利用跨步一致性信息进行token提交决策的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出TACG，一种无需训练的扩散语言模型解码门控方法，通过轨迹感知信号减少去噪步数并提高每前向传播的token数（TPF），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）范畴。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for diffusion language models, a key subtopic, with a training-free method that improves token throughput. Signals: diffusion language model, efficient inference, training-free decoding, trajectory-aware commit gating, code and math benchmarks

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked positions, exposing a trajectory of predictive distributions rather than a single instantaneous belief. Most existing decoders ignore this trajectory and commit tokens from the current snapshot alone, conflating confidence with commitment readiness: a transient top-1 peak under incomplete context can be locked in, while candidates with consistent cross-step support are delayed. We propose Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating (TACG), a training-free gate-level decoder that anchors token identities to the base posterior and uses trajectory-aware signals only to decide whether the current proposal is ready to commit. TACG combines Temporal Implicit Logits Guidance (TILG), which keeps an exponential moving average of past logits as a self-reference and contrasts the current logits against this reference in natural-parameter space, with a History Gate (HG) that enforces short-term proposal persistence before commitment. Together with a capped extra-promotion budget, these components yield a stability-constrained commit rule without auxiliary networks or extra forward passes. We evaluate TACG on LLaDA, Dream, and LLaDA2-Mini across code (HumanEval, MBPP) and math (GSM8K, MATH500) benchmarks; it typically improves or preserves accuracy while reducing denoising steps and increasing tokens per forward (TPF). The code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [113] [RotateAttention: RoPE-Aware Rotation and Range Rectification for INT4 Quantized Attention in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02584)
*Yaofu Liu, Wanli Lan, Jinxi Li, Binhang Yuan, Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RotateAttention 是一个面向带有3D RoPE的DiT视频生成模型的混合精度INT4 FlashAttention框架，通过RoPE感知的旋转矩阵和范围优化的P量化，在保持生成质量的同时实现最高1.68倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT视频生成模型中注意力机制是主要计算瓶颈，现有低比特量化方法未解决两个关键问题：(1) 在线旋转矩阵与RoPE难以兼容；(2) 非负注意力矩阵P的对称量化浪费了INT4一半的动态范围。

Method: 提出RotateAttention框架，包含两项核心技术：(1) RoPE-aware Rotation——使用可合并到RoPE中的旋转矩阵或低开销矩阵来缓解RoPE引起的Q/K异常值；(2) Range-optimized P Quantization——使用固定尺度和零点充分利用INT4数值范围。采用选择性FP16回退策略，对精度敏感的注意力块和去噪步骤保留高精度。

Result: 在保持与全精度基线几乎相同的视频生成质量的同时，实现了最高1.68倍端到端加速和2.2倍kernel级加速。

Conclusion: RotateAttention有效解决了DiT视频生成模型中3D RoPE带来的量化挑战，为视频生成模型的硬件加速提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向DiT视频生成模型的INT4量化FlashAttention框架，核心是加速视频生成中的注意力计算，属于生成模型效率优化，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for video generation DiTs, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel quantization method. Signals: video generation acceleration, INT4 quantization, 3D RoPE, DiT efficiency, mixed-precision attention

Abstract: In \textbf{DiT-based video generation models equipped with 3D Rotary Position Embeddings (3D RoPE)}, the attention mechanism remains a primary computational bottleneck due to its quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length. While quantized \textbf{FlashAttention} offers a promising path toward hardware acceleration, existing low-bit quantization methods overlook two critical challenges in this setting: \textbf{1)} applying online rotation matrices -- a widely used technique for mitigating outliers in Queries ($Q$) and Keys ($K$) -- is difficult to reconcile with \textbf{RoPE}; and \textbf{2)} the non-negative attention matrix $P = \exp(QK - \max(QK))$ makes symmetric quantization waste half of the 4-bit dynamic range. In this work, we observe that the outlier distributions of $Q$ and $K$ are strongly affected by the dimensional partitioning of \textbf{3D RoPE}. Based on this finding, we propose \textbf{RotateAttention}, an efficient \textbf{mixed-precision INT4 FlashAttention} framework tailored for \textbf{DiT-based video generation models with 3D RoPE}, using selective \textbf{FP16 fallback} for accuracy-sensitive attention blocks and denoising steps. RotateAttention introduces two core techniques: \textbf{1) RoPE-aware Rotation}, which employs either mergeable rotation matrices that can be fused into RoPE or negligible-overhead matrices to mitigate RoPE-induced outliers in $Q$ and $K$; and \textbf{2) Range-optimized $P$ Quantization}, which uses fixed scales and zero-points to fully exploit the \textbf{INT4 numerical range} with minimal computational overhead. Experiments show that \textbf{RotateAttention} preserves video generation quality nearly identical to full-precision baselines while achieving up to 1.68$\times$ end-to-end speedup and 2.2$\times$ kernel-level acceleration.

</details>


### [114] [When Geometry Aligns: Dihedral Hidden-State Transformations in UNet, ViT, and DiT Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03580)
*Mojtaba Faramarzi, Alex Lamb, Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (77.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究扩散架构（UNet、ViT、DiT）中隐藏状态的二面体群反射变换，发现几何一致的变换能提升特征稳定性，而不一致的变换会导致架构特定的可预测失败模式，并提出了SCS、AMS、Drift等激活级诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散架构（UNet、ViT、DiT）已被广泛使用，但结构化几何扰动（如反射变换）对中间隐藏状态的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统性的理解。

Method: 提出统一框架，将二面体群的反射元素作为受控内部干预应用于中间隐藏状态，对比几何一致与不一致的变体。使用Self-Consistency Shift (SCS)、Activation Mass Scatter (AMS)、Drift等激活级诊断指标分析特征稳定性。在Stable Diffusion 2.1 UNet上评估7种干预模式，并用FID、KID、CLIP score、LPIPS多样性等图像级指标补充验证。

Result: 几何一致的变换能提升特征稳定性，不一致的变换会导致架构特定的可预测失败模式。在SD 2.1 UNet上的实验验证了该结论，ViT和DiT的辅助分析也支持这一发现。

Conclusion: 几何一致性是空间结构视觉与扩散模型中稳定隐藏状态干预的关键原则，为理解扩散架构的内部工作机制提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 论文研究扩散架构（UNet、ViT、DiT）中隐藏状态的几何变换（二面体群反射）对特征稳定性和生成质量的影响，属于扩散模型内部机制分析与稳定性优化，与"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）这一规范主题相关，因为该主题涵盖扩散模型效率与稳定性优化。

Importance reason: Focuses on geometric consistency in diffusion architectures but lacks direct application to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Dihedral group analysis, UNet/ViT/DiT architectures, activation diagnostics (SCS, AMS, Drift), Stable Diffusion 2.1, geometric consistency principle

Abstract: Diffusion architectures now encompass convolutional UNets as well as transformer-based designs such as Diffusion Transformers (DiTs), inspired by Vision Transformers (ViTs), yet the effects of structured geometric perturbations within these architectures remain poorly understood. We study this question through a unified framework that applies reflection-based elements of the dihedral group to intermediate hidden states as controlled internal interventions, contrasting geometrically consistent and inconsistent variants. Using activation-level diagnostics, including Self-Consistency Shift (SCS), Activation Mass Scatter (AMS), and Drift, we analyze feature stability and geometric drift. We find that consistent transformations improve stability, while inconsistent ones induce predictable, architecture-specific failures. In the main Stable Diffusion 2.1 U-Net study, we evaluate seven intervention modes over three seeds and complement the internal diagnostics with image-level FID, KID, CLIP score, and LPIPS diversity. Taken together with supporting ViT and controlled DiT analyses, these results establish geometric consistency as a key principle for stable hidden-state interventions in spatially structured vision and diffusion models.

</details>


### [115] [Awakening Diffusion Transformers: Eliciting Stronger Generation and Understanding via Massive Activation Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02968)
*Chaofan Gan, Zicheng Zhao, Yuanpeng Tu, Xi Chen, Ziran Qin, Tieyuan Chen, Supavadee Aramvith, Mehrtash Harandi, Weiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (83.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Diffusion Transformer (DiT)中的Massive Activations (MAs)结构，发现它们分布在图像token的空间维度上但集中在少量固定特征维度，且主要由去噪时间步而非文本条件调制。基于此发现，提出无需训练的EMA框架：生成方面通过MA驱动的Detail Guidance (DG)抑制MA维度构建细节缺失的反事实预测以引导更精细的细节生成，并支持高效部分前向推理；理解方面通过MA-modulated REPresentation extraction (MREP)利用预训练AdaLN通道调制减少MA方向主导性，提升密集特征判别能力。实验表明EMA能一致提升DiT的生成质量和表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Massive Activations (MAs)在Transformer模型中广泛存在，但它们在Diffusion Transformer (DiT)中的结构和功能角色尚未被充分理解。本文旨在系统分析DiT中MAs的结构特性，并探索如何利用MAs作为统一调制信号来同时提升DiT的生成和表征能力。

Method: 1) 系统分析DiT中MAs的空间分布和特征维度集中性，发现其与AdaLN残差缩放因子紧密对齐，主要由去噪时间步调制。2) 提出EMA框架，包含两个组件：a) Detail Guidance (DG)：抑制MA维度构建细节缺失的反事实预测，引导采样朝向更精细的视觉细节，支持高效部分前向推理、与无分类器引导集成以及token级局部DG；b) MREP：利用预训练AdaLN通道调制减少MA方向主导性，拼接空间归一化的MA图以保留有用空间结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，EMA框架在多个DiT模型上一致提升了生成质量（更精细的细节合成）和表征能力（改善密集特征判别性），且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了DiT中Massive Activations的结构特性及其任务依赖的双重效应，并证明通过EMA框架可以无需训练地利用MAs作为统一调制信号，同时增强DiT的生成和表征能力，为理解和优化DiT提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究Diffusion Transformer (DiT)中的Massive Activations结构，并提出无需训练的EMA框架来同时提升生成质量和表征能力。生成方面提出MA-driven Detail Guidance (DG)支持高效部分前向推理，属于生成模型效率优化；理解方面提出MREP提升DiT的密集特征判别能力，也涉及多模态理解。因此匹配到efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）和vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and generation quality in diffusion transformers, a key subtopic with moderate weight. Signals: diffusion transformer, efficient inference, training-free modulation, generation quality improvement, representation enhancement

Abstract: Massive Activations (MAs) have been widely observed in Transformer-based models, yet their structure and functional roles in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we systematically analyze MAs in representative DiTs and find that they are spatially distributed across image tokens while concentrated in a small set of fixed feature dimensions. We further show that these dimensions are closely aligned with AdaLN residual scaling factors and are primarily modulated by the denoising timestep rather than text conditions. This structure leads to two task-dependent effects: for generation, MAs are critical for fine-grained detail synthesis while having limited influence on global semantics; for understanding, their shared high-magnitude directions make raw DiT features overly similar across spatial tokens and weaken dense feature discrimination. Based on these findings, we introduce Eliciting Massive Activation (EMA), a training-free framework that leverages Massive Activations (MAs) as a unified modulation signal to improve both generative and representational capabilities of DiTs. For generation, EMA proposes MA-driven Detail G}uidance (DG), which suppresses MA dimensions to construct a detail-deficient counterfactual prediction and guides sampling toward finer visual details. DG further supports efficient partial-forward inference, integration with classifier-free guidance, and token-level Local DG for refining selected image regions. For understanding, EMA introduces MA-modulated REPresentation extraction (MREP), which uses pretrained AdaLN channel-wise modulation to reduce MA directional dominance and concatenates spatially normalized MA maps to preserve useful spatial structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMA consistently improves both the generation quality and representation capability of DiTs.

</details>


### [116] [HyperVAttention: Efficient Sparse Attention with Spatio-Temporal Clustering for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03012)
*Dongyeun Lee, Amir Zandieh, Vahab Mirrokni, Junmo Kim, Insu Han*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HyperVAttention 是一种训练无关的稀疏注意力框架，通过 3D 局部窗口聚类和硬件感知聚类合并，解决视频扩散 Transformer 中自注意力的二次复杂度问题，实现高达 2.13 倍的端到端加速，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer（VDiT）在高质量视频生成中表现出色，但自注意力机制的二次复杂度严重限制了生成长视频的能力。现有的基于聚类的稀疏注意力方法虽然改善了质量-速度权衡，但面临聚类开销大和 GPU 利用率低两个瓶颈。

Method: 提出 HyperVAttention (HVA)，包含三个核心组件：(1) 3D 局部窗口聚类，利用视频 token 的时空局部性将质心搜索限制在固定局部邻域内，并通过自定义 Triton 核高效执行；(2) 混合聚类策略，仅在锚定步骤进行完整聚类，中间步骤只更新子集 token，利用去噪步骤间聚类分配的时序稳定性；(3) 硬件感知聚类合并，通过并行凝聚合并最小化 CTA 对齐的执行成本，利用空闲 tile 容量提高块密度和近似保真度。

Result: 在文生视频任务上，HVA 建立了训练无关稀疏注意力在视频扩散中的新帕累托前沿，端到端延迟降低高达 2.13 倍，同时相比现有训练无关稀疏注意力基线提升了生成保真度。

Conclusion: HVA 通过联合解决聚类开销和 GPU 利用率两个瓶颈，实现了视频扩散中高效且高质量的稀疏注意力，为生成长视频提供了实用的加速方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HyperVAttention，一种面向视频扩散 Transformer 的训练无关稀疏注意力框架，通过 3D 局部窗口聚类和硬件感知聚类合并来加速自注意力计算，显著降低端到端延迟。这属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for video diffusion transformers, a high-priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's boost keywords and direction weights. Signals: video diffusion transformer, efficient inference, training-free sparse attention, 2.13x speedup, hardware-aware clustering

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (VDiTs) have demonstrated significant capabilities in high-fidelity video generation. However, their ability to produce long-duration videos is fundamentally constrained by the quadratic complexity of the self-attention mechanism. Recent clustering-based sparse attention methods improve the quality-speed trade-off by grouping semantically similar tokens, but their practical efficiency remains limited by two bottlenecks: substantial clustering overhead and low CTA utilization caused by irregular cluster-induced blocks. We propose HyperVAttention (HVA), a training-free sparse attention framework that addresses both bottlenecks jointly. To reduce clustering overhead, we introduce 3D local-window clustering, which exploits the spatio-temporal locality of video tokens to restrict centroid search to fixed local neighborhoods, and implement it with a custom Triton kernel for efficient execution. We further propose a hybrid clustering strategy that performs full clustering only at anchor steps and updates only subset tokens at intermediate steps, leveraging the temporal stability of cluster assignments across denoising steps. To improve CTA utilization, we present hardware-aware cluster merging that minimizes CTA-aligned execution cost through parallel agglomerative merging, improving block density and approximation fidelity by utilizing idle tile capacity. Together, these components reduce clustering overhead, avoid redundant updates, and better align sparse attention with the fixed tile structure of modern GPU kernels. Experiments on Text-to-Video generation show that HVA establishes a new Pareto frontier for training-free sparse attention in video diffusion, reducing end-to-end latency by up to $2.13\times$ while improving fidelity over existing training-free sparse attention baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Tensor-Train Joint Modeling for Few-Step Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03788)
*Byoungkwon Kim, Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (62.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于张量分解（CPD和TTD）显式建模离散扩散中条件联合分布的框架，通过轻量微调预训练MDM，显著提升少步生成质量，解决了离散扩散模型因条件独立性假设导致的并行化偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在少步生成场景下，由于条件独立性假设引入的系统性并行化偏差会随每步去掩码token数量增加而严重累积，限制了其快速生成的能力。现有方法无法显式建模条件联合分布，亟需一种可控表达力的联合建模框架。

Method: 提出基于张量分解的框架，用低秩张量（CPD和TTD）表示条件干净分布，实现可控表达力的显式联合分布建模。利用Oseledets定理揭示TTD对邻近token依赖的结构性偏好，适用于序列数据。设计迭代边际推理过程，支持预定义位置调度，并通过轻量微调集成到预训练MDM中。

Result: 在自然语言和分子数据（SMILES行记法）上，该方法在少步生成场景下显著优于基线离散扩散模型，且微调成本远低于从头训练。

Conclusion: 张量分解为离散扩散模型提供了一种有效的联合分布建模方式，TTD的结构性偏差天然适合序列数据，为离散扩散实现快速高质量生成开辟了新路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于张量分解的离散扩散模型少步生成方法，核心贡献是加速离散扩散模型的生成过程（few-step generation），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Proposes tensor-train joint modeling to fix discrete diffusion's few-step generation bias, relevant to efficient inference and continuous token modeling. Signals: discrete diffusion acceleration, tensor decomposition for joint distribution, few-step generation improvement, lightweight fine-tuning of pretrained MDMs, structural bias of TTD for sequential data

Abstract: Discrete diffusion promises orders-of-magnitude faster generation than autoregressive (AR) models for sequential discrete data, yet its full potential of few-step generation has remained out of reach due to a fundamental structural limitation. The conditional-independence assumption underlying current discrete diffusion models introduces a systematic parallelization bias that compounds with the number of tokens unmasked per step, becoming severe in the few-step regime that fast generation requires. We address this with the first framework for explicit joint distribution modeling in discrete diffusion via tensor decomposition, which represents the conditional clean distribution as a low-rank tensor with controllable expressivity. The framework supports both Canonical Polyadic (CPD) and Tensor-Train (TTD) decompositions, and we identify a structural bias of TTD toward dependencies between nearby tokens, formalized through Oseledets' theorem relating TT-rank to unfolding-matrix rank, which is well-suited to sequential data such as natural language and line notations for molecular data. To enable efficient generation, we present an iterative marginal inference procedure with specialization for predetermined position schedules. Our framework integrates into pretrained MDMs through lightweight fine-tuning, yielding substantial improvements in few-step generation at a fraction of the cost of training from scratch.

</details>


### [118] [Perceptual Flow Matching for Few-Step Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03524)
*Chuyang Zhao, Yifei Song, Hongfa Wang, Jianlong Yuan, Yuan Zhang, Siming Fu, Zhineng Chen, Huilin Deng, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 提出 Perceptual Flow Matching (PFM)，在预训练感知模型的特征空间中进行 flow matching 监督，替代传统 VAE 潜空间的速度回归，将采样步数从 35-50 步降至 4-8 步，无需教师模型或辅助分数网络，在图像生成、视频生成和图像编辑任务上均取得高质量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 flow matching 模型需要大量采样步数（35-50步）才能生成高质量结果，而现有的加速和蒸馏方法需要教师模型或辅助分数网络，增加了训练复杂度。作者希望找到一种简单有效的方法，在不引入额外模型的情况下实现少步生成。

Method: PFM 的核心思想是将 flow matching 的监督信号从 VAE 潜空间（L2 损失）替换为预训练感知模型（如 LPIPS、DINOv2）的特征空间中的感知损失。这种感知监督将回归的极小值从均值搜索（mean-seeking）转变为模态搜索（mode-seeking），使预测偏向于流形上的模态，从而在粗粒度的少步积分下仍保持准确性。PFM 可直接集成到标准 flow matching 训练流程中，无需教师模型或辅助分数网络。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 图像生成上，PFM 仅用 4 步即可达到与 50 步标准 flow matching 相当的质量；在视频生成和图像编辑任务上，PFM 在 4-8 步内生成的结果质量优于现有蒸馏方法，且伪影更少。

Conclusion: 标准 flow matching 训练在合适的表征空间中进行监督时，可以自然地产生高质量的少步生成器。感知监督将回归目标从均值搜索转变为模态搜索，这是少步生成成功的关键。希望这一发现能启发未来对表征感知目标函数的研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Perceptual Flow Matching (PFM)，通过感知特征空间监督 flow matching 实现少步生成（4-8步），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances few-step flow matching for efficient generation, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and continuous multimodal models. Signals: Few-step flow matching, perceptual feature space supervision, reduces sampling steps 35-50 to 4-8, no teacher model needed, improves image/video generation and editing

Abstract: We propose Perceptual Flow Matching (PFM), a simple yet effective framework for few-step generation in flow-matching models. Rather than performing velocity regression in the conventional VAE latent space, PFM supervises flow matching in a perceptual feature space using pretrained perceptual models. This simple change substantially improves the few-step generation capability of flow-matching models, reducing the number of sampling steps from 35-50 to 4-8 while preserving generation quality. Unlike existing acceleration and distillation approaches, PFM requires neither teacher models nor auxiliary score networks and can be integrated into standard flow-matching training pipelines with minimal modifications. Extensive experiments on image generation, video generation, and image editing tasks demonstrate that PFM consistently produces high-quality results while producing fewer artifacts than existing distillation-based methods. We further show that perceptual supervision shifts the regression minimizer from mean-seeking to mode-seeking, biasing predictions toward on-manifold modes that remain accurate under coarse few-step integration. Our results reveal that standard flow-matching training can naturally yield high-quality few-step generators when supervised in an appropriate representation space. We hope this insight inspires future research into representation-aware objectives for efficient generative modeling.

</details>


### [119] [CineMobile: On-Device Image-to-Video Diffusion for Cinematic Camera Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03803)
*Xuyao Huang, Zelai Deng, Xu Wang, Xizhong Xiao, Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CineMobile 提出了一种面向移动设备的图像到视频扩散模型优化方法，通过蒸馏引导剪枝、扩散蒸馏+强化学习四步生成、混合后训练量化，将模型压缩至1GB以下，在保持视觉质量的同时实现40倍加速，可在移动端生成49帧480p视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上图像到视频生成的需求日益增长，尤其是子弹时间、推拉变焦等电影级运镜效果。然而，Diffusion Transformer（DiT）参数量大、多步迭代去噪计算开销高，难以在移动端高效部署。现有方法缺乏针对移动端图像到视频生成的系统优化方案。

Method: CineMobile 采用三阶段优化策略：(1) 蒸馏引导剪枝（distillation-guided pruning），从 Wan 2.1 架构的教师模型中剪枝得到紧凑模型，保留关键视频生成能力；(2) 结合扩散蒸馏与强化学习将压缩模型优化为4步生成器；(3) 混合后训练量化策略将模型压缩至1GB以下。

Result: 相比教师模型（Wan 2.1），CineMobile 实现40倍加速，同时保持可比的视觉质量。在 NVIDIA H200 GPU 上每步去噪延迟0.6秒，在 MediaTek Dimensity 8400 Ultimate 5G 平台上每步20秒，峰值内存1.8GB，可生成49帧480p视频。

Conclusion: CineMobile 证明了通过剪枝、蒸馏和量化的联合优化，可以将图像到视频扩散模型高效部署到移动设备上，实现电影级运镜效果生成，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于将图像到视频扩散模型通过蒸馏、剪枝和量化压缩到移动端可部署，属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and on-device video generation, aligning with model efficiency and video generation priorities. Signals: On-device video generation, Diffusion Transformer acceleration, Distillation-guided pruning, 4-step generation, Hybrid quantization

Abstract: The growing demand for image-to-video creation on mobile devices has increasingly focused on cinematic motion effects like bullet time, dolly zoom, slow motion, etc. While Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) exhibit strong performance in video generation, their large parameter sizes and multi-step iterative denoising processes lead to substantial computational overhead, making efficient generation on mobile devices challenging. We propose CineMobile to bridge the gap. In particular, CineMobile adopts a three-fold optimization strategy: (1) leveraging a distillation-guided pruning approach to derive a compact yet efficient model that retains the essential video generation capabilities required for cinematic effects; (2) optimizing the compressed model into a 4-step generator via a combination of diffusion distillation and reinforcement learning; (3) employing a hybrid post-training quantization strategy to compress the model footprint to under 1 GB. Experimental results show that compared to the teacher model with the Wan 2.1 architecture, CineMobile achieves a 40x speedup in generation while maintaining comparable visual quality. Specifically, CineMobile generates 49-frame 480p videos with a per-step denoising latency of 0.6s on an NVIDIA H200 GPU and 20s on the MediaTek Dimensity 8400 Ultimate 5G platform, with a peak memory usage of 1.8 GB, demonstrating its practical applicability for mobile-based image-to-video creation.

</details>


### [120] [EMPURPLE: A Free Lunch for Diffusion Distillation based on the Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04276)
*Zilai Li, Lujia Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文分析了扩散蒸馏模型FID退化的原因，通过PAC泛化界指出早期步骤速度场重定向导致蒸馏目标难以学习、训练-测试差距扩大。提出EMPURPLE，一种无需训练的中间潜变量回收方法，在DMD2、Hyper-SD等多个蒸馏模型上提升FID 7%-20%。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过PAC式泛化界分析蒸馏模型FID退化的原因：早期步骤速度场重定向使蒸馏目标更难学习，导致训练-测试分布不匹配。提出EMPURPLE方法，在推理时回收原始模型（未蒸馏）的中间潜变量，替换蒸馏模型对应步骤的输入，无需额外训练。

Result: EMPURPLE在DMD2、Hyper-SD、FlashSD、SDXL-Lightning等多个蒸馏模型上，FID提升7%-20%，且模型无关、无需训练。

Conclusion: 扩散蒸馏的FID退化源于早期步骤速度场重定向导致的分布不匹配，EMPURPLE通过回收原始模型中间潜变量可有效缓解该问题，是一种简单通用的改进策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型的蒸馏加速（diffusion distillation），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，具体关注蒸馏后FID退化问题的分析与改进。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-free fix for diffusion distillation FID degradation, directly improving model efficiency and generation quality. Signals: diffusion distillation, FID improvement, training-free method, model efficiency, generative model acceleration

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve impressive image-generation quality but remain expensive at inference time. Diffusion distillation reduces sampling steps, yet many distilled models, including SDXL-Lightning and distribution matching distillation methods, suffer from degraded Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). We analyze this phenomenon through a PAC-style generalization bound. Our analysis suggests that aggressive early-step redirection of the velocity field makes the distillation target harder to learn, enlarging the train-test gap. As a result, early-step output distributions differ between training and inference, causing distribution mismatch in the intermediate noisy latent used as next-step inputs. We empirically validate this mechanism by showing reduced diversity in both intermediate features and final outputs. To address this issue, we propose EMPURPLE, a simple training-free method that recycles intermediate latents sampled from the original model. EMPURPLE is model-agnostic and improves FID by 7\% to 20\% across DMD2, Hyper-SD, FlashSD, and SDXL-Lightning. The repo is:

### [121] [Flash-BoN: Instant Drafts for Inference-Time Scaling in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04461)
*Ruchit Rawal, Reza Shirkavand, Sayak Paul, Yuxin Wen, Heng Huang, Yizheng Chen, Tom Goldstein, Gowthami Somepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Flash-BoN 提出一种面向扩散模型推理时扩展的高效策略：结合时间步截断、层跳过和激活代理三种加速手段，生成大量廉价草稿候选，再通过多阶段验证选出最优候选进行完整质量精炼。实验表明，在固定墙钟时间预算下，Flash-BoN 在多个基准和模型规模上一致优于现有方法，且与提示优化等正交技术互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法（如引导搜索）关注在去噪过程中何时以及如何频繁地进行验证，但将生成本身的成本视为固定不变。此外，标准评估方式仅计数去噪前向传播次数（NFEs），忽略了验证器的开销，导致效率排名失真。作者发现，在墙钟时间评估下，简单的 Best-of-N 采样已能与多种引导搜索方法匹敌或更优，表明计算资源更适合用于更广泛的探索而非重复的中间验证。这启发作者设计一种能生成大量廉价草稿候选的高效策略。

Method: Flash-BoN 将三种互补的加速手段（时间步截断、层跳过、激活代理）组合成一个统一的配置，针对每个模型优化一次。该配置用于生成大量低成本的草稿候选图像。然后通过一个高效的多阶段验证流程（使用轻量级奖励模型）从草稿池中筛选出最有潜力的候选，最后对该候选进行完整质量的精炼去噪。该方法在固定墙钟时间预算下运行，不增加额外计算开销。

Result: 在三个基准和三种模型规模上，Flash-BoN 在固定墙钟预算下一致优于所有基线方法，且在大模型上增益更大（AUC 提升 +8%）。与基于反思的提示优化等正交技术结合，可进一步提升性能（AUC 提升 +16%）。增益与候选多样性正相关，且草稿引导的选择还能加速 RL 后训练收敛。

Conclusion: Flash-BoN 证明了在扩散模型推理时扩展中，通过生成大量廉价草稿候选并进行高效验证，可以在固定墙钟时间预算下显著提升生成质量。该方法简单有效，与现有正交技术兼容，且在大规模模型上优势更明显。研究还揭示了候选多样性与性能提升之间的正相关关系。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Flash-BoN，通过时间步截断、层跳过和激活代理等加速手段生成廉价草稿候选，再经多阶段验证选择最优候选进行完整质量精炼，属于扩散模型推理效率优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and inference-time scaling for diffusion models, aligning with high-priority subtopics on acceleration and generation. Signals: efficient inference, diffusion model acceleration, inference-time scaling, candidate diversity, wall-clock evaluation

Abstract: Inference-time scaling for text-to-image generation has progressed from simple Best-of-$N$ (BoN) sampling to guided search methods that verify and steer candidate trajectories at intermediate denoising steps. These approaches focus on when and how often to verify during denoising but largely treat the cost of generation itself as fixed. Moreover, the standard practice of comparing methods by number of function evaluations (NFEs) counts only denoising forward passes and ignores verifier overhead, which can distort efficiency rankings. We show that under wall-clock evaluation, simple BoN already matches or outperforms several guided search techniques, suggesting that compute is better spent on broader exploration than on repeated intermediate verification. This motivates Flash-BoN, which generates a large pool of inexpensive draft candidates by combining three complementary acceleration knobs: timestep truncation, layer skipping, and activation proxies into a single configuration optimized once per model. An efficient multi-stage verification procedure then identifies the most promising draft, which is refined at full quality. Across three benchmarks and three model scales, Flash-BoN consistently outperforms all baselines under fixed wall-clock budgets, with gains that grow at larger model scales (+8% AUC). We further show that our strategy combines well and improves existing orthogonal techniques such as reflection-based prompt optimization (+16% AUC). The gains correlate with increased candidate diversity, which also enables draft-guided selection to accelerate RL post-training convergence.

</details>

### [122] [Efficient bias mitigation in T2I diffusion models using Concept Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03397)
*Mansi, Avinash Kori, Francesco Leofante*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (70.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出CO-ALIGN，一种基于概念图对齐的T2I扩散模型偏见缓解方法，通过在文本编码器和去噪器内部对齐概念本体，在减少偏见的同时保持生成质量，并提升概念遗忘鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型常从训练数据中继承有害偏见，而现有偏见缓解方法要么仅干预文本编码器，要么提供推理时引导，往往导致生成结果语义不连贯。需要一种更有效的偏见缓解方法，既能大幅减少偏见，又能保持生成完整性。

Method: 提出CO-ALIGN（Concept Ontology Alignment），基于概念图对齐的偏见缓解方法。该方法作用于模型内部的概念本体（concept ontology），通过在文本编码器和去噪器中对齐概念来实现偏见缓解。支持三种范式：文本编码器本体对齐、去噪器本体对齐、以及联合文本-去噪器本体对齐。

Result: CO-ALIGN在公平性上提升30%，ΔFID=11.4（图像质量提升），图像保真度提升2.8%，语义不连贯输出减少88%。此外，更好的内部本体对齐还能增强概念遗忘（concept unlearning）的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 概念图对齐是一种有效的T2I扩散模型偏见缓解方法，能在大幅减少偏见的同时保持生成质量，并且对下游任务（如概念遗忘）也有积极影响。

Classification reason: 该论文研究T2I扩散模型中的偏见缓解（bias mitigation），属于生成模型的对齐与可控性问题。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖对齐、偏好优化、可控性等，偏见缓解是其中的重要子方向，因此映射到此已有主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on bias mitigation in T2I diffusion, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Bias mitigation, Concept graph alignment, T2I diffusion, Fairness improvement, Concept unlearning

Abstract: Text-to-Image diffusion models often propagate harmful bias inherited from the training data. Existing bias mitigation techniques typically intervene only at the text encoder or provide inference-time guidance, often leading to generations that collapse into semantically incoherent outputs. To address these limitations, we introduce CO-ALIGN (Concept Ontology Alignment), a novel bias mitigation approach based on concept-graph alignment that operates on the model's internal concept ontology. By aligning concepts within the text encoder and denoiser, CO-ALIGN achieves substantial bias reduction while preserving generative integrity. We demonstrate the effectiveness of concept-graph alignment across three paradigms: text-encoders, denoisers and joint text-denoiser ontology alignment. CO-ALIGN outperforms the state of the art, improving fairness by $30\%$, $\Delta FID=11.4$ in image quality, $2.8\%$ in image fidelity, all while reducing semantically incoherent outputs by $88\%$. Beyond bias mitigation, we show that CO-ALIGN benefits other downstream tasks as well. In particular, our experiments demonstrate that better-aligned internal ontologies enhance concept unlearning robustness across multiple unlearning techniques.

</details>


### [123] [BiSLW: Bi-Spectral Latent Watermarking for Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02643)
*Aryan Pandit*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (46.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BiSLW 提出一种双谱潜空间水印框架，利用扩散模型潜空间的频率结构，在低频（全局语义）和高频（细粒度纹理）两个互补频带中联合嵌入对齐的身份信号，实现高保真度与强鲁棒性的水印嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉内容合成中广泛应用，但面临未经授权使用和缺乏可靠归属方法的问题。现有水印技术要么将潜张量视为静态空间特征图，要么依赖像素域修改，未能显式利用潜空间的内部频率结构进行双频带冗余嵌入，因此易受扩散随机性和再生攻击的影响。

Method: BiSLW 利用扩散模型解码潜空间的固有频率结构，设计双频带水印框架：低频分量编码全局语义，高频分量捕获细粒度纹理。水印通过可学习的编码器独立注入两个频带，重组后解码；双谱解码器从每个频带恢复水印，并通过跨频带一致性约束强制语义与纹理嵌入对齐。

Result: 相比先前的潜扩散水印方法，BiSLW 在 PSNR 上提升超过 3 dB，在强再生攻击和常见失真下保持近乎完美的比特准确率，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: BiSLW 通过显式利用扩散潜空间的频率结构进行双频带冗余水印嵌入，在感知保真度和鲁棒性之间取得了良好的平衡，为扩散模型的归属保护提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出扩散模型的潜空间水印方法，属于生成模型的可控性与归属对齐问题，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment），该 subtopic 涵盖 guidance、可控性等主题，水印作为归属控制的一种形式与之相关。

Importance reason: Watermarking for diffusion models is a niche security topic, not aligned with user's priority on generative modeling and world models. Signals: diffusion model watermarking, latent frequency watermark, dual-band embedding, robustness focus, not autoregressive or video/image generation

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have transformed visual content synthesis, yet they remain vulnerable to unauthorized usage and lack reliable attribution methods. Existing watermarking techniques often treat latent tensors as static spatial feature maps or depend on pixel-domain modification, and most do not explicitly leverage the internal frequency structure of the latent space for dual-band redundant embedding, leaving them susceptible to the stochastic nature of diffusion and regeneration attacks. We introduce BiSLW, a trainable bi-spectral latent watermarking framework that jointly embeds aligned identity signals across complementary spectral bands of the decoded diffusion latent using learned encoders and decoders, going beyond fixed-pattern frequency approaches. We leverage the inherent frequency structure of diffusion latents to design a dual-band watermarking framework. Low-frequency components encode global semantics, while high-frequency components capture fine texture. We exploit this structure to embed watermarks across complementary spectral bands. The watermark is independently injected into both bands via learned encoders and recombined before decoding, ensuring it becomes intrinsic to the generative trajectory. Dual spectral decoders recover the watermark from each band, while a cross-band consistency constraint enforces alignment between semantic and textural embeddings. Experiments show that BiSLW achieves a strong balance between perceptual fidelity and robustness, improving PSNR by over 3 dB compared to prior latent diffusion watermarking methods while preserving near-perfect bit accuracy under aggressive regeneration and common distortions, all with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [124] [CONFLUX: A Latent Diusion Model for 3D Chest-CT Synthesis with RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02998)
*Max Van Puyvelde, Halil Ibrahim Gulluk, Wim Van Criekinge, Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (39.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CONFLUX 是一个用于3D胸部CT合成的潜扩散模型，采用3D VAE压缩体积、整流流Transformer在潜空间生成，并通过结构化放射学元数据（18种异常发现、性别、年龄等）进行条件控制。进一步使用在线强化学习（GRPO）后训练，以分类器对生成体积中临床发现的恢复可靠性为奖励，显著提升了条件控制的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控的3D医学图像生成模型可以合成具有指定临床属性的体积数据，但现有方法在高保真度、原生3D生成和对条件控制的忠实性方面存在不足。作者希望构建一个既能生成高质量3D CT图像，又能精确控制临床特征（如异常发现）的生成模型。

Method: CONFLUX 包含两个阶段：(1) 预训练阶段：训练3D VAE压缩CT体积，使用整流流（Rectified Flow）Transformer在潜空间生成，通过自适应层归一化（AdaLN）对结构化放射学元数据（18种异常发现、性别、年龄、重建核）进行条件控制。(2) RL后训练阶段：采用在线强化学习（Group Relative Policy Optimization, GRPO），以预训练分类器对生成体积中临床发现的恢复可靠性作为奖励信号，优化生成模型的条件控制忠实度。

Result: CONFLUX 在三平面FID上达到32.3，显著优于MAISI的74.6。RL后训练后，由独立分类器评估，条件控制的忠实度提升了47%（相对于真实扫描可靠性的差距减少了47%）。作者还发布了模型和一个约20万张合成胸部CT数据集。

Conclusion: CONFLUX 展示了潜扩散模型结合RL后训练在3D医学图像生成中的有效性，能够同时实现高保真度和精确的临床条件控制。RL后训练是提升生成模型条件忠实度的有效手段，为可控医学图像生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出3D医学图像潜扩散模型CONFLUX，核心贡献之一是使用在线强化学习（GRPO）后训练来增强生成模型对临床条件（异常发现）的控制能力，属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic 'generation_alignment'（生成模型对齐与奖励）。

Importance reason: Medical domain and negative keywords (clinical, drug discovery) heavily penalize relevance despite strong technical methodology. Signals: 3D latent diffusion, rectified-flow transformer, RL post-training (GRPO) for conditioning fidelity, structured radiological metadata conditioning, 3D VAE for CT volumes

Abstract: Controllable generative models of 3D medical images can synthesize volumes with specified clinical attributes, but this demands samples that are simultaneously high-fidelity, natively 3D, and faithful to the requested conditioning. We present CONFLUX, a latent diffusion model for chest computed tomography (CT): a 3D variational autoencoder compresses each volume, and a rectified-flow transformer generates in the latent space. Generation is conditioned on structured radiological metadata (18 abnormality findings, sex, age, and reconstruction kernel) through adaptive layer normalization. The model leads strong volumetric baselines on tri-planar Frechet distance (FID 32.3 vs. 74.6 for MAISI) while exposing direct control over clinical attributes. To strengthen that control we add an online reinforcement-learning post-training stage (group-relative policy optimization) that rewards how reliably a classifier recovers the requested findings from each generated volume. Judged by a separate, independent classifier, post-training removes 47% of the shortfall relative to real-scan reliability. We release the model and a ~200k synthetic chest-CT dataset with conditioning metadata spanning a wide variety of clinical findings.

</details>


### [125] [FairFlow: Demystifying and Mitigating Stereotype Bias in Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03180)
*Chen Chen, Yuanmin Huang, Zhenfei Zhang, Mi Zhang, Xiaohan Zhang, Yun Xiong, Xiaoyu You, Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (82.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过机制分析揭示了MM-DiT中刻板偏见在特定语义枢纽层的阶段式传播规律，并提出FairFlow方法，通过在稀疏的语义枢纽层注入属性特定的公平方向向量，在不增加推理开销的前提下有效缓解性别、种族和交叉偏见，实现公平性与保真度的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）已成为文生图的主流架构，但在良性提示下仍系统性表现出严重的刻板偏见，存在算法歧视风险。现有偏见缓解方法主要针对传统U-Net架构设计，不适用于MM-DiT，因此亟需针对MM-DiT架构的偏见缓解方案。

Method: 首先通过机制分析发现MM-DiT中偏见表征并非均匀分布，而是由稀疏的"语义枢纽层"介导，呈现阶段式传播：早期枢纽建立易受偏见影响的结构模板，中期枢纽从文本条件中提取核心刻板概念，晚期枢纽通过视觉自注意力全局固化偏见。基于此，提出FairFlow框架，学习属性特定的公平方向向量，仅在识别出的语义枢纽层和受限推理窗口内注入这些方向，实现稀疏引导的内部调节。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Stable Diffusion 3上的实验表明，FairFlow在性别、种族和交叉场景下有效中和了刻板偏见，实现了最优的公平性-保真度平衡，且推理开销近乎为零，对复杂提示具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FairFlow提供了一种轻量级、实用的偏见缓解方案，适用于大规模部署的MM-DiT系统，为扩散Transformer的对齐与公平性研究提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生图扩散Transformer中的刻板偏见缓解，属于生成模型的对齐与可控性问题。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖对齐、偏好优化、可控性等，与此论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong mechanistic analysis of bias in MM-DiTs, but topic is niche for your focus on continuous models and generation. Signals: MM-DiT bias analysis, FairFlow mitigation, gender/race fairness, FLUX.1-dev, Stable Diffusion 3

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion transformers (MM-DiTs) have emerged as the prevalent backbone for modern text-to-image generation systems. However, they exhibit critical alignment vulnerabilities, systematically manifesting severe stereotype biases even under benign prompts. This poses a significant risk of algorithmic discrimination in deployed systems. Since most existing mitigation strategies were tailored for legacy U-Net architectures, the precise remediation of these vulnerabilities in MM-DiTs remains a critical open challenge. In this work, we first investigate the root cause of this vulnerability via mechanistic analysis. We reveal that bias representations in MM-DiTs are not uniformly distributed across depth, but are mediated by a sparse set of layers functioning as internal semantic binding hubs. These hubs exhibit a stage-wise propagation driving bias manifestation: early hubs establish the structural templates susceptible to bias, middle hubs actively extract core stereotypical concepts from textual conditioning, and late hubs globally solidify these biases through visual self-attention. Leveraging these architectural insights, we propose FairFlow, an intrinsic, mechanism-guided mitigation framework. FairFlow acts as an internal regulator by employing sparse steering: it learns attribute-specific fair directions and injects them exclusively at the identified semantic hubs within a constrained inference window. Evaluations on FLUX.1-dev and Stable Diffusion~3 demonstrate that FairFlow effectively neutralizes these stereotypical vulnerabilities across gender, race, and intersectional settings, achieving an optimal fairness-fidelity balance. With near-zero inference overhead and robustness to complex prompts, FairFlow provides a lightweight and practical bias mitigation for large-scale deployed MM-DiT systems. Code and datasets will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [126] [Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03770)
*Hyunsoo Kim, Jungmyung Wi, Soobin Um, Donghyun Kim, Suhyun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (77.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MiPO（Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization），通过 minority preference rewards 微调扩散模型，改善扩散分类器在数据流形低密度区域的感知能力，仅使用任意文本数据、LoRA 和 GRPO，无需额外图像数据或外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散分类器在零样本分类中表现良好，但其性能严重依赖于预训练数据分布：在多数、高密度区域表现好，在少数、低密度区域准确率显著下降。现有 minority sampling 工作主要关注生成更多 minority 图像，但 minority sampling 对感知能力的提升作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 MiPO 框架：使用任意文本数据生成候选样本，通过 minority preference rewards 奖励更好覆盖 minority 区域的样本，利用 LoRA 和 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 优化扩散模型，无需额外图像数据、外部基础模型或外部奖励模型。

Result: 在五个标准数据集上评估，MiPO 实现了稳定、prompt 自适应的 minority 采样，将低密度生成覆盖转化为改进的零样本扩散分类性能，有效缓解了扩散分类器对 majority 区域的偏好偏差。

Conclusion: 揭示了扩散分类器感知偏向 majority 区域的问题，证明了通过 minority preference optimization 可以缓解这一偏差，MiPO 为提升扩散模型在低密度区域的分类能力提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MiPO，使用 minority preference rewards 和 GRPO 对扩散模型进行微调以改善分类性能，属于生成模型的对齐与偏好优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion classifier improvement via minority sampling, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: diffusion classifier, minority preference optimization, zero-shot classification, LoRA fine-tuning, GRPO

Abstract: Prior studies have demonstrated that diffusion classifiers achieve robust zero-shot classification performance. However, their effectiveness is strongly tied to the pretraining data distribution: they perform well in majority, high-density regions of the data manifold, but are significantly less accurate in minority, low-density regions. Although prior works on minority sampling have focused on generating more minority-like images, what minority sampling fundamentally enables beyond generation remains underexplored. In this paper, we reveal a direct relationship between minority sampling in generation and the perception capability of diffusion classifiers. Specifically, we show that enhancing minority sampling broadens the coverage of underrepresented regions on the data manifold, thereby improving diffusion-based recognition. To exploit this connection, we propose \textit{Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization} (MiPO), which fine-tunes a pretrained diffusion model using minority preference rewards. Using only arbitrary caption data, MiPO generates candidate samples, rewards those that better cover minority regions, and optimizes the model with LoRA and Group Relative Policy Optimization, without additional image data, external foundation models, or external reward models. This enables stable, prompt-adaptive minority sampling and translates low-density generative coverage into improved zero-shot diffusion classification. To sum up, we show that diffusion classifier perception is biased toward majority regions, demonstrate that this bias can be alleviated through minority preference optimization, and evaluate MiPO on five standard datasets.

</details>


### [127] [DICT: Data Injection and Contrastive Trajectory Refinement for Conditional Image Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03899)
*Chunnan Shang, Xin Zhang, Zhizhong Wang, Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (71.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DICT，一种无需训练的条件图像生成推理方法，通过数据注入（将噪声扰动后的条件信号融入早期去噪阶段）和对比轨迹优化（相邻去噪状态间的成对比较）来提升扩散模型在风格迁移、超分辨率、去模糊等任务上的生成质量与保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件图像生成方法要么是任务特定设计（泛化性差），要么是训练自由的损失引导（将丰富条件压缩为标量目标，导致信息瓶颈和误差累积）。亟需一个统一、有效且无需训练的条件图像生成框架。

Method: DICT 包含两个核心组件：(1) 数据注入（Data Injection）：将噪声扰动后的条件信号注入早期去噪阶段，通过引导去噪自适应地选择和提取条件中的任务关键信息，保留空间丰富性；(2) 对比轨迹优化（Contrastive Trajectory Refinement）：在相邻去噪状态间进行成对比较，逐步提升样本质量。整个方法无需训练，保持推理简洁性。

Result: 在风格迁移、图像超分辨率和图像去模糊等多个条件图像生成任务上，DICT 在保真度和感知质量上均一致优于代表性的任务特定方法和损失引导基线。

Conclusion: DICT 提供了一种有效的统一条件图像生成推理框架，无需任务特定架构或额外训练，通过数据注入和对比轨迹优化实现了跨任务的良好泛化与性能提升。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种训练自由的推理方法 DICT，通过数据注入和对比轨迹优化来增强扩散模型的条件图像生成，属于条件生成中的 guidance/可控性优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的 guidance 与可控性方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Training-free conditional generation method but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: conditional image generation, training-free inference, diffusion models, data injection, contrastive trajectory refinement

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for conditional image generation, yet existing approaches generally follow two directions: task-specific designs that can improve performance but limit generalization, and training-free loss guidance that compresses rich conditions into scalar objectives and applies stepwise guidance, leading to information bottlenecks and error accumulation along the sampling trajectory. Given the urgent need for an effective unified framework across diverse conditional image generation tasks, we propose Data Injection and Contrastive Trajectory Refinement (DICT), a training-free inference method that enhances conditional image generation without introducing task-dependent architectures. DICT introduces Data Injection, where noise-perturbed conditional signals are integrated into early denoising stages; by performing guided denoising on these injected signals, DICT adaptively selects and distills task-salient information from the raw condition, effectively preserving spatial richness and ensuring precise condition-to-generation alignment. Furthermore, DICT applies Contrastive Trajectory Refinement across adjacent denoising states, enabling pairwise comparisons that progressively improve sample quality. These designs keep inference simple while improving cross-task transfer under a unified diffusion formulation. Extensive experiments on conditional image generation tasks (e.g., style transfer, image super-resolution, and image deblurring) show consistent gains in fidelity and perceptual quality over representative task-specific and loss-guided baselines.

</details>


### [128] [Reward Lightning: Fast Video Generation via Homologous Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03960)
*Jiaxiang Cheng, Bing Ma, Xuhua Ren, Kai Yu, Peng Zhang, Tianxiang Zheng, Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Reward Lightning 提出了一种统一框架，通过在共享潜空间中对视频扩散模型同时进行偏好对齐和蒸馏加速，解决了两个目标在分离表征空间优化时的梯度冲突问题。核心包括潜空间奖励模型（LRM）和同源偏好蒸馏（HPD），仅需1-4步即可生成高质量视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在视频扩散模型中分别优化偏好对齐和蒸馏加速两个目标，但它们在不相交的表征空间上优化，导致改进一个目标往往会损害另一个。作者希望在一个共享表征空间中同时实现两个目标，避免梯度冲突。

Method: 首先引入潜空间奖励模型（LRM），直接在潜空间中对视频进行评分，无需解码回像素空间。然后基于 LRM，提出同源偏好蒸馏（HPD），复用共享骨干网络联合进行对抗蒸馏和偏好对齐，生成少步（1-4步）的视频生成器。

Result: LRM 在偏好准确率上分别超过像素级和潜空间级奖励基线 11.0% 和 14.7%。Reward Lightning 仅需 1-4 步即可生成高保真视频，平均 VBench 分数提升 2.1%，在文本对齐、运动质量和视觉质量方面均领先。

Conclusion: 同源偏好蒸馏能够有效解决视频扩散模型中偏好对齐与蒸馏加速之间的冲突，在极少的采样步数下实现高质量、高对齐的视频生成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Reward Lightning，在视频扩散模型中联合进行偏好对齐（preference alignment）和蒸馏加速（distillation acceleration），核心贡献包括潜空间奖励模型（LRM）和同源偏好蒸馏（HPD）。这属于生成模型的对齐与奖励优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel unified framework for video diffusion alignment and acceleration in shared latent space, directly relevant to user's high-priority video generation and model efficiency subtopics. Signals: video diffusion, few-step generation, preference alignment, latent reward model, distillation acceleration

Abstract: Achieving simultaneous preference alignment and distillation acceleration in video diffusion models remains an open challenge. Existing methods optimize the two objectives over mismatched representation spaces, where improving one objective often compromises the other. To overcome this, we propose Reward Lightning, a unified framework that aligns and accelerates a video diffusion model within a single shared representation. Its central principle is homology: both objectives are evaluated on identical latent features, which mitigates the gradient conflicts that arise when they are optimized over disjoint representations. As a foundational component, we first introduce a latent reward model (LRM) that scores videos directly in the latent space, without decoding back to the pixel space. Building on the LRM, homologous preference distillation (HPD) reuses this shared backbone to perform adversarial distillation and preference alignment jointly, yielding few-step generators that remain faithful and well aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the LRM surpasses pixel-level and latent-level reward baselines by $11.0\%$ and $14.7\%$ in preference accuracy, and that Reward Lightning generates high-fidelity videos in merely $1$ to $4$ steps, improving the average VBench score by $2.1\%$ while leading in text alignment, motion quality, and visual quality. Project page: .

</details>


### [129] [FUSE: FK-Steered Multi-Modal Flow Matching for Efficient Simulation-Based Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05252)
*Weichen Qin, Yufan Xie, Peihao Wang, Chia-Jui Chou, Minghui Du, Peng Xu, Ziren Luo, Yi Yang, Jingyi Yu, Bo Liang, Jiakai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FUSE提出了一种基于Feynman-Kac引导的多模态flow matching方法，用于高效的仿真后验估计（SBI）。它采用双轨架构分别处理参数与观测数据的模态差异，并通过FK引导的采样策略利用中间观测似然来优化生成轨迹，在标准SBI基准和真实系外行星轨道估计任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真后验估计（SBI）在科学发现中至关重要，但现有生成模型方法在多模态建模上存在不足，常采用暴力融合策略忽略参数与观测之间的结构差异，限制了估计精度。

Method: 提出FUSE框架：1）双轨架构（dual-track architecture），分别对参数和观测数据进行独立特征提取并动态交互；2）FK-steered采样策略，利用中间观测似然引导生成轨迹，提升推理阶段的样本质量。

Result: 在标准SBI基准上优于现有方法，生成的后验分布与真实MCMC高度吻合；在真实系外行星轨道估计任务中，成功解决了现有方法难以处理的复杂参数简并问题。

Conclusion: FUSE通过多模态flow matching与FK引导采样，有效提升了SBI的估计精度，展示了在天体物理学等科学发现中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出FUSE，一种基于FK-steered多模态flow matching的仿真后验估计方法，属于生成模型在科学推理中的应用。其核心是flow matching生成框架与采样引导策略，与"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的guidance/可控性主题相关，且flow matching属于生成模型范畴。论文主要关注多模态flow matching的采样引导，而非连续语言模型或世界模型，因此归入多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on simulation-based inference with flow matching, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: flow matching for posterior estimation, dual-track multimodal architecture, FK-guided sampling, exoplanet orbit estimation, not autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) is critical for scientific discovery, with generative models offering a promising path toward efficient inference. However, existing methods struggle with effective multimodal modeling. They often rely on brute-force fusion strategies that ignore the structural disparities between parameters and observations, thus limiting estimation fidelity. In this work, we introduce FUSE (Feynman-Kac steered mUlti-modal flow matching for efficient Simulation-based posterior Estimation). Unlike prior work, FUSE employs a dual-track architecture that preserves the distinct features of multimodal inputs while facilitating dynamic interaction. Additionally, we propose an FK-steered sampling strategy that leverages intermediate observation likelihoods to guide the generative trajectories, effectively improving the sample quality during inference. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors that closely match ground-truth MCMC. Furthermore, in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task, FUSE successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies that challenge existing methods, highlighting its potential to accelerate complex scientific discoveries in astrophysics and beyond.

</details>


### [130] [RADIANCE: Relative Adaptive Denoising with IP-Adapter for Novel Concept Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05088)
*Zi-Xiang Ni, Bo-Lun Huang, Teng-Fang Hsiao, Bo-Kai Ruan, Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (70.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RADIANCE 是一种无需训练的推理框架，通过组合相似性监控（CSM）、双向尺度控制器（BSC）和反馈引导调度器（FGS）三个模块，在去噪过程中动态调整 IP-Adapter 的正负尺度，以改善文生图扩散模型对罕见概念（不寻常属性-物体组合）的生成质量，避免概念遗漏或语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型在处理罕见概念（如不寻常的属性-物体组合）时，常出现概念遗漏或语义漂移问题，即某个主导实体压制了其他概念的生成。作者将这一问题归因于去噪轨迹中缺乏组合平衡性，因此希望提出一种无需训练的推理框架来动态校正生成过程中的组合偏差。

Method: RADIANCE 包含三个核心模块：(1) 组合相似性监控器（CSM），利用 CLIP 在中间潜变量中追踪物体和属性的出现情况；(2) 双向尺度控制器（BSC），通过正负 IP-Adapter 尺度施加类似"恢复力"的校正来重新平衡偏斜的去噪轨迹；(3) 反馈引导调度器（FGS），协调各时间步的更新。针对多物体提示，还提出延迟适配器激活（DAA）和逐层交替引导（LAG）以防止过早的概念融合。通过流水线执行重叠监控和去噪过程以保持较低延迟。

Result: 在 RareBench 和 T2I-CompBench 上的实验表明，RADIANCE 在组合对齐和感知质量上持续优于现有最先进基线方法，同时通过流水线执行保持了有竞争力的推理延迟，显著提升了每样本成功率和有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: RADIANCE 通过将推理过程视为闭环反馈系统，有效改善了文生图扩散模型在罕见概念组合上的生成质量，且无需额外训练。该方法在组合对齐和感知质量上均取得了显著提升，为扩散模型的可控生成提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RADIANCE，一种无需训练的推理框架，通过 CLIP 反馈监控和 IP-Adapter 双向尺度控制来改善文生图扩散模型中的组合对齐（compositional alignment），属于生成模型的可控性与对齐优化，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）。

Importance reason: Novel training-free inference-time adaptation for compositional generation, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous models and autoregressive generation. Signals: training-free inference, IP-Adapter adaptation, compositional generation, diffusion model enhancement, rare concept generation

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved striking progress but still struggle to synthesize rare concepts involving unusual attribute-object pairings, often resulting in concept omission or semantic drift where a dominant entity overwhelms the generation. Tracing these failures to a lack of compositional balance during the denoising trajectory, we propose RADIANCE, a training-free framework that treats inference as a closed-loop feedback process. RADIANCE augments pretrained backbones with three modular components: (1) a Compositional Similarity Monitor (CSM) that tracks the emergence of objects and attributes in intermediate latents via CLIP-based feedback; (2) a Bidirectional Scale Controller (BSC) that applies a reactive "restoring force" using positive and negative IP-Adapter scales to rebalance biased trajectories; and (3) a Feedback Guidance Scheduler (FGS) that coordinates these updates across timesteps without additional training. We further extend the framework to multi-object prompts via Delayed Adapter Activation (DAA) and Layer-wise Alternating Guidance (LAG) to prevent premature concept fusion. By overlapping monitoring and denoising through pipelined execution, RADIANCE maintains competitive latency while significantly enhancing the per-sample success rate and effective throughput. Experiments on RareBench and T2I-CompBench demonstrate that RADIANCE consistently enhances compositional alignment and perceptual quality over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Erasing Without Collateral Damage: Precise Concept Removal in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05274)
*Parth Upman, Nishita Jain, Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (67.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CARE，一种闭式概念擦除算子，通过保留子空间感知的方向替换原始目标方向，在交叉注意力值空间中直接编辑，无需微调即可精确擦除扩散模型中的目标概念，同时最小化对语义相关非目标概念的附带损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的概念擦除方法在移除目标概念时，会错误地移除与目标共享视觉结构的语义相关非目标概念，造成附带损害。作者认为这是因为现有方法将目标身份与共享视觉结构视为同一信号，需要更精确的擦除机制。

Method: CARE 从少量保留概念锚点中计算"保留子空间感知方向"（kept-subspace-aware direction），替代原始的擦除目标方向，在交叉注意力值空间中直接应用闭式编辑。该方法无需模型微调，仅需少量离线计算，通过单一收缩参数控制擦除与保留的权衡。该算子具有最小扰动解释，其投影形式保持保留子空间不变。

Result: 在标准概念擦除协议下，CARE 在实例、风格和名人概念擦除任务中，在保持竞争性擦除效果的同时，比现有方法更忠实地保留了非目标概念，显著减少了附带损害。

Conclusion: CARE 通过保留子空间感知的方向设计，有效解决了训练无关概念擦除中的附带损害问题，为扩散模型的安全可控生成提供了一种精确、高效且无需微调的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出训练无关的概念擦除方法（CARE），用于控制文生图扩散模型中的概念移除，属于生成模型的可控性与对齐范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion model concept erasure, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in autoregressive generation, continuous models, or world models. Signals: Training-free concept erasure, diffusion model editing, cross-attention value space, closed-form operator, minimal collateral damage

Abstract: Training-free concept erasure is an attractive mechanism for controlling text-to-image diffusion models, but precise erasure often comes at the cost of damaging semantically related non-target concepts. Existing value-space methods remove the component of each cross-attention value along the target concept direction, implicitly treating target identity and shared visual structure as the same signal. We argue that this is the source of much of the collateral damage in prior preservation. We introduce CARE, a closed-form concept erasure operator that replaces the raw target direction with a kept-subspace-aware direction computed from a small bank of retained concept anchors. The resulting edit is applied directly in cross-attention value space, requires no model fine-tuning, and adds only a negligible offline computation. A single shrinkage parameter controls the erase-preserve trade-off. We further show that the operator admits a minimum-disturbance interpretation and, in its projection form, leaves the kept subspace invariant. Experiments under the standard concept-erasure protocol show that our method preserves non-target concepts more faithfully while maintaining competitive erasure across instance, style, and celebrity concepts. </details>


### [132] [Optimal Mixture-of-Experts Model Averaging for Conditional Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04360)
*Shijin Gong, Baihua He, Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG, stat.ML

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoEMA（Mixture-of-Experts Model Averaging），一种面向条件生成模型的最优模型平均框架。通过基于样本的最大均值差异（MMD）设计静态权重（StaticMA）和输入自适应权重（MoEMA），并证明了渐近最优性和权重函数一致性。在表格、图像、文本等多种模态上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中常存在多个条件生成模型，其性能随任务、数据或输入条件变化，但缺乏有效的组合方法。现有模型平均方法通常需要可处理的密度函数，而许多生成模型仅能提供条件样本。因此需要一种仅基于样本的、输入自适应的模型平均框架。

Method: 基于条件分布之间的样本级最大均值差异（MMD）构建损失函数。首先提出 StaticMA，为各候选模型分配固定权重；进一步提出 MoEMA，通过 softmax 神经网络门控网络学习输入条件相关的自适应权重。建立了样本内和样本外的渐近最优性理论，并在正则条件下证明了自适应权重函数的一致性。对于非结构化数据（如图像、文本），结合固定表征映射（如预训练编码器）扩展应用。

Result: 在表格数据、图像生成（CIFAR-10、CelebA）和文本生成等多个模态的仿真与真实数据实验中，MoEMA 在多数指标上优于单一最佳模型、简单平均、贝叶斯模型平均等基线方法，展示了输入自适应模型组合的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 MoEMA 框架为条件生成模型提供了一种仅基于样本的、输入自适应的最优组合方法，具有理论保证和广泛的模态适用性，为生成模型的集成与对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出条件生成模型的最优模型平均框架（MoEMA），通过最大均值差异（MMD）和软门控网络实现输入自适应的模型组合，属于生成模型的对齐与组合优化方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中奖励建模、偏好优化、可控性等主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a model averaging framework for conditional generative models, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Model averaging, conditional generative models, MMD-based weighting, asymptotic optimality, tabular, image, text modalities

Abstract: Conditional generative models have emerged as powerful tools for sampling from target conditional distributions, driving substantial advances across a wide range of scientific and applied domains. As these models proliferate, practitioners often face multiple plausible generators whose performance can vary with the task, data, or input condition. We propose an optimal model averaging framework for conditional generative models, allowing candidate generators to be combined even when they are accessible only through conditional samples without tractable densities. Specifically, we use a sample-based maximum mean discrepancy between conditional distributions, which first leads to a static model averaging method, StaticMA, assigning fixed weights to different candidates. In addition, we develop MoEMA (mixture-of-experts model averaging), an input-adaptive method that parameterizes covariate-dependent weights through a softmax neural-network gate. We establish in-sample and out-of-sample asymptotic optimality for the proposed methods, together with consistency of the estimated adaptive weight function under regularity conditions. The framework applies directly to Euclidean responses and extends to unstructured data by combining our formulation with fixed representation maps. Across a broad set of simulations and real-data studies spanning tabular, image, and text modalities, MoEMA generally improves over competing baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed methods.

</details>


### [133] [Constrained Flow Matching via Lagrangian Dual Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04513)
*Vince Kurtz, Alexander Davydov*

Main category: cs.LG, math.OC

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (88.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lagrangian Dual Flows，一种基于拉格朗日对偶动力学的约束流匹配生成方法。通过在去噪过程中引入对偶协态变量，该方法能够保证非线性约束的满足，无需昂贵的优化子问题、伪逆或投影步骤，在机器人、规划和物理等应用中具有重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配在生成建模中表现出色，但机器人、规划和物理等新兴应用要求对生成输出施加推理时约束。现有方法多针对线性约束（如图像修复），难以处理复杂非线性约束；而投影或基于优化的替代方案计算成本过高。因此需要一种既能处理非线性约束又保持计算高效的方法。

Method: 提出 Lagrangian Dual Flows 框架，将拉格朗日对偶动力学引入流匹配生成过程。在生成样本的同时，引入一个对偶协态变量（dual co-state）随样本一同流动，通过 primal-dual 动力学保证非线性约束的满足。该方法无需昂贵的优化子问题、伪逆或投影步骤，在去噪过程中自然地实现约束满足。

Result: 在多个约束生成任务上验证了方法的有效性，包括非线性约束下的生成。实验表明 Lagrangian Dual Flows 能够可靠地满足复杂非线性约束，同时保持生成质量，且计算开销远低于传统的投影或优化替代方法。

Conclusion: Lagrangian Dual Flows 为流匹配模型提供了一种简单、有效的约束生成技术，建立了流匹配与数值优化中 primal-dual 方法之间的新理论联系，为机器人、规划和物理等领域的约束生成应用开辟了新途径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lagrangian Dual Flows，一种在 flow matching 生成过程中施加非线性约束的方法，属于生成模型的可控性与约束满足（guidance/可控性），与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的 guidance 与可控性方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel constrained generation method for flow matching with strong theoretical connections, but lacks direct alignment with priority subtopics. Signals: constrained flow matching, Lagrangian dual dynamics, nonlinear constraint satisfaction, generative model alignment, robotics and planning applications

Abstract: Flow matching is a powerful tool for generative modeling, but emerging applications in robotics, planning, and physics require inference-time constraints on generated outputs. Such constraints are often complex and highly nonlinear. As a result, methods designed for linear constraints like image inpainting are rarely sufficient, and projection or optimization-based alternatives can be prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Lagrangian Dual Flows, a new family of constrained generation techniques based on Lagrangian dual dynamics. By simply flowing a dual co-state alongside generated samples, we can guarantee nonlinear constraint satisfaction without expensive optimization subproblems, pseudoinverses, or projection steps during the denoising process. The resulting constrained generation algorithms are simple, effective, and open new theoretical connections between flow matching and primal-dual methods in numerical optimization.

</details>
