### [159] [CrossHallu: Do Hallucination Signals Generalize Across Languages and Domains in Large Language Model's Internals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04029)
*Aisha Alansari, Malak Alkhorasani, Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CrossHallu 首次系统评估了基于LLM内部表征的幻觉检测方法在跨语言（阿拉伯语↔英语）和跨领域场景下的泛化能力，实验表明内部幻觉信号在大多数模型中能够跨语言和跨领域迁移，但跨语言性能高度依赖于特征空间中的类别可分性和语言对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM内部表征的幻觉检测方法主要在英语环境下验证，尚不清楚这些内部信号能否泛化到其他语言和领域。作者希望填补这一空白，系统评估跨语言和跨领域的幻觉检测泛化能力。

Method: 使用6个LLM在生成式问答任务上，通过TruthfulQA、阿拉伯语翻译版TruthfulQA和HalluScore数据集，进行单语训练测试、跨语言迁移、跨领域迁移以及跨语言+跨领域联合迁移的系统性实验，分析内部表征中幻觉信号的可迁移性。

Result: 内部状态幻觉信号在大多数模型中能够跨语言和跨领域迁移；跨语言性能高度依赖于特征空间中的类别可分性和语言对齐程度；跨领域迁移在阿拉伯语中的表现取决于训练和测试数据集的选择。

Conclusion: 基于LLM内部表征的幻觉检测方法具有一定的跨语言和跨领域泛化能力，但性能受特征空间结构和数据分布影响，未来需要进一步研究更鲁棒的跨语言幻觉检测方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM内部表征中的幻觉检测信号在跨语言和跨领域的泛化能力，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）范畴下的幻觉检测与可靠性研究。虽然论文主要关注文本LLM而非多模态，但幻觉检测与可靠性是该子主题的核心内容，且该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性、可靠性等广泛方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on hallucination detection generalization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: cross-lingual hallucination detection, internal representation analysis, Arabic-English evaluation, limited to QA task, no generation or world model focus

Abstract: Recent hallucination detection techniques in large language models (LLMs) focus on directly extracting features from a model's internal representations and training a classifier on these features to detect hallucinations, demonstrating promising results. Notwithstanding this advancement, most internal-state hallucination detection techniques have been explored predominantly in English, raising the question of whether such internal signals generalize across different languages and domains. To address this gap, we present CrossHallu, the first study to evaluate the cross-lingual and cross-domain generalization of hallucination detection using internal representations from six LLMs on the generative question-answering task. We conduct a systematic Arabic <-> English evaluation using TruthfulQA, an Arabic translated version of TruthfulQA, and HalluScore. This evaluation encompasses monolingual training and testing, cross-lingual transfer, cross-domain transfer, and combined cross-lingual and cross-domain transfer. The results reveal that internal-state hallucination signals in LLMs transfer across languages and domains for most models, with cross-lingual performance highly dependent on both class separability and language alignment in the feature space, whereas cross-domain transfer within Arabic varies depending on the training and testing datasets used for the hallucination detector. The code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [160] [Detecting Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented Generation through Grounding-Aware Sensitivity by Perturbation (GASP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04223)
*Mohamed Aly Bouke*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出GASP（Grounding-Aware Sensitivity by Perturbation），一种基于扰动的span级幻觉检测方法，通过衡量每个回答句子对检索证据的依赖程度（即"接地敏感性"）来检测RAG中的幻觉。GASP在固定答案的情况下，分别在全上下文、无上下文和逐块移除上下文下重新评分，利用对数似然下降和Jensen-Shannon散度作为检测信号。在RAGTruth、TofuEval和RAGBench三个基准上，使用Qwen2.5和SmolLM2系列模型进行评估，在RAGTruth上达到约0.73的响应级AUC和约0.67的span级AUC，显著优于困惑度、长度、NLI和自一致性等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统虽然能减少幻觉，但无法完全消除；而现有的幻觉检测器仅返回一个整体答案级别的分数，无法指出具体哪个句子缺乏证据支持及其原因。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一种能够定位到句子级别的幻觉检测方法。

Method: GASP方法的核心思想是"接地敏感性"（grounding sensitivity）：一个被证据支持的句子在移除其支撑段落时，其似然度会显著下降；而一个幻觉句子则几乎不受影响。具体做法是：固定答案文本，分别在全上下文、无上下文和逐块移除上下文三种条件下重新计算每个句子的对数似然，然后测量似然下降量和Jensen-Shannon散度（JSD），以此作为幻觉检测特征。作者还将解码过程解释为随机非线性迭代函数系统（RNIFS）来理论化这一现象。

Result: 在RAGTruth基准上，GASP达到响应级AUC约0.73和span级AUC约0.67，显著优于困惑度基线，并明显超过长度、全上下文NLI和自一致性基线。在span级别上，唯一具有竞争力的基线是配置良好的chunk级蕴含验证器（需额外模型），而GASP无需训练数据即可匹配该有监督分类器的性能。该方法在TofuEval上具有迁移性，但在RAGBench的短答案问答任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: GASP是一种有效的、无需训练的span级幻觉检测方法，特别适用于由检索上下文构建的答案场景，而非依赖参数化知识即可回答的短答案任务。该方法通过接地敏感性特征实现了与有监督分类器相当的性能，为RAG系统的幻觉检测提供了细粒度的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究RAG系统中的幻觉检测问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性与可靠性提升，与本文的span级幻觉检测方法高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on RAG hallucination detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: RAG hallucination detection, span-level detection, perturbation-based method, not aligned with priority subtopics, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) reduces but does not eliminate hallucination, and existing detectors return a single answer-level score that does not indicate which sentence is unsupported, or why. To close this gap, we introduce Grounding-Aware Sensitivity by Perturbation (GASP), a span-level detector that scores each answer sentence by how strongly its likelihood depends on the retrieved evidence, a quantity we term grounding sensitivity. GASP holds the answer fixed and re-scores it under the full context, under no context, and with each chunk removed, then measures the log-likelihood drops and Jensen-Shannon divergences (JSD). The likelihood of a grounded sentence collapses once its supporting passage is removed, whereas a hallucinated sentence is almost unaffected, a contrast we interpret by casting decoding as a random nonlinear iterated function system (RNIFS). We evaluate GASP on three benchmarks (RAGTruth, TofuEval, RAGBench) with three instruction-tuned scorers from two model families (Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and SmolLM2-1.7B) under a leakage-clean protocol. On RAGTruth it reaches a response-level area under the ROC curve (AUC) of about 0.73 and a span-level AUC of about 0.67, improving significantly over perplexity and by clear margins over length, whole-context natural language inference (NLI), and self-consistency baselines. The only baseline competitive at the span level is a well-configured chunk-level entailment verifier, which requires a separate model, whereas a training-free threshold on the grounding features matches the trained classifier without labeled data and serves as the default detector. Beyond RAGTruth, the signal transfers to TofuEval but not to short-answer question answering in RAGBench, showing GASP is best suited to outputs constructed from the retrieved context rather than answers recoverable from parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [161] [Does It Fail to See or Fail to Know? Attributing Errors in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04683)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo, Artem Vazhentsev, Artem Shelmanov, Timothy Baldwin, Yova Kementchedjhieva*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，用于解耦VLM在视觉问答中的不同失败模式（感知/识别瓶颈 vs. 知识检索瓶颈），并发现这些失败来源可以在解码前通过视觉token表示或提示条件隐藏状态进行预测，从而实现对不确定案例的定向干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要超出直接可见范围的知识时表现不佳，但不确定性量化仅能指示模型可能失败，无法诊断失败原因（如感知问题、实体识别问题还是知识检索问题）。先前工作要么孤立研究单一失败模式，要么将所有错误视为整体，缺乏统一解耦框架。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将VLM错误归因于两类失败源：(1) 视觉/识别瓶颈（recognition-related failures），(2) 识别后仍存在的知识检索瓶颈（post-recognition failures）。通过分析视觉token表示和提示条件隐藏状态等预生成信号，训练分类器在解码前预测失败来源。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型家族上发现一致的错误模式：部分失败源于视觉或识别瓶颈，另一些则在相关实体被识别后仍然存在。识别相关失败最好由视觉token表示捕获，而识别后仍存在的失败则由提示条件隐藏状态更好地捕获。预生成信号可在模型产生答案前高效预测失败来源。

Conclusion: VLM的错误可以归因于不同的失败模式，且这些模式可以在解码前通过不同的内部表示信号进行预测。这一发现使得不确定案例可以被路由到针对性的干预措施（如图像修复、实体识别支持或外部检索），从而提升VLM的可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在视觉问答中的错误归因，区分感知/识别失败与知识检索失败，并利用预生成信号预测失败来源。这属于多模态模型的可靠性、事实性与忠实性分析，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM error attribution rather than core priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: VLM failure analysis, pre-generation error prediction, perception vs. knowledge bottlenecks, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) perform well on visual question answering with high-quality images but struggle when questions require knowledge beyond what is clearly and directly visible. In such settings, uncertainty quantification should not only indicate whether the model is likely to fail but also diagnose why it is uncertain, across dimensions such as perception, entity recognition, and knowledge retrieval. While prior work has focused on individual failure modes in isolation or treated incorrect answers as monolithic failures, we propose a unified framework for disentangling these failure modes and investigate whether pre-generation signals can predict these failure sources. Across a range of datasets and model families, we find a consistent pattern in VLM errors: some failures arise from visual or recognition bottlenecks, while others persist after the relevant entity is identified. Our main finding is that these failure sources can be predicted before decoding: recognition-related failures are best captured by visual-token representations, while failures that remain after recognition are better captured by prompt-conditioned hidden states. This pre-generation signal enables efficient failure-source prediction before the model produces an answer, allowing uncertain cases to be routed to targeted interventions such as image repair, entity recognition support, or external retrieval.

</details>


### [162] [Do Medical Vision Language Models Actually See? A Counterfactual Grounding Framework and Hard-Negative Contrastive Training for Visually-Reliant Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03647)
*Anas Zafar, Leema Krishna Murali, Siddhant Bharadwaj, Ashish Vashist, Jia Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实评估框架（VRS、VHR等指标）来量化医学VLM的视觉依赖程度，并引入CORAL方法——基于对比学习目标（CGO）对Qwen2.5-VL-7B进行LoRA微调，在四个医学VQA基准上提升准确率6.7个百分点，降低视觉幻觉率8.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM在问答任务上表现优异，但难以判断其是否真正依赖视觉证据还是利用文本捷径（如疾病先验）。现有评估方法无法有效解耦视觉与文本贡献，亟需系统性的视觉依赖评估框架和针对性训练方法。

Method: 1) 提出反事实评估框架：用空白图、像素打乱图、无图、CLIP检索的难负样本替换输入图像，解耦视觉与文本贡献，定义视觉依赖分数(VRS)和视觉幻觉率(VHR)等指标。2) 提出CORAL方法：基于对比学习目标(CGO)对Qwen2.5-VL-7B进行LoRA微调，惩罚难负样本图像替换下答案不变性，迫使模型真正关注视觉内容。

Result: 在PathVQA、PMC-VQA、SLAKE、VQA-RAD四个医学VQA基准上（n=400），CORAL相比Qwen2.5-VL-7B基座模型宏平均准确率提升+6.7个百分点（P(Δ>0)=0.988），VHR降低8.0个百分点（P<0.001）。跨领域诊断显示医学任务上图像替换仅造成≤6.5个百分点的性能下降，而通用领域任务下降48-61个百分点，揭示了医学VLM的视觉依赖缺口。

Conclusion: 医学VLM存在显著的视觉依赖不足问题，CORAL通过对比学习目标有效提升了模型对视觉证据的依赖并降低了幻觉。该工作为医学VLM的视觉可靠性评估与改进提供了系统框架，并开源了代码和模型权重以支持可复现的评估。

Classification reason: 该论文关注医学VLM是否真正基于视觉证据推理，提出了反事实评估框架和对比学习训练方法来缓解视觉幻觉、提升视觉可靠性，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM hallucination, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: Medical VLM, Counterfactual evaluation, Visual grounding, Hard-negative contrastive training, LoRA fine-tuning

Abstract: Large vision language models (VLMs) report strong accuracy on medical question-answering, yet it remains unclear whether they reason from visual evidence or exploit textual shortcuts. We introduce a counterfactual evaluation framework that decouples visual and textual contributions by substituting input images with controlled surrogates blank, pixel-shuffled, image-absent, and CLIP-retrieved hard negatives and derive a suite of grounding metrics including the Visual Reliance Score (VRS) and Visual Hallucination Rate (VHR). We further introduce CORAL (COntrastive Retrieval-Augmented Learning), a 7B-parameter LoRA fine-tune of Qwen2.5-VL-7B trained with a Contrastive Grounding Objective (CGO) that penalises answer invariance under hard-negative image swaps. On a paired controlled evaluation across four closed-form medical VQA benchmarks (PathVQA, PMC-VQA, SLAKE, VQA-RAD; n=400 total), CORAL improves macro accuracy by +6.7 pp (P(Delta>0)=0.988) and reduces VHR by 8.0 pp (P<0.001) over the matched Qwen2.5-VL-7B base; neither MedVLThinker RL variant achieves a significant gain on either metric. Cross-domain diagnostics further reveal that image substitution costs only <=6.5 pp on medical benchmarks versus 48-61 pp on general-domain tasks, situating the grounding gap that CGO targets. We discuss evaluation limitations openly including train/eval benchmark overlap and underpowered secondary metrics and release our framework, training code, and model weights to support reproducible grounding audits of medical VLMs.

</details>


### [163] [SeeMe: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models through Effective Visual Token Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04163)
*Kai Tang, Jinhao You, Bohua Zhang, Yichen Guo, Yiding Sun, Dongxu Zhang, Chenxi Li, Xiande Huang, Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SeeMe 提出了一种无需训练的视觉 token 工程框架，通过三阶段处理（筛选、增强、重组）抑制 LVLM 中由噪声视觉 token 引发的幻觉，在 MME、POPE、AMBER 等基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 幻觉缓解方法主要干预解码阶段，忽略了幻觉的一个关键来源——无关或噪声视觉 token 误导了解码过程。作者希望从视觉 token 本身入手，通过特征工程思想来抑制幻觉源。

Method: SeeMe 是一个无需训练的框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 视觉 token 筛选——去除与任务无关的噪声 token；(2) 视觉 token 增强——强化信息丰富的 token 表征；(3) 视觉 token 重组——将处理后的 token 重新组织输入模型。整个过程无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在 MME、POPE 和 AMBER 三个基准上，对四个不同的 LVLM 模型进行实验，SeeMe 一致地降低了幻觉率，提升了输出一致性。

Conclusion: SeeMe 通过视觉 token 工程为 LVLM 幻觉缓解提供了新的视角，验证了从视觉输入端而非仅解码端处理幻觉的有效性，且无需训练即可部署。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LVLM中的幻觉缓解问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（CANONICAL）子主题，该子主题专门涵盖视觉幻觉缓解、事实性与可靠性提升等工作。

Importance reason: Addresses LVLM hallucination via visual token engineering, but is not aligned with priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Training-free framework, Visual token engineering, Hallucination mitigation, LVLM reliability, No priority subtopic match

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. However, they remain susceptible to hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the actual visual input. Existing methods primarily intervene at the decoding stage, while overlooking a critical source of hallucinations: irrelevant or noisy visual tokens that mislead the decoding process. To address this issue, we propose SeeMe, a training-free framework that introduces the concept of feature engineering from traditional machine learning into LVLMs. SeeMe restructures visual tokens through a three-stage token engineering process to suppress hallucination sources while preserving informative visual evidence. Experiments on MME, POPE, and AMBER benchmarks across four LVLMs demonstrate that SeeMe consistently reduces hallucinations and improves output consistency, providing a novel perspective for mitigating hallucinations in LVLMs.

</details>

### [164] [Safe Inference-Time Alignment via Lagrangian Reward Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02781)
*Yaswanth Chittepu, Ativ Joshi, Sohini Chintala, Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LARA 是一种推理时对齐框架，通过拉格朗日对偶将安全约束转化为增强奖励信号，在不重新训练模型的情况下改善有用性与无害性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法仅优化单一标量奖励分数，安全约束要么被忽略，要么通过手动调参的惩罚项编码，缺乏原则性的约束处理方式。

Method: 从 KL 正则化的约束优化目标出发，引入奖励模型和成本模型，通过拉格朗日对偶将约束问题转化为关于非负对偶变量的一维凸优化问题。在小校准集上估计对偶变量，定义增强奖励作为推理时对齐方法的即插即用评分信号。对序列级采样（如 Best-of-N 重排序）提供精确约束保证，对 token 级奖励引导解码提供对偶校准启发式。

Result: LARA 在序列级和 token 级推理时对齐方法上均改善了有用性-无害性权衡，其中 Best-of-N 方法达到最佳性能，接近基于微调的直接对齐基线。

Conclusion: LARA 提供了一种原则性的推理时安全约束对齐框架，无需重新训练模型即可有效平衡有用性与安全性，且可兼容多种现有推理时对齐方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出推理时对齐框架 LARA，通过拉格朗日对偶将安全约束融入奖励增强，属于推理时对齐方法。虽然论文主要实验在语言模型上，但其方法框架通用，可应用于多模态模型的对齐，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment) 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on safe alignment for language models, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: inference-time alignment, safety constraints, Lagrangian dual, reward augmentation, helpfulness-harmlessness tradeoff

Abstract: Inference-time alignment steers a frozen language model during decoding using auxiliary reward signals, avoiding the cost of repeated weight updates. However, existing inference-time alignment methods typically optimize a single scalar score, so explicit safety constraints must either be ignored or encoded through manually tuned penalties. We propose Lagrangian Reward Augmentation (LARA), a general inference-time alignment framework under safety constraints. Starting from a KL-regularized constrained objective with a reward model and a cost model, LARA dualizes the constraint and reduces the optimization problem to a one-dimensional convex problem over a nonnegative dual variable. Estimated on a small calibration set, this dual variable defines an augmented reward that can be used as a drop-in scoring signal within existing inference-time alignment methods. For sequence-level sampling methods, such as Best-of-N reranking, the calibrated dual variable corresponds to the solution of the expected-cost constrained problem. For token-level reward-guided decoding methods, the same construction yields a principled dual-calibrated heuristic rather than an exact constrained-policy guarantee. We evaluate LARA on both sequence-level and token-level inference-time alignment methods, and find that LARA improves the helpfulness-harmlessness tradeoff, with Best-of-N achieving the best performance among inference-time methods, approaching finetuning-based direct alignment baselines.

</details>


### [165] [Oyster-II: Reinforcement Learning for Constructive Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02914)
*Jiyang Guan, Yong Xie, Jun Chen, Jiexi Liu, Zipeng Ye, Defeng Li, Jiayu Shen, Jialing Tao, Hui Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Oyster-II提出基于强化学习的建设性安全对齐框架，通过零RL范式与多阶段强化学习，解决Oyster-I中SFT方案的安全泛化不足和安全CoT过度泛化问题，在安全维度上超越Qwen3-14B和Oyster-I，达到与Qwen3-Max和Qwen3.5-397B相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拒绝导向对齐策略虽然能缓解有害内容生成，但系统性地无法满足用户的合法需求，常常拒绝回答本可以安全且建设性回应的敏感问题。Oyster-I提出了建设性安全范式，但其基于SFT的方案存在两个关键局限：对分布外场景的安全泛化不足，以及安全CoT过度泛化（将安全推理模式过度应用于良性查询，损害有用性）。

Method: 提出Oyster-II框架，采用Zero-RL范式结合多阶段强化学习。具体包括：1）构建建设性安全对齐数据集；2）设计多阶段RL训练策略，逐步优化模型的安全性与有用性平衡；3）采用RL而非SFT来学习建设性安全响应模式，提升泛化能力并缓解CoT过度泛化。

Result: 在广泛基准测试上，Oyster-II在安全维度上全面超越Qwen3-14B和Oyster-I，实现了与Qwen3-Max和Qwen3.5-397B相当的跨规模性能。

Conclusion: 基于RL的建设性安全对齐框架Oyster-II有效克服了SFT方案的安全泛化不足和CoT过度泛化问题，为LLM安全对齐提供了新的有效范式，在保持有用性的同时显著提升了安全性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM的安全对齐问题，提出基于强化学习的建设性安全对齐框架Oyster-II，属于多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）范畴下的RLHF/偏好学习方向。虽然论文主要聚焦文本LLM，但其对齐方法论与多模态对齐高度相关，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等对齐方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM safety alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: RL-based safety alignment, zero-RL paradigm, safety CoT over-generalization, constructive safety, Qwen3 comparison

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse applications, yet ensuring their simultaneous safety, helpfulness, and trustworthiness remains a persistent challenge. Conventional refusal-oriented alignment strategies mitigate harmful content generation but systematically fail to serve legitimate user needs, often withholding information that could safely and constructively address the underlying intent of sensitive queries. Building upon the constructive safety paradigm pioneered by Oyster-I, which moves beyond blanket refusal toward thoughtful, response-oriented safety alignment, we identify two critical limitations of its Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT)-based scheme: insufficient safety generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios and a phenomenon we term safety chain-of-thought (CoT) over-generalization, wherein safety-oriented reasoning patterns are excessively applied to benign queries, degrading helpfulness and user experience. To address these limitations, we propose Oyster-II, a reinforcement learning (RL)-based constructive safety alignment framework that adopts a Zero-RL paradigm combined with a multi-stage reinforcement learning across extensive benchmarks, Oyster-II comprehensively surpasses both Qwen3-14B and its predecessor Oyster-I on safety dimensions, achieving cross-scale performance comparable to Qwen3-Max and Qwen3.5-397B.

</details>


### [166] [Bridging Interleaved Multi-Modal Reasoning as a Unified Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03748)
*Zican Hu, Xuyang Hu, Yiming Liu, Zuwei Long, Wei Liu, Yunzhuo Hao, Jiawei Gu, Linjie Li, Yu Cheng, Zhenhong Sun, Weibo Gu, Xing Sun, Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (90.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BRAID 框架，将交错多模态推理（文本-图像-文本）建模为统一的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过强化学习联合优化文本 token 和图像去噪路径，并引入 VLM 裁判为中间图像提供推理效用评分以解决长程信用分配问题，在空间推理和视觉感知基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）在交错文本-图像推理中展现出潜力，但强化学习仅应用于文本步骤，图像生成仍依赖监督代理损失，导致策略梯度无法通过完整的交错轨迹传播，限制了 RL 在 UMM 中的潜力。

Method: BRAID 将多轮文本-图像-文本推理建模为统一 MDP，计算共享的轨迹级优势函数，并将其一致地传播到文本 token 和图像去噪路径中，每种模态使用其原生策略梯度机制优化。同时引入 VLM 裁判对每个中间图像进行推理效用评分，提供密集的回合级反馈以解决长程信用分配问题。

Result: 在空间推理和视觉感知基准上，BRAID 一致优于多种基线方法，验证了统一 MDP 公式结合视觉思维引导对有效多模态推理的必要性。

Conclusion: 统一 MDP 公式与视觉思维引导是多模态推理中有效利用 RL 的关键，BRAID 为 UMM 的联合优化提供了简洁而有效的框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BRAID 框架，将多轮文本-图像-文本推理建模为统一 MDP，通过强化学习联合优化文本和图像生成。核心贡献在于将 RL 应用于统一多模态模型的完整轨迹优化，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal reasoning via RL, aligning with user's high-weight continuous multimodal and unified MLLM priorities. Signals: unified multimodal reasoning, reinforcement learning for interleaved text-image, VLM judge for credit assignment, MDP formulation, spatial reasoning gains

Abstract: Unified multi-modal models (UMMs) have shown promising interleaved text-image reasoning capabilities, yet effectively optimizing such multi-turn generation via reinforcement learning (RL) remains an open challenge. Existing approaches apply RL exclusively to text steps, relegating image generation to supervised surrogates, preventing policy gradients from propagating through the full interleaved trajectory across heterogeneous modalities. This leaves the potential of RL for UMMs largely untapped. In the paper, we introduce \textbf{BRAID} (\textbf{B}ridging inte\textbf{R}le\textbf{A}ved mult\textbf{I}-modal reasoning as a unified \textbf{D}ecision process), a simple framework that casts multi-turn text-image-text reasoning as a unified Markov decision process (MDP), enabling joint optimization of textual and visual generation via a single, principled RL objective. BRAID computes a shared trajectory-level advantage and propagates it coherently into both text tokens and image denoising paths, each optimized through its modality-native policy gradient mechanism. To further address long-horizon credit assignment, BRAID employs a vision-language model (VLM) judge that scores each intermediate image on its reasoning utility, supplying dense turn-level feedback to sharpen learning at critical visual branches. Experiments on spatial reasoning and visual perception benchmarks show that BRAID consistently outperforms various baselines, confirming that a unified MDP formulation with vision-thinking guidance is essential for effective multi-modal reasoning.

</details>


### [167] [H-OPD: Confidence Aware Heterogeneous Multi-Teacher Multimodal On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02592)
*Qixiang Yin, Huanjin Yao, Yuchen Cai, Jianghao Chen, Ziyi Wang, Min Yang, Fei Su, Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (56.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: H-OPD 提出了一种置信度感知的异构多教师 on-policy 蒸馏框架，在 token 级别动态组合视觉-语言模型和纯文本教师模型，用于多模态推理的后训练，在 11 个推理基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 on-policy distillation (OPD) 方法在多模态推理中通常采用静态教师路由，即根据模态或任务类型将每个样本分配给单一教师。然而，视觉定位和抽象推理可能主导不同的解码步骤，单一教师无法为完整的生成轨迹提供充分监督。

Method: H-OPD 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 异构多教师设置，包括视觉-语言模型 (VLM) 和纯文本 LLM 教师；(2) 视觉到语言描述转换 (V2L)，使纯文本教师能够获取关键视觉语义；(3) 置信度感知的仲裁机制，在 token 级别动态选择最合适的教师进行监督，通过教师置信度分数决定每个 token 的监督来源。

Result: 在 11 个广泛使用的多模态推理基准上进行了全面评估，H-OPD 显著优于现有的 OPD 方法和静态教师路由策略，展示了异构多教师 token 级动态仲裁的有效性。

Conclusion: H-OPD 通过 token 级别的置信度感知异构多教师蒸馏，有效解决了多模态推理中单一教师监督不足的问题，为多模态模型的后训练提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 H-OPD，一种置信度感知的异构多教师多模态 on-policy 蒸馏框架，用于多模态推理。On-policy distillation 属于知识蒸馏与对齐方法，与多模态 RL/对齐方向高度相关，因此映射到 canonical 子主题「多模态强化学习与对齐」。

Importance reason: Novel multi-teacher distillation for multimodal reasoning, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous models and generation. Signals: Multimodal reasoning, On-policy distillation, Heterogeneous teachers, Token-level routing, Confidence-aware arbitration

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm by providing supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, existing OPD methods for multimodal reasoning usually rely on a static teacher routing, assigning each sample to a single teacher based on modality or task type. This ignores that visual grounding and abstract reasoning may dominate different decoding steps, making a single teacher insufficient for the full trajectory. To this end, H-OPD is proposed as a confidence-aware heterogeneous multi-teacher OPD framework for multimodal reasoning. By verifying the complementarity of heterogeneous teachers in the same reasoning process, H-OPD replaces task or sample level teacher routing with token-level teacher arbitration along the shared student trajectory. H-OPD employs vision-to-language description transfer to enable text-only teachers to access key visual semantics, and uses a confidence-aware arbitration mechanism to dynamically combine vision-language teacher and text-only teachers at each token. Extensive evaluations over 11 widely-used reasoning benchmarks showcase the superior performance of our method.

</details>


### [168] [Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance for Multimodal LLMs Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02593)
*Jaehyun Jang, Eunseop Yoon, Hee Suk Yoon, SooHwan Eom, Mark A. Hasegawa-Johnson, Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance (TRAG)，一种针对多模态大模型知识蒸馏的新方法。通过将蒸馏目标从提示-视觉注意力转向响应-视觉注意力，并基于注意力熵为每个 token 自适应加权 KL 散度，TRAG 显著优于现有蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 知识蒸馏仅依赖输出 token 分布，压缩效果有限。虽然已有工作探索蒸馏注意力信号，但究竟哪些注意力信号最有用尚未被充分研究。本文发现响应-视觉注意力的师生相似度与下游性能强相关，而提示-视觉注意力则无关，且注意力分布在不同 token 间差异显著，统一蒸馏目标并非最优。

Method: 提出 TRAG 蒸馏框架：(1) 将蒸馏焦点从 prompt-to-vision attention 转移到 response-to-vision attention；(2) 基于注意力熵为每个 token 自适应加权 KL 散度，高熵（不确定性高）的 token 赋予更大权重，低熵（确定性高）的 token 赋予更小权重，从而引导学生精确模仿教师的视觉关注模式。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上，TRAG 显著优于先前蒸馏基线方法，验证了响应-视觉注意力蒸馏和 token 级自适应加权的有效性。

Conclusion: 响应-视觉注意力是 MLLM 知识蒸馏中更有效的监督信号，且 token 级自适应蒸馏目标比统一目标更优。TRAG 为轻量级 MLLM 的高效训练提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型的知识蒸馏，属于多模态模型训练中对齐与优化方法，与 canonical 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的偏好学习、对齐等方向高度相关，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM distillation via attention guidance, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM knowledge distillation, response-to-visual attention, token-level adaptive weighting, outperforms baselines, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: While knowledge distillation (KD) is widely adopted for training lightweight models by leveraging supervision from larger teacher models, relying solely on output token distributions has proven insufficient for compressing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Since output tokens are a byproduct of the model attending to visual inputs, prior works have explored explicitly distilling attention to provide a direct supervisory signal. While promising, the precise utility of which attention signals to distill remains under-explored. In this work, we challenge the conventional reliance on prompt-to-vision attention by revealing that downstream performance correlates strongly with response-to-vision attention similarity to the teacher, but negligibly with that of prompt-conditioned attention. Furthermore, we observe that attention distributions exhibit significant variance across individual tokens, indicating that a uniform distillation objective is suboptimal. To this end, we introduce Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance (TRAG), a distillation objective that 1) shifts the focus to response-to-vision signals and 2) employs token-specific objectives by adaptively weighting the Kullback-Leibler divergence based on attention entropy, effectively guiding the student to mirror the teacher's precise visual focus. Extensive experimental results on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that TRAG significantly outperforms prior distillation baselines.

</details>


### [169] [ACPO: Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization via Fine-Grained Surrogate Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03126)
*Zijun Xie, Yuyang You, Yongzhi Li, Enlei Gong, Zeyu Chen, Quan Chen, Yanhua Cheng, Peng Jiang, Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ACPO提出了一种基于模态局部代理熵的token级信用分配框架，通过非对称地调节策略更新——在成功轨迹中强调不确定决策、在失败轨迹中强调过度自信token——来改进LLM的强化学习推理能力，在数学推理和代码基准上超越DAPO、GTPO、SAPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法在提升LLM推理能力时面临稀疏结果奖励导致的token级信用分配困难。传统方法将轨迹级奖励均匀分配给所有token，而最近的熵感知方法要么依赖粗糙的分离启发式，要么直接优化真实熵，引入与采样token策略更新不一致的非局部梯度分量。

Method: 提出Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization (ACPO)，基于模态局部代理熵进行token级信用分配。ACPO非对称地调节策略更新：在成功轨迹中强调不确定决策，在失败轨迹中强调过度自信token。代理熵具有确定性熵界，在模态对齐和近端更新下保持策略梯度方向至主阶。

Result: 在数学推理（AIME 2025）和代码生成（HumanEvalPro）基准上，ACPO一致优于DAPO、GTPO、SAPO等强RL基线。

Conclusion: ACPO通过细粒度代理熵实现更精确的token级信用分配，有效提升了LLM的推理能力，为RL优化LLM提供了新的视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ACPO，一种基于细粒度代理熵的自适应信用分配策略优化方法，用于提升LLM的推理能力（数学推理和代码生成）。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题，因为该方法本质上是一种RL优化方法，与RLHF、偏好优化、策略梯度等对齐技术密切相关。

Importance reason: Novel token-level credit assignment for LLM RL, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: LLM reasoning, token-level credit assignment, surrogate entropy, RL for LLM, math/coding benchmarks

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), but sparse outcome rewards still make token-level credit assignment difficult. Existing scalable RL methods typically assign trajectory-level rewards uniformly across tokens, while recent entropy-aware approaches either rely on coarse detached heuristics or directly optimize true entropy, which can introduce non-local gradient components misaligned with sampled-token policy updates. We propose Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization (ACPO), a token-level credit assignment framework based on a mode-local surrogate entropy. ACPO asymmetrically modulates policy updates by emphasizing uncertain decisions in successful rollouts and overconfident tokens in failed rollouts. We show that the surrogate admits deterministic entropy bounds and, under modal alignment and proximal updates, preserves the policy-gradient direction to leading order. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, including AIME 2025 and HumanEvalPro, show that ACPO consistently improves over strong RL baselines such as DAPO, GTPO, and SAPO.

</details>


### [170] [Unbiased Alignment for Large Language Models with Noisy Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03248)
*Jialiang Wang, Xianming Liu, Xiong Zhou, Hui Liu, Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对偏好数据中存在显著噪声的问题，提出了无偏对齐的理论框架，包括无偏奖励模型（URM）损失和无偏直接偏好优化（UDPO）损失，通过数学修正偏好噪声带来的偏差，实现在噪声数据集上的无偏模型训练，无需干净的真实标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF和DPO等方法严重依赖偏好数据的质量，但真实世界的偏好数据集往往包含大量噪声（标注者不一致、标注错误等），导致模型对齐效果下降。本文旨在从理论上解决偏好噪声对对齐训练的干扰问题。

Method: 提出URM损失和UDPO损失，通过数学推导修正偏好噪声引起的分布偏移，使得在噪声偏好数据上训练能够达到与干净数据上训练等价的效果。提供了噪声容忍性、参数向下兼容性和分类校准性的严格理论分析。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，URM和UDPO方法在存在偏好噪声的情况下显著优于现有基线方法，能够有效缓解噪声对对齐训练的负面影响。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上建立了无偏对齐框架，证明了所提方法在噪声偏好数据下的有效性，为实际场景中大规模偏好对齐提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大语言模型与人类偏好对齐中的噪声问题，提出无偏奖励模型和无偏DPO方法，属于偏好学习与对齐方向。虽然论文主要聚焦LLM而非多模态，但其方法可直接迁移至多模态对齐场景，且现有taxonomy中"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）是[CANONICAL]条目，涵盖DPO、偏好学习等核心主题，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses noisy preference alignment for LLMs, but is outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Noisy preference alignment, Unbiased reward model, Direct preference optimization, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: The alignment of large language models with human preferences is commonly achieved through Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback or Direct Preference Optimization. However, these methods are vulnerable to the significant noise prevalent in real-world preference datasets. To address this critical issue, we present a theoretical framework for unbiased alignment, introducing the Unbiased Reward Model (URM) loss and the Unbiased Direct Preference Optimization (UDPO) loss. By mathematically correcting the distortion induced by preference noise, our novel objectives enable unbiased model training directly from noisy datasets, without requiring clean ground-truth supervision. We provide rigorous theoretical analyses demonstrating that our methods are noise-tolerant, parameter downward compatible, and classification-calibrated. Comprehensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our approaches outperform state-of-the-art baselines. Code available at: .

</details>


### [171] [CARL: Constraint-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Planning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04854)
*Qiuyi Qi, Jinjian Zhang, Mutian Bao, Tian Liang, Guocong Li, Dongnan Liu, Wei Zhou, Jie Liu, Ming Kong, Linjian Mo, Feng Zhang, Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CARL 框架，通过约束感知的强化学习奖励设计，增强 LLM 在规划任务中对约束条件的内在关注，无需外部工具即可实现端到端的约束感知规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在规划任务中经常违反任务约束，现有方法依赖外部工具或任务分解，未能增强模型内在的约束感知能力。

Method: 提出 Constraint-Aware RL (CARL) 框架，通过比较模型在约束输入和无约束输入下的输出分布来设计约束感知奖励，鼓励模型关注约束并惩罚忽视约束的行为，兼容多种 RL 框架。

Result: 在 BlocksWorld、TravelPlanner 和 T-Eval 三个规划基准上，CARL 显著优于标准强化微调 (RFT) 基线和最先进的推理模型，模型对约束的关注度明显提升。

Conclusion: CARL 通过强化学习有效增强了 LLM 的约束感知能力，是一种可扩展的端到端约束感知规划方法，无需外部求解器或顶级模型。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Constraint-Aware RL (CARL) 框架，通过强化学习增强 LLM 对约束的内在关注，属于多模态/语言模型强化学习与对齐方向。虽然论文主要聚焦 LLM 规划，但其 RL 框架与约束对齐方法可映射到多模态 RL 与对齐的 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM planning via RL, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: RL for constraint-aware planning, no external tools, BlocksWorld/TravelPlanner/T-Eval, not aligned with user's priority keywords

Abstract: Despite their strong reasoning capabilities and extensive world knowledge, Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plans that violate task constraints, undermining their reliability in real-world applications. This deficiency arises from a lack of systematic mechanisms to incorporate constraint information during the generation process. While existing approaches attempt to mitigate this by relying on external tools or task decomposition, they fail to enhance the model's intrinsic constraint awareness. To address this, we propose Constraint-Aware Reinforcement Learning (CARL), a novel RL framework designed to strengthen LLMs' intrinsic focus on constraints. CARL introduces a constraint-aware reward by comparing the model's output distributions under constrained and unconstrained inputs, encouraging constraint focus and penalizing neglect. Compatible with various RL frameworks and requiring no external solvers or top models, CARL enables scalable, end-to-end constraint-aware planning. Extensive experiments on BlocksWorld, TravelPlanner, and T-Eval demonstrate that CARL significantly outperforms standard Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) baselines and state-of-the-art reasoning models, exhibiting a markedly increased focus on constraints.

</details>


### [172] [Anchored Self-Play for Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03523)
*Caroline Choi, Zeyneb Kaya, Shirley Wu, Tengyu Ma, Tatsunori Hashimoto, Ludwig Schmidt*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Anchored Self-Play (ASP)，一种用于代码修复的强化学习方法。通过 generator-fixer 自博弈训练单个模型同时生成 bug 和修复 bug，并引入锚定参考集（anchor set）的代码嵌入相似度奖励来防止自博弈漂移到不真实的 bug 类型。在 BugSourceBench 基准上，ASP 相比标准自博弈在各类 bug 源上平均修复率提升 24%（相对）/ 7.0 pp（绝对）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码修复数据有限，难以扩展监督信号。现有自博弈方法虽然能自动生成训练数据，但容易偏向生成困难但不真实的 bug，导致在真实人类编写的 bug 上性能下降。需要一种方法既能利用自博弈的自动课程学习优势，又能保持对真实 bug 的修复能力。

Method: 提出 Anchored Self-Play (ASP)，包含两个关键设计：(1) 在 generator 训练中加入代码嵌入相似度奖励，鼓励生成与参考集中真实 bug 相似的 bug；(2) 在 fixer 训练中混合参考集中的真实 bug 数据，防止 fixer 遗忘真实 bug 的修复能力。使用单个模型同时作为 generator 和 fixer，通过强化学习交替训练。

Result: 在 BugSourceBench（包含人类编写代码、LM 生成代码、人类编辑 LM 生成代码三种 bug 源）上，ASP 在所有 bug 源上均取得最佳修复率，相比标准自博弈平均提升 24% 相对 / 7.0 pp 绝对。消融实验验证了锚定机制对防止漂移的关键作用。

Conclusion: Anchored Self-Play 通过引入少量参考数据锚定自博弈过程，有效解决了自博弈漂移到不真实 bug 的问题，在保持对合成 bug 修复能力的同时显著提升了对真实 bug 的修复性能。该方法为代码修复任务提供了一种可扩展的监督信号生成范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用强化学习（self-play RL）训练语言模型进行代码修复，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题，因为其核心方法是 RL 训练（generator-fixer self-play），且涉及偏好/奖励设计（code-embedding similarity reward）。

Importance reason: Focuses on code repair self-play, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: code repair, self-play reinforcement learning, anchor set for stability, BugSourceBench benchmark, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Code repair is an important capability for language models (LMs): given a buggy program and unit tests, an LM must produce a fixed program that passes the tests. Because code repair data is limited, we aim to scale supervision by using an LM to generate bug--fix tasks. We propose __generator--fixer self-play__, in which a single model is trained with reinforcement learning to generate bugs and fix them. As the fixer improves, the generator adapts to produce more difficult bugs, yielding an automatic curriculum. To test whether this curriculum generalizes, we introduce BugSourceBench, a repair benchmark spanning realistic bug sources: bugs in human-written code, LM-generated code, and human-edited LM-generated code. On BugSourceBench, we find that self-play drifts toward difficult but unrealistic bugs, improving on synthetic bugs but degrading on human-authored ones. We propose Anchored Self-Play (ASP), which anchors self-play with a small reference set by adding a code-embedding similarity reward for generation and mixing reference bugs into fixer training. Across bug sources, ASP achieves the best fix rates, improving average fix rate over standard self-play by $+24\%$ relative / $+7.0$ pp absolute, with gains on bugs from both LMs and humans.

</details>


### [173] [Reward-Gated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04037)
*Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh, Vijay Lingam, Atula Tejaswi, Chanakya Ekbote, Sujay Sanghavi, Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RG-OPD（奖励门控在线策略蒸馏），通过验证器奖励信号动态决定是否信任教师模型的 logits，在推理和代码任务上显著优于 vanilla reverse-KL 蒸馏和 TSD-KD 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏中，教师模型对学生采样轨迹提供的密集 token 级监督并不可靠：教师可能给看似合理但错误的解分配高概率，或给正确但推理路径不同的学生解分配低概率。无条件蒸馏会强化错误模式或抹去有用的学生行为。

Method: 提出 RG-OPD，在在线策略蒸馏中引入验证器（verifier）奖励作为门控信号，决定何时信任教师 logits。稀疏的验证器奖励与密集的教师 logits 相结合，在保留 token 级监督的同时过滤误导性的教师信号。

Result: 在推理和代码基准上，RG-OPD 优于 vanilla reverse-KL 蒸馏和 TSD-KD 基线。在 1K 生成长度下，比 reverse-KL 提升 2.9 分，比 TSD-KD 提升 4.9 分；在长文本生成设置下，比未调优的学生模型提升 8.2 分。

Conclusion: RG-OPD 通过奖励门控机制有效解决了在线策略蒸馏中教师监督不可靠的问题，是一种简单有效的蒸馏对齐方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Reward-Gated On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD)，利用验证器奖励信号决定何时信任教师模型的 logits，以改进知识蒸馏中的对齐问题。这属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好学习范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment) 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on distillation with verifier feedback, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: on-policy distillation, reward gating, verifier feedback, reasoning tasks, coding benchmarks

Abstract: On-policy distillation is a powerful way to transfer reasoning ability from a strong teacher to a smaller student: the student samples trajectories from its own policy, and the teacher provides dense token-level supervision on the states the student actually visits. However, this supervision is not always reliable: a teacher can assign high likelihood to plausible but incorrect solutions, or low likelihood to correct student solutions that follow different reasoning paths. Unconditionally distilling the teacher can therefore reinforce bad modes or erase useful student behavior. To address these limitations, we introduce RG-OPD: Reward-Gated On-Policy Distillation that uses verifier feedback to decide when teacher logits should be trusted. RG-OPD bridges sparse verifier rewards and dense teacher logits, preserving token-level supervision while filtering misleading teacher signals. Across reasoning and coding benchmarks, RG-OPD produces stronger distilled students, outperforming both vanilla reverse-KL distillation and the recent TSD-KD baseline. At 1K generation length, RG-OPD improves over reverse-KL by 2.9 points and over TSD-KD by 4.9 points; in the long-generation setting, it improves over the untuned student by 8.2 points. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [174] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394)
*Shiyuan Feng, Huan-ang Gao, Haohan Chi, Hanlin Wu, Zhilong Zhang, Zheng Jiang, Bingxiang He, Wei-Ying Ma, Ya-Qin Zhang, Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (82.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD)，一种将弱模型RL训练产生的策略偏移作为隐式奖励信号蒸馏到强模型的方法，无需显式奖励模型或对强模型运行完整RL，即可实现弱到强的泛化提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）能有效提升语言模型推理能力，但每次在新强模型上运行RL都需要大量rollout，计算成本高昂。随着模型规模增长，后训练本身成为瓶颈。因此，研究者希望探索一种弱到强的替代方案：在更小、更便宜的模型上运行RL，然后将RL学到的知识迁移到更强的目标模型上。

Method: Direct-OPD 的核心思想是：不直接蒸馏RL后的弱教师模型（因为其最终策略混合了有用RL增益和小模型的局限性），而是转移教师模型RL诱导的策略偏移。具体地，比较RL后教师与其RL前参考模型的logits比值，将其作为稠密隐式奖励信号，在强学生模型自己的on-policy状态上应用该信号进行蒸馏。这直接复用了弱模型的RL监督信号，无需训练显式奖励模型或在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励RL。

Result: 在AIME 2024上，Direct-OPD将Qwen3-1.7B从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅需8张A100 GPU训练4小时。该方法优于步数匹配的直接RL，并且支持多个策略偏移的顺序组合。实验一致表明，弱教师模型能够有效提升更强目标模型的性能。

Conclusion: RL的结果可以跨模型规模作为隐式奖励信号复用，而不仅仅是作为最终模型来模仿。Direct-OPD提供了一种高效、可扩展的弱到强泛化方法，为大规模语言模型的后训练提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过弱模型RL训练得到的隐式奖励信号来蒸馏增强强模型，核心是RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）与策略蒸馏，属于多模态/语言模型强化学习与对齐方向。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment），该子主题涵盖RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等对齐方法，本文的Direct-OPD方法正是RL信号跨模型规模迁移的对齐技术。

Importance reason: Novel weak-to-strong RL distillation method, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Weak-to-strong generalization, On-policy distillation, RLVR efficiency, Implicit reward transfer, Language model reasoning

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.

</details>


### [175] [USE: A Unified Self-Ensembling Framework for Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03900)
*Siru Jiang, Jian Liang, Ran He, Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 USE（Unified Self-Ensembling）框架，用于 CLIP 等视觉语言模型的测试时提示调优。通过自集成策略在优化和推理阶段保持一致性，利用测试图像自身及其增强视图获得更可靠的伪标签，显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时提示调优（TPT）方法在优化阶段使用多视图增强，但推理阶段仅用单视图，导致两阶段目标不一致。此外，TPT 的优化可被理解为隐式地从自生成伪标签中学习，但伪标签的可靠性有待提升。

Method: 提出自集成（SE）策略：在优化阶段，自适应地强调测试图像自身而非其增强视图，以获得更可靠的伪标签；在推理阶段应用相同策略，统一两阶段目标。进一步提出 USE 框架，将 SE 同时应用于优化和推理。SE 也可作为轻量级免优化 TTA 方法使用。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，SE 和 USE 分别优于对应的基线方法。SE 与现有 TTA 方法集成时也能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过统一优化与推理阶段的目标，自集成策略能有效提升测试时提示调优的性能和可靠性，是一种简单而有效的 TTA 改进方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 CLIP 视觉语言模型的测试时提示调优（Test-Time Prompt Tuning），属于多模态对齐与适应方法。虽然论文主要关注 TTA 而非 RLHF/DPO，但其核心是优化视觉语言模型在下游任务上的对齐表现，且 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" 涵盖多模态对齐与偏好学习，与本文的测试时自适应对齐目标高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on test-time prompt tuning for CLIP, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: Test-time adaptation, CLIP, prompt tuning, self-ensembling, pseudo labels

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a popular paradigm for improving the performance of vision-language models (e.g., CLIP) on downstream tasks. Among existing CLIP-based TTA methods, Test-Time Prompt Tuning (TPT) is a pioneering work that optimizes textual prompts using multiple test-time augmentations and remains a strong baseline to date. In this work, we revisit TPT and reveal that its optimization can be interpreted as implicitly learning from self-generated pseudo labels. Building on this perspective, we propose a unified self-ensembling framework (USE) that ensures consistency between the optimization and inference stages. During optimization, we introduce a simple yet effective self-ensembling (SE) strategy that emphasizes the test image itself over its augmented views adaptively to obtain more reliable pseudo labels. To fully exploit the potential of augmentations, we further apply the same strategy at inference time, unifying the objectives of both stages. Notably, SE can also act as a lightweight optimization-free TTA method. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that SE and USE outperform their counterparts, respectively. Furthermore, SE yields consistent performance gains when integrated with existing TTA methods. The code is available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_reasoning_cot'></div>

## 多模态推理与思维链 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态 CoT、视觉推理、数学/代码/科学推理、推理时计算扩展。

### [176] [MentalThink: Shaping Thoughts in Mental SVG World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03530)
*Kangheng Lin, Jisheng Yin, Dingming Li, En Yu, Yana Wei, Han Zhou, Liang Zhao, Hongyu Zhou, Hongbo Peng, Jianjian Sun, Zheng Ge, Xiangyu Zhang, Daxin Jiang, Jingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (80.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MentalThink 是一种视觉-符号推理范式，让多模态大模型通过生成、渲染和解释 SVG 代码作为中间视觉表征，实现类似人类心理意象的多轮空间推理。采用两阶段训练（SFT + 多轮 RL），在空间理解和推理基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在空间推理和视觉思维方面存在不足，缺乏可验证的中间视觉表征来支持多步推理。人类在解决空间问题时会在脑海中构建心理意象，而现有模型缺乏这种"可视化思考"的机制。

Method: 提出 MentalThink 范式，核心是 think-with-SVG 流水线：模型学习生成 SVG 代码，通过确定性渲染得到矢量草图作为中间视觉表征，然后基于渲染结果进行多轮推理。训练采用两阶段框架：第一阶段用 SFT 进行 SVG 语法对齐，第二阶段用多轮强化学习（RL）鼓励模型迭代检查、修正和优化中间视觉假设。

Result: 在 VSIBench 上达到 55.1%，在 MindCube 上达到 76.0%，在多个空间理解和推理基准上取得最优性能，验证了可执行矢量图形作为可验证视觉工作空间的有效性。

Conclusion: MentalThink 表明，通过 SVG 代码生成作为中间视觉表征，MLLM 能够实现动态视角转换、视觉反思和组合场景构建，为多模态推理提供了一种新的可验证视觉工作空间范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MentalThink，一种通过生成 SVG 代码作为中间视觉表征进行多轮推理的视觉-符号推理范式，核心是多模态推理与思维链（CoT）。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel visual-symbolic reasoning paradigm using SVG as intermediate representation, relevant to multimodal reasoning and continuous visual token concepts. Signals: multimodal reasoning, SVG as intermediate visual representation, multi-turn RL training, spatial understanding benchmarks, visual-symbolic paradigm

Abstract: We introduce MentalThink, a visual-symbolic reasoning paradigm that equips Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) with an executable mechanism for "mental" visualization. The core of MentalThink is a think-with-SVG pipeline, where the model learns to generate, render, and interpret scalable vector graphics (SVG) code as an intermediate visual representation for multi-turn reasoning. By creating structured vector sketches, the model can externalize spatial hypotheses, inspect them through deterministic rendering, and reason within a constrained geometric space, effectively mimicking the human process of mental imagery. We instantiate this paradigm through a two-stage training framework, combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) for SVG syntactic alignment with multi-turn Reinforcement Learning (RL) to encourage iterative inspection, revision, and refinement of intermediate visual hypotheses. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that MentalThink achieves superior performance on spatial understanding and reasoning benchmarks (e.g., 55.1% on VSIBench, 76.0% on MindCube), showing that executable vector graphics provide a verifiable visual workspace for dynamic perspective taking, visual reflection, and compositional scene construction.

</details>


### [177] [Latent Visual Cache for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02607)
*Yongheng Zhang, Zhipeng Xu, Hao Wu, Yinghui Li, Di Yin, Xing Sun, Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (67.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent-VC（潜视频缓存），一种插入到解码器中的循环潜视觉缓存机制，用于在视频推理过程中保持紧凑的视觉记忆，缓解"视觉锚定衰减"问题。该方法通过监督对比缓存对齐和基于视觉锚定的 GRPO 训练，在六个视频基准上一致优于 CoT 和 SFT+GRPO 基线，且响应更短、准确率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理大模型采用"一次读取、多次生成"范式，在生成过程中视觉锚定逐渐衰减（Visual Anchoring Decay），导致推理后期缺乏视觉证据支撑。作者希望设计一种机制，在解码过程中持续保留紧凑的视觉记忆，从而提升视频推理的准确性和证据忠实性。

Method: 提出 Latent-VC，在 LMM 解码器中插入循环潜视觉缓存模块，通过解码器原生隐藏状态维护紧凑的视觉记忆。训练采用两阶段：1）监督对比缓存对齐（supervised contrastive cache alignment）；2）基于视觉锚定的 GRPO（vision-grounded GRPO），引入潜锚定奖励（latent grounding reward）。整个方法保持训练与推理严格一致。

Result: 基于 Qwen3.5-9B，在六个视频基准上一致超越强 CoT 和 SFT+GRPO 基线，在锚定密集和长视频任务上提升尤为明显。同时，Latent-VC 在更短响应长度下实现了更高准确率，表明潜视觉缓存通过保持视觉证据而非依赖更长的文本链来提升推理效果。

Conclusion: Latent-VC 通过循环潜视觉缓存有效缓解了视频推理中的视觉锚定衰减问题，在多个基准上取得一致提升，且推理更高效。该方法为视频推理中视觉记忆的持续保持提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Latent-VC，通过循环潜视觉缓存增强视频推理中的视觉证据保持，属于多模态推理范畴。虽然方法涉及连续潜空间缓存，但核心贡献是视频推理任务上的推理增强，而非连续语言模型或连续多模态模型本身。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel latent visual cache mechanism to solve visual anchoring decay in video reasoning, directly relevant to continuous multimodal representation and efficient inference. Signals: video reasoning, latent visual cache, visual anchoring decay, continuous multimodal representation, efficient inference

Abstract: Video reasoning requires Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to remain grounded in dense evidence, yet existing systems largely adopt "read-once, generate-many" paradigm, in which visual grounding weakens during generation. This phenomenon has been widely observed and is known as Visual Anchoring Decay. To fill this gap, we introduce Latent Video Cache (Latent-VC), a recurrent latent visual cache inserted into the decoder to preserve compact visual memories throughout reasoning. The cache is trained with supervised contrastive cache alignment and vision-grounded GRPO with a latent grounding reward, while maintaining strict train-inference alignment through native decoder hidden states. Built on Qwen3.5-9B, Latent-VC consistently outperforms strong CoT and SFT+GRPO baselines across six video benchmarks, with especially clear gains on grounding-intensive and long-video tasks. In addition, it also achieves higher accuracy with substantially shorter responses, suggesting that latent visual caching improves video reasoning by preserving visual evidence rather than relying on longer textual chains.

</details>


### [178] [Learning Taxonomic Trees with Hierarchical Representation Regularization for Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02909)
*Hulingxiao He, Zhi Tan, Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiR²，一种即插即用的层次表示正则化方法，通过语义感知的视觉树构建、双曲蕴含损失和判别性分散损失，提升大多模态模型在层次化视觉识别中的分类学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多模态模型（LMM）在微调时仅依赖语言建模目标，缺乏显式的分类学感知正则化，导致层次化视觉识别（HVR）一致性较低，即模型难以正确识别概念间的语义层次关系。

Method: 提出 HiR² 框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 语义感知视觉树构建——从 LLM 中间层提取由文本线索引导的粗到细视觉特征；(2) 分类学蕴含损失——利用 Lorentz 模型中的双曲蕴含锥强制层次结构；(3) 判别性分散损失——在单位球面上促进语义相似嵌入的角分离，同时不干扰径向层次结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiR² 能有效捕获分类学结构，在不同 LMM 和微调方法上均显著提升层次化视觉识别一致性。

Conclusion: HiR² 作为一种简单有效的即插即用正则化器，能够增强 LMM 对分类学知识的建模能力，提升层次化视觉识别的性能，且具有良好的通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注大多模态模型（LMM）的层次化视觉识别一致性，通过引入层次表示正则化（HiR²）来增强模型对分类学结构（taxonomic tree）的推理能力。这属于多模态推理范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Novel regularization for hierarchical visual recognition, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous models and generation. Signals: hierarchical visual recognition, taxonomic entailment loss, hyperbolic embedding, LMM fine-tuning, not generation-focused

Abstract: Taxonomies provide key information about the semantic relationships between concepts and the inherent organization of vision and language. Despite their impressive capabilities, large multimodal models (LMMs) often lack taxonomic knowledge, leading to low hierarchical visual recognition (HVR) consistency. These models typically only rely on language modeling objectives during fine-tuning and lack explicit taxonomy-aware regularization. To address this, we propose Hierarchical Representation Regularization ($HiR^2$), a simple plug-and-play regularizer that improves hierarchical consistency in LMMs. Specifically, we introduce a semantic-aware visual tree construction framework that extracts coarse-to-fine visual features from intermediate LLM layers guided by textual cues. The regularizer combines two complementary objectives: a taxonomic entailment loss that enforces hierarchy via hyperbolic entailment cones in the Lorentz model, and a discriminative dispersive loss that promotes angular separation of semantically similar embeddings on the unit sphere without disturbing the radial hierarchical structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $HiR^2$ effectively captures taxonomic structures across diverse LMMs and fine-tuning methods. Code is available at .

</details>


### [179] [Incentivizing Vision Language Models to Search for Long Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02959)
*Harsh Goel, S P Sharan, Sahil Shah, Minkyu Choi, Joungbin An, Kristen Grauman, Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VSeek 是一个基于强化学习后训练的智能体框架，将长视频问答从单次感知任务转化为多轮检索过程。它通过神经符号方法将自然语言问题编译为时序逻辑规范，提供可验证的奖励信号，从而优化搜索查询和推理能力，在长视频理解基准上提升了 Pass@1 和 Pass@4 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答（LVQA）面临两大挑战：一是视频内容过长，单次前向感知难以定位相关信息；二是缺乏可验证的奖励信号来指导强化学习后训练，限制了 RL 在长视频理解中的应用。

Method: VSeek 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 自然语言驱动的搜索机制，将长视频问答转化为多轮检索过程；(2) 神经符号方法，将复杂用户查询编译为形式化时序逻辑规范，分解为原子视觉基元（关键对象、活动及其时序关系）的检查清单；(3) 基于这些可验证视觉元素的密集奖励信号进行 RL 后训练，优化搜索查询生成和检索片段推理。

Result: 在长视频理解基准上，VSeek 相比基础模型将 Pass@1 分数提升最多 8%，Pass@4 分数提升 15%。

Conclusion: 通过将可验证的神经符号奖励引入 RL 后训练，VSeek 有效提升了长视频问答的检索和推理能力，证明了将 RL 从符号推理领域扩展到长视频理解的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VSeek 框架，将长视频问答转化为多轮检索过程，并利用强化学习后训练优化搜索查询与推理。这属于多模态推理（长视频推理+检索增强推理），与 canonical 子主题"多模态推理与思维链"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel RL-based agentic framework for long-video QA with neuro-symbolic rewards, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: RL post-training for video understanding, neuro-symbolic temporal logic rewards, long-video QA agent, multimodal reasoning, not continuous token or generation focused

Abstract: We introduce VSeek, an agentic framework that transforms long-video question answering (LVQA) from a passive, single-pass perception task into a multi-turn retrieval process. VSeek utilizes a natural language-driven search to identify relevant context within long videos and is post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to jointly formulate targeted search queries and reason over retrieved clips for LVQA. While RL post-training has revolutionized reasoning in symbolic domains such as mathematics and code, its application to long-video understanding remains hindered by a lack of verified rewards. To ensure that the retrieved context is relevant, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic approach that bridges open-ended natural language with discrete visual verification. Specifically, complex user queries are compiled into formal temporal logic specifications for systematically decomposing natural language questions into a definitive checklist of required atomic visual primitives, such as key objects and activities, along with their temporal ordering. These systematically derived grounding events provide the critical feedback signal for RL post-training, enabling dense, verifiable rewards based on the successful retrieval of these specific visual elements rather than relying entirely on outcome-only answer accuracy. By explicitly optimizing for this verifiable evidence-seeking behavior, VSeek improves Pass@1 scores by up to 8% and Pass@4 scores by 15% on long-video understanding benchmarks compared to base models. We open-source our code at .

</details>


### [180] [TacReasoner: A Dynamic Tactile-Language Framework for Interactive Reasoning in Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05131)
*Kailin Lyu, Di Wu, Long Xiao, Jianning Zeng, Jianwei He, Chang Lin, Lianyu Hu, Lin Shu, Jie Hao, Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TacReasoner 是一个动态触觉-语言交互推理框架，通过动态触觉编码器和首个触觉思维链数据集 TouchCoT-10k，实现了触觉信号的时序建模与结构化推理，在触觉常识推理任务上以 7B 参数超越 14B 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对智能系统在真实世界中的交互至关重要，但现有工作存在两个关键挑战：(1) 对动态触觉信号的建模不足，限制了其时序演化属性的推理能力；(2) 触觉基础模型缺乏显式推理机制，导致幻觉和推理不稳定。

Method: 提出 TacReasoner 框架，包含：(1) Dynamic-aware Tactile Encoder，增强动态触觉信号的感知与表征；(2) TouchCoT-10k，首个触觉链式思维数据集，用于结构化推理训练；(3) DynTac-Bench，系统评估动态触觉感知与真实世界常识推理的基准。

Result: TacReasoner 在多个数据集上达到与 SOTA 模型竞争的性能。仅用 7B 参数，就在大多数子任务上超越了 14B 的 VTV-LLM 模型，展示了其在触觉常识推理中的高效性和有效性。

Conclusion: 动态触觉建模与显式 CoT 推理机制的结合能有效提升触觉-语言推理的性能和稳定性，为触觉感知融入多模态智能系统提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了触觉模态的 Chain-of-Thought 推理框架 TacReasoner，并构建了首个触觉 CoT 数据集 TouchCoT-10k，属于多模态推理与思维链方向。虽然触觉模态较为特殊，但核心贡献是 CoT 推理机制在多模态（触觉-语言）中的应用，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Novel tactile chain-of-thought dataset and dynamic encoder, but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords not triggered. Signals: tactile reasoning, chain-of-thought dataset, dynamic tactile encoder, 7B beats 14B, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Among the five primary human senses, tactile is arguably the most fundamental to survival, as it enables the perception of physical contact and interaction in real-world environments. In this paper, we explore two key challenges of integrating tactile sensing into intelligent systems for multimodal reasoning: (i) insufficient modeling of dynamic tactile signals, which restricts reasoning over temporally evolving properties, and (ii) hallucination in tactile foundation models caused by the absence of explicit reasoning mechanisms, leading to unstable real-world inference. To address these challenges, we propose TacReasoner, a dynamic tactile-language framework for interactive reasoning in real-world scenarios. First, TacReasoner incorporates a Dynamic-aware Tactile Encoder to enhance the perception and representation of dynamic tactile signals. More importantly, we introduce TouchCoT-10k, the first tactile chain-of-thought dataset for structured reasoning over tactile inputs. Upon it, we establish DynTac-Bench to systematically evaluate dynamic tactile perception and real-world commonsense reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that TacReasoner achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models across multiple datasets. Notably, despite using only 7B parameters, TacReasoner outperforms the 14B VTV-LLM model on most subtasks, highlighting its effectiveness and efficiency in tactile commonsense reasoning.

</details>


### [181] [Attending to Multimodal Generation One Token at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03738)
*Varun Gupta, Vineet Gandhi, Makarand Tapaswi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (86.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 OTaT（One Token at a Time）分析方法，系统研究多模态大语言模型在生成过程中逐 token 的注意力转移模式，发现注意力在需要图像信息时达到峰值、任务转换时重新关注指令、生成后期更多关注已生成 token 等规律，并基于此提出测试时注意力干预方法以提升多模态任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 可解释性研究主要关注单层或电路层面的机制（where），而忽略了生成过程中逐 token 的多模态计算动态（when）。作者希望填补这一空白，理解模型在生成不同语义角色时如何动态切换注意力。

Method: 设计需要显式切换视觉和文本上下文的多模态任务，跟踪模型对图像、文本、指令和已生成 token 的注意力分布。在两种主流模型家族（LLaVA、Qwen-VL）的四个开源 MLLM 上进行实验，通过因果注意力阻断干预验证注意力模式的功能作用，并提出测试时注意力增强干预方法。

Result: 发现一致模式：注意力在需要图像信息的 token 处达到峰值；任务转换时重新关注指令；生成后期更多关注已生成 token。因果干预验证了这些模式的功能重要性。提出的测试时注意力干预显著提升了多模态任务性能。

Conclusion: MLLM 在生成过程中展现出系统性的注意力动态模式，理解这些模式有助于设计更有效的干预策略。简单的测试时注意力增强即可带来显著的性能提升，为未来 MLLM 可解释性和优化提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 在生成过程中逐 token 的注意力动态，分析模型何时关注图像、文本、指令和已生成 token，并提出测试时干预提升多模态任务性能。这属于多模态推理中关于推理过程动态分析的研究，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度相关。

Importance reason: Provides novel token-level attention dynamics analysis and test-time intervention for MLLMs, aligning with multimodal understanding and generation priorities. Signals: multimodal large language model, attention dynamics, test-time intervention, token-level analysis, multimodal generation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate responses autoregressively, integrating visual and linguistic information in an evolving context. Prior work on interpretability has focused on individual layers and circuits (where), leaving the token-level dynamics of multimodal computation during generation (when) underexplored. We address this gap and study attention shifts as per semantic role; tracking model attention to image, text, instruction, and previously generated tokens, One Token at a Time (OTaT). We introduce multimodal tasks that require explicit switching between visual and textual context within a single response. Across two mainstream model families and four open-weight MLLMs of varying sizes, we establish consistent patterns: attention to image peaks at tokens requiring image-derived information, instruction tokens are revisited during task transitions, and attention to previously generated tokens increases as the generation progresses. Causal attention blocking interventions validate the functional role of these trends. We profile model behavior under disrupted attention and observe responses falling back to language priors, or exhibiting cross-modal leakage, denial, or recovery. Finally, informed of the attention dynamics through our novel analysis, we propose a simple test-time intervention to boost attention to the relevant modality at the right time, significantly improving multimodal task performance.

</details>


### [182] [DGSeg: Dynamic Gating of Semantic-Spatial Guided Predictions for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04779)
*Ruizhe Zeng, Siyu Cao, Lu Zhang, Zhiyong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (69.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DGSeg提出了一种动态门控机制，将MLLM推理得到的语义线索和空间线索分别送入独立分割分支，再通过轻量级动态门控模块自适应融合预测结果，抑制噪声和冲突区域，在ReasonSeg等基准上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法将MLLM推理得到的丰富信息压缩为稀疏线索（如点或框）来引导分割模型，但稀疏线索难以准确保留推理意图，引入歧义和噪声。虽然多个互补线索可以丰富目标信息，但现有方法将它们联合输入单一分割过程，导致歧义或错误线索影响整个预测。

Method: DGSeg框架包含：(1) MLLM同时推理目标身份和空间位置，生成互补的语义线索和空间线索；(2) 两条独立分割分支分别处理语义和空间线索；(3) 轻量级动态门控模块，通过相对分支质量监督训练，自适应融合两个分支的预测，抑制噪声或冲突区域。

Result: 在ReasonSeg验证集和测试集上分别达到69.6%和67.3% gIoU，在多个基准上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: DGSeg通过解耦语义和空间线索的独立分割路径并引入动态门控融合，有效缓解了推理分割中线索歧义和噪声问题，验证了多线索分离处理与自适应融合的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理分割（reasoning segmentation），利用MLLM进行视觉语言推理并生成分割掩码，属于多模态推理范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）涵盖多模态推理、视觉推理等内容，与该论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning segmentation with MLLMs, not directly aligned with priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: reasoning segmentation, dynamic gating, MLLM-guided cues, semantic-spatial fusion, ReasonSeg benchmark

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to predict pixel-wise masks for targets given complex language queries. Existing approaches leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for vision-language reasoning and generate intermediate target cues (e.g., points or boxes) to guide a segmentation model. However, compressing rich reasoning into sparse cues often introduces ambiguity and noise, preventing these cues from accurately preserving the reasoning intent. While multiple complementary cues can enrich target information, existing methods typically feed them jointly into a single segmentation process, allowing ambiguous or erroneous cues to affect the entire prediction. Therefore, we propose DGSeg, a reasoning segmentation framework that learns to fuse predictions guided by semantic and spatial cues. Specifically, the MLLM jointly reasons about both target identity and spatial location, producing complementary semantic and spatial cues that are fed into separate segmentation branches. Their predictions are adaptively integrated by a lightweight dynamic gating module trained with relative branch-quality supervision to suppress noisy or conflicting regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DGSeg consistently outperforms strong baselines on multiple benchmarks and achieves 69.6% and 67.3% gIoU on the challenging ReasonSeg validation and test splits. Code is available at .

</details>


### [183] [TimeThink: Reasoning with Time for Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05089)
*Handong Li, Longteng Guo, Zikang Liu, Dongze Hao, Yepeng Tang, Zijia Zhao, Jie Jiang, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (69.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TimeThink 提出一种面向视频 LLM 的强化学习框架，通过步骤级时间过程奖励显式引导模型在推理中发现时序证据，并构建了 TimeThink-RFT-20K 数据集，在视频推理、时序定位和通用视频理解任务上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 LLM 的强化学习对齐方法通常仅对最终预测结果提供奖励（outcome-based reward），缺乏对模型如何在中间推理步骤中发现相关时序证据的引导，导致模型难以准确定位视频中的关键时间区间。

Method: 将每个推理步骤视为引用一个候选时间区间（temporal clue step），提出步骤级时间过程奖励（step-wise temporal process reward）为这些线索提供局部信用分配，并设计联合过程-结果优化目标（joint process-outcome optimization）来平衡推理忠实性与任务正确性。同时构建了 TimeThink-RFT-20K 数据集，自动标注时序证据片段。

Result: 在视频推理、时序定位和通用视频理解等多个基准上，TimeThink 一致提升了时序定位和推理性能，在开源视频 RL 模型中达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式引导视频 LLM 在推理过程中发现时序证据是提升视频推理能力的有效途径，步骤级过程奖励比纯结果奖励能提供更细粒度的监督信号。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TimeThink，一个面向视频 LLM 的强化学习框架，通过步骤级时间过程奖励引导模型在推理中发现时序证据。这属于多模态推理（视频推理）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel step-wise temporal process reward for video LLM reasoning, directly advancing multimodal reasoning and temporal grounding. Signals: video LLM reasoning, reinforcement learning, temporal process reward, temporal grounding, state-of-the-art results

Abstract: Video reasoning requires models to identify and verify temporally localized evidence within long video sequences. Recent Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have shown promising reasoning abilities when aligned with reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches typically rely on outcome-based rewards that supervise only the final prediction. Such supervision provides limited guidance on how models should discover the relevant temporal evidence during intermediate reasoning. In this work, we propose TimeThink, a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly guides temporal evidence discovery in Video-LLMs. Our key idea is to treat temporal clue steps as the fundamental optimization primitive of video reasoning, where each reasoning step references a candidate time interval in the video. We introduce a step-wise temporal process reward that provides localized credit assignment for these clues and a joint process--outcome optimization objective that balances reasoning fidelity with task correctness. To enable scalable training, we construct TimeThink-RFT-20K, a dataset with automatically derived temporal evidence segments. Extensive experiments across video reasoning, temporal grounding, and general video understanding benchmarks show that TimeThink consistently improves both temporal localization and reasoning performance, achieving state-of-the-art results among open-source video RL models.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_retrieval'></div>

## 多模态检索 [[Back]](#toc)
