### [184] [VideoSearcher: Empowering Video Deep Research with Multi-Tool Agentic Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02927)
*Zhenkun Gao, Yicheng Bao, Jinlong Peng, Xueheng Li, Theo Huang, Bangwei Liu, Kunquan Li, Zhenye Gan, Tao Hu, Chengjun Xie, Mingqian Yang, Xuanhua He, Zhizhong Zhang, Xin Tan, Chengjie Wang, Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VideoSearcher 是一个基于强化学习（BiSPO）的多工具智能体框架，赋予视觉语言模型时序定位、空间聚焦与多模态搜索能力，用于开放世界视频深度研究（VDR），并构建了首个 VDR 基准 VideoSearch-QA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解局限于封闭上下文感知，缺乏开放世界证据探索能力；多模态搜索智能体主要针对静态图像，而现有 VDR 基准依赖文本检索，丢弃了关键视觉信息。

Method: 提出 VideoSearcher 闭环智能体框架，统一时序定位、空间聚焦与多模态搜索于单一推理轨迹中；提出 BiSPO（双分支序列策略优化）强化学习算法，将工具调用优化与答案准确性优化解耦，提供稳定的学习信号。

Result: 在 VideoSearch-QA 及多个面向搜索和多模态理解的基准上，VideoSearcher 显著优于现有开源智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: VideoSearcher 通过多工具智能体推理与强化学习优化，有效提升了开放世界视频深度研究中的证据检索与推理能力，为视频理解从封闭感知走向开放探索提供了可行范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VideoSearcher，一个面向视频深度研究（VDR）的多工具智能体框架，核心是结合多模态搜索、时序定位与空间聚焦进行证据检索与推理。这属于多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）范畴，且该 subtopic 是 [CANONICAL] 条目，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Strong agentic framework for video deep research but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Reinforcement learning for tool use, Video deep research benchmark, Multimodal search agent, BiSPO algorithm, Temporal and spatial grounding

Abstract: Video understanding is moving beyond closed-context perception toward open-world evidence exploration, a paradigm formalized as Video Deep Research (VDR). However, existing multimodal search agents primarily target static images, and the current VDR benchmark relies on text-centric retrieval that discards crucial visual information. To address these limitations, we propose VideoSearcher, a closed-loop agentic framework that empowers Vision-Language Models with multi-tool reasoning for VDR. VideoSearcher unifies temporal localization, spatial focusing, and multimodal search within a single reasoning trajectory, enabling agents to progressively ground visual clues, retrieve relevant evidence, and synthesize answers. To optimize knowledge-intensive reasoning trajectories, we propose Bi-branch Sequence Policy Optimization (BiSPO), a reinforcement learning algorithm that decouples tool-invocation optimization from answer-accuracy optimization. This design provides stable learning signals for both evidence-grounded reasoning and purposeful tool use. Furthermore, we construct VideoSearch-QA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate open-world video information grounding and multimodal search-based reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VideoSearcher significantly outperforms prior open-source agentic baselines across various search-oriented and multimodal understanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [185] [Text as Partial Constraint: Core-Residual Alignment for Robust Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03143)
*Chengzhen Yu, Canran Xiao, Siyuan Ma, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Text as Partial Constraint (TPC)框架，将文本视为对图像的部分约束而非完整描述，通过核心-残差对齐（core-residual alignment）学习鲁棒的视觉-语言表征，在零样本识别、对抗鲁棒性和LVLM迁移上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言描述通常是不完整的（underspecified），导致视觉-语言相似度在释义和细节缺失时过于脆弱且过度自信。现有对比学习方法假设文本是对图像的完整描述，这在实际场景中不成立。

Method: 提出TPC框架：(1) 从多视角字幕中蒸馏出共识语义核心作为对齐目标；(2) 学习单视角核心预测器用于标准推理；(3) 显式抑制视觉-语言相似度依赖于正交的"未说出的残差"；(4) 不确定性感知的对比学习目标，在字幕视角不一致时软化对齐强度。

Result: 在ImageNet上达到81.42/64.05 Top-1干净/鲁棒准确率，Avg-14迁移套件上76.19/52.03；在LLaVA-1.5-7B上实现85.16 POPE F1和59.57 OKVQA准确率。

Conclusion: 将文本建模为部分约束是一种实用且原则性的方法，能够在语言监督不完整的情况下获得更可靠的视觉-语言表征。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Text as Partial Constraint (TPC)框架，核心是改进视觉-语言对齐的鲁棒性，属于跨模态表征学习与检索方向。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）涵盖跨模态检索与对齐，与该论文的开放词汇识别、检索、视觉-语言对齐等核心内容高度匹配。

Importance reason: Core-residual alignment for robust VLM is solid but not aligned with user's priority on continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: robust vision-language alignment, zero-shot recognition, adversarial robustness, LVLM transfer, not generative or continuous

Abstract: Vision-language alignment powers open-vocabulary recognition, retrieval, and LVLM grounding, yet natural captions are often underspecified, making similarity brittle and overly confident under paraphrase and omitted details. We aim to learn representations whose matching is stable across caption views and whose confidence reflects how strongly text constrains an image. We propose Text as Partial Constraint (TPC), a core-residual alignment framework that treats multi-view captions as incomplete supervision. It distills a consensus semantic core as the alignment target, learns a single-view core predictor for standard inference with one query, and explicitly discourages vision-language similarity from depending on the orthogonal unsaid residual. An uncertainty-aware contrastive objective further softens alignment when caption views disagree, reducing overconfident updates under weak language constraints. Across zero-shot recognition and adversarial robustness, TPC achieves 81.42/64.05 Top-1 clean/robust accuracy on ImageNet and 76.19/52.03 on an Avg-14 transfer suite, while improving LVLM transfer with 85.16 POPE F1 and 59.57 OKVQA accuracy under an LLaVA-1.5-7B stack. These results suggest that modeling text as a partial constraint is a practical and principled route to more reliable vision-language representations under underspecified language supervision.

</details>


### [186] [Rethinking Brain Decoding with CLIP: The Role of Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03165)
*Byeongseo Bok, Futa Waseda, Jun Liu, Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗鲁棒性是否有助于提升CLIP在fMRI脑解码中的表现。通过固定fMRI解码器、仅替换目标表征（标准CLIP vs 鲁棒变体），在NSD和GOD数据集上的fMRI-图像检索和零样本分类任务中，鲁棒CLIP一致优于标准CLIP，表明对抗鲁棒性可作为脑解码的有用准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP因其丰富的视觉-语言嵌入空间被广泛用于fMRI脑解码，但CLIP并非为神经对齐优化，其表征可能包含仅部分反映在大脑活动中的统计预测线索，限制了解码性能。作者希望探究对抗训练产生的鲁棒表征是否能更好地与大脑活动对齐，从而提升解码效果。

Method: 固定fMRI解码器不变，仅将目标表征从标准CLIP替换为对抗鲁棒CLIP变体（如RobustCLIP），在NSD和GOD数据集上进行fMRI-图像检索和零样本分类任务，通过多种对齐指标（如检索准确率、分类准确率、表征相似性分析）进行对比评估，并利用归因分析探究特征重要性的变化。

Result: 鲁棒CLIP变体在fMRI-图像检索和零样本分类任务上一致优于标准CLIP，多个对齐指标均有提升。归因分析显示标准CLIP与鲁棒变体之间的特征重要性一致性较低，表明对抗鲁棒性重新组织了视觉表征中的特征重要性。

Conclusion: 目标表征的选择显著影响神经解码性能，对抗鲁棒性可作为脑解码的一个有用准则。对抗训练抑制了非鲁棒特征，促进了更稳定、更具感知结构的表征，从而更好地与大脑活动对齐。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于fMRI的脑解码，核心任务是将fMRI信号与CLIP的视觉-语言表征对齐，实现fMRI-图像检索和零样本分类，属于跨模态检索范畴。因此映射到[多模态检索]（multimodal_retrieval）这一CANONICAL subtopic。

Importance reason: The paper's focus on adversarial robustness for CLIP-based brain decoding is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Brain decoding, CLIP, adversarial robustness, fMRI, zero-shot classification

Abstract: Brain decoding aims to uncover neural mechanisms by inferring stimulus-related representations from brain signals. In fMRI studies, this is typically achieved by mapping fMRI responses to the latent representations of computational models. Recently, CLIP has become a popular choice for brain decoding due to its rich vision--language embedding space. However, aligning fMRI signals with CLIP representations remains challenging. As CLIP is not explicitly optimized for neural alignment, its representations may capture statistically predictive cues that are only partially reflected in brain activity, limiting decoding performance. In this paper, we investigate whether adversarially robust representations improve neural decoding with CLIP. Adversarial training suppresses non-robust features and promotes more stable, perceptually structured representations, which may better align with brain activity. We evaluate this by fixing the fMRI decoder and varying only the target representation (standard CLIP vs. robust variants) on fMRI-image retrieval and zero-shot classification tasks across NSD and GOD datasets. Empirical results show that this simple change consistently improves task performance and yields stronger alignment across multiple metrics. Attribution analysis further reveals consistently low agreement between standard CLIP and its robust variants, suggesting that adversarial robustness reorganizes feature importance in the visual representation. These findings suggest that the choice of target representation influences neural decoding performance and that adversarial robustness may serve as a useful criterion for brain decoding.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Post-Quantization: Native Hash Learning with a Dedicated HASH Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03328)
*Xinze Liu, Ding Wang, Hengjie Zhu, Dayan Wu*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HashViT，一种基于 Vision Transformer 的原生哈希学习框架。通过引入专用的 HASH token（包含 Hash Register 和 Semantic Workspace）并在 Transformer 层间逐步优化，实现二进制哈希码的端到端学习，避免了传统后量化范式的特征-代码不一致问题，在多个基准上取得 SOTA 检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度哈希方法（CNN/ViT）采用后量化范式：先学习连续视觉特征，再通过末层哈希投影或二值化操作生成二进制码。这种晚期代码生成导致连续优化空间与离散汉明空间之间存在特征-代码不一致（feature-to-code discrepancy），限制了检索性能。

Method: 提出 HashViT 框架：(1) 在 ViT 中引入专用的 HASH token，分解为 Hash Register（直接生成二进制码）和 Semantic Workspace（保留辅助连续语义）；(2) 设计轻量级 Hash Refinement Adapter，在 Transformer 各层间逐步优化 Hash Register；(3) 统一优化目标：可学习语义中心监督 + 类 token 相似度蒸馏 + 量化正则化，使 HASH token 编码语义结构化且紧凑的二进制表示。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的图像检索基准上，HashViT 取得了 SOTA 或极具竞争力的检索性能，同时保持了紧凑汉明码的高效性。

Conclusion: 通过将哈希学习从末层投影转变为 backbone 内部的 token 演化，HashViT 有效解决了特征-代码不一致问题，为深度哈希提供了一种新的原生学习范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大规模图像检索中的深度哈希方法，属于跨模态/多模态检索范畴，与现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel native hash learning method for retrieval, but the topic is outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: Novel hash token design, addresses feature-code discrepancy, SOTA retrieval performance, cs.IR/cs.CV, no overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Efficient large-scale image retrieval requires compact representations that preserve semantic similarity under fast Hamming-space search. Deep hashing is appealing, but most existing CNN- and ViT-based methods still follow a post-quantization paradigm, where continuous visual features are first learned and binary codes are then produced by a terminal hash projection or binarization operation. This late code generation creates a feature-to-code discrepancy between the continuously optimized representation space and the discrete Hamming space used for retrieval. To address this limitation, we propose HashViT, a Vision Transformer framework for native hash token learning. Instead of treating hashing as a terminal readout, HashViT introduces a dedicated HASH token that serves as a persistent, hash-oriented retrieval state inside the transformer. The HASH token is structurally decomposed into a Hash Register for direct binary code generation and a Semantic Workspace for preserving auxiliary continuous semantics. To enable effective workspace-to-register interaction, we further design a lightweight Hash Refinement Adapter that progressively refines the Hash Register across transformer layers. As a result, binary-oriented representations are formed through token evolution within the backbone, rather than being abruptly induced by an output-level projection. HashViT is optimized with a unified objective that combines learnable semantic center supervision, class-token similarity distillation, and quantization regularization, encouraging the HASH token to encode semantically structured and compact binary representations. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks demonstrate that HashViT achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive retrieval performance while preserving the efficiency of compact Hamming codes. Code is available at .

</details>


### [188] [Do All Visual Tokens Matter Equally? Object-Evidence Preserving Token Merging for Vision-Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04605)
*Suhyeong Park, Junha Jung, Jungwoo Park, Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (66.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SaMer 是一种面向多向量视觉-语言检索的物体感知 token 合并框架，利用训练时的物体标注作为合并先验，将图像侧 token 压缩为 K 个代表性质心，在去除 93%+ token 的同时提升检索性能，并保持原有的后期交互接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉-语言检索（如 ColPali）通过最大相似度后期交互保留细粒度视觉证据，但密集的图像侧 token 导致存储和评分成本高昂。现有 token 压缩方法可能移除或混淆物体级和区域级证据，影响后续查询 token 的选择能力。

Method: 提出 SaMer（Object-aware token Merging），一种物体感知的 token 合并框架。在训练阶段利用物体标注作为合并先验，避免跨实例混合；仅适配共享投影层，冻结视觉和语言主干；推理时无需边界框或检测器，将图像侧 post-projector token 压缩为 K 个代表性质心，同时保留原始后期交互接口。

Result: 在 K=64 时，SaMer 移除超过 93% 的图像侧 token，ColPali 存储压缩 16.09 倍，同时在 Flickr30K 和 MSCOCO 上提升 R@1 指标。相比剪枝和特征池化基线，SaMer 表现更优，并展现出更强的短语级定位能力。

Conclusion: 高效的 multi-vector 检索不仅依赖于减少 token 数量，更在于保留未来查询 token 需要选择的证据。物体感知的 token 合并能够有效保留可查询的物体证据，从而在压缩的同时提升检索性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉-语言检索（Vision-Language Retrieval）中的token压缩方法，属于多模态检索范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配，故直接复用。

Importance reason: Token merging for retrieval is useful but not central to your priority subtopics on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: token merging, vision-language retrieval, object-aware compression, late interaction, ColPali

Abstract: Multi-vector vision-language retrieval preserves fine-grained visual evidence through maximum-similarity late interaction, but dense image-side tokens make storage and scoring expensive. Existing token compression methods reduce this cost, yet they can remove or collapse object- and region-level evidence that future query tokens may need to select. We propose SaMer, an object-aware token merging framework that compresses image-side post-projector tokens into $K$ representative centroids while preserving the original late-interaction interface. SaMer uses object annotations only during training as a merge prior to discourage cross-instance mixing, requires no ground-truth bounding boxes or detectors at inference time, and adapts only the shared projection layer with frozen vision and language backbones. With $K=64$, SaMer removes more than 93% of image-side tokens and reduces ColPali storage by $16.09\times$, while improving R@1 on Flickr30K and MSCOCO. These gains arise because object-aware merging preserves query-selectable object evidence that pruning or feature-only pooling can remove or collapse. SaMer also outperforms compression baselines and shows stronger phrase-level grounding, suggesting that efficient multi-vector retrieval depends not only on reducing token count, but on preserving the evidence future query tokens need to select.

</details>


### [189] [QSVideo: Query-Conditioned Semantic Temporal Retrieval for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04559)
*Wei Ao, Lan Wang, Vishnu Naresh Boddeti*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: QSVideo 是一个统一的视频检索框架，通过查询条件语义排序器（QSRanker）将任意问题转化为检索友好的查询，并沿物体、动作、位置三个维度估计结构化相关性，结合多样性优化和时序对齐策略，显著提升长视频和流式视频下 VLM 的理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在长视频理解中性能下降，因为与查询无关的视频片段会混淆语言组件。多模态检索方法存在相关性估计偏差、多样性不足和时序坍塌等问题，亟需一个系统性的解决方案。

Method: 提出 QSVideo 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) QSRanker——将任意问题转化为检索友好的查询，沿物体、动作、位置三个维度估计结构化相关性；(2) QSRetrieval——联合优化相关性和多样性以选择更具信息量的帧；(3) 针对长视频和流式视频的时序对齐策略，提高证据召回率。

Result: 在长视频和流式视频基准上，QSVideo 在严格的帧数限制下显著提升了视频 VLM 的性能，验证了其在相关性、多样性和时序建模方面的有效性。

Conclusion: QSVideo 通过系统性地解决相关性、多样性和时序建模问题，为视频理解中的多模态检索提供了一种统一有效的框架，能够大幅提升 VLM 在长视频场景下的表现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 QSVideo，一个面向视频理解的查询条件语义时序检索框架，核心是多模态检索（query-conditioned semantic retrieval for video understanding），属于多模态检索范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video retrieval for VLM understanding, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: video retrieval, query-conditioned ranking, long video understanding, VLM enhancement, diversity optimization

Abstract: The performance of vision-language models (VLMs) in video understanding declines with increasing video duration, as video moments unrelated to the query confuse their language components. Multimodal retrieval has emerged as a critical component of video understanding, addressing this challenge by localizing key visual evidence. However, existing multimodal retrieval methods suffer from biased relevance estimation, limited diversity, and temporal collapse. In this paper, we propose QSVideo, a unified framework that systematically addresses relevance, diversity, and temporal modeling in video retrieval. We first introduce a query-conditioned semantic ranker, QSRanker, which reformulates arbitrary questions into retrieval-friendly queries and estimates structured relevance along object, action, and location dimensions. Building upon this, we design QSRetrieval to jointly optimize relevance and diversity for more informative frame selection. Moreover, we propose temporal alignment strategies tailored for both long and streaming videos to improve evidence recall. Extensive experiments on long and streaming video benchmarks demonstrate that QSVideo greatly enhances video VLM performance under strict frame limit constraints. The code is available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_benchmark'></div>

### [190] [Echoes of Unrest: A Multimodal NLP Framework for Early Warning of Fake News and Violence-Driven Mob Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02734)
*Md. Maruf Bangabashi, Tahmid Hasan, Golam Mahmud, Md. Mostafijur Rahman, Md. Toufiqur Rahman, Jahanur Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (48.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多语言、多模态的NLP框架，融合XLM-RoBERTa文本编码、CLIP视觉编码和多头注意力机制，结合讽刺检测与地理空间元数据，在13.8万条孟加拉语和英语混合数据集上实现98%的假新闻早期检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上虚假信息和煽动性内容的快速传播已导致现实世界的社会动荡和暴力事件，而传统的事实核查速度往往跟不上虚假信息的传播。现有方法多局限于单语言或单模态，缺乏对多语言环境和多模态信息的综合利用。

Method: 构建了包含138,256条孟加拉语和英语样本的融合数据集；使用XLM-RoBERTa进行多语言文本表示，CLIP进行视觉嵌入，通过多头注意力机制进行多模态融合，并引入讽刺检测和地理空间元数据作为辅助特征。

Result: 在分层抽样的30%子集上达到98%的测试准确率，具有较高的精确率和召回率，验证了多模态方法在早期虚假信息检测中的有效性，并展示了地理空间信号对预测现实世界事件升级的附加价值。

Conclusion: 多模态融合方法在早期虚假信息检测中效果显著，地理空间元数据等辅助特征能有效提升对现实世界暴力升级的预测能力，为构建早期预警系统提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了多语言多模态假新闻检测框架，融合文本（XLM-RoBERTa）和视觉（CLIP）特征进行早期虚假信息检测，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因为其核心是构建数据集和评测框架用于假新闻检测任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on fake news detection with multimodal fusion, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal NLP, fake news detection, XLM-RoBERTa + CLIP, sarcasm detection, geospatial metadata

Abstract: Rapid growth in social media has transformed global communication by enabling fast information exchange, but it has also accelerated the spread of misinformation. Fake news, manipulated content, and provocative narratives are increasingly linked to social unrest, political instability, and mob violence. Incidents in South Asia and elsewhere demonstrate how false information disseminated via platforms such as Facebook and WhatsApp can trigger real-world harm, often spreading faster than fact-checking efforts can respond. To address this challenge, this chapter presents a multilingual, multimodal Natural Language Processing (NLP) framework for early detection of misinformation and violence-prone dynamics. A fused dataset of 138,256 Bangla and English samples was created by combining multiple benchmark datasets. The framework integrates XLM-RoBERTa for multilingual text representation, CLIP for visual embedding, and a multi-head attention mechanism for multimodal fusion, enhanced with auxiliary features such as sarcasm and geospatial metadata. Experiments on a stratified 30% subset achieved 98% test accuracy with strong precision and recall. The outcomes show the efficacy of multimodal approaches in early misinformation detection and highlight the added value of geospatial signals for anticipating real-world escalation.

</details>


### [191] [BanglaMemeEvidence: A Multimodal Benchmark Dataset for Explanatory Evidence Detection in Bengali Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03981)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria, Mukaffi Bin Moin, Md. Mahfuzur Rahman, Pronay Debnath, Asif Iftekher Fahim, Faisal Muhammad Shah*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BanglaMemeEvidence，一个包含 2,917 个孟加拉语模因的多模态基准数据集，用于模因解释性证据检测任务（MemeEvidenceDetect）。同时提出了 BengaliMemeEvidenceNet 混合多模态框架，整合文本和视觉特征进行模因表示，F1 达到 0.74。这是首个针对孟加拉语模因证据检测的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模因研究主要关注有害内容检测、网络欺凌识别和情感分析等情感维度，但缺乏对模因深层语境理解的研究。特别是对于孟加拉语等低资源语言，模因的上下文理解和解释性证据检测尚未被探索。

Method: 构建了 BanglaMemeEvidence 数据集（2,917 个孟加拉语模因），每个模因标注了 OCR 文本、模因上下文、证据句子和相关性分数。提出了 BengaliMemeEvidenceNet 混合多模态框架，融合文本和视觉特征进行模因表示，用于证据检测任务。

Result: BengaliMemeEvidenceNet 在证据检测任务上取得了 0.74 的 F1 分数。

Conclusion: 本文首次关注孟加拉语模因的证据检测任务，构建了基准数据集并验证了混合多模态方法的有效性，为低资源语言的模因分析研究迈出了重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个孟加拉语模因（meme）的多模态基准数据集 BanglaMemeEvidence，并定义了证据检测任务。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态数据集和评测任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on a low-resource language meme benchmark, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Bengali meme dataset, evidence detection task, low-resource language, multimodal benchmark, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Memes have become influential communication tools on social media, combining viral visuals with concise messaging to convey impactful ideas. While substantial research has examined the affective dimensions of memes, key challenges such as detecting harmful content, identifying cyberbullying, and performing accurate sentiment analysis remain critical, largely due to the need for deeper contextual understanding. In this paper, we introduce MemeEvidenceDetect, a hybrid task aimed at analyzing a meme and its contextual information to identify specific sentences that explain or elucidate its meaning and humor. To support this task, we present BanglaMemeEvidence, a curated dataset of 2,917 Bengali memes, emphasizing its significance as a resource for the Bangla language. Each meme is annotated with natural language explanations, including Meme OCR, Meme Context, and Evidence Sentences, alongside relevance scores that reflect the relationship between a meme and its corresponding annotations. To address the gap in dynamically inferring a meme's context, we propose BengaliMemeEvidenceNet, a hybrid multimodal framework that integrates textual and visual features for comprehensive meme representation. Our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of BengaliMemeEvidenceNet, achieving an F1 score of 0.74. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to focus on evidence detection in Bengali memes, marking a notable step forward in the analysis of memes in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [192] [Criterion-Conditional In-Context Learning: Evaluating Criterion-Shift Adaptation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02575)
*Kaiyun Yang, Ruilin Yang, Zhimin Yao, J. Wang, Wei Ge*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Criterion-Conditional In-Context Learning (CC-ICL) 新设置，要求视觉语言模型在固定任务语义下根据上下文推断潜在准则并调整预测。构建了 CC-Bench 多领域基准，并提出 Criterion Invariance 和 Criterion Sensitivity 两个互补指标。实验发现大多数模型存在刚性边界偏差，而简单的多准则训练策略可显著缓解该偏差，使 7B 模型超越闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 ICL 设置中任务诱导后决策准则固定不变，但现实应用中许多任务的高层意图稳定而决策准则会随具体需求变化。现有模型缺乏在这种准则变化情境下灵活调整的能力，因此需要新的评测设置和基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 提出 CC-ICL 设置，模型需从上下文中推断潜在准则并据此调整预测。构建 CC-Bench 多领域基准，采用双层数据层次结构（任务级 + 准则级），使同一任务在不同准则下具有不同的真实标签。提出 Criterion Invariance（准则不变性，衡量准则不变时预测稳定性）和 Criterion Sensitivity（准则敏感性，衡量准则变化时预测适应性）两个互补指标。实验采用简单多准则训练策略来缓解模型偏差。

Result: 大多数现有 VLM 在 CC-Bench 上表现出刚性边界偏差，难以将决策与潜在准则对齐。简单的多准则训练策略能显著减少这种偏差，提升 Criterion Sensitivity，使 7B 规模模型在准则适应能力上超越闭源模型，且不降低通用多模态性能。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在准则变化情境下的上下文学习能力存在显著不足，但通过多准则训练可以有效改善。CC-ICL 设置和 CC-Bench 为评估和提升 VLM 的灵活决策能力提供了新的视角和工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 CC-ICL 新设置和 CC-Bench 多领域基准，用于评测视觉语言模型在准则变化下的上下文学习能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Novel evaluation paradigm for VLM in-context learning, but tangential to user's priority on continuous/autoregressive generation and world models. Signals: Criterion-conditional ICL, VLM evaluation, rigid boundary bias, multi-criterion training, CC-Bench benchmark

Abstract: Vision-language models can perform new tasks without parameter updates through in-context learning (ICL), whose core mechanism is utilizing the support set for task induction. In the standard ICL setting, once the task is induced, its decision criterion remains fixed. However, in real-world applications, many tasks exhibit a stable high-level intent, while their decision criteria shift according to specific requirements. Thus, we introduce a new setting, denoted as Criterion-Conditional In-Context Learning (CC-ICL), where models must infer the latent criterion from context and adjust predictions accordingly under fixed task semantics. To evaluate this capability, we propose two complementary metrics, Criterion Invariance and Criterion Sensitivity, capturing the model's robustness and adaptability under criterion shifts. We further construct CC-Bench, a multi-domain benchmark that supports evaluation under the CC-ICL setting. By employing a dual-level data hierarchy, CC-Bench enables legitimate ground-truth variation conditioned on the active criterion even when the task remains fixed. Experiments on CC-Bench reveal that most models exhibit a rigid boundary bias, struggling to align their decisions with the latent criterion. We also find that even a simple multi-criterion training strategy can significantly reduce this bias, improving Criterion Sensitivity and enabling 7B-scale models to surpass proprietary models without degrading general multimodal performance.

</details>


### [193] [MTEB-PT: A Text Embedding Benchmark for Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04581)
*Tardelli Ronan Coelho Stekel*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MTEB-PT，首个面向巴西葡萄牙语的原生文本嵌入评测基准，包含22个任务、7个类别，评估了93个模型（73个开源+20个商业API），并提供了统计显著性分析、项目反应理论区分度分析和跨排行榜相关性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄牙语文本嵌入缺乏专用评测基准，现有评测依赖翻译语料（如英文MS MARCO）或稀疏的多语言覆盖，原生任务分散且未整合，导致无法准确评估葡萄牙语嵌入模型的实际表现。

Method: 构建22个原生巴西葡萄牙语任务（涵盖分类、多标签分类、配对分类、语义文本相似度、聚类、检索和重排序7个类别），严格排除翻译数据。评估93个模型（73个开源权重模型+20个商业API），使用bootstrap置信区间、配对bootstrap显著性检验、基于项目反应理论的任务区分度分析以及跨排行榜相关性分析。

Result: 排行榜可清晰区分约十几个模型层级，但前6名统计上无法排序；一个开源可自托管模型达到领先层级，表明高质量葡萄牙语嵌入不依赖商业API；模型在全球多语言排行榜上的排名与葡萄牙语排名仅中等相关（Spearman ρ=0.75），说明原生基准能衡量多语言排行榜无法捕捉的信息。

Conclusion: MTEB-PT为巴西葡萄牙语文本嵌入提供了首个原生评测基准，揭示了多语言排行榜不能完全反映本地语言性能，并证明开源模型可以达到商业API水平。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了面向巴西葡萄牙语的文本嵌入评测基准 MTEB-PT，属于评测基准类工作。虽然论文聚焦于文本嵌入而非多模态，但"多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）是现有[CANONICAL] subtopic中最为匹配的，因为该 subtopic 涵盖评测基准、评测协议等广义评测工作，且论文所属方向（cs.CL, cs.IR）与多模态理解与生成方向有交叉。论文构建了22个原生葡萄牙语任务、评估93个模型，核心贡献是评测基准本身。

Importance reason: Focuses on a Portuguese-specific benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Brazilian Portuguese benchmark, text embedding evaluation, no continuous language or multimodal generation, no video or world model, low personal relevance

Abstract: Text embeddings for Portuguese have no dedicated benchmark: evaluation rests on translated corpora such as English MS MARCO or on thin multilingual coverage, with native tasks scattered and unconsolidated. We introduce MTEB-PT, a benchmark of 22 native Brazilian-Portuguese tasks across seven categories (classification, multilabel classification, pair classification, semantic textual similarity, clustering, retrieval, and reranking), admitting only data created or found in Portuguese and excluding translations by construction. We evaluate 93 models spanning 23M to 27B parameters: 73 open-weight and 20 closed commercial APIs. Alongside the leaderboard we report a statistical layer for every headline comparison: per-task bootstrap confidence intervals, paired-bootstrap significance, a task- and instance-level discrimination analysis (how sharply each task separates models) adapted from Item Response Theory, and a cross-leaderboard correlation. Three findings stand out. The benchmark cleanly separates about a dozen tiers of models, though the top six are statistically too close to order. An openly licensed, self-hostable model reaches that leading tier, so strong Portuguese embedding quality does not require a commercial API. And a model's rank on the global multilingual leaderboard predicts its Portuguese rank only moderately (Spearman rho = 0.75 over 55 shared models; one model ranks 3rd there and 49th here), so a native benchmark measures something the multilingual boards do not. We release every task, our code, and a public leaderboard, so practitioners can choose Portuguese embedding models on native evidence.

</details>


### [194] [K9-Bench: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Canine-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02680)
*Khush Attarde, Yusuf Ali, Megha Thukral, Divye Bhutani, Thomas Ploetz, Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: K9-Bench是一个专注于真实世界家犬视频的多模态LLM评测基准，包含907个视频、约5000个QA对，覆盖5类任务，用于评估MLLM在犬类动作与交互理解上的零样本能力。实验发现前沿MLLM在此类任务上表现有限，闭源模型优于开源模型，但在长时序细粒度推理上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物是数百万家庭的重要组成部分，但当前多模态LLM在动物中心场景（尤其是犬类行为理解）上的能力尚未被系统评估。构建能够理解宠物行为（如识别痛苦信号、支持响应式机器人伴侣）的AI系统具有重要意义，因此需要一个专门的评测基准。

Method: 提出K9-Bench基准，包含907个真实家犬视频和约5000个QA对，覆盖5类任务（犬类动作识别、人犬交互、多跳推理等）。采用可扩展的VLM/LLM驱动的数据生成流水线，自动从网络挖掘犬类视频并生成细粒度QA对，同时设计了偏差缓解策略消除VLM引入的偏差。

Result: 实验表明，前沿MLLM在犬类中心任务上零样本表现有限。闭源模型（如GPT-4o）优于开源模型，但在长时序的细微姿态和交互线索的组合推理上仍存在困难。通用思维链提示对长时序推理的提升效果有限。

Conclusion: K9-Bench揭示了当前MLLM在动物行为理解上的不足，为未来研究提供了新的评测方向。同时，其数据构建流水线可推广到其他低数据领域，具有通用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了K9-Bench，一个针对犬类视频的多模态LLM评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Niche benchmark on canine videos has low alignment with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Multimodal LLM evaluation, Animal-centric video benchmark, Low relevance to continuous language/video generation, No autoregressive or diffusion focus, Negative keywords absent but topic too narrow

Abstract: MLLMs have shown strong zero-shot capabilities across diverse inputs such as across images, video, audio, and text. A crucial, yet underexplored, application of these models lies in understanding and modeling animal-centric scenarios. As animals are integral to millions of households, benchmarking next-generation AI models on pet-focused tasks, ranging from recognizing distress signals to enabling responsive robotic companions, is essential for building AI systems that can work alongside us. We introduce K9-Bench, a novel benchmark focused on real-world domestic dog videos, specifically targeting canine action and interaction understanding via approximately 5000 question-answer pairs across 907 videos spanning 5 distinct task categories that test long-form, canine-centric multimodal reasoning in MLLMs. To create this dataset, we propose a scalable, VLM/LLM-powered data generation pipeline that automatically mines canine-centric videos from the web and curates QA pairs requiring fine-grained, multi-hop reasoning over canine actions and temporally extended interaction sequences. We implement bias mitigation strategies designed to eliminate biases introduced by VLMs during dataset curation. Through extensive experimentation, we find that frontier MLLMs exhibit limited zero-shot performance on canine-centric tasks: although state-of-the-art closed-source models outperform open-source counterparts, they still struggle with compositional reasoning over subtle posture and interaction cues spread over long horizons. We observe that generic chain-of-thought prompting provides only modest performance for such long-horizon reasoning. Beyond a novel dataset for canine activity analysis, K9-Bench provides a general-purpose dataset construction pipeline that can be adapted to other low-data domains for quantitative analysis. Our project website is available at: .

</details>


### [195] [S-EMBER: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Streaming Egocentric Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02689)
*Xiaodong Wang, Xuanyi Zhao, Pedro Rodriguez, Devendra Singh Sachan, Barlas Oguz, Seungwhan Moon, Shang-Wen Li, Gargi Ghosh, Xin Dong, Wen-Tau Yih*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: S-EMBER 是一个大规模流式第一人称情景记忆检索基准，包含 3,141 个视频（388 小时）和 9,448 个 QA 对，基于 Ray-Ban Meta 智能眼镜采集。它定义了从全局离线搜索到因果流式主动回忆的范式转变，并揭示了定位悖论：语义推理随模型规模提升，但时间定位精度是停滞的架构瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情景记忆基准通常依赖离线评估（可访问完整视频文件），无法模拟可穿戴设备流式推理的真实场景。随着智能眼镜等可穿戴设备的普及，AI 助手需要在连续流中实时回忆过去经历，亟需一个硬件真实的流式评测基准。

Method: 作者使用 Ray-Ban Meta 智能眼镜采集了 3,141 个视频（共 388 小时），涵盖 10 种日常活动。构建了 9,448 个 QA 对，每个问题需要精确时间定位和视觉证据。基准设计强调流式（streaming）设定：模型只能因果地访问过去帧，不能全局搜索。评测了多种前沿多模态模型（如 GPT-4o、Gemini 等）在不同参数规模、分辨率和帧率下的表现。

Result: 发现了一个"定位悖论"：语义推理能力随模型参数规模提升而改善，但时间定位精度是一个停滞的架构瓶颈，无法通过增加模型大小、分辨率或帧密度来突破。这表明当前多模态模型在细粒度时间定位方面存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: S-EMBER 为可穿戴 AI 代理的流式情景记忆检索建立了硬件真实的评测基础，揭示了现有模型在时间定位方面的架构性瓶颈，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 S-EMBER，一个大规模流式第一人称记忆检索基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴。该基准包含 388 小时的真实智能眼镜视频和 9,448 个 QA 对，用于评估 AI 在流式场景下的情景记忆检索能力，完全符合该 canonical subtopic 的定义。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for egocentric memory but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: streaming egocentric memory, temporal localization bottleneck, wearable AI benchmark, Ray-Ban Meta glasses, causal active recall

Abstract: As wearable devices enable continuous first-person recording, AI assistants must reason across long time horizons to recall past experiences-a capability known as episodic memory. Current benchmarks often rely on offline evaluation with access to entire video files, failing to simulate the streaming reality of wearable intelligence. We introduce S-EMBER (Streaming Egocentric Memory Benchmark for Episodic Retrieval), a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,141 videos totaling 388 hours of organic activity captured via Ray-Ban Meta smart glasses. S-EMBER formalizes grounded streaming episodic retrieval, a paradigm shift from global offline search to causal, active recall triggered by visual events in a continuous stream. We provide 9,448 QA pairs requiring manual visual proof through precise temporal localization and supporting flexible response lengths to simulate natural human-AI interaction. Our extensive benchmarking of frontier models uncovers a localization paradox: while semantic reasoning improves with parameter scale, temporal grounding precision remains a stagnant architectural bottleneck that does not benefit from brute-force increases in model size, resolution, or frame density. S-EMBER establishes a hardware-authentic foundation for developing grounded, reliable episodic memory in the next generation of wearable AI agents.

</details>


### [196] [Prior Bias in Vision Language Models on UML Diagram Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02853)
*Zaiyu Cheng, Khai-Nguyen Nguyen, Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现VLM在解释UML类图时严重依赖先验知识（如类之间的典型关系）而非真正阅读图表。作者构建了受控UML基准，将先验一致的图与仅反转关系箭头的先验冲突图配对。在8个开源VLM上，反转箭头导致关系方向准确率平均下降33.48%，GPT-5.4 Mini仍有10%差距。在更难的三类条件下，开源模型准确率骤降45.28%，GPT-5.4系列平均下降18.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM越来越多地应用于软件工程制品（尤其是UML类图），但其是否真正理解图表视觉符号还是仅依赖预训练先验知识尚不清楚。现有基准无法区分这两种行为，因此需要一种诊断性基准来揭示VLM的"先验偏差"问题。

Method: 构建受控UML基准：每个先验一致的图与先验冲突的图配对，后者保持相同类名和布局但仅反转关系箭头方向。评估8个开源VLM（InternVL3.5和Qwen3系列）和2个闭源模型（GPT-5.4和GPT-5.4 Mini），分析关系方向准确率。

Result: 开源模型在反转箭头后关系方向准确率平均下降33.48%；GPT-5.4 Mini仍有10%差距。三类条件下开源模型准确率骤降45.28%，GPT-5.4系列平均下降18.62%。模型规模扩展带来的改进有限且依赖模型家族。

Conclusion: VLM在UML类图解释中存在显著的先验偏差，即依赖预训练知识而非真正阅读图表视觉符号。该基准可作为诊断VLM在图表理解中先验驱动失败的工具，提示需要更好的图表理解能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个受控的UML类图基准来诊断VLM是否真正理解图表或仅依赖先验知识，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到CANONICAL subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Reveals a critical prior-bias failure in VLM diagram understanding, but the UML-specific domain is outside your priority subtopics. Signals: VLM prior bias, UML diagram understanding, controlled benchmark, accuracy drop on arrow reversal, software engineering artifact

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to software engineering artifacts, especially UML class diagrams whose meaning depends on visual notation. Yet, it is unclear whether VLMs actually read such diagrams or instead answer from pretrained priors about how classes typically relate. We introduce a controlled UML benchmark in which each prior-conforming diagram is paired with its prior-conflicting counterpart that (1) preserves the same class names and layout while (2) reverses only the relation arrow. We evaluate eight open-source VLMs from two model families, InternVL3.5 and Qwen3, alongside two closed-source frontier models GPT-5.4 and GPT-5.4 Mini. Across the eight open-source models, reversing the arrow reduces relation-direction accuracy by 33.48% on average, while GPT-5.4 Mini retains a 10% gap. In the harder three-class condition, accuracy drops sharply by 45.28% for open-source models, and even 18.62% for the GPT-5.4 family on average. Scaling provides only limited improvements and is family-dependent. Our benchmark presents a diagnostic prior-driven failure in diagram-grounded software understanding. Our artifact is available at .

</details>


### [197] [R3D: Quantitative 3D Spatial Reasoning for Egocentric Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02921)
*Maxwell Horton, Wei Lu, Quan Tran, Yury Astashonok, Kirmani Ahmed, Babak Damavandi, Anuj Kumar, Xiao Zhang, Seungwhan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 R3D-Bench，一个包含 3,033 个定量 3D 空间推理问题的基准，基于 Aria Digital Twin 的 57 个第一视角视频序列构建，涵盖多选、距离和体积推理等 15 种问题类型。同时提出 R3D 框架，通过分割和深度提升物体表示构建 3D 场景，并提供 8 个可组合空间工具供 LLM 调用，在 Qwen3-VL 235B 上达到 73.5% 的平均相对准确率，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能同时处理自然第一视角视频、RGB-D 视频输入以及具有挑战性的定量 3D 空间推理问答，亟需一个专门针对可穿戴设备场景的 3D 空间推理基准。

Method: 构建 R3D-Bench 基准（3,033 个问题，15 种类型，57 个 Aria Digital Twin 第一视角视频序列）；提出 R3D 模型无关的空间工具调用框架，通过分割和深度提升物体表示构建 3D 场景，提供 8 个可组合空间工具供 LLM 调用以回答空间推理问题。

Result: R3D + Qwen3-VL 235B 在 R3D-Bench 上达到 73.5% 平均相对准确率，优于深度基线 CuTR+Tools（61.9%）和 RGB 基线 Gemini 3 Flash（46.5%）。

Conclusion: R3D-Bench 填补了第一视角可穿戴设备定量 3D 空间推理评测的空白，R3D 框架展示了通过工具调用结合 3D 场景信息提升 LLM 空间推理能力的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 R3D-Bench，一个面向第一视角可穿戴设备的定量 3D 空间推理问答基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。同时论文也涉及空间推理方法，但核心贡献是基准数据集。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for egocentric 3D spatial reasoning but falls outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 3D spatial reasoning benchmark, egocentric RGB-D video, tool-calling framework, Qwen3-VL 235B baseline, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Quantitative 3D spatial reasoning from egocentric RGB-D video is a critical capability for next-generation wearable assistants. Yet existing benchmarks do not reflect the challenges of handling (1) natural egocentric video, (2) posed RGB-D video inputs, and (3) challenging quantitative 3D spatial reasoning Q&A. To fill this gap, we introduce R3D-Bench (Reasoning in 3D), a benchmark of 3,033 quantitative spatial reasoning questions across 15 types -- spanning multiple-choice, distance-based, and volumetric reasoning questions -- built on top of 57 egocentric video sequences from Aria Digital Twin. To set a strong baseline on this dataset, we introduce R3D, a model-agnostic spatial tool-calling framework. In contrast to existing approaches that directly embed 3D information into the model's input representation, R3D constructs a 3D scene from video using segmentation and depth-lifted object representations. It provides this information to an LLM through eight composable spatial tools. On R3D-Bench, R3D with Qwen3-VL 235B achieves 73.5% mean relative accuracy, substantially outperforming the best depth-enabled baseline (CuTR+Tools, 61.9%) and the best RGB-only baseline (Gemini 3 Flash, 46.5%).

</details>


### [198] [MatPhaseBench: A Semantics-Guided Benchmark for Materials Phase Diagrams Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02934)
*Hanwen Wang, Sihan Liang, Zhiwei Liu, Yangang Wang, Wei Yan, Yuqin Liu, Zongguo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MatPhaseBench 是一个面向材料科学相图理解的高质量评测基准，从 3681 篇论文中精选 200 个图-文对，覆盖 189 种材料体系和 70 种元素。实验表明当前 VLM 在相图理解上远落后于专家水平，仅具备表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测在科学图像理解方面缺乏对需要深层机制解释的复杂逻辑图像的系统性评估，而材料相图作为材料科学的核心知识表征，恰好为此提供了具有挑战性的测试平台。

Method: 从 3681 篇经典材料科学期刊论文中筛选出 200 个高质量相图-文本对，覆盖 189 种材料体系和 70 种元素。基准设计具有三个关键特征：(1) 面向复杂科学图像理解，超越简单客观测试，包含需要深度理解的开放任务；(2) 完整的图像-文本对齐，文献挖掘和匹配过程中完整保留图像相关语义信息；(3) 高质量人工监督文本获取，所有描述均经过严格人工验证。

Result: 实验表明当前 VLM 在相图理解上远落后于专家水平：主要局限于表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理，领域意识和专家分析经验有限，在复合或多图设置中区分细粒度差异的能力较差。

Conclusion: MatPhaseBench 构成了一个具有挑战性的研究级基准，为复杂科学图像理解、相图分析和可信多模态科学 AI 提供了基础平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MatPhaseBench，一个面向材料科学相图理解的多模态评测基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。该基准评估 VLM 在复杂科学图像上的理解与推理能力，完全符合该子主题的定义。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for scientific VLM evaluation but outside user's core priority on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Materials science benchmark, VLM evaluation, phase diagram understanding, limited deep reasoning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Materials phase diagrams are a core knowledge representation in materials science, encoding temperature,composition, phase stability, and phase transformation pathways, with their full understanding requiring thermodynamic mechanism analysis and scientific reasoning. Although VLMs have shown promise in scientific image understanding, their systematic evaluation on such logically complex images demanding deep mechanistic interpretation remains limited, and phase diagrams provide a challenging testbed for this purpose. We introduce MatPhaseBench, a high-quality, high-reliability benchmark for complex scientific image understanding, focused on materials phase diagrams. MatPhaseBench is constructed from 3681 papers in classical materials science journals, from which 200 high-quality diagram-text pairs were selected, covering 189 material systems and 70 elements. The benchmark has three key features: (1)targeting complex scientific image understanding-it moves beyond simple objective tests to open-ended tasks requiring deep comprehension; (2)comprehensive image-text alignment-semantic information associated with images is fully preserved during literature mining and matching; (3) high-quality human-supervised text acquisition-all descriptions undergo strict manual validation. Experimental results show that current VLMs remain substantially behind expert-level understanding: they are largely limited to surface visual perception, lack deep reasoning grounded in thermodynamic mechanisms, have limited domain awareness and expert analytical experience, and perform poorly in distinguishing fine-grained differences in composite or multi-diagram settings. Overall, MatPhaseBench constitutes a challenging research-grade benchmark, providing a foundational platform for complex scientific image understanding, phase diagram analysis, and trustworthy multi-modal AI in science.

</details>


### [199] [MORE: A Multilingual Document Parsing Benchmark and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02956)
*Long Xu, Binghong Wu, Tinghao Yu, Hao Feng, Zhenyu Huang, Haoqing Jiang, Yunhao Wang, Shuo Huang, Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MORE是一个覆盖149种语言的大规模多语言文档解析评测基准，包含代码块、表格、目录等结构化元素，所有样本来自真实文档并通过人工精标。论文评估了多个SOTA VLM，建立了长尾语言性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析评测基准主要关注英语和中文等高资源语言，对低资源语言的模型性能评估存在盲区。尽管许多VLM声称支持数百种语言，但缺乏真实标注数据来验证这些能力。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖149种语言的大规模多语言文档解析基准MORE，采用模型辅助+人工精标的标注流程，从真实文档中收集样本，评测范围从纯文本扩展到代码块、表格、目录等结构化元素。

Result: 在MORE上评估了多个SOTA VLM，建立了长尾语言的性能基线，验证了基准在诊断模型在真实多样化场景下能力的有效性。

Conclusion: MORE填补了多语言文档解析评测的空白，为评估VLM在低资源语言上的表现提供了可靠基准，揭示了现有模型在多语言文档理解上的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了MORE，一个覆盖149种语言的多语言文档解析评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。论文聚焦于评估VLM在多语言文档解析上的能力，核心贡献是构建了一个大规模、多语言、结构化的评测基准。

Importance reason: Good multilingual benchmark but not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Multilingual document parsing, 149 languages, VLM evaluation, structural elements, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Multilingual documents encapsulate rich regional cultures, scientific discoveries, and historical records. Parsing this content into structured, machine-readable formats is critical for unlocking global knowledge. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on high-resource languages like English and Chinese, creating an evaluation blind spot concerning model performance on other languages. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) claim support for hundreds of languages, the lack of ground truth makes it impossible to empirically verify these capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MORE, a large-scale benchmark designed for multilingual document parsing evaluation. MORE distinguishes itself through three key dimensions: (1) Unprecedented Scale: It covers 149 languages, making it the most linguistically diverse benchmark to date; (2) Structural Complexity: Unlike previous works, it extends evaluation beyond plain text to include structural elements such as code blocks, tables, and catalogs; and (3) Data Authenticity: All samples are curated from real-world documents via a model-assisted, human-refined annotation pipeline. We evaluate state-of-the-art models using MORE, establishing new performance baselines for long-tail languages and validating the benchmark's effectiveness in diagnosing model capabilities in realistic, diverse scenarios. The MORE dataset will be available at .

</details>


### [200] [GuideMe: Multi-Domain Task Guidance and Intervention in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02991)
*Fang Liu, Jinpeng Chen, Ke Xu, Yuhao Liu, Huankang Guan, Xudong Lu, Bo Yang, Gerhard Hancke, Rui Liu, Rynson W.H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了GuideMe，首个面向流式视频中闭环交互任务指导的多领域基准，包含2458个视频、47775个交互样本，覆盖烹饪、物体操作、日常生活指导和健身等领域。设计了包含时序语义匹配、行为分类和LLM评判的三组件评估框架，实验发现现有MLLM在提供指令方面表现良好，但在识别执行错误和提供纠正反馈方面持续失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在离线视频理解上表现出色，但能否作为实时程序教练（持续监控执行、检测错误、提供纠正指导的闭环交互）尚不清楚。缺乏一个专门用于训练和评估MLLM在流式视频中进行闭环交互任务指导的基准。

Method: 构建GuideMe基准：收集2458个视频（223.7小时），涵盖烹饪、物体操作、日常生活指导和健身四个领域，标注47775个交互样本（包括下一步指令、完成反馈、错误检测和纠正指导）。设计三组件评估框架：时序语义二分图匹配（序列级对齐）、行为分类（干预时机）、LLM-as-a-Judge（内容质量）。

Result: 实验表明现有MLLM在提供指令方面表现良好，但在识别执行错误和提供纠正反馈方面持续失败，存在关键性能不对称性。

Conclusion: GuideMe揭示了当前MLLM在实时闭环任务指导中的严重不足，特别是在错误检测和纠正反馈方面，为未来研究提供了基准和方向。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了GuideMe基准，用于评测MLLM在流式视频中的闭环交互任务指导能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准包含多领域视频、交互样本及评估框架，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for streaming video task guidance, but low alignment with user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: streaming video benchmark, closed-loop interaction, task guidance, MLLM evaluation, error detection

Abstract: While multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at offline video understanding, an interesting question of how far they are from serving as a real-time procedural coach remains unknown. Such a role typically requires an MLLM to continuously monitor the execution, detect mistakes, and provide corrective guidance in a closed-loop interaction. In this paper, we construct GuideMe, the first multi-domain benchmark for streaming video that supports training and evaluation of MLLMs for closed-loop interactive task guidance. It comprises 2,458 videos spanning 223.7 hours across diverse domains (\eg, cooking, object manipulation, daily-life guidance, and fitness), with 47,775 interaction samples covering next-step instructions, completion feedback, error detection, and corrective guidance. To evaluate existing models on GuideMe, we design a three-component assessment framework to measure the capabilities of representative MLLMs, which consists of temporal-semantic bipartite matching for sequence-level alignment, behavioral classification for intervention timing, and LLM-as-a-Judge for content quality. Extensive experiments highlight a critical performance asymmetry: despite excelling at providing instructions, existing MLLMs consistently fail to identify execution errors and respond with corrective feedback. Code and data are released at .

</details>


### [201] [Natural Language Camera Movement Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03043)
*Yuwen Tan, Joey Huang, Jin Huang, Haoxiang Li, Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在理解自然语言描述的摄像机运动方面表现极差，常混淆平移与旋转、左右方向、物体运动与摄像机运动。为此，作者将自然语言摄像机运动理解确立为独立研究任务，提出了两级电影摄影学分类体系、一个包含真实与合成视频的原子级基准测试集，以及一个大规模多源训练集。微调的VLM-8B在基准上超越Gemini 3.1 Pro 10%-11%，但与人类表现仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在理解自然语言描述的摄像机运动方面存在严重缺陷，而这一能力对视频生成模型的训练与评估至关重要。作者希望将摄像机运动理解确立为独立的研究任务，并推动该领域的发展。

Method: 1) 建立两级电影摄影学分类体系（粗粒度运动类型与细粒度运动参数）；2) 构建原子级基准测试集，包含真实视频和合成视频；3) 策划大规模多源训练集，并加入针对性的摄像机运动数据增强；4) 在VLM-8B上进行微调。

Result: 微调后的VLM-8B在真实视频和合成视频上分别比Gemini 3.1 Pro高出10%和11%，但与人类表现仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 自然语言摄像机运动理解是一个具有挑战性的独立研究任务，现有VLM表现不佳，本文的工作为未来研究提供了基准、数据和基线方法，但仍有很大提升空间。

Classification reason: 该论文建立了自然语言摄像机运动理解这一独立研究任务，并引入了包含真实与合成视频的原子级基准测试集，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴。同时，论文指出该任务对视频生成模型的训练与评估至关重要，因此也与多模态生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Establishes a new task and benchmark for camera movement understanding, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: New task definition, VLM failure analysis, Cinematographic taxonomy, Real+synthetic benchmark, Fine-tuned VLM-8B outperforms Gemini

Abstract: Understanding camera movement in natural language is critical for training and evaluating video generation models, among other applications. However, we demonstrate that existing vision-language models (VLMs) fail this task in surprising ways, frequently confusing translation with rotation, left with right, and object movement with camera movement. To address these limitations, we establish natural language camera movement understanding as a standalone research task. We introduce a two-level cinematographic taxonomy and an extensive, atomic benchmark featuring both real and synthetic videos. Furthermore, we curate a large-scale, multi-source training set enhanced by targeted camera movement augmentation. Our fine-tuned VLM-8B outperforms Gemini 3.1 Pro by 10% and 11% on our benchmark's real and synthetic videos, respectively. Despite these gains, a significant gap remains relative to human performance, underscoring the need to promote and facilitate future research on natural language camera movement understanding.

</details>


### [202] [Evaluating and Understanding Model Editing for Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05310)
*Guli Zhu, Chenwei Wu, Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 M3Bench，一个面向医学视觉语言模型编辑的临床基准，包含16,276个问题，覆盖多种解剖结构、模态和专业领域，评估了4种编辑方法在6个模型上的可靠性、精确性和泛化性，发现梯度方法迁移强但破坏局部性，记忆方法保留局部性但缺乏组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型编辑基准主要面向通用任务，无法反映临床领域的真实需求和多样性。医学VLM部署后需要快速、精准地修正错误，但缺乏专门评估编辑方法在临床场景下可靠性、精确性和泛化性的基准。

Method: 构建M3Bench基准，包含16,276个问题，涵盖图像/文本变化、模态/协议迁移、临床知识组合和时间演进等挑战，支持单次和顺序编辑。在6个医学和通用VLM上评估4种代表性编辑方法（梯度基和记忆基方法），并通过潜空间几何分析归因编辑失败原因。

Result: 没有方法在所有标准上表现优异。梯度基编辑方法迁移性强但严重破坏局部性（catastrophic locality violations），记忆基方法保留局部性但缺乏组合泛化能力，且对骨干模型超参数高度敏感。潜空间几何分析揭示了不同编辑方法对VLM潜空间景观的偏移影响。

Conclusion: M3Bench为多模态模型编辑提供了严格的临床压力测试，揭示了现有方法在医学领域的局限性，并为安全的后部署模型适配提供了可操作的指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 M3Bench，一个面向医学视觉语言模型编辑的评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM editing, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical domain, model editing benchmark, not aligned with continuous models or generation, negative keywords present

Abstract: Model editing promises a fast, targeted way to correct post-deployment mistakes in medical vision-language models (VLMs) without costly retraining. However, existing multimodal model editing benchmarks focus on general-purpose tasks and do not reflect realistic clinical domain requirements and variability. To address this, we introduce M3Bench, a clinically grounded benchmark for multimodal model editing that evaluates whether an edit remains reliable, precise, and generalizable under the challenges of image and text variation, modality and protocol shifts, clinical knowledge composition, and temporal progression. M3Bench contains 16,276 questions spanning diverse anatomy, modalities, and specialties, and supports both single and sequential edits. By evaluating 4 representative editors across 6 medical and general VLMs, we find that no method excels across all criteria. Gradient-based editors achieve strong transfer but suffer from catastrophic locality violations, whereas memory-based methods preserve locality but lack compositional generality and exhibit high backbone-dependent hyperparameter sensitivity. We further attribute these failures to the latent space geometry of VLMs and how different editing methods shift its landscape. Overall, M3Bench establishes a rigorous clinical stress test for multimodal model editing and offers actionable guidance for safer post-deployment adaptation. The benchmark is publicly available at .

</details>


### [203] [OmniLayout: A Schematic-Coupled Multimodal Benchmark for Constraint-Aware Geometric Reasoning in PCB Layout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03261)
*Taiting Lu, Kaiyuan Lin, Mingjia Wang, Haolin Ye, Runze Liu, Yuxin Tian, Vahe Melkonyan, Haoyu Wang, Muchuan Wang, Chufan Hong, Yifan Yang, Sung-Liang Chen, Yi-Chao Chen, Yicheng Jin, Mahanth Gowda*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniLayout是首个评估大语言模型在PCB布局放置中几何推理能力的基准，包含1681个工业级原理图耦合的PCB布局和四个任务，揭示了当前LLM在密集约束下的几何推理、可布线性优化和电气功能保持方面的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在3D空间推理和几何理解方面取得了进展，但在严格空间和功能约束下的密集物体放置推理能力尚未被探索，而这正是电子设计自动化（EDA）中的基础挑战。

Method: 构建了包含1681个工业级原理图耦合PCB布局的OmniLayout基准，设计了四个任务：(1) IC物理放置的几何推理；(2) 可布线性感知的放置推理；(3) 电气功能保持；(4) 工具增强的智能体推理。对多种LLM进行了系统评估。

Result: 当前LLM在PCB布局放置任务中表现显著不足，包括几何推理能力弱、可布线性优化差、电气功能保持不一致。

Conclusion: OmniLayout揭示了LLM在约束感知几何推理方面的重大局限性，为未来研究提供了新的评测方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了OmniLayout基准，用于评估LLM在PCB布局中的几何推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Highly specialized EDA benchmark with no direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: PCB layout benchmark, geometric reasoning under constraints, tool-augmented agentic reasoning, no overlap with priority subtopics, negative keywords absent but domain mismatch

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress in 3D spatial reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained geometric understanding. However, their ability to reason about densely packed object placement under strict spatial and functional constraints remains largely unexplored, despite being a fundamental challenge in practical electronic design automation (EDA) workflows. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniLayout, the first benchmark designed to evaluate LLMs on printed-circuit-board (PCB) layout placement reasoning under real-world geometric, routing, and connectivity constraints. OmniLayout contains 1,681 industrial-grade schematic-coupled PCB layouts and includes four tasks: (1) geometric reasoning for IC physical placement, with 77.24K placement instances constrained within PCB board boundaries; (2) routability-aware placement reasoning, generating physically valid component placements; (3) electrical functionality, preserving schematic-specified connectivity and electronic functional correctness; and (4) tool-augmented agentic reasoning for invoking external tools to accomplish tasks (1)-(3). Our results reveal substantial limitations of current LLMs in PCB layout placement, including weak geometric reasoning, poor routability optimization, and inconsistent preservation of electrical functionality.

</details>


### [204] [IDEAL-Bench: Indoor Dataset and Evaluation suite for Analyzing 3D Layout reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03614)
*Yuening Cai, Junwei Zhou, Youran Qu, Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: IDEAL-Bench 是一个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）3D 布局推理能力的新基准，包含 1000 个程序化生成的室内场景，要求模型预测结构化 3D 布局（物体位姿与范围），通过五个数值维度和渲染对比协议进行评分。评测 15 个主流 VLM 发现该任务远未解决，最强模型仅达 62.1/100，且模型在物体识别与几何回归之间存在显著不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间问答（Spatial QA）范式无法全面评估 VLM 的空间智能，特别是缺乏对整体 3D 布局推理（从单张图像预测所有可见物体的位姿与范围）的评测。现有基准多使用 CLEVR 风格的简单几何体，无法反映真实场景的复杂性。

Method: 构建了 IDEAL-Scenes 数据集（1000 个可重新渲染的 Blender 室内场景，10 种房间类型，含真实布局标注），并设计了 IDEAL-Bench 评测套件，要求 VLM 预测结构化 3D 布局，沿五个数值维度（物体识别、位置、尺寸、方向、整体）评分，并引入渲染对比协议（render-and-compare）进行感知评估。

Result: 评测 15 个主流 VLM，最强模型（GPT-4o）仅达 62.1/100；所有模型在物体识别与几何回归之间存在显著不对称性（识别强、几何弱）；模型排名与 QA 基准和原始几何重建基准部分不一致——头部模型共识一致，但中部模型排名差异显著。

Conclusion: IDEAL-Bench 揭示了当前 VLM 在真实 3D 布局推理上的严重不足，表明现有 VLM 更擅长描述场景而非测量场景。该基准可作为诊断工具，评估下一代 VLM 是否真正具备空间理解能力而非语言近似。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 IDEAL-Bench，一个用于评估 VLM 3D 布局推理能力的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for 3D layout reasoning but only tangentially touches user's priority on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: 3D layout reasoning benchmark, VLM spatial evaluation, object recognition vs. geometric regression asymmetry, indoor scene dataset, render-and-compare protocol

Abstract: Spatial question answering is the dominant paradigm for evaluating spatial intelligence in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but it leaves a complementary axis of spatial competence under-evaluated: holistic 3D layout inference, which predicts every visible object's pose and extent from a single image in a structured form. To this end, we introduce IDEAL-Bench, an evaluation suite that requires VLMs to predict structured 3D layouts on photorealistic indoor scenes across 10 room types, scored along five numerical dimensions and a perceptual render-and-compare protocol. By operating on semantically realistic scenes with full asset substitution under controlled lighting and viewpoint, IDEAL-Bench moves beyond CLEVR-style simple geometric primitives so that any image-space discrepancy reflects spatial reasoning alone. The benchmark is built on IDEAL-Scenes, a procedurally generated dataset of 1,000 re-renderable Blender environments with ground-truth layouts. Evaluating 15 prominent VLMs reveals three findings: the task remains substantially unsolved, with the strongest model reaching only 62.1/100 overall; all models exhibit a sharp asymmetry between object recognition and geometric regression, indicating that current VLMs are trained to describe scenes rather than to measure them; model rankings partially diverge from those on QA-based and primitive-reconstruction benchmarks: top-tier consensus holds, but mid-tier rankings shift substantially. Collectively, these findings establish IDEAL-Bench as a diagnostic suite, targeting the geometric and structural competencies that QA-based evaluation cannot surface, and paving the way towards more rigorous evaluation of spatial intelligence in next-generation VLMs. Together, these findings position IDEAL-Bench as a principled diagnostic for whether future VLMs achieve genuine spatial understanding rather than linguistic approximations of it.

</details>


### [205] [HCSU: A Dataset and Benchmark for Fine-Grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04147)
*Yinsheng Yao, Yan Liu, Chen Ye*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了HCSU，首个面向细粒度历史书法风格理解的数据集与评测基准，包含39,307张来自49位书法家、跨越10个朝代的字符图像，解耦了墨迹与碑刻模态，并提供层次化美学描述，用于评估LVLM的细粒度风格判别与可解释美学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有书法风格数据集存在模态混合（墨迹与碑刻混杂）和扁平化标签的问题，无法支持细粒度风格感知与美学推理，制约了LVLM在文化遗产保护中的应用。

Method: 构建HCSU数据集：从49位历史著名书法家的作品中收集39,307张字符图像，系统解耦墨迹（Tie）与碑刻（Bei）两种模态，并提供层次化专家撰写的美学描述。基于该数据集设计了两个评测协议：细粒度风格判别和可解释美学推理。

Result: 评估表明，当前最先进的LVLM在书法风格理解上表现出一定能力，但对字体层级、文本内容和来源模态线索敏感，且难以将美学判断与细粒度笔触证据联系起来，揭示了视觉风格感知与书法知识之间的差距。

Conclusion: HCSU基准暴露了当前多模态架构在专家级视觉推理方面的根本局限性，为文化遗产保护领域推动更细粒度的视觉风格理解研究提供了重要资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了HCSU数据集与基准，用于细粒度书法风格理解评测，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a niche cultural heritage benchmark, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Fine-grained calligraphy understanding, LVLM benchmark, Cultural heritage, Modal mixture problem, Hierarchical aesthetic descriptions

Abstract: Automated fine-grained perception of calligraphy styles--a task vital to cultural heritage preservation--remains a critical challenge for Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), largely constrained by existing datasets that suffer from modal mixture and flattened labels. To bridge this gap, we introduce HCSU, the first comprehensive dataset tailored for fine-grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding. HCSU comprises 39,307 meticulously curated character images from 49 historically prominent calligraphers across 10 dynasties, systematically decoupling authentic ink manuscripts (Tie) from stone rubbings (Bei) to resolve the long-standing modal mixture problem. Moving beyond conventional flattened labels, HCSU provides hierarchical expert-written aesthetic descriptions, enabling two rigorous evaluation protocols: fine-grained style discrimination and interpretable aesthetic reasoning. Extensive evaluations reveal a persistent gap between calligraphy-related knowledge and visually grounded style perception: state-of-the-art LVLMs show non-trivial performance but remain sensitive to script-level, textual, and source-specific cues, and often struggle to ground aesthetic judgments in fine-grained brushwork evidence. Ultimately, the HCSU benchmark exposes fundamental limitations in current multimodal architectures, aiming to inspire the evolution of expert-level visual reasoning for cultural heritage preservation. The dataset is available at .

</details>


### [206] [Solve the Missing First Step: Can VLMs Standardize Raw Heterogeneous Medical Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04694)
*Xin Chen, Dongliang Xu, Cunhao Zhu, Xudong Luo, Haoyang Lyu, Xiaoxiao Sun, Serena Yeung-Levy, Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现现有医学 VLM 评测忽略了真实临床中原始数据异构、碎片化的问题，构建了 MDS-Bench 基准（1939个标准化任务），评估 VLM 识别格式、转换图像、提取文本并组织为结构化图文对的能力。实验表明最佳模型 Gemini 3 Flash 端到端成功率仅 48.6%，揭示原始数据标准化是医学 AI 诊断的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 VLM 评测假设输入已经是标准化的图像和文本，但真实临床中医学数据往往是原始、异构、碎片化的，缺乏从原始数据到 VLM 可用输入的标准化步骤。作者希望研究这一被忽视的"缺失第一步"。

Method: 构建 MDS-Bench 基准，包含 1,939 个手工标注的原始医学数据标准化任务，覆盖多种临床实践、放射学模态、标注格式和目录布局。评估 VLM 在四个子任务上的表现：识别源格式、转换原始医学图像为 VLM 兼容输入、提取相关文本信息、将结果组织为结构化图文对。

Result: 最佳模型 Gemini 3 Flash 端到端成功率仅 48.6%，所有模型在原始数据标准化任务上表现远低于传统诊断评测。不同子任务难度差异大，格式识别相对较好，但图像转换和结构化组织是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 原始医学数据标准化是当前医学 AI 诊断在真实临床部署中的关键瓶颈，现有 VLM 在此任务上能力严重不足，需要未来研究重点关注。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向医学原始数据标准化的新基准 MDS-Bench，评估 VLM 处理非结构化、异构医学数据的能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical data standardization, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Medical data standardization, VLM benchmark, Low end-to-end success rate, Clinical practice gap, Multimodal understanding evaluation

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to medical AI, existing benchmarks mainly focus on evaluating their diagnosis ability over given medical images and texts, implicitly assuming that standardized medical images, texts or question-answer pairs are already prepared. However, this assumption does not hold when we apply VLMs in real clinical practice, where medical data is often raw, heterogeneous, and fragmented across different sources. In this paper, we study this missing step, i.e., raw medical data standardization. Specifically, models are given raw dataset folders and evaluated on their ability to identify source formats, convert raw medical images into VLM-compatible visual inputs, extract relevant textual information, and organize the results into structured image-text pairs. To construct this Medical Data Standardization Benchmark (MDS-Bench), we manually annotate 1,939 raw medical data standardization tasks covering diverse clinical practice, radiology modalities, annotation formats, and directory layouts. Extensive experiments show that even the best performing VLMs, i.e., Gemini 3 Flash, achieve only 48.6% end-to-end success rate. Our research highlights raw medical data standardization as a critical bottleneck for medical AI diagnosis in real practice.

</details>


### [207] [A Multimodal Reasoning Typology for Grounding Chart-Image Coherence in Science Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05222)
*Avina Nakarmi, Sohom Sen, Xun Song, Sreyashi Samaddar, Aritra Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态推理分类体系（R1-R5），用于刻画科学出版物中图表与图像之间的语义连贯性及推理缺口，并通过专家与非专家对VLM描述的评估实验验证了该分类体系在识别理解分歧点上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学出版物中图表与图像经常同时出现，但现有计算工作缺乏系统描述两者之间语义连贯性的方法。读者需要不同程度的推理工作才能理解图表-图像对共同传达的科学主张，而这种推理差异尚未被系统分类和评测。

Method: 基于沟通的接地理论（grounding theory），从79篇脑外伤论文和32个图表-图像对中自下而上地推导出R1-R5五级推理缺口分类体系。随后设计实验，让一位领域专家和三位非专家对VLM生成的25对图表-图像描述进行判断，分析分类级别与判断一致性之间的关系。

Result: R1-R5分类体系能够系统预测专家与非专家在评估VLM描述时何时一致、何时分歧，准确隔离出需要领域上下文知识而非图表本身来维持连贯性的关键点。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为科学图表设计者提供了一种系统方法，用于平衡文本与图表-图像对之间的关系，弥合专家与非专家在理解科学结论时的鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种用于描述图表-图像对（chart-image pairs）多模态推理的分类体系（R1-R5），并基于该分类体系评估了VLM对科学图表-图像对的理解与描述质量。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因为论文的核心贡献是建立了一个新的评测框架/分类体系来系统性地评估多模态理解中的推理缺口，并验证了该体系在评测VLM描述一致性方面的有效性。

Importance reason: Proposes a reasoning typology for chart-image coherence but is a niche evaluation benchmark with no direct connection to the user's priority subtopics. Signals: chart-image coherence, reasoning typology, VLM evaluation, science communication, grounding theory

Abstract: Charts and images appear together throughout scientific publications, yet most computational work does not characterize their coherence. We argue that a chart, its accompanying image, and the caption that links them form a multimodal unit, and that the inferential work required to read it varies systematically. To capture this variation, we develop a typology of reasoning gaps, R1 through R5, that characterizes how chart, image, and text jointly convey a scientific claim, and the interpretive work this demands of the reader. Some pairs restate the same data, while in other pairs, charts are used to quantify a structure the image localizes, project image content onto an external variable, audit an image-based claim, or jointly construct a frame that neither panel can establish alone. The typology is anchored in the grounding theory of communication and was derived bottom-up, with a neuroscience expert, from a corpus of 79 traumatic brain injury papers and 32 chart-image pairs. Crucially, the levels provide a systematic mechanism for identifying where grounding succeeds or breaks down, rather than leaving it to subjective inference. We show this in a study in which a domain expert and three non-experts judge vision-language model (VLM) descriptions of 25 pairs: the level predicts where their judgments align and where they diverge, isolating the points at which contextual knowledge, not the figure, carries coherence. This typology thus offers figure designers a systematic way to balance text against chart-image pairs, bridging the expert-to-non-expert divide in reading a scientific takeaway.

</details>


### [208] [SteelBench: Evaluating Vision-Language Models in Real-World Industrial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05264)
*Suryanarayana Reddy Yarrabothula, Manisha Chawla, Kunal Sinha, Gagan Raj Gupta, Sashank Lekkala, Ashirvadhan Dosapati, Saikamal Nannuri, Katragadda Ajay RamaSwamy Chowdary Gowtham*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SteelBench 是一个面向真实工业 CCTV 环境的 VLM 诊断基准，包含 1,345 个密集标注的工业监控视频片段，评估模型在工人动作识别、安全规则推理和标注溯源方面的能力。实验表明，最佳模型仅达到 42.6% 的动作准确率（人类基准 84.6%），且存在严重的标注溯源偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准主要评测消费级视频、自我中心视频或模拟工业环境，无法反映真实工业 CCTV 中工人作为远处小目标、存在灰尘/蒸汽/低光/遮挡等复杂视觉条件以及重叠活动的挑战。此外，模型辅助标注可能引入评估偏差，现有基准缺乏对标注溯源的审计机制。

Method: 从 149 小时真实工厂监控视频中，经过时序去重、类别平衡和可见性感知分层采样，筛选出 1,345 个密集标注片段。每个片段包含逐工人的动作标签、PPE 属性、空间上下文和安全规则标注。同时提出溯源感知审计协议，测量标注来源对模型评估的影响。

Result: 在 9 个 VLM（来自 4 个架构家族）上评测，最佳模型仅 42.6% 动作准确率（人类 84.6%）。未审计的 VLM 标注可使同族模型准确率虚高 17 个百分点。即使动作预测正确，37-58% 的情况下安全判断仍错误。没有模型通过 5 项诊断检查中的 2 项以上。

Conclusion: SteelBench 揭示了当前 VLM 在真实工业监控场景中的严重不足，尤其是在小目标识别、复杂环境鲁棒性和安全推理方面。标注溯源审计对公平评估至关重要，该基准为工业级 VLM 评估提供了重要参考。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SteelBench，一个面向工业监控场景的 VLM 评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。该主题是 [CANONICAL] 条目，直接匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on industrial VLM benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Industrial CCTV benchmark, Low model accuracy vs human, Annotation provenance bias, Not generative or continuous, No video generation or world model

Abstract: Existing video benchmarks evaluate action recognition on consumer videos, egocentric recordings, or simulated industrial environments. They do not test vision-language models under the visual and procedural conditions of real industrial CCTV, where workers appear as distant figures amid dust, steam, low light, glare, occlusion, and overlapping activities. We introduce STEELBENCH, a diagnostic benchmark for industrial surveillance that jointly evaluates per-worker activity recognition, safety-rule reasoning, and annotation provenance. SteelBench contains 1,345 densely annotated clips, curated from 149 hours of operational plant footage and 10,024 candidate clips using temporal deduplication, class balancing, and visibility-aware stratified sampling. Each clip includes dense per-worker action labels, PPE attributes, spatial context, and safety-rule annotations. Because model-assisted annotation can shape the labels later used for model evaluation, SteelBench includes a provenance-aware audit protocol. The protocol measures label influence, evaluates sensitivity to ground-truth provenance, and reports a human reference from expert-reviewed labels. Applying this audit, we find that unaudited VLM-sourced ground truth can inflate same-family model accuracy by up to 17 percentage points. Across nine VLMs from four architectural families, the best model reaches only 42.6% action accuracy, compared with an 84.6% human benchmark. Performance also fragments across recognition, robustness, calibration, and safety reasoning. Even when models predict the correct action, 37-58% of cases still yield incorrect safety judgments, and no model passes more than 2 of 5 diagnostic checks. The dataset is publicly available on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [209] [Is the Geometry Doing the Work? An Operating-Point Audit of Hierarchy in Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05268)
*Jaeyoung Kim, Eunseok Kim, Dongsuk Jang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对双曲视觉语言模型（MERU, HyCoCLIP, PHyCLIP）进行系统性审计，发现这些模型并未真正利用双曲几何的径向/锥体机制：操作点始终接近欧几里得（√cρ<1），蕴含锥体饱和或未激活，层级评测结果由角度距离而非曲率驱动。作者提出了一套五维几何报告作为未来层级表征声明的诊断标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间被广泛用于建模视觉语言表征中的层级结构，但现有工作仅通过曲率参数或下游任务性能来声称模型利用了双曲几何。本文质疑这一假设，旨在通过系统诊断检验模型是否真正激活了双曲空间的径向和锥体机制。

Method: 作者提出了一套必要条件的诊断工具（battery of necessary-condition diagnostics），对三个已发表的双曲VLM系列（MERU, HyCoCLIP, PHyCLIP）进行审计，包括：操作点（√cρ）分析、锥体激活检测、层级遍历测试、径向深度分析，以及控制实验（释放曲率下限、关闭蕴含损失等）。

Result: 发现三种失败模式：(1) 曲率未被主动利用，操作点始终接近欧几里得；(2) 锥体和遍历机制无效，蕴含锥体饱和或未对齐；(3) 层级评测结果被角度距离和组合监督信号驱动，而非曲率。还发现蕴含目标存在低曲率宽锥捷径，且参数无关的孔径恒等式（锥体饱和当且仅当√cρ≤2K）定位了所有训练收敛点。

Conclusion: 这些已发布的模型并未实例化其几何所激发的径向/锥体机制。作者将审计结果提炼为五维几何报告，为未来层级表征声明提供诊断标准，并指出双曲VLM领域需要更严谨的验证方法。

Classification reason: 该论文对双曲视觉语言模型（MERU, HyCoCLIP, PHyCLIP）的几何机制进行系统性审计与诊断，提出了一套诊断工具和评估协议来检验模型是否真正利用了双曲几何的层级表征能力。这属于对多模态模型（VLM）的评测方法论研究，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Rigorous audit of hyperbolic VLM geometry, but tangential to user's priority on continuous/multimodal models and generation. Signals: hyperbolic geometry audit, operating-point analysis, entailment cone failure, near-Euclidean shortcut, five-number diagnostic report

Abstract: Whether a hyperbolic representation model uses its geometry cannot be read off its curvature parameter: what matters is the dimensionless operating point $\sqrt{c}\rho$ and whether the radial and cone machinery is active there. We develop a battery of necessary-condition diagnostics and audit three published hyperbolic vision-language families -- MERU, HyCoCLIP, and PHyCLIP -- across released checkpoints and controlled interventions on a fixed GRIT snapshot, identifying three failure modes. First, curvature is not an active resource: the operating point stays near-Euclidean ($H(u)\approx 1$; no audited converged checkpoint reaches $\sqrt{c}\rho>1$), and releasing the curvature floor moves curvature and norms but keeps the operating point near-Euclidean, without substantial downstream degradation. Second, the cone and traversal machinery is measured inoperative: entailment cones are inactive, saturated, or misaligned, and graded traversal fails under controlled readouts, while directed radial depth is a bounded non-detection above shuffle-null controls at quantified sensitivity; the one surviving native-relation residual remains non-operative. Third, hierarchy-looking evaluations are underdetermined: taxonomy correlations are carried by angular distance, and coarse-retrieval gains track box/compositional supervision, not curvature. A mechanistic account explains why: the entailment objective admits a low-curvature, wide-cone shortcut, and a parameter-free aperture identity (cones saturate iff $\sqrt{c}\rho\le 2K$) locates the edge where every entailment-trained unclamped run settles; entailment-off runs show no arrest there. The shortcut is the dominant accelerator of collapse, not its sole cause. These formulations, as released, do not instantiate the radial/cone mechanism their geometry motivates; we distill the audit into a five-number geometry report for future hierarchy claims.

</details>
