### [210] [OmniFocus: Query-Guided Modality-Balanced Token Compression for Omni-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03050)
*Shijie Cao, Qingyu Zhang, Boxi Yu, Yuzhong Zhang, Boxi Cao, Yaojie Lu, Hongyu Lin, Xianpei Han, Le Sun*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.SD, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (77.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniFocus 是一种免训练的查询引导 token 压缩方法，针对全模态大语言模型（OmniLLM）的音视频输入，通过独立重要性估计实现模态对称压缩，在低 token 保留率下保持性能并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大语言模型在处理音视频输入时会产生大量 token 序列，导致推理成本高昂。现有音视频 token 压缩方法通常依赖单模态引导，忽略了查询相关证据在音视频输入中的时序局部性，并隐含假设两种模态具有时序对齐的信息密度分布，导致模态偏差问题。

Method: 提出 OmniFocus，一种免训练的查询引导 token 压缩方法。对视频和音频分别进行独立的重要性估计，采用模态对称的压缩设计，保留各模态特有的显著证据，同时维持音视频对齐，从而缓解单模态引导压缩可能导致的模态偏差问题。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Omni 模型家族上的四个音视频基准测试中，OmniFocus 在低 token 保留率下保持强劲的压缩性能，在 25% token 保留率下在多个主要基准得分上优于现有基线。在 DailyOmni 上使用 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 在 25% token 保留率下保持 59.40 准确率，同时实现相对于全 token 基线最高 1.38 倍的预填充加速。

Conclusion: OmniFocus 通过查询引导的模态对称 token 压缩，在音视频全模态大语言模型中实现了良好的精度-效率权衡，验证了独立模态重要性估计的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OmniFocus，一种针对全模态大语言模型（OmniLLM）的免训练查询引导 token 压缩方法，旨在降低音视频输入带来的推理开销。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴下的 token 压缩技术，与现有 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses token compression for omni-modal LLMs, aligning with user's efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: training-free token compression, query-guided modality balancing, audio-visual alignment, efficient inference for OmniLLM, Qwen2.5-Omni evaluation

Abstract: Omni modal large language models (OmniLLMs) have attracted wide attention for their ability to jointly process audio and video, but they generate large token sequences under audio-visual inputs, leading to substantial inference cost. Existing audio-visual token compression methods often rely on unimodal guidance, overlooking the temporal locality of query-relevant evidence in audio-visual inputs and implicitly assuming that the two modalities share a temporally aligned information density distribution. We propose \textbf{OmniFocus}, a training-free query-guided token compression method for OmniLLMs that performs independent importance estimation for video and audio, enabling a modality-symmetric compression design that preserves modality-specific salient evidence while maintaining audio-visual alignment, thereby mitigating the modality bias issue that can arise from unimodal-guided compression. Experiments on the Qwen2.5-Omni model family across four audio-visual benchmarks show that OmniFocus maintains strong compressed performance at low token retention ratios and outperforms existing baselines on several major benchmark scores at 25\% token retention. On DailyOmni with Qwen2.5-Omni-7B at 25\% token retention, OmniFocus maintains 59.40 accuracy while delivering up to 1.38$\times$ prefill speedup relative to the full-token baseline, highlighting a favorable practical accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [211] [Seeing Once is Enough? Online Geometry-Aware Token Pruning for 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04079)
*Ruei-Chi Lai, Bolivar Solarte, Chin-Hsuan Wu, Yi-Hsuan Tsai, Min Sun*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (80.62) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个无需额外训练的在线 token 剪枝方法，利用深度信息和相机位姿将输入帧投影到共享体素空间，识别跨帧空间重叠区域并选择性剪枝冗余图像 token，在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上实现高达 50% 的 token 节省，并在 ScanQA、SQA3D、OpenEQA-HM3D 基准上取得改进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 问答 MLLM 需要处理场景的多视角图像，导致推理时计算开销巨大。现有方法依赖离线预处理（如策略性帧选择或 token 合并），无法实现在线高效处理。本文旨在设计一种无需额外训练、可在线运行的 token 剪枝方法，降低计算成本。

Method: 提出在线几何感知 token 剪枝方法：利用深度信息和相机位姿将每帧图像投影到共享体素空间，检测跨帧之间的空间重叠区域，在图像 token 进入语言模型之前选择性剪枝冗余 token。该方法无需额外训练，可直接集成到现有 MLLM 中。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上应用该方法，在 ScanQA、SQA3D、OpenEQA-HM3D 基准上取得改进性能，同时减少高达 50% 的 token 使用量。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在线几何感知 token 剪枝可以有效降低 3D 问答 MLLM 的计算开销，无需额外训练即可实现 token 节省与性能提升，为 3D 多模态模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出在线 token 剪枝方法以减少 3D 问答中 MLLM 的计算开销，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Proposes an online token pruning method for 3D QA that reduces inference cost, but the specific 3D QA task and offline baseline comparison limit its direct impact on your core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: online token pruning, 3D question answering, efficient inference, no additional training, spatial overlap reduction

Abstract: Recent Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on 2D question answering tasks. However, extending these models to the 3D question answering remains challenging, as they typically require multiple views of the scene, which incurs substantial computational cost at inference. To mitigate this issue, existing solutions rely on strategic frame selection or token-merging algorithms that require preprocessing in advance all frames of the scene, i.e., an offline fashion. In contrast, we propose the first online token-pruning method that can be integrated seamlessly with current MLLM models for 3D question answering tasks, without additional training and with lower memory key insight is to project each input frame into a shared voxel space using depth information and camera pose, identifying spatially-overlapped regions across frames and selectively pruning redundant image tokens before they enter the language model. Our method enables efficient online processing while reducing up to 50% of token usage. We apply this approach to Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B, demonstrating improved performance on the ScanQA, SQA3D, and OpenEQA-HM3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [212] [TORINO: Token Reduction via Interpretable Concept Overlap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04593)
*Riccardo Renzulli, Gabriele Spadaro, Shruthi Gowda, Alaa Eddine Mazouz, Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (69.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TORINO 是一种即插即用的视觉 token 缩减框架，利用稀疏自编码器将视觉 token 投影到可解释的潜空间，通过概念重叠度分析对语义相似的 token 进行分组并剪枝或合并，从而在不微调模型的情况下自适应减少 VLM 中的视觉 token 数量，实现效率与精度的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 中大量视觉 token 输入到语言模型导致计算开销巨大，现有 token 缩减方法依赖注意力分数或成对相似度，缺乏对每个 token 的显式语义表示，无法在保留重要语义信息的同时有效去除冗余。

Method: TORINO 利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将视觉 token 投影到可解释的潜空间，通过共享概念激活（active SAE latents）定义 token 间的概念重叠度，据此将语义相似的 token 分组，然后在每组内进行剪枝（pruning）或合并（merging），实现自适应 token 缩减，无需微调底层模型。

Result: 在多个视觉语言基准上，TORINO 在显著减少视觉 token 数量的同时保持了极小的性能损失，实现了优于固定预算方法的效率-精度权衡，且能根据输入复杂度动态调整缩减率。

Conclusion: TORINO 提供了一种无需微调、即插即用的 VLM token 缩减方案，通过 SAE 可解释潜空间实现语义感知的自适应 token 压缩，为高效 VLM 推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TORINO，一种通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）对视觉 token 进行可解释概念重叠分析并自适应减少 token 数量的方法，属于 VLM 的 token 压缩与高效推理，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in multimodal models via interpretable token reduction, aligning with high-priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: token reduction, efficient inference, sparse autoencoder, interpretable concept overlap, plug-and-play

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities across different tasks, but their computational cost is dominated by the large number of visual tokens fed to the language model. Existing token reduction methods rely on attention-based scores or pairwise similarity, without an explicit semantic representation of each token. We introduce TORINO (TOken Reduction via Interpretable coNcept Overlap), a plug-and-play framework for adaptive visual token reduction in VLMs that requires no fine-tuning of the underlying model. TORINO leverages Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to project visual tokens into an interpretable latent space where token relationships can be analyzed through shared concept activations. Specifically, we define concept overlap as the degree of agreement between active SAE latents and use it to group tokens that share semantic content. Reduction within each group is then performed by either pruning or merging, providing a unified framework that preserves semantically important visual information while removing redundancy. Unlike fixed-budget approaches, TORINO dynamically adapts the reduction rate to input complexity, allowing different images to retain different numbers of tokens. Experiments across multiple vision-language benchmarks show that TORINO achieves favorable efficiency-accuracy trade-offs, reducing the number of visual tokens with minimal performance loss.

</details>

### [213] [Dashboard2Code: Evaluating Multimodal Models on Reconstructing Interactive Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04727)
*Tianhao Niu, Ziyu Han, Qiguang Chen, Shiqi Zhou, Baocai Shan, Hengjie Fang, Qingfu Zhu, Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SE, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dashboard2Code 任务，要求多模态大模型通过交互式探索仪表盘（点击、筛选等）获取信息并生成复现该仪表盘的代码。同时构建了首个 Plotly+Dash 基准 DashboardMimic（180 个仪表盘-代码对），并设计了结合代码语义分析与动态交互测试的自动化评估框架。实验表明，即使最强模型在高复杂度仪表盘上仍表现不佳，开源与闭源模型间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动数据可视化生成工作主要关注静态图表，忽略了实际数据分析中广泛使用的交互式仪表盘。作者希望填补这一空白，评估多模态模型在交互式仪表盘重建任务上的能力。

Method: 定义 Dashboard2Code 任务：模型需主动探索交互式仪表盘（通过点击、筛选等操作获取反馈），并生成 Plotly+Dash 代码复现目标仪表盘。构建 DashboardMimic 基准（180 个手动验证的仪表盘-代码对，分三个难度等级，覆盖 8 种常见交互模式）。设计自动化评估框架，结合代码语义分析与动态交互测试来评估视觉和交互一致性。

Result: 在多种开源和闭源多模态模型上的实验表明：最强模型在高复杂度仪表盘上仍表现不佳；开源模型与闭源模型之间存在显著性能差距；自动化评估与人类判断具有高度一致性。

Conclusion: Dashboard2Code 是一个具有挑战性的新任务，揭示了当前多模态模型在交互式仪表盘重建方面的局限性，为未来研究提供了基准和方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Dashboard2Code 新任务和 DashboardMimic 基准数据集，并设计了自动化评测框架，属于多模态生成/理解领域的评测基准工作，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on dashboard code generation, not core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: New task and benchmark for interactive dashboards, strong evaluation framework, significant gap between open/closed models, low overlap with user's priority subtopics and boost keywords

Abstract: Automatic data visualization generation has advanced rapidly with multi-modal large language models, yet existing efforts largely focus on static charts and overlook the interactive dashboards commonly used for real-world data exploration. We introduce Dashboard2Code, a novel task that requires a model to proactively explore an interactive dashboard, acquire and integrate feedback from its own interactions (e.g., clicking and filtering), and generate code that reproduces the target dashboard. To support comprehensive evaluation, we present DashboardMimic, the first Plotly+Dash benchmark for Dashboard2Code, comprising 180 carefully designed and manually verified dashboard-code pairs spanning three difficulty levels and covering eight common real-world interaction patterns. We further propose an automated evaluation framework tailored to dashboards that combines code semantic analysis with dynamic interaction-based testing to assess visual and interaction consistency, showing strong agreement with human judgments. Experiments across a range of open- and closed-source multi-modal models reveal that even the strongest systems struggle on high-complexity dashboards and that a substantial performance gap remains between open-source and closed-source models on the Dashboard2Code task.

</details>

### [214] [G$^2$TAM: Geometry Grounded Track Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03789)
*Chenming Zhu, Peizhou Cao, Jingli Lin, Wenbo Hu, Yunlong Ran, Jiangmiao Pang, Tai Wang, Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: G²TAM 是一个基于几何空间一致性的可提示实例跟踪框架，利用前馈 3D 重建模型提供的空间对齐几何表征作为隐式记忆，实现跨视角和时序的稳定实例跟踪与分割，并构建了大规模数据集 InsTrack 用于训练和评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分割模型依赖显式外观记忆库进行实例跟踪，在视角大幅变化和长期遮挡场景下容易失效。人类的空间理解能力来源于几何与语义的联合感知，因此作者希望利用 3D 几何一致性来增强实例跟踪的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 G²TAM 框架，核心是一个跨模态空间编码器，将视觉和文本提示整合到共享几何空间中，实现端到端空间重建与实例一致的掩码预测。利用前馈 3D 重建模型生成空间对齐的几何表征作为隐式记忆，无需显式外观记忆库。同时构建了 InsTrack 大规模数据集支持训练与评测。

Result: 实验表明 G²TAM 在跨视角一致性、可提示实例空间跟踪、视频目标分割和空间重建等任务上均取得了优异性能，验证了几何引导方法在实例跟踪中的有效性。

Conclusion: G²TAM 通过几何空间引导实现了鲁棒的跨视角实例跟踪，为交互式、几何引导的空间推理奠定了基础，展示了将 3D 几何先验融入视频分割任务的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于几何空间一致性的实例跟踪框架 G²TAM，将视觉与文本提示映射到共享几何空间进行 3D 重建与实例分割，属于空间推理与 3D 视觉定位范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理" (spatial_3d_reasoning)。

Importance reason: Focuses on video instance tracking with geometry, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: video instance tracking, 3D geometry, promptable segmentation, cross-view consistency, InsTrack dataset

Abstract: Human spatial understanding arises from jointly perceiving geometry and semantics, enabling consistent object identification and localization across viewpoints and time. Current video segmentation models depend on explicit object appearance memory banks for instance tracking, yet they remain vulnerable to large viewpoint changes and long-term occlusions. Leveraging the spatial consistency afforded by modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction models, we propose the Geometry Grounded Tracking Anything Model (G$^2$TAM), a unified framework for promptable instance tracking in 3D using only unordered RGB images or videos. G$^2$TAM employs spatially aligned geometric representations as implicit memory, ensuring stable instance identity and localization across frames and views. At its core is a cross-modal spatial encoder that integrates visual and textual prompts into a shared geometric space, enabling end-to-end spatial reconstruction and instance-consistent mask prediction. To support training and evaluation, we construct InsTrack, a large-scale dataset with a dedicated validation split for benchmarking. Extensive experiments show that G$^2$TAM delivers strong cross-view consistency, promptable instance spatial tracking, video object segmentation and spatial reconstruction, establishing a foundation for interactive, geometry-grounded spatial reasoning.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-unified_multimodal_model'></div>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Unified Audio Intelligence Without Regressing on Text Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05196)
*Zhifeng Kong, Sang-gil Lee, Jaehyeon Kim, Boxin Wang, Zihan Liu, Sungwon Kim, Yang Chen, Arushi Goel, Rajarshi Roy, Wenliang Dai, Zhuolin Yang, Yangyi Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Sreyan Ghosh, Tuomas Rintamaki, Andrew Tao, Jonathan Raiman, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Wei Ping*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (85.13) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Nemotron-Labs-Audex-30B-A3B (Audex)，一个基于 MoE LLM 构建的统一音频-文本 LLM。采用单一 Transformer decoder 架构，将音频输入编码投影到文本嵌入空间，文本 token 与量化音频输出 token 统一处理。通过多阶段监督训练和文本 Cascade RL + 多域 on-policy 蒸馏，Audex 在音频理解、语音识别/翻译、TTS、音频生成和语音到语音生成上达到 SOTA，同时保持文本 LLM 的推理、对齐、知识、长上下文和智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频 LLM 通常在音频任务上表现优异，但会显著退化文本智能（推理、知识、长上下文等能力）。作者希望构建一个统一的音频-文本模型，既能实现 SOTA 的音频理解和生成，又不牺牲文本 LLM 的原有能力。

Method: 基于 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B（强文本 MoE LLM）构建 Audex。架构上采用单一 Transformer decoder：音频输入经编码器编码后投影到文本嵌入空间，文本 token 与量化音频输出 token 在生成时统一处理。训练数据精心筛选 1574 亿音频 token 和 3205 亿文本 token，采用多阶段监督训练，再结合文本 Cascade RL 和多域 on-policy 蒸馏。

Result: Audex 在音频理解、语音识别/翻译、TTS、音频生成和语音到语音生成上达到 SOTA，同时在文本推理、对齐、知识、长上下文和智能体能力上与原始文本 LLM 相比几乎没有退化或完全保持。

Conclusion: 统一音频-文本 LLM 可以在不牺牲文本智能的前提下实现 SOTA 音频智能，验证了简单统一的架构设计和多阶段训练策略的有效性。模型权重已开源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Audex，一个统一的音频-文本 LLM，同时支持音频理解、语音识别/翻译、TTS、音频生成和语音到语音生成，同时保持文本 LLM 的推理能力。这属于"统一多模态模型"（理解与生成一体化、Any-to-Any 多模态模型）范畴，该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向下均有存在，优先复用 canonical 条目。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal LLM and continuous representation priorities, but lacks direct focus on continuous token or flow matching methods. Signals: Unified audio-text LLM, MoE architecture, Multi-stage supervised training, Preserves text LLM capabilities, SOTA on multiple audio tasks

Abstract: Audio intelligence involves understanding, reasoning about, and generating both audio and speech. In this work, we introduce Nemotron-Labs-Audex-30B-A3B (Audex), a unified audio-text LLM built on Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B, a strong text-only MoE LLM. Audex adopts a simple unified design with a single Transformer decoder: audio inputs are encoded and projected into the text embedding space, while text tokens and quantized audio output tokens are treated uniformly during generation. This architecture enables strong audio-text fusion, seamless multimodal generation, and compatibility with standard LLM training and inference infrastructure. For training, we meticulously curate audio-text datasets comprising 157.4B audio tokens and 320.5B text tokens. We apply multi-stage supervised training on these datasets, followed by text-only Cascade RL and multi-domain on-policy distillation. Audex delivers state-of-the-art audio understanding, speech recognition and translation, text-to-speech, audio generation, and speech-to-speech generation, while preserving very compelling reasoning, alignment, knowledge, long-context, and agentic capabilities of its text-only LLM backbone with marginal or no regression. We release the model checkpoints to facilitate open research.

</details>


### [216] [Transferability Between Understanding and Generation in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04423)
*Jiwon Kang, Heeji Yoon, Jaewoo Jung, Jaewon Min, Minkyeong Jeon, Biyeon Hwang, Sangwon Jung, Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (90.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了统一多模态模型中理解与生成任务之间的可迁移性，发现完全共享Transformer主干和统一视觉编码器的架构能实现跨任务迁移，而松散耦合的设计则不能。基于此，提出通过训练理解任务来间接提升生成能力（如计数、空间关系、文本识别/生成），从而避免直接微调生成任务带来的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）将图像理解与生成集成在单一架构中，但两种任务之间如何相互影响、是否存在可迁移性尚未被充分研究。理解这一机制有助于设计更高效的训练策略。

Method: 通过控制变量实验，在三种不同架构（完全共享Transformer+统一视觉编码器、部分共享、松散耦合）上，分别训练理解或生成任务中的特定能力（计数、空间关系、文本识别/生成），然后评估该能力在另一任务上的表现，以量化跨任务可迁移性。基于发现，提出"通过训练理解任务来间接提升生成能力"的训练策略。

Result: 完全共享Transformer主干和统一视觉编码器的UMMs表现出一致的跨任务可迁移性；松散耦合设计几乎没有可迁移性。利用可迁移性，训练理解任务可以提升对应的生成能力（如计数、空间关系、文本生成），同时比直接微调生成任务更好地保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: UMMs中理解与生成之间存在架构依赖的可迁移性，可以系统性地利用这一特性来优化训练策略，在提升特定生成能力的同时避免视觉质量下降。

Classification reason: 该论文研究统一多模态模型（UMMs）中理解与生成任务之间的可迁移性，属于"统一多模态模型"这一现有子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Directly addresses unified multimodal models and cross-task transferability, a core priority subtopic with high weight. Signals: unified multimodal model, cross-task transferability, understanding-to-generation transfer, distribution shift mitigation, controlled experiments on counting, spatial relations, text

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) integrate image understanding and generation within a single architecture, yet how the two tasks interact remains understudied. We investigate $\boldsymbol{\mathsf{transferability}}$ in UMMs: whether training a capability on one task improves the same capability on the other without explicit supervision. Through controlled experiments, we empirically find that transferability depends on architecture-models with fully shared transformer backbone and a unified visual encoder exhibit consistent cross-task transfer, while loosely coupled designs show little or none. Leveraging this transferability, we propose a practical training strategy. The most straightforward way to improve a target generative capability (e.g., counting) is to fine-tune generation directly, but this can degrade visual quality due to distribution shift. Instead, we train the corresponding understanding task and let it transfer into generation, which improves capability-specific generative performance while minimizing distribution shift. We validate this across three capabilities-counting, spatial relation, and text recognition/generation-showing that cross-task transferability can be systematically exploited in UMMs.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>
