### [217] [UI-MOPD: Multi-Platform On-Policy Distillation for Continual GUI Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04425)
*Niu Lian, Alan Chen, Zhehao Yu, Chengzhen Duan, Fazhan Liu, Hui Liu, Pei Fu, Jian Luan, Yaowei Wang, Shu-Tao Xia, Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出UI-MOPD，首个将多教师在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）引入持续学习的GUI智能体方法。构建了Uni-GUI跨平台GUI交互数据集，通过平台条件蒸馏实现跨平台能力保持与新平台适应，在OSWorld和MobileWorld上分别达到38.2%和12.0%的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体从单平台向跨平台交互发展，但面临两大挑战：高质量跨平台交互轨迹数据稀缺且平台覆盖有限；不同平台交互习惯差异大，联合或持续训练容易导致行为模式混淆、平台特定能力退化和灾难性遗忘。

Method: 构建Uni-GUI高质量跨平台GUI交互数据集；提出UI-MOPD方法，在持续学习框架中引入多教师在线策略蒸馏，根据当前环境动态选择平台特定教师，通过平台条件蒸馏将平台特定行为先验迁移到共享策略中，实现新平台适应同时保持已有平台能力。

Result: 在OSWorld和MobileWorld基准上，UI-MOPD分别达到38.2%和12.0%的任务成功率，验证了其在跨平台能力保持与新平台适应之间取得平衡的有效性。

Conclusion: UI-MOPD通过多教师在线策略蒸馏有效解决了多平台GUI智能体持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和能力退化问题，为构建跨平台GUI智能体提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多平台GUI智能体，基于多模态基础模型构建跨平台交互智能体，属于视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）在GUI智能体领域的应用。虽然涉及持续学习，但核心是VLM驱动的GUI智能体，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agent continual learning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: GUI agent, continual learning, on-policy distillation, cross-platform, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models and agent systems have driven GUI agents from single-platform task execution toward cross-platform interaction. However, building multi-platform GUI agents remains challenging. On one hand, high-quality and executable cross-platform interaction trajectories are still scarce, and existing data often suffer from limited platform coverage. On the other hand, different platforms exhibit distinct interaction conventions, making joint or continual training prone to behavioral pattern mixing, platform-specific capability degradation, and catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we construct Uni-GUI, a high-quality cross-platform GUI interaction dataset, and propose UI-MOPD, the first method that incorporates multi-teacher on-policy distillation into continual learning for GUI agents. UI-MOPD dynamically selects a platform-specific teacher according to the current environment and transfers platform-specific behavioral priors to a shared policy through platform-conditioned distillation, enabling adaptation to new platforms while preserving capabilities on existing ones. Experiments on OSWorld and MobileWorld show that UI-MOPD achieves task success rates of 38.2% and 12.0%, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness in balancing cross-platform capability retention and new-platform adaptation. Project page: .

</details>


### [218] [Gemma 4 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02770)
*Gemma Team, Sherif El Abd, Vaibhav Aggarwal, Robin Algayres, Alek Andreev, Olivier Bachem, Ian Ballantyne, Cormac Brick, Victor Cărbune, Michelle Casbon, Mayank Chaturvedi, Victor Cotruta, Alice Coucke, Phil Culliton, Robert Dadashi, Lucas Dixon, Mohamed Elhawaty, Utku Evci, Clément Farabet, Johan Ferret, Filippo Galgani, Sertan Girgin, Jean-Bastien Grill, Maarten Grootendorst, Jiaxian Guo, Cassidy Hardin, Yanzhang He, Steven M. Hernandez, Omri Homburger, Léonard Hussenot, Juyeong Ji, Armand Joulin, Aishwarya Kamath, Parnian Kassraie, Olivier Lacombe, Preethi Lahoti, Gaël Liu, Gus Martins, Luciano Martins, Tatiana Matejovicova, Ramona Merhej, Nikola Momchev, Sneha Mondal, Ryan Mullins, Sindhu Raghuram Panyam, Shreya Pathak, Sarah Perrin, André Susano Pinto, Etienne Pot, Angéline Pouget, Alexandre Ramé, Sabela Ramos, Douglas Reid, David Rim, Morgane Rivière, Karsten Roth, Louis Rouillard, Omar Sanseviero, Pier Giuseppe Sessa, Shane Settle, Danila Sinopalnikov, Sara Smoot, Piotr Stanczyk, Andreas Steiner, Lawrence Stewart, Ilya Tolstikhin, Michael Tschannen, Anton Tsitsulin, Nino Vieillard, Renjie Wu, Pingmei Xu, Haichuan Yang, Edouard Yvinec, Li Zhang, Joe Zou, Nicolas Aagnes, Abdelrahman Abdelhamed, Shivani Agrawal, Shubham Agrawal, Ibrahim Alabdulmohsin, Jean Baptiste Alayrac, Uri Alon, Chandramouli Amarnath, Ankesh Anand, Chrysovalantis Anastasiou, Setareh Ariafar, François-Xavier Aubet, Kyriakos Axiotis, Federico Barbero, Joelle Barral, Alexei Bendebury, Urs Bergmann, Stanley Bileschi, Kat Black, Mathieu Blondel, Sebastian Borgeaud, Arthur Bražinskas, Ryan Burnell, Robert Busa-Fekete, Mu Cai*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (90.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Gemma 4 是 Google 推出的新一代开源多模态语言模型系列，包含 Dense 和 MoE 架构（2.3B-31B 参数），支持文本、图像和音频输入，采用统一无编码器架构（12B 模型）和思考模式（thinking mode），在 STEM、多模态和长上下文基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动开源多模态语言模型的计算效率和推理能力，缩小与闭源前沿模型的差距，同时保持模型权重开放。

Method: Gemma 4 系列包含 Dense（2.3B、9B）和 MoE（12B、31B）架构。改进视觉和音频编码器；12B 模型采用统一无编码器架构，直接处理原始音频和图像 patch。集成思考模式（thinking mode），在回答前生成推理轨迹。通过关键设计选择提升推理速度、内存和计算效率以及长上下文能力。

Result: 在 STEM、多模态和长上下文基准上取得显著性能提升，在人工评测任务中与更大的前沿开源模型竞争。

Conclusion: Gemma 4 通过架构创新（MoE、无编码器设计、思考模式）在开源多模态语言模型中实现了计算效率和推理能力的重大进步，为社区提供了强大的开放权重基础模型。

Classification reason: 该论文介绍 Gemma 4 系列多模态语言模型，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal MLLM with encoder-free architecture and thinking mode, highly relevant to user's priority subtopics. Signals: Unified encoder-free multimodal architecture, Thinking mode for reasoning traces, MoE and dense architectures, Strong STEM and multimodal benchmarks, Open-weight release

Abstract: We introduce Gemma 4, a new generation of open-weight, natively multimodal language models in the Gemma model family. Designed to advance compute efficiency and reasoning, the Gemma 4 model suite features dense and Mixture-of-Experts architectures, ranging from 2.3B to 31B parameters. Alongside improved vision and audio encoders for all model sizes, we propose a unified, encoder-free architecture for our 12B model, which ingests raw audio and image patches. Furthermore, we integrate a thinking mode, enabling Gemma models to generate reasoning traces prior to responding. We improve inference speed, memory, and compute efficiency, as well as long-context abilities through critical design choices. Gemma 4 establishes a leap in performance across STEM, multimodal, and long-context benchmarks, and rivals larger, frontier open models in human-rated tasks.

</details>


### [219] [CL-Anomaly: Layer-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts with Multimodal Large Language Model for Continual Learning in Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02930)
*Wen Dong, Zhao Wang, Shuangqing Zhang, Kai Sun, Ben Li, Guo-Sen Xie, Caifeng Shan, Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CL-Anomaly 提出了一种基于层自适应混合专家（MoE）与多模态大语言模型的持续学习框架，用于异常检测。通过任务私有专家 PrivLoRA 隔离任务特定参数空间，层自适应共享专家维护跨任务表征，并利用动量机制实现层自适应知识迁移，在类增量、跨域和跨模态三种持续学习场景中均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在异常检测中表现优异，但现实世界知识不断演化，全参数重训练计算成本过高。现有持续学习方法面临任务间参数空间语义纠缠的问题，而异常检测任务本身具有模态、领域和缺陷尺度的三重异质性，进一步加剧了多任务知识迁移的难度。

Method: 提出 CL-Anomaly 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务私有专家 PrivLoRA，在参数空间中物理隔离任务特定子空间，防止不同场景下异常知识的语义纠缠；(2) 层自适应共享专家，在统一特征空间中维护跨任务表征，实现新旧任务间的知识共享；(3) 层自适应知识迁移策略，通过动量机制自动选择并动态更新每层的关键共享专家，促进相关异常检测任务间的有效知识迁移。

Result: 在类增量、跨域和跨模态三种持续学习异常检测场景中，CL-Anomaly 均优于现有最先进方法，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并实现了正向知识迁移。

Conclusion: CL-Anomaly 通过隔离-共享协作的参数高效微调框架，成功解决了 MLLM 在异常检测持续学习中的语义纠缠问题，为多模态模型在动态知识环境中的持续部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于MLLM的持续学习异常检测框架，属于视觉语言模型的应用与微调方法，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on anomaly detection continual learning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: anomaly detection, continual learning, multimodal large language model, mixture-of-experts, parameter-efficient fine-tuning

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in diverse vision tasks, but full-parameter retraining is computationally expensive as real-world knowledge evolves. Existing continual learning methods often suffer from semantic entanglement in parameter spaces across tasks, impeding the continuous deployment of models. This challenge is especially pronounced in Anomaly Detection (AD), which exhibits triple heterogeneity across modalities, domains, and defect scale variability, significantly complicating multi-task knowledge transfer. In this paper, we propose CL-Anomaly, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework based on an isolation-sharing collaboration to enable continual learning for anomaly detection with MLLMs. We introduce the task-private expert PrivLoRA, which physically isolates task-specific subspaces in the parameter space to prevent semantic entanglement of anomaly knowledge in diverse scenarios. The Layer-Adaptive Shared Experts maintain cross-task representations within a unified feature space, enabling knowledge sharing between previous and new tasks. Furthermore, we propose a Layer-Adaptive Knowledge Transfer strategy that automatically selects and dynamically updates the layer-wise key shared experts of each task via a momentum-based mechanism, promoting effective knowledge transfer across related anomaly detection tasks. Extensive experiments across three continual learning scenarios for anomaly detection, including class-incremental, cross-domain, and cross-modal, demonstrate that CL-Anomaly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at .

</details>


### [220] [VLM-CASE: Vision-Language Model Enabled Context-Adaptive Safety Envelopes for Anticipatory Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05180)
*Tianjia Yang, Ke Li, Ruwen Qin, Xianbiao Hu*

Main category: eess.SY, cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM-CASE 提出一个框架，利用 LoRA 微调的 VLM 从车前摄像头图像推理路面和能见度条件，动态参数化基于物理的安全包络（CASE），并通过模型预测控制器（MPC）在安全包络内进行自主驾驶控制，在 CARLA 仿真中优于传统 MPC 和现有 VLM 集成控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣驾驶条件（如坏天气）同时降低车辆的感知能力和物理操控能力，而人类驾驶员能通过预判提前调整速度、跟车距离和转向，但现有自动驾驶系统最多只能事后反应。因此需要一种能赋予自动驾驶车辆预判能力且始终受形式化安全模型约束的框架。

Method: 使用 LoRA 微调 VLM（视觉语言模型），从车前摄像头图像推理路面条件和能见度；基于物理极限和责任敏感安全（RSS）推导出上下文自适应安全包络（CASE），通过共享摩擦预算耦合制动和转向；模型预测控制器（MPC）在安全包络内自由驾驶，VLM 异步运行不阻塞实时控制循环。

Result: 在 CARLA 仿真中，VLM-CASE-MPC 在所有横向和纵向控制任务中均成功完成，优于传统 MPC 基线和最新的 VLM 集成控制器。消融实验证实性能提升来自上下文自适应，摩擦和能见度自适应具有互补性。

Conclusion: VLM-CASE 框架通过 VLM 驱动的上下文自适应安全包络，有效提升了自动驾驶在恶劣条件下的安全性和预判能力，且控制器无关，可搭配多种底层控制器，为安全自动驾驶提供了有前景的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 VLM（视觉语言模型）进行场景理解（路面和能见度条件），属于通用视觉语言模型的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general 子主题匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving safety with VLM, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generative video/image generation. Signals: VLM for autonomous driving, LoRA fine-tuning, safety envelope, MPC control, CARLA simulation

Abstract: Adverse driving conditions, such as bad weather, remain a principal barrier to autonomous driving because they degrade two things at once: what the vehicle can perceive and what it can physically do. Human drivers cope by anticipation, reasoning about the scene and re-budgeting speed, following distance, and steering before grip or sight is lost, whereas current autonomous driving systems at best react after the fact. This paper proposes VLM-CASE, a framework that gives an autonomous vehicle this anticipatory capacity while keeping its motion bounded by a formal safety model at all times. A vision-language model (VLM), fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), reasons about the scene from the front-camera image and reports the road surface and visibility conditions. This output parametrizes a context-adaptive safety envelope (CASE), derived from physical limits and the guarantees of responsibility-sensitive safety, that couples braking and steering through a shared friction budget. A model predictive controller then drives freely within the envelope, while the VLM runs asynchronously so it never blocks the real-time control loop. We validate the framework in closed-loop CARLA simulation on tasks that demand both lateral and longitudinal control, across a range of weather, road-surface, and lighting conditions. The resulting controller, VLM-CASE-MPC, completes all trials, outperforming a conventional MPC baseline and a state-of-the-art VLM-integrated controller. Ablations confirm that the gains come from context adaptation, with the friction and visibility adaptations proving complementary. Furthermore, the framework is controller-agnostic and pairs with almost any low-level controller, offering a promising direction for safe autonomous driving. The dataset and supplementary materials for VLM-CASE are available at .

</details>


### [221] [OpenGlass: A Sensing-Computing Split Architecture for Local MLLM-Driven Real-Time Visual Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03213)
*Mengzhang Li, Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OpenGlass 是一个开源的、隐私优先的本地实时视觉辅助系统，采用感知-计算分离架构：ESP32 眼镜端采集视觉数据，本地消费级设备运行 MLLM 推理和语音输出，无需上传数据到云端。在真实 Wi-Fi 环境下达到 993ms 的中位端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端 MLLM 助手虽然视觉理解能力强，但需要上传第一人称视觉数据，存在隐私风险且网络延迟可达数秒；可穿戴眼镜适合感知但无法承载大模型。OpenGlass 旨在填补这一空白，实现低延迟、隐私保护的本地视觉辅助。

Method: 采用感知-计算分离架构：眼镜端使用 ESP32 摄像头采集图像，通过 Wi-Fi 传输到附近的消费级设备（如笔记本电脑），本地运行 MLLM 进行视觉理解推理，并输出语音结果。系统支持用户主动触发查询，包含障碍物/危险感知、标志/物体查询、图像质量自检等功能。

Result: 在真实 ESP32 Wi-Fi 环境下，OpenGlass 的中位端到端延迟（查询到音频输出）为 993ms（压缩图像）和 1625ms（原始 1280×720 图像）；97.5% 和 93.3% 的测试在 2 秒内完成。系统还评估了响应质量、安全感知的拒绝回答和可审计日志。

Conclusion: OpenGlass 证明了在本地消费级设备上运行 MLLM 实现实时视觉辅助的可行性，在隐私保护和延迟方面优于云端方案。该系统作为用户主动触发的视觉辅助参考平台，而非认证导航工具，为低视力人群提供了实用的开源解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个基于本地 MLLM 的实时视觉辅助系统，属于通用视觉语言模型的应用系统方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on system architecture for local MLLM visual assistance, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation advances. Signals: sensing-computing split, local MLLM, real-time visual assistance, privacy-first, ESP32

Abstract: We present OpenGlass, an open-source, privacy-oriented, local-first system for low-latency multimodal visual assistance, with a primary focus on blind and low-vision users. Cloud MLLM assistants offer strong visual understanding, but often require uploading first-person visual data and can suffer multi-second network delays; wearable glasses are ideal for sensing, but cannot host large models under tight compute and power budgets. OpenGlass addresses this gap with a sensing-computing split: an ESP32-based glasses-side unit captures visual context, while a nearby consumer-grade device performs local MLLM inference and local speech output, reducing cloud reliance and keeping raw egocentric visual data on user-controlled devices by default. We evaluate response quality, query-ready-to-audio latency, safety-aware abstention, and auditable logs. Under real ESP32 Wi-Fi capture, OpenGlass reaches 993 ms median user-to-audio latency with resized payloads and 1625 ms with raw 1280 x 720 payloads; 97.5% and 93.3% of trials fall below 2 s, respectively. OpenGlass is a user-initiated visual-assistance reference platform for obstacle/hazard awareness, sign/object queries, and image-quality self-checking, rather than a certified navigation aid. We release source code, hardware instructions, prompts, evaluation data, and logs.

</details>


### [222] [Pathways of Visual Information Flow in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03358)
*Israfel Salazar, Stella Frank, Dan Oneata, Desmond Elliott, Constanza Fierro*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预（causal patching）研究VLM中视觉信息的流动路径，发现存在两条独立通路：直接通路（图像token保留信息，由最终token在深层读出）和文本中介通路（视觉信息先传递到查询token再被读出）。通路选择取决于任务类型、数据分布和提示设计，且模型在干预下可灵活切换通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解VLM内部视觉信息如何流动和路由，揭示模型解决视觉任务的机制，统一先前工作中看似矛盾的发现。

Method: 使用因果修补（causal patching）方法，在受控合成数据集和自然数据集上对VLM进行干预实验，包括注意力消融（attention knockout）和损坏输入修补（corrupted-input patching），分析视觉信息在图像token和文本token之间的传递路径。

Result: 发现两条视觉信息通路：直接通路（图像token保留信息，最终token在深层读出）和文本中介通路（视觉信息先传递到查询token再读出）。通路选择具有任务依赖性，且模型在主要通路被消融时可灵活切换到备用通路。

Conclusion: VLM内部存在灵活可切换的视觉信息路由机制，干预实验揭示的是模型"能做什么"而非"通常做什么"，这为理解VLM内部机制提供了新的视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）中视觉信息的流动路径与内部机制，属于通用VLM架构与机理分析，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" (vlm_mllm_general)。

Importance reason: Provides mechanistic insight into VLM visual information flow, relevant to unified multimodal models and multimodal understanding. Signals: VLM mechanistic interpretability, causal patching, visual information pathways, task-dependent routing, flexible model mechanisms

Abstract: We study how visual information is routed in vision-language models (VLMs). Using causal patching on controlled synthetic and natural datasets, we find that models rely on two distinct pathways to solve visual tasks: A direct pathway, where visual information is retained in image token representations and read out by the final token at later layers, and a text-mediated pathway, where visual information is first transferred to the query tokens and then read out by the final token. Across three visual tasks, we show that pathway selection is task-dependent, and that data distribution and prompt design can also modulate which pathway is used to solve the image-based query. Moreover, using attention knockouts and corrupted-input patching, we find that these pathways are flexible, under certain interventions, models can rely on the text-mediated pathway as a fallback when the usual pathway is ablated. This behavior unifies findings in prior work and shows that ablation-based interventions can reveal what models could do rather than what they normally do. Together, our results provide a mechanistic characterization of visual information flow in VLMs and highlight the flexibility of their internal mechanisms under intervention.

</details>


### [223] [Token-Based Affordance Grounding with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03595)
*Seung Il Lee, Qinqian Lei, Daguang Xu, Dong Yang, Robby T. Tan, Yixin Chen, Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TokAG，一种零样本 affordance grounding 框架，通过利用 LVLM 输出 token 的注意力图选择空间感知的 token，将隐式语义信号转化为零样本 affordance 热力图，无需外部监督即可定位动作相关区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 affordance grounding 方法依赖弱监督学习，难以处理视觉模糊的 exocentric 图像和语义相似的动作；而 LVLM 虽蕴含丰富动作语义和空间线索，但未直接提供动作特定的空间定位能力。

Method: 提出 TokAG 框架，包含空间感知 token 选择机制：系统评估 LVLM 每个输出 token 的注意力图，选择对目标物体具有主导激活的 token，将其注意力图转化为零样本 affordance 热力图。

Result: 在 AGD20K 的未见分割上 NSS 提升 10.7%，在 HICO-IIF 上提升 29.7%，一致优于先前的弱监督方法。

Conclusion: TokAG 证明了 LVLM 的 token 级注意力图可以有效用于零样本 affordance grounding，为无需外部监督的动作相关区域定位提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文利用大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的注意力图进行零样本 affordance grounding，属于视觉语言模型的应用与扩展，与 canonical 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on affordance grounding with LVLMs, not directly aligned with priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: zero-shot affordance grounding, token-level attention selection, LVLM spatial cues, outperforms weakly supervised methods, robotics and embodied perception

Abstract: Affordance grounding aims to localize image regions that support a specific action, serving as a core capability for physical intelligence and embodied perception. Previous studies have primarily relied on weakly supervised learning with action labels from exocentric images. However, these methods often struggle with visually ambiguous exocentric images containing co-occurring actions; moreover, they fail to distinguish semantically similar actions because existing methods typically rely on brief action phrases that lack rich semantic details for action-specific localization. Although large vision-language models (LVLMs) encode rich action semantics and their action-conditioned textual outputs implicitly contain spatial cues, they do not directly provide action-specific spatial localization. To address these problems, we propose TokAG, a zero-shot affordance grounding framework that exploits the token-level semantic-spatial signals in LVLMs to localize action-relevant regions without external supervision. We observe that attention maps associated with different LVLM output tokens vary significantly, with many attending to irrelevant regions such as the background. Thus, we introduce a spatial-aware token-selection mechanism to systematically evaluate each output token and select the one whose attention maps exhibit dominant activation over the target object, instead of relying on arbitrary attention maps. By extracting these object-focused attention maps, we transform the LVLM's implicit semantic signals into zero-shot affordance heatmaps. Our zero-shot framework consistently outperforms prior weakly supervised approaches across multiple benchmarks, improving NSS by 10.7% on the unseen split of AGD20K and by 29.7% on HICO-IIF. The code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [224] [ViPo-MLLM: Visual-Pose Multimodal LLM for Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03657)
*Ahmed Abul Hasanaath, Bicheng Xu, Mir Rayat Imtiaz Hossain, Leonid Sigal, Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViPo-MLLM 是一种融合时空 RGB 和人体姿态特征的多模态大语言模型，用于无标注手语翻译（Gloss-Free SLT），在 PHOENIX14T 和 CSL-Daily 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标注手语翻译（Gloss-Free SLT）避免了昂贵的 gloss 标注，但需要精细建模手部、身体和面部线索。现有方法多使用单模态或弱融合特征，性能受限。

Method: 提出 ViPo-MLLM 框架，包含：(1) 专用编码器分别建模 RGB 和人体姿态的帧内动态；(2) 跨模态注意力捕获长程依赖；(3) 融合表示与结构化提示结合，输入 LLM；(4) 使用对比学习和语言建模目标联合训练。

Result: 在 PHOENIX14T 和 CSL-Daily 数据集上取得新 SOTA 结果，且与基于 gloss 的识别方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 姿态线索和跨模态注意力机制能有效提升无标注手语翻译性能，验证了多模态融合在 SLT 中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ViPo-MLLM，将手语视频的 RGB 和人体姿态特征融合后输入 LLM 进行翻译，属于视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）架构设计，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on sign language translation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: Gloss-free SLT, multimodal LLM, pose features, SOTA on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily, no continuous language model or video generation

Abstract: Gloss-free Sign Language Translation (SLT) translates sign language videos into spoken-language sentences without gloss annotations, avoiding costly labeling but requiring fine-grained modeling of hands, body, and facial cues. Existing methods often use single-modality or weakly fused features, limiting performance. We propose ViPo-MLLM, a framework that integrates spatio-temporal RGB and human pose features. Dedicated encoders model intra-modal dynamics and cross-modal attention captures long-range dependencies. The fused representation is conditioned with a structured prompt and processed by an LLM trained with contrastive and language modeling objectives. The proposed model was evaluated on the PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily datasets and achieved new state-of-the-art results on both datasets. Moreover, the ViPo-MLLM model attained competitive performance compared to gloss-based recognition approaches, confirming the effectiveness of the proposed pose cues and cross-modal attention mechanisms.

</details>


### [225] [From Geometric Labels to Semantic Understanding of Indoor Building Components Using Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03661)
*Shuju Jing, Chao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Building-MLLM，一个以点云为中心的多模态大语言模型，用于室内建筑组件的语义理解。模型通过点云信息增强器、几何保持正则化、固定文本前缀和多维度 LoRA 四个机制实现语义聚焦，并在自建的合成点云-文本数据集上完成简单识别、复杂描述和多工程问答三类任务，分别达到 88.00%、65.10% 和 68.14% 的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点云的室内建筑组件理解方法仅输出离散标签，无法解释组件功能或支持自然语言交互，缺乏语义层面的理解能力。

Method: 提出 Building-MLLM，包含四个关键机制：(1) Point Information Enhancer 提取任务相关语义；(2) Geometry-Preserving Regularization 防止几何信息丢失；(3) 固定文本前缀稳定领域语义；(4) 多维度 LoRA 平衡识别与推理能力。同时开发多约束渐进式指令生成引擎，构建包含 4198 个物体、37782 条指令对、47 个类别的合成点云-文本数据集。

Result: 在简单识别、复杂描述和多工程问答三类任务上分别达到 88.00%、65.10% 和 68.14% 的准确率，并在其他真实数据集上展示了初步的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Building-MLLM 有效实现了室内建筑组件的语义理解与自然语言交互，为点云中心的多模态大语言模型在设施运维中的应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Building-MLLM，一个面向室内建筑组件的点云中心多模态大语言模型，属于通用 MLLM 架构与多模态指令微调范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on indoor building components with point cloud MLLM, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: point cloud MLLM, indoor building components, synthetic dataset, domain-specific mechanisms, limited generalizability

Abstract: Point cloud-based understanding has become an important enabler for facility operation and maintenance involving indoor building components. However, existing methods output only discrete labels without explaining component functions or natural language interactions. This paper proposes Building-MLLM, a point cloud-centered multimodal large language model (MLLM) for indoor components, which models point clouds and instructions to generate responses across Simple Recognition, Complex Captioning, and Multi-Engineering Question Answering tasks. Building-MLLM addresses semantic concentration through four domain-specific mechanisms: Point Information Enhancer for task-relevant semantics, Geometry-Preserving Regularization preventing geometric erosion, fixed textual prefix for domain stabilization, and multi-dimensional LoRA balancing recognition with reasoning. A multi-constraint progressive instruction-generation engine is developed to compile a synthetic point cloud-text dataset with 4198 objects, 37,782 instruction-following pairs, and 47 categories. Experiments show that Building-MLLM achieves 88.00%, 65.10%, and 68.14% on the three task types, respectively, demonstrating superior indoor component language understanding and providing initial generalizability in transfer inference on other real-world datasets.

</details>


### [226] [Global Logic and Local Search: Dual-Stream Multimodal In-Context Learning for Verifiable Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03817)
*Runzhi Deng, Yundi Hu, Yiming Zhong, Zhao Wang, Xixi Liu, Hongsong Wang, Caifeng Shan, Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GLLS，一种无需训练的多模态上下文学习框架，用于可验证的工业异常检测。通过 Part-Aware Visual-Logical Atlas 组织参考样本，结合全局逻辑流（SAM 3 提取局部视觉事实）和细粒度动作流（MCTS 在固定预算下选择证据裁剪），实现可追溯的异常诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测中缺陷小、输入分辨率有限、文本标准难以对齐视觉证据。现有优化方法需要大量缺陷样本微调，但早期部署阶段缺陷样本稀缺。因此需要一种无需训练、可追溯证据的参考引导多模态验证方法。

Method: GLLS 框架包含：(1) Part-Aware Visual-Logical Atlas，将正常参考样本和结构化规格说明组织到推理上下文中；(2) 全局逻辑流（Global & Logic Stream），使用 SAM 3 提取可局部检查的视觉事实；(3) 细粒度动作流（Fine-Grained & Actions Stream），使用 MCTS（蒙特卡洛树搜索）在固定预算下选择局部证据裁剪。最终将两流结果结合，生成可追溯视觉证据的诊断决策。

Result: 在 MMAD-QA 及其他异常检测数据集上，GLLS 在无需训练的情况下一致优于匹配的基线和通用基线方法，且最终诊断决策可追溯到明确的视觉证据。

Conclusion: GLLS 证明了无需微调的多模态上下文学习在工业异常检测中的有效性，通过双流架构实现了可验证、可追溯的异常诊断，为低资源场景下的工业检测提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于大多模态模型（LMM）的工业异常检测框架，属于视觉语言模型在特定场景下的应用。虽然任务聚焦于工业异常检测，但其核心方法是多模态上下文学习（in-context learning），属于通用 VLM/MLLM 的研究范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Focuses on industrial anomaly detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong overlap with user's key interests in generative models and continuous representations. Signals: Training-free in-context learning, Industrial anomaly detection, MCTS for evidence selection, SAM 3 visual fact extraction, No generative or continuous modeling focus

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) show strong few-shot generalization, but industrial anomaly detection remains difficult because defects are small, input resolution is limited, and textual standards are not always grounded in visual evidence. Recent optimization-based methods improve alignment through fine-tuning, but they often require many defective samples, which are unavailable in early deployment. We present Global Logic and Local Search (GLLS), a training-free framework for reference-guided multimodal in-context verification. GLLS uses a Part-Aware Visual-Logical Atlas to organize normal references and structured specifications in the inference context. It combines a Global & Logic Stream, where SAM 3 extracts partially checkable visual facts, with a Fine-Grained & Actions Stream, where MCTS selects local evidence crops under a fixed budget. Experiments on MMAD-QA and additional anomaly detection datasets show consistent gains over matched and general-purpose baselines, while keeping the final diagnostic decision traceable to explicit visual evidence throughout the inspection trace.

</details>


### [227] [CogRad: A Cognitively-Inspired Multi-Agent Framework for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03853)
*Saif Ur Rehman Khan, Hasaan Maqsood, Sebastian Vollmer, Andreas Dengel, Muhammad Nabeel Asim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (66.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CogRad 是一个受认知科学启发的多智能体框架，模拟放射科医生的四阶段阅读流程（侦察-调查-撰写-验证），通过 slot attention 发现解剖区域、疾病门控视觉前缀引导 LLM 生成报告、以及逐句验证机制，在 CheXpert Plus 和 IU X-Ray 上取得了领先的 NLG 指标和临床准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成系统通常一次前向传播生成完整报告，缺乏对图像中已陈述发现的回溯验证机制，导致报告可能包含不准确或缺乏视觉依据的陈述。

Method: CogRad 包含四个智能体：(1) Scout 通过 slot attention 从图像块中发现解剖区域并分配区域/疾病级分诊分数；(2) Investigator 将表征能力集中在 Scout 标记的可疑区域；(3) Writer 利用疾病门控视觉前缀引导 LLM 生成报告；(4) Verifier 在训练时使用视觉蕴含损失监督，推理时逐句检查并重新生成视觉依据不足的句子。

Result: 在 CheXpert Plus 上 BLEU-4 0.316、CIDEr 0.322；在 IU X-Ray 上 BLEU-4 0.201、CIDEr 0.724，在所有标准 NLG 指标上超越基线。RadGraph F1、CheXbert F1 和幻觉分析进一步验证了临床准确性。

Conclusion: CogRad 通过模拟放射科医生认知流程的多智能体架构，有效提升了放射学报告生成的准确性和视觉可溯源性，验证了结构化多阶段生成范式在医学报告生成中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于多智能体的放射学报告生成框架，属于视觉语言模型在医学领域的应用，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong clinical NLP work but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: multi-agent radiology report generation, cognitive-inspired reading pipeline, slot attention for anatomical regions, disease-gated visual prefix, visual entailment verification

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation (RRG) can ease radiologist workload, yet most existing systems produce a report in a single forward pass, with no mechanism to check a claim against the image or revisit a finding once stated. We present CogRad, a cognitively inspired multi-agent framework that structures generation around four stages of a radiologist's reading process. A Scout agent discovers anatomical regions directly from image patches via slot attention and assigns region and disease-level triage scores; an Investigator agent concentrates representational capacity on the regions Scout flags as suspicious; a Writer agent compiles these signals into a disease gated visual prefix for a large language model; and a Verifier agent supervises training with a visual entailment loss and, at inference, re-examines its own draft sentence by sentence, regenerating any report it judges insufficiently grounded. On CheXpert Plus, CogRad attains a BLEU-4 of 0.316 and a CIDEr of 0.322, the best scores among the methods we compare against. On IU X-Ray, it attains a BLEU-4 of 0.201 and a CIDEr of 0.724, leading every baseline on every standard NLG metric. We further evaluate CogRad with RadGraph F1, CheXbert F1, and a hallucination analysis to assess clinical accuracy beyond standard text-overlap metrics, complemented by ablation studies and Grad-CAM-based visualizations that characterize each agent's contribution and the model's visual grounding.

</details>


### [228] [PixelPilot: Scalable Vision-Language-Action Models for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04637)
*Pin Tang, Guoqing Wang, Xiangxuan Ren, Zhongdao Wang, Guodongfang Zhao, Bailan, Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PixelPilot 提出了一种解耦规划与升维的 VLA 范式，将自动驾驶轨迹预测从 2D-to-3D 改为传感器无关的 2D-to-2D 图像平面规划，推理时再确定性升维到 3D，并采用 GRPO 策略学习实现从视觉感知到空间规划的因果链，在开环和闭环设置中均达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法直接从 2D 图像预测 3D 轨迹，存在两个问题：(1) 2D-to-3D 预测与相机参数耦合，导致跨异构数据集的可扩展性差；(2) 直接在 3D 空间优化容易收敛到平凡解（模型依赖 ego 状态而非视觉场景理解）。因此需要一种传感器无关、充分挖掘视觉线索的规划范式。

Method: 提出 PixelPilot，包含两个阶段：(1) 规划阶段：将场景理解与轨迹预测重新定义为传感器无关的 2D-to-2D 图像平面任务，实现跨数据集的规模化训练；(2) 升维阶段：推理时将规划的 2D 轨迹确定性升维到 3D。同时提出知识注入的策略学习（knowledge-instilled policy learning），通过 GRPO 施加密集的中间奖励，强制模型遵循从视觉感知到空间规划的因果链。

Result: 在多个自动驾驶数据集上的开环和闭环实验中，PixelPilot 均取得了 SOTA 性能，验证了其优越的可扩展性和视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: PixelPilot 通过解耦规划与升维、2D-to-2D 图像平面规划以及 GRPO 策略学习，有效解决了现有 VLA 在数据可扩展性和视觉推理方面的关键瓶颈，为端到端自动驾驶提供了一种更鲁棒和可扩展的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PixelPilot，一种视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA），用于端到端自动驾驶。其核心是结合 VLM 的推理能力进行视觉场景理解与轨迹规划，属于视觉语言模型在具身/自动驾驶场景的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Novel VLA paradigm for autonomous driving, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: VLA, autonomous driving, GRPO, 2D-to-2D planning, sensor-agnostic

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs), which leverage the advanced reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), show promising generalization in complex autonomous driving scenarios. Existing VLAs typically predict and optimize 3D trajectories from 2D images. While intuitive, this 2D-to-3D prediction is inherently entangled with camera parameters, leading to limited data scalability across heterogeneous driving datasets. Moreover, directly optimizing in 3D space induces severe convergence to trivial solutions, where VLAs rely on ego-status rather than visual scene understanding. To address these issues, we propose PixelPilot, a novel VLA featuring a decoupled planning and lifting paradigm. In the planning phase, PixelPilot reformulates scene understanding and trajectory prediction as sensor-agnostic 2D-to-2D tasks in the image plane, thereby facilitating scalable training across diverse datasets. The planned 2D trajectories are then deterministically lifted to 3D only during inference, ensuring the full exploitation of visual cues and generalization across different vehicles. To realize this paradigm, we propose a knowledge-instilled policy learning strategy that applies dense, intermediate rewards via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to enforce a rigorous causal chain from visual perception to spatial planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PixelPilot achieves state-of-the-art performance in both open-loop and closed-loop settings, validating its superior scalability and visual reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [229] [Vision Pretraining for Dense Spatial Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05247)
*Zelin Fu, Bin Tan, Changjiang Sun, Shaohui Liu, Kecheng Zheng, Yinghao Xu, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Nan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (85.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出掩码边界建模（Masked Boundary Modeling），一种自监督视觉预训练范式，通过学习亚像素边界表征并将边界承载的视觉token作为掩码目标，促进密集空间感知（如深度估计）的视觉token学习。基于此方法训练的LingBot-Vision在多个下游任务上超越DINOv3基线，并推动了深度补全模型LingBot-Depth的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉基础模型倾向于优先保持语义不变性，往往牺牲了详细的空间理解能力。然而，密集空间感知（如深度估计）对于物理智能至关重要。作者希望探索一种以边界为中心的视觉预训练方法，利用边界和形状不连续性提供的几何线索来学习空间结构化的视觉表征。

Method: 提出掩码边界建模（Masked Boundary Modeling），一种自监督范式。该方法动态学习亚像素级别的边界表征，然后利用发现的边界承载token作为掩码目标，促进密集视觉token的学习。通过扩展该框架，训练得到LingBot-Vision视觉编码器，并应用于深度补全任务，从LingBot-Depth 1.0演进到LingBot-Depth 2.0。

Result: LingBot-Vision在多个下游视觉任务上以DINOv3为强基线取得了显著提升。在深度补全任务中，LingBot-Depth 2.0相比1.0版本实现了深度估计性能的增强，为具身人工智能提供了关键支撑。

Conclusion: 边界建模不仅仅是简单的线段检测，而是可以作为学习空间结构化视觉表征的可扩展预训练原则。该方法证明了以边界为中心的预训练范式能够有效提升视觉系统对密集空间感知任务的能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种自监督视觉预训练方法（Masked Boundary Modeling），旨在提升视觉基础模型对密集空间感知（如深度估计）的能力。虽然不直接是VLM/MLLM，但属于视觉基础模型预训练范式，与多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉表征学习密切相关。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 可涵盖视觉基础模型预训练这一主题。

Importance reason: Strong on spatial perception pretraining but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: self-supervised visual pretraining, dense spatial perception, boundary modeling, depth estimation, DINOv3 baseline

Abstract: Dense spatial perception is essential for physical intelligence, where visual systems are expected to recover structured, metric, and actionable representations from pixel observations. Modern visual foundation models tend to prioritize semantic invariance, often at the expense of detailed spatial understanding. In this work, we study vision pretraining through a boundary-centric lens, motivated by the premise that boundaries and shape discontinuities offer essential cues for perceiving geometric properties. Concretely, we propose masked boundary modeling, a self-supervised paradigm that dynamically learns sub-pixel boundary representations and subsequently leverages the discovered boundary-bearing tokens as masked targets to facilitate dense visual token learning. By scaling this framework, we develop LingBot-Vision and demonstrate its efficacy across a diverse set of downstream vision tasks with DINOv3 as a strong baseline. Remarkably, LingBot-Vision drives the progression from LingBot-Depth 1.0 to LingBot-Depth 2.0 for depth completion, and thereby yields enhanced depth estimation, a key pillar for embodied artificial intelligence. Our findings reveal that boundary modeling goes beyond simple line segments and instead serves as a scalable pretraining principle for learning spatially structured visual representations.

</details>


### [230] [Repurposing CLIP to Localize at Pixel Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05253)
*Jiaxiang Fang, Shiqiang Ma, Jing Wang, Siyu Chen, Fei Guo, Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLIPix 框架，通过回溯 CLIP 的分类过程提取物体特定注意力区域作为像素级定位线索，并引入噪声抵抗校正和定位嵌入策略，将 CLIP 从图像级定位扩展到像素级分割，在 PASCAL 和 COCO 上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 在图像级开放集定位上表现出色，但将其扩展到像素级密集预测（如分割）面临全局特征偏差带来的挑战。现有方法难以充分利用 CLIP 的泛化能力进行精确的像素级定位。

Method: CLIPix 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 回溯 CLIP 分类过程，识别物体特定的注意力区域作为像素级定位线索；(2) 提出噪声抵抗校正策略（Noise-Resistant Correction），消除全局偏差引入的噪声，细化定位线索；(3) 引入定位嵌入策略（Localization Embedding），融合定位信息与丰富的细节信息，实现高分辨率分割。

Result: 在 PASCAL 和 COCO 数据集上的大量实验表明，CLIPix 达到了最先进的性能，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: CLIPix 成功保留了 CLIP 的泛化能力，并解锁了其对任意物体进行分割的潜力，为将视觉语言模型从图像级理解扩展到像素级密集预测提供了简单有效的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文利用 CLIP（视觉语言模型）进行像素级定位与分割，属于对 VLM 能力的挖掘与扩展，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on CLIP repurposing for segmentation, not on user's priority continuous multimodal or video generation topics. Signals: CLIP pixel-level localization, noise-resistant correction, localization embedding, PASCAL/COCO SOTA, not autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language Models like CLIP have demonstrated impressive open-set localization capabilities at the image level. However, adapting this capability to pixel-level dense prediction poses challenges due to global feature biases. In this paper, we introduce CLIPix, a simple yet effective framework that repurposes CLIP to perform pixel-level localization. By tracing back CLIP's classification process, CLIPix identifies object-specific attentive regions and repurposes them as pixel-level localization cues. To address noise introduced by global biases, we propose a Noise-Resistant Correction strategy, refining these cues for more precise segmentation. Additionally, we introduce a Localization Embedding strategy to integrate both localization and enriched detail information, enabling accurate, high-resolution segmentation. Our approach preserves CLIP's generalization strength and unlocks its potential for segmenting arbitrary objects. Extensive experiments on the PASCAL and COCO datasets demonstrate that CLIPix achieves state-of-the-art performance, underscoring its effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [231] [BVS: Bayesian Visual Search with Multimodal Large Language Model for Fine-grained Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03184)
*Geng Li, Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BVS（贝叶斯视觉搜索）框架将MLLM的细粒度感知形式化为连续空间-尺度流形上的全局优化问题，通过早期停止注意力展开构建推理感知先验，并利用尺度感知非平稳核与GP-UCB进行后验校正，在超高分辨率图像的小目标感知上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在超高分辨率图像中对杂乱场景中的小目标细粒度感知能力不足。已有方法要么依赖低效的无先验扫描，要么依赖静态先验驱动的启发式方法，缺乏后验校正来修正初始模型偏差。

Method: 提出BVS（贝叶斯视觉搜索）框架，将感知建模为连续空间-尺度流形上的全局优化问题。具体包括：(1) 利用MLLM的早期停止注意力展开构建推理感知先验；(2) 采用尺度感知非平稳核和GP-UCB（高斯过程上置信界）通过迭代局部观测动态校正噪声并恢复先验中缺失的信息。

Result: 在多个超高分辨率图像细粒度感知基准上，BVS显著优于现有最先进方法，在准确率和效率之间取得了更优的权衡。同时提供了亚线性遗憾界的理论保证。

Conclusion: BVS通过贝叶斯优化框架将先验引导与后验校正有机结合，有效解决了MLLM在超高分辨率图像中的细粒度感知难题，为MLLM的精细视觉理解提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注MLLM在超高分辨率图像中的细粒度感知（tiny objects in cluttered scenes），属于视觉问答与细粒度理解范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a Bayesian optimization framework for fine-grained MLLM perception, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: MLLM fine-grained perception, Bayesian visual search, ultra-high-resolution images, GP-UCB posterior correction, no direct link to continuous models or video generation

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate impressive general capabilities, they struggle with fine-grained perception in ultra-high-resolution (UHR) images, particularly for tiny objects in cluttered scenes. Existing methods face a dilemma: they either rely on inefficient prior-free scanning, or depend on static prior-driven heuristics that lack posterior correction to rectify initial model biases. To address this, we propose BVS (Bayesian Visual Search), a framework that formulates perception as a global optimization problem over a continuous spatial-scale manifold. Specifically, BVS bridges prior guidance with posterior correction: it utilizes an early-stop attention rollout of MLLM to construct reasoning-aware priors, while employing a scale-aware non-stationary kernel and GP-UCB to dynamically rectify noise and recover missing information in the prior through iterative local observations. We provide theoretical guarantees via sub-linear regret bounds, and extensive experiments demonstrate that BVS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with a superior trade-off between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [232] [Responsibility Distribution Estimation in Ego-View Accident Videos with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03591)
*Ryosei Tamura, Andrew Shin*

Main category: cs.CY, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (69.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出自视角事故视频中的责任分配估计新任务，利用多模态大语言模型预测各涉事方的责任百分比，构建了LLM辅助标注流程，并在多种输入设置下微调MLLM，建立了强初始基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通事故理解研究多依赖基础设施摄像头、卫星图像或结构化事故记录，这些数据成本高、难以大规模部署，且无法客观反映驾驶员实际观察到的情况。自视角事故视频能直接呈现驾驶员的视觉视角，更适合推理事故的可避免性和驾驶员责任。

Method: 构建了LLM辅助的责任标注流程，对自视角事故视频进行责任分配标注。在多种输入设置下微调多模态大语言模型（MLLM），包括原始帧、分割增强输入和文本描述，让模型预测各涉事方的责任百分比。

Result: 实验建立了强初始基准，证明多模态LLM能够有效执行这种细粒度、基于约束的推理任务。

Conclusion: 自视角事故视频为超越传统事故分类和解释的社会及法律意义的多模态推理提供了有前景的基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从自视角事故视频中估计责任分配比例的新任务，本质上是多模态视觉问答（VQA）的一种特殊形式——模型需要基于视频帧进行约束推理并输出数值比例。这与 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的 VQA 任务高度吻合，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on traffic accident responsibility estimation, which is outside your core priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: Ego-view accident videos, Multimodal LLM fine-tuning, Responsibility distribution, LLM-assisted annotation, Low relevance to continuous models, video generation, or world models

Abstract: Recent studies on multimodal traffic accident understanding have mainly relied on infrastructure-camera footage, satellite imagery, or structured crash records. However, such data sources are costly to deploy and maintain at large scale, and they cannot objectively capture what the driver was actually able to observe before the accident. In contrast, ego-view accident videos directly represent the driver's visual perspective, making them suitable for reasoning about avoidability and driver responsibility. In this paper, we introduce responsibility distribution estimation for ego-view traffic accident videos, a new task in which a model predicts the percentage of responsibility assigned to each involved agent. We construct an LLM-assisted responsibility annotation pipeline and fine-tune multimodal large language models under multiple input settings, including raw frames, segmentation-enhanced input, and textual descriptions. Experimental results establish a strong initial benchmark, demonstrating that multimodal LLMs can effectively perform this nuanced, constraint-based reasoning task. Our findings suggest that ego-centric accident videos provide a promising foundation for socially and legally meaningful multimodal reasoning beyond conventional accident classification and explanation tasks.

</details>


### [233] [Q-TriM: Question-Guided Tri-Modal Attention for Audio-Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03825)
*SungHun Kim, SeungJun Baek*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Q-TriM 提出了一种基于问题引导的三模态注意力机制，以浅层并行融合替代深层顺序融合，用于音视频问答（AVQA），在三个基准上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AVQA 系统依赖深层堆叠的自注意力和交叉注意力层进行文本、视频和音频的融合，这种顺序堆叠方式会导致跨模态信息在逐层传递中丢失，且误差累积严重。作者希望设计一种浅层并行融合机制来缓解这些问题。

Method: 提出 Q-TriM 框架，核心是 Tri-Modal Attention 操作，其中 Query、Key、Value 分别来自不同模态（文本、视频、音频），在单阶段并行计算多种注意力表示（包括标准交叉注意力和三模态注意力），避免深层顺序堆叠带来的信息损失和误差累积。

Result: 在 MUSIC-AVQA、AVQA 和 MUSIC-AVQA-R 三个基准上取得 SOTA 性能，尤其在分布外泛化评测集 MUSIC-AVQA-R 上提升显著。

Conclusion: 浅层并行三模态注意力融合能够有效缓解深层顺序融合中的信息丢失和误差累积问题，在 AVQA 任务上展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音视频问答（AVQA），属于多模态问答的范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）直接相关，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio-visual QA, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks boost keywords. Signals: AVQA, tri-modal attention, shallow parallel fusion, SOTA on MUSIC-AVQA-R, out-of-distribution generalization

Abstract: Audio-Visual Question Answering (AVQA) extends classical VQA by requiring joint reasoning over video and synchronized audio. However, many AVQA systems rely on deeply stacked layers of self- and cross attention across text, video, and audio. Such sequential stacking may incur loss of information such as subtle inter-modal cues over the layers, causing errors to accumulate across sequential attention layers during the fusion. We introduce Q-TriM which performs multi-modal fusion in a shallow and parallel manner instead of a deep and sequential manner. For Q-TriM, we propose a novel framework for attention operation incorporating video and audio conditioned on text. As a result, we obtain not only standard cross attention outputs but also Tri-Modal Attention representations in which Query, Key, and Value come from distinct modalities. These attention representations are combined in parallel at a single stage, thus avoiding the multi-modal fusion with deep stacks in order to mitigate error accumulation and depth-induced issues. Q-TriM achieves state-of-the-art performance on three AVQA benchmarks, including substantial gains on MUSIC-AVQA-R, which demonstrates its robustness and out-of-distribution generalization. Code is available at

</details>


### [234] [Beyond Scene Priors: Fine-Grained Traffic Scene Reasoning with Benchmarking and Query-Guided Small-Object Focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04149)
*Waikit Xiu, Qiang Lu, Zian Wang, Xinjie Yang, Zhiwei Chen, Chen Sun, Xiying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对交通场景中MLLM对小目标关注不足的问题（"关键证据稀释"），构建了细粒度交通推理基准FGTR-Bench（40,236道多选题），并提出TSR-MLLM模型，通过查询引导的小目标聚焦（TG-SOF）图在解码器边界增强问题相关视觉token，在4B参数规模下取得74.1%准确率，超越基线2.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键的交通场景中，回答复杂问题依赖于微小的局部视觉线索。然而，标准MLLM倾向于过度关注背景，导致关键小目标在视觉-语言对齐中被淹没，即"关键证据稀释"问题。现有VQA数据集缺乏大规模、含干扰项的细粒度评测来暴露这一缺陷。

Method: 1) 构建FGTR-Bench：通过多智能体生成、一致性检查和专家审核创建40,236道单图多选题，含4,947样本盲测集。2) 提出TSR-MLLM：基于Qwen3-VL-4B，在解码器边界引入查询引导的小目标聚焦（TG-SOF）图，对最问题相关的视觉槽位添加稀疏Top-K门控残差，保持单次推理，无需外部检测器或图像重编码。

Result: 在匹配设置下，TSR-MLLM在FGTR-Bench上以74.1%整体准确率超越最强4B基线2.1个百分点，在证据定位轨道上提升更大。在DriveQA-V（CARLA Signs）上，无需任务特定微调，贪心解码下仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了MLLM在交通场景中的"关键证据稀释"问题，通过构建细粒度基准和提出查询引导的小目标聚焦机制，有效提升了模型对局部关键线索的感知能力，为安全关键场景的VQA提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个细粒度交通场景推理基准（FGTR-Bench）和一个面向小目标推理的MLLM（TSR-MLLM），核心任务是视觉问答（VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题。该子主题为[CANONICAL]条目，直接匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on traffic scene VQA, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Traffic scene reasoning, MLLM small-object focus, Benchmark FGTR-Bench, Query-guided attention, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: In safety-critical traffic scenarios, answering complex questions relies on minute, localized visual cues. However, standard Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) tend to over-attend to backgrounds, overwhelming crucial small objects during visual-language alignment, a failure mode we term 'critical evidence dilution.' Furthermore, existing visual question answering (VQA) datasets rarely expose this flaw, as they lack large-scale, distractor-heavy evaluations that require pinpointing local evidence. To bridge this evaluation and architecture gap, we introduce the Fine-Grained Traffic Reasoning Benchmark (FGTR-Bench) and the Text-Guided Small-Object Reasoning MLLM (TSR-MLLM). FGTR-Bench comprises 40,236 single-image Multiple-Choice Questions (MCQs) created via multi-agent generation, consistency checks, and expert audits, alongside a disjoint 4,947-sample blind test split. To resolve evidence dilution, TSR-MLLM, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, uses a query-conditioned Text-Guided Small-Object Focus (TG-SOF) map. Applied once at the decoder boundary, the map adds sparse Top-K gated residuals to the most question-relevant vision slots while leaving text tokens unchanged. Together with lightweight decoder adaptation, TSR-MLLM preserves single-pass inference without external detectors or image re-encoding. Under matched settings, TSR-MLLM outperforms the strongest 4B baseline by 2.1 points on FGTR-Bench (74.1% overall), with larger gains on evidence-local tracks. Furthermore, it remains competitive on DriveQA-V (CARLA Signs) under greedy decoding without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [235] [IRIS: An Intelligent Vision-Language System for Ocular Surface Diseases via Topic Tree and Scene-Driven VQA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04344)
*Hao Wei, Wenjin Qi, Dasen Dai, Minqing Zhang, Wu Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出IRIS系统，通过主题树（TFT）和场景驱动策略生成高质量眼表疾病VQA数据集IRIS-120K，微调4B参数VLM实现超越34B通用及医学VLM的细粒度眼表疾病理解性能，证明了结构化知识注入优于参数规模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM在眼表疾病等专业领域临床推理能力严重不足，主要瓶颈在于缺乏高质量、多模态的指令微调数据。传统图像-文本描述对语义浅层，无法提供细粒度的医学推理所需的结构化临床先验知识。

Method: 提出双分支数据生成框架：1）主题发现树（TFT）将视觉特征层次化地锚定到精确的解剖和病理概念，强制执行严谨的医学推理逻辑；2）场景驱动策略合成角色自适应的临床对话以确保实用泛化能力。基于此生成IRIS-120K数据集，微调4B参数VLM。

Result: IRIS在眼表疾病理解任务上全面超越通用和专用医学VLM（包括高达34B参数的模型），证明了结构化知识注入比单纯扩大参数规模更有效，且支持在移动边缘设备上部署。

Conclusion: 结构化知识注入是提升专业领域VLM性能的关键路径，可以超越参数规模扩展的效果，为资源高效的专家级AI在移动设备上的可扩展眼表疾病筛查提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于眼表疾病领域的视觉问答（VQA）数据集构建与VLM微调，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一规范子主题。虽然领域是医学眼表疾病，但核心任务是VQA数据生成与VLM微调，与现有规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Specialized medical VLM dataset generation, but outside user's core priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: medical diagnosis, clinical, remote sensing, structured knowledge injection, VQA dataset generation

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate remarkable generic capabilities, their clinical reasoning in specialized domains like ocular surface diseases (OSDs) is severely hindered by a paucity of high-fidelity, multimodal instruction-tuning data. To dismantle this data bottleneck, we introduce IRIS, an Intelligent Recognition and Interaction System tailored for fine-grained OSD understanding via external eye photography. First, we curate IRIS-120K, the largest and most comprehensive OSD visual question-answering (VQA) dataset to date. Crucially, to overcome the semantic shallowness of conventional image-caption pairs, we propose a synergistic data generation paradigm to explicitly inject clinical priors. Our data engine operates via a dual-branch framework: 1) a Topic Finding Tree (TFT) that hierarchically anchors visual features to precise anatomical and pathological concepts, enforcing rigorous medical deduction logic; and 2) a Scene-driven strategy that synthesizes role-adaptive clinical dialogues to ensure pragmatic generalization. By explicitly aligning a compact 4B-parameter VLM on this structurally enriched corpus, IRIS achieves state-of-the-art performance, comprehensively outperforming both generalist and specialized medical VLMs with up to 34B parameters. Our findings underscore that structured knowledge injection profoundly prevails over sheer parameter scaling, unlocking the potential for resource-efficient, expert-level AI deployment on mobile edge devices for scalable OSD screening. Code, datasets, and model weights will be publicly released by this repo.

</details>


### [236] [Hierarchical Evidence-Driven Reasoning for Long Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04625)
*Junyu Xiong, Yonghui Wang, Rongjian Gu, Chenyu Liu, Bing Yin, Wengang Zhou, Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出HIEVI-RAG，一种层次化证据驱动的多模态RAG框架，通过问题分解、粗粒度页面检索、细粒度页面验证（基于GRPO训练的EVIAGENT）和记忆引导迭代生成四个阶段，系统性地解决长文档理解中检索误导和单次遗漏导致级联错误的问题，在四个基准上平均超越最强基线8.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG管道面临两个关键挑战：一是标准语义相似度检索常检索到主题相关但缺乏答案的干扰页面，误导下游生成；二是刚性单次管道严重依赖初始检索成功，核心证据遗漏会导致级联错误。

Method: 提出HIEVI-RAG框架，将复杂查询分解为四阶段流水线：(1)层次化问题分解，将多跳根问题拆解为原子子问题；(2)粗粒度视觉页面检索，利用多模态检索器基于语义相似度获取候选页面；(3)细粒度页面验证，通过GRPO训练的EVIAGENT（专用多页面验证器）对多图像块进行跨页面推理；(4)记忆引导迭代生成，利用累积的子问题上下文对优先级序列进行多轮动态推理。

Result: 在四个基准上的广泛评估表明，该框架显著优于现有开源基线，平均准确率超过最强基线8.05%。

Conclusion: HIEVI-RAG通过层次化证据驱动的多阶段设计，有效缓解了多模态RAG中检索误导和级联错误问题，为长文档理解提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于长文档理解中的多模态RAG框架，涉及文档页面检索、跨页面推理与问答，属于[视觉问答与文档理解]（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一规范子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on document understanding RAG, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: RAG framework, long document understanding, hierarchical reasoning, GRPO training, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) streamlines long-document understanding by leveraging retrieval mechanisms to restrict input images to a highly curated subset. However, existing multimodal RAG pipelines primarily face two critical challenges: first, standard semantic similarity retrievers frequently fetch topically overlapping yet answer-void distractor pages that mislead downstream generation; second, rigid single-pass pipelines heavily depend on initial retrieval success, where any omission of core evidence inevitably causes cascading errors. To address these challenges, we introduce HIEVI-RAG, a hierarchical, evidence-driven multimodal RAG framework for closed-domain document understanding. HIEVI-RAG systematically factorizes complex queries into a cooperative four-stage pipeline: (1) hierarchical question decomposition to break multi-hop root queries into atomic child questions; (2) coarse visual page retrieval leveraging a multimodal retriever to fetch candidate pages based on semantic similarity; (3) fine-grained page verification via EVIAGENT, a specialized multi-page verifier trained with GRPO to execute cross-page reasoning over multi-image blocks; and (4) memory-guided iterative generation that leverages accumulated sub-question context to execute multi-round, dynamic reasoning over the prioritized sequence. Extensive evaluations across four benchmarks demonstrate the robust efficacy and synergy of our framework, which significantly outperforms existing open-source baselines and exceeds the strongest reported baseline by an average of 8.05% in accuracy.

</details>


### [237] [Enhancing Large Multimodal Models in Key Information Extraction via Scene-Aware Document Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04636)
*Zhipeng Xu, Zulong Chen, Qing Liu, Junhao Ji, Jinxin Hu, Yipeng Yu, Jianqiang Wan, Jun Tang, Zhao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SAYRE 是一个场景感知文档合成框架，通过少量示例文档自动生成大规模 KIE 训练数据，并引入错误驱动生成策略来增强困难样本，有效提升轻量级多模态大模型在关键信息提取任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键信息提取（KIE）在实际部署中面临两难：强性能依赖昂贵的服务器端大模型，而可本地部署的轻量模型缺乏足够的 KIE 监督数据。现有数据合成方法依赖手工模板设计，难以覆盖真实场景的多样性。

Method: SAYRE 框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 场景感知文档合成——从少量示例文档中捕获类别特定的内容模式和布局惯例，自动生成文档-模式-标注三元组；(2) 错误驱动生成——将真实场景中的失败案例扩展为困难训练样本，同时保留其结构模式。

Result: 在受限类别和开放类别 KIE 任务上，SAYRE 一致提升了 Qwen3-VL 骨干模型的性能，在端侧 LMM 中取得最佳整体表现。数据规模扩展实验显示合成数据越多效果越好，尤其对小模型和开放类别提取提升显著。错误分析表明合成训练通过改善对密集表格、业务标识和合同条款的模式感知提取，减少了字段级错误。

Conclusion: 场景感知文档合成是一种有效的数据中心方法，能够显著提升实际多模态 KIE 的性能，为轻量级模型提供高质量训练数据。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于关键信息提取（KIE），即从视觉丰富的文档中提取结构化数据，属于文档理解范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）涵盖文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能等，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on document KIE data synthesis, not on core priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Document synthesis, Key information extraction, Multimodal understanding, Data augmentation, On-device LMMs

Abstract: Key Information Extraction (KIE) converts visually rich documents into structured data, but practical deployment remains challenging: strong performance often relies on costly on-server Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), while compact locally deployable models lack sufficient KIE supervision. We present SAYRE, a scene-aware document synthesis framework for generating scalable KIE training data without hand-crafted template design. Given a few exemplar documents, SAYRE captures category-specific content patterns and layout conventions to synthesize document-schema-annotation triples. It further introduces error-driven generation, which expands real-world failure cases into hard training examples while preserving their structural patterns. Experiments on constrained- and open-category KIE show that SAYRE consistently improves Qwen3-VL backbones and achieves the strongest overall performance among on-device LMMs. Data scaling experiments show an overall upward trend as more synthesized data is introduced, especially for smaller models and open-category extraction. Error analysis further shows that synthesized training reduces field-level errors by improving schema-aware extraction over dense tables, business identifiers, and contract clauses. These results establish scene-aware synthesis as an effective data-centric approach for improving practical multimodal KIE.

</details>


### [238] [HunyuanOCR-1.5: Making Lightweight OCR VLMs Faster and Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04884)
*Gengluo Li, Xingyu Wan, Shangpin Peng, Weinong Wang, Hao Feng, Yongkun Du, Binghong Wu, Zheng Ruan, Zhiqiong Lu, Liang Wu, Pengyuan Lyu, Huawen Shen, Zibin Lin, Shijing Hu, Jieneng Yang, Hongbing Wen, Guanghua Yu, Hong Liu, Bochao Wang, Can Ma, Han Hu, Chengquan Zhang, Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HunyuanOCR-1.5是一个轻量级端到端OCR专用视觉语言模型，通过DFlash加速OCR解码实现6.37倍Transformer推理加速，并提出Agentic Data Flow智能数据构建系统，显著提升古籍OCR、细粒度图表解析、多图像文档问答等长尾能力，在OmniDocBench上达到顶尖水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级OCR VLM在推理速度（长结构化输出如密集文档、表格、公式时延迟高）和长尾能力（古籍OCR、细粒度图表解析、多图像文档问答等）方面存在不足，亟需在不增加模型规模的前提下同时提升效率和能力覆盖范围。

Method: 1) 将DFlash（一种推测解码方法）适配到OCR解码场景，在不改变输出分布的前提下显著加速长结构化输出；2) 提出Agentic Data Flow，一种智能体驱动的数据构建系统，自动将模型弱点转化为可执行的数据需求，自主完成素材搜索、质量验证和流程开发；3) 升级预训练和后训练策略，扩展高分辨率、长上下文和多任务场景能力。

Result: DFlash实现6.37倍Transformer推理加速和2.14倍vLLM加速，在轻量级OCR VLM中达到最快推理速度；在OmniDocBench v1.6上达到端到端OCR解决方案的顶尖水平，并在古籍OCR、细粒度图表表格解析、多图像文本问答、低资源多语言解析等长尾任务上取得新的性能里程碑。

Conclusion: HunyuanOCR-1.5证明了轻量级端到端OCR VLM可以在推理速度和能力覆盖上同时达到高水平，DFlash和Agentic Data Flow为OCR领域的高效推理和数据构建提供了有效方案，将开源模型权重和训练代码以支持后续研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出轻量级OCR专用视觉语言模型HunyuanOCR-1.5，统一了文档解析、文本检测、信息提取、多图像文档理解等任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on OCR efficiency and data construction, not on core priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: OCR VLM, DFlash acceleration, Agentic Data Flow, lightweight model, document understanding

Abstract: We present HunyuanOCR-1.5, a lightweight end-to-end OCR-specialized vision-language model. HunyuanOCR unifies document parsing, text spotting, information extraction, text-image translation, and multi-image document understanding within a single end-to-end VLM. Building upon the lightweight architecture of HunyuanOCR-1.0, HunyuanOCR-1.5 does not redesign the backbone, but systematically improves both efficiency and capability. For efficiency, we adapt DFlash to OCR decoding, significantly reducing the latency of long structured outputs such as dense documents, tables, and formulas while preserving output distribution. Powered by DFlash, HunyuanOCR-1.5 achieves a 6.37x Transformer inference speedup and a 2.14x speedup under vLLM, delivering the fastest inference among lightweight OCR VLMs. For capability, we propose Agentic Data Flow, an agent-driven data construction system that transforms model weaknesses into executable data requirements and autonomously performs material search, quality verification, and pipeline development. It substantially improves long-tail capabilities in ancient-script OCR, fine-grained chart and table parsing, multi-image text-centric QA, low-resource multilingual parsing, and document hallucination evaluation. HunyuanOCR-1.5 ranks among the top-tier end-to-end OCR solutions on OmniDocBench v1.6 while achieving new performance milestones across these long-tail tasks. Combined with an upgraded pretraining and post-training recipe, HunyuanOCR-1.5 further extends its capability in high-resolution, long-context, and multi-task scenarios. Experiments demonstrate faster inference, broader OCR capability coverage, and the deployment advantages of a lightweight end-to-end model. We will release the model weights and training code to support future research and real-world OCR applications.

</details>

### [239] [Parallelized Autoregressive Decoding for Omni-Modal Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02963)
*Wenzheng Zeng, Siyi Jiao, Chen Gao, Hwee Tou Ng, Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (65.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种并行化自回归解码框架用于全模态密集视频描述，通过利用跨事件弱局部依赖关系重构因果依赖图，实现无损并行生成，显著提升推理效率与时序定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集视频描述需要生成带时间定位的事件描述，自回归视频大语言模型虽能力强，但逐 token 生成效率极低，随着视频长度和事件密度增加，推理效率成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出并行化自回归框架：(1) 潜在全局规划机制（latent global planning），自动学习事件级结构并生成紧凑 token 编码全局跨事件因果性，同时自适应聚合事件级音视频语义；(2) 事件分解并行解码机制（event-factorized parallel decoding），平衡局部关注与全局跨事件感知。跨事件弱依赖 token 并行解码，事件内紧密耦合 token 保持顺序解码以保证局部语义连贯性。

Result: 在多个基准上，该方法在效率和性能上均显著优于现有方法，在全模态事件定位和描述方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过利用跨事件弱局部依赖关系重构因果图，可以在不损失生成质量的前提下实现并行解码，为密集视频描述的高效推理提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究密集视频描述（dense video captioning），属于视频理解与时序推理方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes parallelized autoregressive decoding for dense video captioning, improving efficiency and temporal grounding, which aligns with user's interest in efficient inference and video understanding. Signals: parallelized autoregressive decoding, dense video captioning, efficient inference, weak local dependencies, event-factorized decoding

Abstract: Dense video captioning aims to generate temporally grounded descriptions of video events, benefiting both event-level video understanding and generation. In this domain, autoregressive video large language models have emerged as a prevalent paradigm due to their strong generative and cross-modal modeling capacity. However, generating dense captions under the token-by-token paradigm severely limits inference efficiency and hinders scalability as video length and event density increase. In this work, we propose a parallelized autoregressive framework that not only improves generation efficiency but also enhances temporally grounded captioning performance. Our key insight is to exploit the weak local dependencies across temporally distinct events to restructure the causal dependency graph, thereby enabling lossless parallel generation. Specifically, tokens with weak cross-event dependencies can be decoded in parallel, while tightly coupled tokens within each event retain sequential decoding to preserve local semantic coherence. To realize this insight, we introduce two key components for lossless parallel decoding: (1) a latent global planning mechanism that automatically learns the event-level structure and produces compact tokens encoding global inter-event causality while adaptively aggregating event-level audio-visual semantics, guiding subsequent dependency restructuring and parallel decoding; and (2) an event-factorized parallel decoding mechanism that effectively balances local focus with global inter-event awareness. Experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the clear advantage of our approach in both efficiency and performance in omni-modal event grounding and captioning. Project website: .

</details>


### [240] [DELTAVID: Enhancing Fine-Grained Spatiotemporal Perception with Cross-Video Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02551)
*Yankai Yang, Yancheng Long, Bin Wen, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Han Li, Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DELTAVID 提出一种基于跨视频差异的代理任务框架，通过让模型比较相似视频找出局部变化、判断时间边界并组织空间证据，来增强视频多模态大模型的细粒度时空感知能力。同时构建了 DELTAVID-10K 训练集和 DELTAVID-Bench 评测基准，实验表明该方法显著提升了跨视频差异理解能力，并能泛化到多个通用视频理解基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频多模态大模型在开放域视频理解上取得了显著进展，但在细粒度时空感知方面仍存在不足。当两个视频全局语义几乎相同、仅在小时间跨度或小区域存在差异时，现有模型往往无法发现变化并提供可靠证据。作者希望将"找不同"这一人类视觉感知能力引入视频 MLLM，作为一种可扩展的代理监督信号来提升模型的细粒度时空证据推理能力。

Method: DELTAVID 的核心思想是将跨视频"找不同"转化为可训练的感知信号。具体包括：(1) 构建 DELTAVID-10K 数据集，通过在真实视频中引入可控局部差异（如区域遮挡、时间片段替换等），生成带有证据标签的训练样本；(2) 设计代理任务框架，让模型识别局部变化、判断时间边界并组织空间证据；(3) 在 DELTAVID-Bench 上评估细粒度感知能力，并在多个通用视频理解基准上验证迁移效果。

Result: DELTAVID 在跨视频差异理解任务上显著提升了性能，并且学到的局部证据能力能够有效迁移到通用视频理解基准，包括 MMVU、MLVU、Video-MME、VideoHolmes、VideoMMMU、LVBench、TempCompass 和 LongVideoBench 等，表明该方法使视频 MLLM 从粗粒度语义理解向细粒度时空证据推理迈进。

Conclusion: 跨视频差异不仅是一种有效的细粒度感知故障诊断方法，也是一种可扩展的代理监督信号，能够推动视频多模态大模型从粗粒度语义理解向细粒度时空证据推理方向发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DELTAVID 框架，通过跨视频差异增强视频多模态大模型的细粒度时空感知能力，属于视频理解领域。论文在多个视频理解基准（MMVU, MLVU, Video-MME, LongVideoBench 等）上评测，核心是提升视频 MLLM 的时空证据推理能力，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的"视频理解与时序推理"子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on video understanding proxy task, not directly on user's priority subtopics like generation or continuous models. Signals: fine-grained spatiotemporal perception, cross-video difference, proxy-task framework, video MLLM, DELTAVID-10K

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models have made strong progress on open-ended video understanding, but they still lack precise local spatiotemporal perception. When two videos share almost the same global semantics and differ only in a short time span or a small region, current models often fail to find the change and provide reliable evidence. We propose DELTAVID, a verifiable proxy-task framework that enhances fine-grained spatiotemporal perception with cross-video differences. The key idea is to turn cross-video spot-the-difference into a trainable perception signal, where a model identifies local changes, judges temporal boundaries, and organizes spatial evidence by comparing similar videos. To make this signal scalable to train and reliable to evaluate, we further introduce DELTAVID-10K and DELTAVID-Bench, which convert controllable local differences in real videos into evidence-labeled training and test samples. Experiments show that DELTAVID substantially improves performance on cross-video difference understanding and transfers the learned local evidence ability to general video understanding benchmarks, including MMVU, MLVU, Video-MME, VideoHolmes, VideoMMMU, LVBench, TempCompass, and LongVideoBench. These results show that cross-video differences are not only an effective way to diagnose fine-grained perception failures, but also a scalable proxy supervision that moves Video MLLMs from coarse semantic understanding toward fine-grained spatiotemporal evidence reasoning.

</details>


### [241] [Homer: Understanding Long-form Videos with Hierarchical Memory and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02588)
*Yixin Ji, Fanghua Ye, Juntao Li, Bo Zhao, Zexuan Qiu, Zhaopeng Tu, Liefeng Bo, Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (82.02) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Homer 提出了一种分层在线记忆探索与推理框架，通过多尺度记忆结构（原始感知→实体→事件）和智能体推理器，在有限记忆下实现长视频的在线理解，在多个长视频基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在短视频上表现优异，但在小时级长视频的在线处理场景中面临挑战：现有方法要么保留紧凑但缺乏语义结构的视觉表征，要么构建基于时间邻近性而非因果关联的高层记忆，导致多跳叙事推理困难。

Method: Homer 构建了分层记忆结构，从原始感知层到实体层再到事件层，事件之间建立显式的时间与因果关联。智能体推理器模拟人类探索记忆的方式，通过多轮记忆检索定位相关场景、查找细节并组合答案，同时配备验证与纠正机制确保每一步的正确性。

Result: 在 M3-Bench-robot、M3-Bench-web 和 Video-MME-Long 上分别比之前最优智能体方法高出 +5.5、+10.8 和 +4.4 个点，且在不同 LLM 骨干上均有一致提升，表明该方法具有模型无关的结构化检索能力。

Conclusion: Homer 通过分层记忆与智能体推理有效解决了长视频在线理解中的多跳叙事推理问题，验证了结构化记忆检索对长视频理解的通用有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注长视频在线理解，提出分层记忆与智能体推理框架，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on unified multimodal models and efficient inference for long-form video understanding. Signals: hierarchical memory, agentic reasoning, long video understanding, online processing, multimodal large language model

Abstract: Multimodal large language models excel on short clips but struggle on hour-long videos in an online setting, where frames are processed incrementally under limited memory. Existing online methods either retain compact visual representations that lack semantic structure, or build higher-level memory stores organized around temporal proximity rather than explicit causal links, leaving multi-hop narrative reasoning to be reconstructed by the LLM at every query. We bridge this gap with \textsc{Homer}, a Hierarchical Online Memory Exploration and Reasoning framework. \textsc{Homer}'s memory mirrors the multi-scale structure of long videos, ranging from raw perception, to recurring entities, to events connected by explicit temporal and causal relations. Its agentic reasoner then explores this memory the way humans do, locating the relevant scene, looking up details, and composing the answer through multi-round memory retrieval, with a harness that verifies and corrects each step. \textsc{Homer} outperforms the previous best agent method by $+5.5$, $+10.8$, and $+4.4$ points on M3-Bench-robot, M3-Bench-web, and Video-MME-Long, and consistently lifts three various LLM backbones, indicating a model-agnostic structural capability for grounded retrieval over long videos.

</details>


### [242] [EventCoT: Event-centric Video Chain-of-thought for Reasoning Temporal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04872)
*Youngkil Song, Yoonjae Baek, Dongwon Kim, Inho Kim, Dongkeun Kim, Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EventCoT 是首个事件中心视频思维链框架，通过事件级 tokenization 将视频压缩为紧凑事件 token，再在识别的事件内推理生成答案，并通过嵌入匹配对齐占位 token 与视觉嵌入来实现时间区间定位。在 ActivityNet-RTL 上达到 SOTA，在 ReXTime 上取得强零样本结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时序定位（RTL）要求模型同时进行高层推理和精确的时间区间定位，现有方法难以兼顾两者。作者希望设计一个统一的框架，既能高效压缩视频信息，又能联合推理与定位。

Method: EventCoT 包含两个阶段：(1) 事件级 tokenization——将输入视频转换为紧凑的事件 token，高效筛选与问题相关的事件；(2) 在识别的事件内进行推理生成答案，并通过嵌入匹配（embedding matching）将占位 token 与视觉嵌入对齐，从而定位时间区间。

Result: 在 ActivityNet-RTL 上达到 SOTA，同时使用的视觉 token 数量远少于之前的工作。在 ReXTime 基准上取得强零样本结果，验证了方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: EventCoT 通过事件中心视频思维链有效解决了推理时序定位任务，在性能和效率上均优于现有方法，为视频时序推理提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EventCoT，面向推理时序定位（RTL）任务，结合事件级 tokenization 和视频思维链进行时序推理与定位。这属于视频理解与时序推理（video_understanding_temporal）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的范畴，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on video reasoning and temporal grounding, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Event-centric tokenization, Chain-of-thought for video, Temporal localization, SOTA on ActivityNet-RTL, Strong zero-shot on ReXTime

Abstract: Reasoning temporal localization (RTL) requires a model to generate an answer that itself contains the time interval supporting it, so high-level reasoning and precise temporal grounding must be produced jointly in a single response. To tackle this challenging task, we propose the first event-centric video chain-of-thought framework, dubbed EventCoT. EventCoT first performs event-centric tokenization of the input video to convert it into compact event tokens, enabling efficient identification of question-relevant events. It then reasons within the identified events to generate the answer, grounding the time interval via embedding matching that aligns placeholder tokens with visual embeddings. EventCoT achieves state-of-the-art results on ActivityNet-RTL for reasoning temporal localization while using substantially fewer visual tokens than previous work. To verify its general performance, we further evaluate EventCoT on the grounded video question answering benchmark ReXTime, where it attains strong zero-shot results.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-continuous_multimodal_reasoning'></div>

## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [243] [CritiqueDriveVLM: From Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning to Latent Thought Distillation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04179)
*Zhaohong Liu, Hao Ye, Xianlin Zhang, Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (70.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CritiqueDriveVLM 提出三阶段框架：先用多轮强化学习训练一个高精度但慢的 System-2 教师模型，再通过潜空间思维蒸馏将推理能力压缩到快速 System-1 学生模型中，在自动驾驶 VLM 上实现 88% 延迟降低的同时保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端 VLM 在自动驾驶中面临推理幻觉和保守偏差问题，传统 CoT 方法虽然能缓解但 token 消耗大、延迟高，无法满足实时部署需求。作者希望在不依赖外部工具和 CoT 的情况下，将深度推理能力内化到 VLM 中，同时保持低延迟。

Method: 提出三阶段框架 CritiqueDriveVLM：(1) 多轮强化学习阶段，使用多维验证器提供细粒度标量反馈和多轮惩罚，训练 System-2 教师模型；(2) 潜空间思维蒸馏阶段，将教师模型的推理状态对齐到学生模型的潜空间表示，压缩为无 CoT 的 System-1 学生模型；(3) 学生模型直接输出简短推理结果。

Result: 在 DriveLMM-01 基准上，教师模型 MCQ 从 55.54% 提升至 76.54%（SOTA）；蒸馏后的学生模型平均仅生成 28 个 token，推理延迟从 3482ms 降至 416ms（降低 88%），同时保持有竞争力的推理深度。

Conclusion: CritiqueDriveVLM 通过验证器引导的强化学习与潜空间思维蒸馏，有效解决了自动驾驶 VLM 中可靠性与效率的权衡问题，为低延迟、高鲁棒性的自动驾驶推理提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Latent Thought Distillation，将教师模型的推理状态蒸馏到学生模型的连续潜空间中，实现无 CoT 的快速推理，属于多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning 的范畴，与 canonical 子主题"连续多模态推理与潜空间思维"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal latent reasoning via Latent Thought Distillation, aligning with top-priority subtopic and boosting personal direction. Signals: Latent Thought Distillation, System-2 to System-1 compression, 88% latency reduction, verifier-guided RL for VLM, autonomous driving VLM

Abstract: End-to-end Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show immense potential in autonomous driving. However, standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) often suffers from reasoning hallucinations and conservative biases. While traditional tool-augmented frameworks and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) approaches mitigate these issues, they incur exorbitant token consumption and unacceptable latency, rendering real-time deployment impractical. To resolve this reliability-efficiency trade-off, we propose CritiqueDriveVLM, a novel unified three-stage framework internalizing reasoning directly into the VLM. First, we introduce Critique-Driven Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning (RL) guided by a multi-dimensional verifier. By providing granular scalar feedback and a multi-turn penalty, we force the policy to internalize logical deduction, cultivating a robust System-2 Teacher that achieves high accuracy without fragile external tools. Subsequently, we propose Latent Thought Distillation to overcome the latency bottleneck. By aligning the Student's latent representations with the Teacher's fully converged reasoning states, we compress deep logical capabilities into a fast, CoT-free System-1 Student. Extensive experiments on the widely-used DriveLMM-01 benchmark demonstrate remarkable improvements. Compared to the base model, our tool-free Teacher significantly boosts Multiple Choice Quality (MCQ) from 55.54% to a state-of-the-art 76.54%. Crucially, our distilled Student preserves competitive reasoning depth while drastically minimizing generation length to an average of merely 28 tokens. This slashes inference latency by 88% (from 3482 ms to 416 ms), paving a highly robust pathway for low-latency autonomous source code is available at .

</details>
